### [1] [Heavy-Tailed Memory Traces in Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00010)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"记忆使用形态"这一被忽视的评估视角，发现智能体记忆在有限上下文下呈核心-长尾分布，并据此设计了基于秩的记忆控制器CTWM，在保持覆盖与准确率的同时显著节省token并降低尾部预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长时程语言智能体依赖外部记忆作为"冻结的世界模型"，但对记忆系统的评价通常只看任务成功率或token成本，忽略了记忆使用的分布形态。作者认为在有限上下文与反复检索下，记忆会集中在一个小核心上，而稀有状态落入长尾，导致预测误差在长尾中累积，因此需要一个能刻画并利用这种形态的诊断与控制手段。

Method: 首先进行保守的尾部审计（tail audit），刻画不同策略下记忆检索的分布形态：随机游走智能体产生与对数正态相容的检索痕迹，语义LLM策略则产生与截断幂律相容的核心-长尾痕迹。基于该审计提出Core–Tail World Model（CTWM）：一种基于秩（rank-based）的记忆控制器，用单一指数τ分配提示预算，同时保留一个经过摘要的尾部表示。

Result: 在Synthetic Graph World上，CTWM保持了完整的状态与转移覆盖，相比图记忆基线减少提示token 5.9%，并使下半尾部预测误差降低13.6%。配对比较在ALFWorld上取得一致的token节省，在LongMemEval上减少24.48%的token且总体准确率持平。

Conclusion: 重尾记忆痕迹不仅是有限检索的一种诊断指标，也可作为实用的控制信号，用于构建token高效、覆盖完整的智能体世界模型，提示应把记忆使用的分布形态纳入评估与控制设计。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents increasingly rely on external memory as a frozen world model, yet current memory systems are usually judged only by task success or token cost. We argue that the missing object is the shape of memory use: under finite context and repeated retrieval, agent memory can concentrate on a small core while leaving rare states in a long tail where prediction errors accumulate. We study this effect through a conservative tail audit and find that concentration is reproducible but policy-dependent. Random-walk agents produce log-normal-compatible retrieval artifacts, whereas semantic LLM policies yield the strongest truncated-power-law-compatible core--tail traces. Motivated by this audit, we propose Core--Tail World Model (CTWM), a rank-based memory controller that allocates prompt budget with a single exponent $τ$ while retaining a summarized tail. On Synthetic Graph World, CTWM preserves full state and transition coverage, reduces prompt tokens by 5.9%, and lowers bottom-half tail prediction error by 13.6% relative to a graph-memory baseline. The same paired comparison gives consistent token savings on ALFWorld and a 24.48% token reduction on LongMemEval with aggregate accuracy parity. These results suggest that heavy-tailed memory traces are not only a diagnostic of finite retrieval, but also a practical control signal for token-efficient agent world models.

</details>


### [2] [When Do Causal World Models Help Modular LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00012)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出FedCausalCompose，一个面向模块化LLM智能体的因果世界模型框架，利用局部动作提供的干预-响应证据恢复跨模块接口，并指出因果结构只有在接口可统计辨识且以智能体可用的形式呈现时才有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化LLM智能体在订单、支付、库存、物流等模块间行动，一个模块的动作会改变其他模块的有效转移；标准世界模型只拟合观测轨迹，而观测轨迹无法区分"支付先于发货"是因果关系、由库存中介，还是隐藏共同触发因素所致，因此不满足干预时规划所需的信息。

Method: 提出FedCausalCompose因果世界模型框架：局部动作提供干预-响应证据，用于跨模块接口的恢复与因果组合；并在诊断性智能体环境中检验由理论推导出的预测。

Result: 理论上：观测世界模型在存在未阻断后门路径时具有不可约的干预误差；接口恢复随干预-响应覆盖度提升而改善；在覆盖度和局部机制误差受控时，oracle因果组合可超越非因果下界。实验上：因果接口在结构化工具环境中最有效，因API签名暴露前置条件与下游效应；而对话和叙事环境常忽略原始边列表，除非用简短注意力锚点使因果信息与决策相关。

Conclusion: 因果世界模型在LLM智能体中奏效有具体条件：跨模块接口既需统计上可辨识，又需以智能体行动时能使用的形式呈现。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act through modular systems, such as order, payment, inventory, and shipment services, where actions in one module change which transitions are valid in another. Standard world models usually fit observational traces, but this is not the quantity needed for intervention-time planning: a trace may show that payment precedes shipment without identifying whether payment authorizes shipment, inventory mediates the effect, or a hidden trigger explains both. We study this gap through FedCausalCompose, a causal world-model framework for modular LLM agents in which local actions provide intervention-response evidence for cross-module interfaces. We first show that observational world models incur an irreducible interventional error under unblocked back-door paths, that interface recovery improves with intervention-response coverage, and that an oracle causal composition can beat the non-causal lower bound when coverage and local mechanism errors are controlled. We then test the resulting prediction in diagnostic agent settings. Causal interfaces help most in structured tool environments, where API signatures expose preconditions and downstream effects. In contrast, dialogue and narrative environments often ignore raw edge lists unless a short attention anchor makes the causal information decision-relevant. These results identify a concrete condition for causal world models in LLM agents: causal structure helps when cross-module interfaces are both statistically identifiable and presented in a form the agent can use at action time.

</details>


### [3] [From Proposal to Verified Effect: Praxa, an Evidence-Bound Harness for Governed AI Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00015)
*Stefan G. Creadore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Praxa 是一个使"提议—授权—派发—效果—晋升"状态显式化的智能体运行框架，但其四类证据均不足以证明优越性，仅支持一个"证据绑定"的可测架构贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体能够提出并执行动作，但"提议、授权、派发、经验证的外部效果、服务晋升"是彼此不同的主张，容易被混为一谈。作者希望把这些状态显式化，使"授权到效果"的转换可测试、可核对。

Method: 提出 Praxa 框架，通过确定性准入、代理执行、外部读回、对账、以及经审查的晋升来显式表征上述状态。证据分四条线：仓库本地审计（固定版本）、提供商支持的 Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 试点（12 个任务、基线 vs 可靠性层各 36 次严格试验）、调试后两阶协调代理开发对比（基线 vs 候选各 180 次试验，含崩溃恢复）、以及已部署的源码/配置证据（反思、召回核算、记忆编译、工具健康路径）。

Result: 证据线一：1,027/1,027 单元测试与 89/89 Workerd 测试通过，363 个源文件全部插桩并满足四项覆盖率底线，但无原始逐测记录且无法独立复现。证据线二：基线与可靠性层各通过 17/36，可靠性层输入/输出 token 分别增加 37.49%/50.73%，不支持优越性。证据线三：基线与候选各完成 180/180，准确率相同、均可全量崩溃恢复、零受保护违规；候选 token 少 37.11%、端点成本低 33.84%、步数少 11.63%，但不代表质量、延迟或生产行为更优。证据线四：显示出有界反思、召回核算、记忆编译与工具健康路径，但无生产结果提升。

Conclusion: Praxa 的受支持贡献是一个证据绑定的架构，使"授权到效果"的转换显式且可测试。当前证据尚不能确立对抗性安全、生产安全、通用专家优越性、自主递归优化或用户收益。

Abstract: Large-language-model agents can propose and execute actions, but proposal, authority, dispatch, verified external effect, and serving promotion are different claims. We present Praxa, an agent harness that represents these states explicitly through deterministic admission, brokered execution, external read-back, reconciliation, and reviewed promotion. We report four evidence lanes. First, an author-run repository-local audit at a pinned revision passed 1,027/1,027 unit tests and 89/89 Workerd tests, instrumented all 363 expected source files, and met four coverage floors; raw per-test transcripts and independent reproduction are unavailable. Second, in a provider-backed Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 pilot across 12 curated tasks, baseline and reliability-layer arms each passed 17/36 strict trials. The reliability layer used 37.49% more input and 50.73% more output tokens, so the pilot does not support superiority. Third, in a post-debug, two-order coordination-proxy development comparison, baseline and a source-authored candidate each completed 180/180 trials with equal measured accuracy, full hermetic crash recovery, and zero protected violations. The candidate used 37.11% fewer tokens, 33.84% lower estimated endpoint cost, and 11.63% fewer steps; this does not establish improved quality, latency, or production behavior. Fourth, deployed source/configuration evidence shows bounded reflection, recall accounting, memory compilation, and tool-health paths, but no production outcome lift. Praxa's supported contribution is an evidence-bound architecture that makes authority-to-effect transitions explicit and testable. Current evidence does not establish adversarial security, production safety, general specialist superiority, autonomous recursive optimization, or user benefit.

</details>


### [4] [What Do Rationales Communicate? A Message-Intervention Study in Role-Specialized QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00018)
*Jiameng Zhang,Hongqiu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"消息干预"诊断方法，在固定证据与候选答案的前提下只改变从推理者传到验证者的理由（rationale），发现忠实的理由几乎不提升答案准确率，但被损坏的理由会显著改变验证者的支持性判断，说明理由共享应被视为一种验证消息机制而非提升准确率的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色分工式问答流水线（推理者→验证者）日益流行，但理由这一消息究竟带来什么并不清楚：是更好的答案、更强的支持性评估，还是新的失败面。作者希望厘清这一跨角色消息传递的真实作用与风险。

Method: 提出消息干预诊断：固定证据和候选答案，仅改变跨推理者-验证者边界传递的理由。在400个MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA样本上，以DeepSeek分别作生成器与验证器进行实验，并设置盲验证提示与显式理由核查提示两种条件作对比，辅以人工审计及跨模型、任务边界检验。

Result: 忠实理由相对无理由几乎不增加答案准确率；损坏理由则强烈改变支持判断。盲提示下无害改写仅使支持度变化0–2.5%，而损坏理由变化10–22%；显式理由核查提示放大至34–55%。最终答案变化较小（2–30%），且仅2.9–35.3%的支持翻转伴随答案改变。人工审计中16/42有效损坏属"过度信任损坏"情形，人类对模型接受的10条损坏理由中有9条拒绝或标记为不清楚。

Conclusion: 理由共享应作为验证消息机制来评估，而不仅仅是通往更高答案准确率的路径；它为验证者引入了可被过度信任的新失败面，尤其在鼓励核查理由时会放大该问题。

Abstract: Role-specialized QA pipelines increasingly pass rationales from a reasoner to a verifier, but it is unclear what this message actually buys: better answers, stronger support assessment, or a new failure surface. We introduce a message-intervention diagnostic that fixes the evidence and candidate answer while varying only the rationale passed across the reasoner-to-verifier boundary. On 400 MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultiHopQA examples with DeepSeek as generator and verifier, faithful rationales add almost no answer accuracy over no rationale, while corrupted rationales strongly alter support judgments. Under a blind verifier prompt, harmless paraphrases shift support by only 0--2.5%, whereas corrupted rationales shift support by 10--22%; an explicit rationale-checking prompt amplifies the same pattern to 34--55%. Final answers move less (2--30%), and only 2.9--35.3% of corrupted support flips co-occur with answer changes. Human audits show why this matters: 16/42 valid corruptions are corruption-overtrust cases, and blind humans reject or mark unclear 9/10 audited corrupted rationales that the model accepts. Cross-model and task-boundary checks show when the channel is active, amplified, inert, or folded into the task label. Rationale sharing should be evaluated as a verification-message mechanism, not merely as a route to higher answer accuracy.

</details>


### [5] [Measuring the Microtask Eligibility Gap: When Is an Off-the-Shelf SLM Enough for an Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00025)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了4个微任务基准（固定提示、自动指标、CI感知阈值），系统测试Qwen3各尺寸及量化版本，发现16/16配置全部不达标，且量化带来的损失主要随模型规模而非精度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent系统越来越多地希望用小语言模型（SLM）承担前沿LLM规划器周边的微任务（如自动批准shell命令、写记忆、选工具、排序历史轮次），但缺乏对小模型是否真的能胜任这些任务的实证检验，也未厘清失败原因及量化是否改变结论。

Method: 构建含4个微任务的基准，每个任务都有固定提示、自动指标、预先设定的阈值τ（锚定于廉价非LLM基线）以及CI感知的合格规则（置信下界须越过τ）。测试Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B在FP16、贪心解码、单一冻结提示下的表现；用logprob决策阈值诊断将失败分为能力缺陷与决策阈值可调两类（4种情形）；再将模型量化至4-bit（RTN/GPTQ/AWQ）检验影响，并在Llama-3.x上复现、做τ扫描与提示改写鲁棒性检验。

Result: 16个配置（4任务×4模型）无一通过合格规则；量化会带来随模型规模变化的损害，但未使任何配置达标；在Llama-3.x上同样12/12不合格；τ扫描与提示措辞（每单元原始提示+3种中性改写，共0/112合格）均稳健；示例中4B重排器在BM25短名单上优于BM25（+0.047 [0.020, 0.073]），但自身仍未获资格认证。

Conclusion: SLM应放在满足CI支撑阈值的基线之后使用——仅当基线未达标处才调用SLM；资格差距主要由模型规模而非量化精度驱动，故不能靠量化来弥补这一差距。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly want to run small language models (SLMs) on the microtasks around a frontier large language model (LLM) planner: auto-approving shell commands, writing memory, selecting tools, ranking past turns. We ask whether off-the-shelf SLMs meet practitioner-defined thresholds and, when they fail, why, and whether quantization changes the answer. We build a benchmark of 4 such microtasks with fixed prompts and automatic metrics, each with a pre-specified threshold $τ$ anchored to a cheap non-LLM baseline and a CI-aware eligibility rule (a configuration passes only if its confidence bound clears $τ$). Sweeping Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B at their best (FP16, greedy, one frozen prompt, no tuning), we find an eligibility gap: 0 of 16 (4 tasks $\times$ 4 models) configurations pass (verified by checking the raw outputs and parser behavior). A logprob decision-threshold diagnostic (T1/T3/T4; T2 via a context-length/cascade probe) separates the failures into capability deficits and failures that can be addressed by changing the decoding threshold (4 regimes). Quantization to 4-bit (RTN/GPTQ/AWQ) does damage that depends on model size and moves no configuration into eligibility (certified on the reconstructable hard-label tasks T1/T3, diagnostic/windowed robustness on T2/T4), so the gap tracks model size more than precision; it replicates on Llama-3.x (12/12 ineligible) and is robust to the anchor choice (a $τ$-sweep) and to prompt wording (0/112 eligible across the original plus 3 neutral paraphrases per cell). The practical implication: place SLMs behind a baseline that meets the CI-backed threshold, and use the SLM only where the baseline fails to meet the threshold; e.g. a 4B re-ranker over a BM25 shortlist beats BM25 ($+0.047$ [0.020, 0.073], without itself certifying eligibility).

</details>


### [6] [Characterizing a Configuration Where Inference-Time PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting Is Inert: Evidence from Three Reasoning LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00047)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文将"PRM剪枝片段移植"（PPFG）这一跨轨迹步骤级迁移机制单独隔离并在其最简操作点进行检验，发现在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct+Math-Shepherd于MATH500上，PPFG与独立并行CoT基线在所有测量维度上统计上不可区分，并通过等值检验将其升级为正向等值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行思维链存在多样性崩溃，现有做法是在解码时干预，其中一个自然设计是：当过程奖励模型（PRM）剪枝某条链时，提取其高PRM前缀并逐字移植为上下文示例注入到仍在解码的兄弟链中。作者希望在最省成本、最纯粹的操作点隔离该机制，检验在先前提工作需额外补偿条件才见增益的设定下，该机制本身是否有效。

Method: 将PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting（PPFG）作为跨轨迹步骤级迁移的最小成本操作化进行隔离测试。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Math-Shepherd上，对完整MATH500（n=500，三个随机种子）测试停滞目标与随机目标两种变体。对322次停滞规则注入事件做四桶分类，分析触发质量。进一步做复合门控精炼与随机对照实验，并在三种基础LM、六个基准、第二个PRM及兼容性门控扫描上复现，使用双单侧检验（TOST）将平价升级为正向等值，另做每事件抽查与幸存兄弟反事实分析，以及后见之明oracle上界估计。

Result: PPFG两种变体在所有测量轴上与独立并行CoT基线统计不可区分。322次注入事件中仅14%针对真正陷入困境的链，其余落在已成功、接近完成或处于平坦PRM平台的链上，救援移植无法改变。无复合门控精炼能同时实现精准触发与足够密度，随机对照在2.4倍触发率下同样平价，说明惰性并非启发式特有。结论在三种基础LM、六个基准、第二个PRM及兼容性门控扫描上复现；TOST将平价提升为全部十二个Qwen/LLaMA单元上的正向等值。每事件抽查显示注入链的剪枝率是匹配步骤的2.75倍，但幸存兄弟反事实证明无种群层面补偿。后见oracle将选PPFG相对独立的单题增益上界限制在+0.13个百分点。

Conclusion: PPFG在测试操作点上是一个无效的推理时干预机制，其惰性源于触发质量差且缺乏种群层面的补偿。作者提供了一个用于确立推理时机制零结果的等值检验模板，所有结论均限定在其测试的操作点范围内。

Abstract: Diversity collapse in parallel chain-of-thought has motivated inference-time interventions built on a natural design: when a process reward model (PRM) prunes a chain, its high-PRM prefix is extracted and grafted verbatim as an in-context demonstration into a still-decoding sibling. We isolate this mechanism, PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting (PPFG), as the most cost-minimal operationalization of cross-trajectory step-level transfer, and test it at the operating point where prior fragment-grafting work reports gains only under additional compensating ingredients. On Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with Math-Shepherd on full MATH500 (n=500, three seeds), PPFG in both stagnation- and random-targeting variants is statistically indistinguishable from an independent parallel-CoT baseline on every measured axis. We characterize why: a four-bucket classification of 322 stagnation-rule injection events shows only 14% targeted a genuinely struggling chain; the rest landed on chains that had already succeeded, were near completion, or sat on a flat PRM plateau, states a rescue graft cannot change. No compound-gate refinement jointly achieves well-targeted firing and adequate density, and a random control matches the same parity at 2.4x the firing rate, so the inertness is not heuristic-specific. The finding replicates across three base LMs, six benchmarks, a second PRM, and a compatibility-gate sweep; two-one-sided-tests analysis promotes the parity to positive equivalence on all twelve Qwen/LLaMA cells. A per-event spot-check finds injected chains prune at 2.75x the matched-step rate, but a surviving-sibling counterfactual finds no population-level compensation. A hindsight oracle bounds any per-problem gain from choosing PPFG over independent at +0.13 pp. We contribute an equivalence-testing template for establishing inference-time mechanism nulls, with every claim scoped to its tested operating point.

</details>


### [7] [Gradient-Aligned Pair Selection for Personalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00061)
*Ruoming Jin,Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Lei Xu,Haixun Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐的优化问题，提出GAP-DPO算法，通过效用感知、几何对齐的偏好对选择提升个性化生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化LLM需要使生成行为对齐用户特定偏好而非整体质量。DPO虽提供稳定的偏好学习框架，但其在个性化场景下的效果关键取决于偏好对的选取方式。现有方法多依赖启发式标准（如基于似然的极值），将优化与显式用户效用解耦，可能导致个性化性能下降。

Method: 作者通过分析期望用户效用梯度与DPO更新方向之间的一阶交互，将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐优化问题。分析表明，在off-policy采样下，当偏好间隔与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO更新从纯误差修正信号转变为类强化学习更新。据此提出GAP-DPO迭代算法，执行效用感知、几何对齐的偏好对选择，并通过逐轮再生控制分布偏移。

Result: 在个性化文本生成基准上，GAP-DPO在风格保真度、偏好对齐和生成质量方面均优于标准DPO变体。

Conclusion: 研究确立梯度对齐作为个性化偏好优化的统一原则，并表明偏好对选择是优化几何的内在组成部分，而非启发式预处理步骤。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires aligning generation behavior with user-specific preferences rather than aggregate quality. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) provides a stable framework for preference learning, its effectiveness in personalized settings critically depends on how preference pairs are selected. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic criteria, such as likelihood-based extremes, which decouple optimization from explicit user utility and can lead to degraded personalization. We formalize personalized preference learning as a geometry-aligned optimization problem by analyzing the first-order interaction between gradients of expected user utility and DPO update directions. Our analysis reveals that, under off-policy sampling, the DPO update transitions from a purely error-corrective signal to a reinforcement-like update when preference margins are directionally aligned with utility gradients. This perspective exposes pair selection as a geometric decision that governs whether preference optimization advances or hinders personalization. Motivated by this insight, we propose GAP-DPO (Geometry-Aligned Preference DPO), an iterative algorithm that performs utility-aware, geometry-aligned pair selection while controlling distribution shift via epoch-wise regeneration. Experiments on personalized text generation benchmarks show that GAP-DPO consistently improves stylistic fidelity, preference alignment, and generation quality compared to standard DPO variants. Together, our results establish gradient alignment as a unifying principle for personalized preference optimization and demonstrate that pair selection is an intrinsic component of the optimization geometry rather than a heuristic preprocessing step.

</details>


### [8] [K-Dense BYOK: An Open-Source AI Research Assistant That Runs Locally and Keeps a Hash-Chained Lab Notebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00074)
*Aubrey M. Brueckner,Darshil Patel,Yuhuan He,Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: K-Dense BYOK 是一款免费开源的本地运行 AI 科研助手，通过科学流程库、活页实验记录本和独立行为日志来提升科研可核查性，在跨学科提示评测中于科学质量与执行上领先两个托管平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用聊天助手和编程智能体在科研场景中缺乏科学脚手架与可追溯记录，且模型过度声称（overclaiming）是最常见失败模式。作者希望让科研工作留在研究者自控的机器上，并让智能体的主张变得可核查，而非简单禁止。

Method: 研究者自带模型访问权限（本地或托管），应用提供运行环境、科学脚手架与完整记录；每个项目是普通文件夹，数据、代码、结果与记录均保存在本地。区别于聊天助手的三点：内置科学流程库、工作流模板、数据源目录及审阅/写作角色；活页实验记录本（条目相互关联、只增不删）；通过观察智能体行为而非采信其自述来记录，日志智能体无写入工具。在20个跨学科科研提示上，用事先固定的评分量表评估。

Result: 在20个跨学科科研提示上，K-Dense BYOK 在科学质量和研究执行两方面均领先两个托管平台。其交付物是唯一记录了所运行软件的，也通常是唯一附带可重新生成结果命令的。其中一个使用相同前沿模型的托管平台两者皆无。环境记录由智能体写入的文件构成，尚不属于被观察日志的一部分，日志目前还未捕获软件环境本身。

Conclusion: K-Dense BYOK 通过可核查的记录机制应对模型过度声称问题，在评测中优于托管平台，并以 MIT 许可证开源（https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok）。但其日志尚未覆盖软件环境记录，仍有改进空间。

Abstract: K-Dense BYOK (bring your own keys) is a free, open-source AI research assistant for scientists in any field that runs on the researcher's own computer. The researcher supplies access to a model of their choice, hosted or running locally, and the application supplies everything else: a place for the work to run, a layer of scientific scaffolding, and a complete record. Each project is an ordinary folder, so the data, the code, the results, and the record stay on a machine the researcher administers and can be read years later without the application. Three things separate it from a chat assistant or a general-purpose coding agent. It ships a library of written scientific procedures, guided workflow templates, catalogs of where research data can be found, and reviewer and writer roles the agent can hand work to. It keeps a Living Lab Notebook whose entries link into an argument and are added to but never erased. And it records what happened by watching what the agent does rather than by taking the agent's word for it, in a log the agent has no tool that can write to. That choice targets the most common failure, model overclaiming, in our earlier benchmark of nine frontier models, by making claims checkable rather than preventing them. On twenty interdisciplinary research prompts, scored under a rubric fixed in advance, K-Dense BYOK led two managed platforms on both scientific quality and research execution. Its deliverables were the only ones that recorded the software they ran in, and the only ones that usually arrived with a command that regenerates the results. One of the managed platforms ran the same frontier model and supplied neither. Those environment records were files the agent wrote, not part of the observed log, which does not yet capture the software environment itself. The code is available under the MIT license at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok.

</details>


### [9] [Scientific Agents: Evaluating Profession-Specific System Prompts on Scientific Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00084)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在503个职业专属AGENTS.md配置与Gemini 3.8 Flash上进行对照实验发现：加载完整职业系统提示并未带来一致的准确率提升，反而使输出token增加1.5-2.3倍、单次成本增加2.2-4.5倍；工具类任务中甚至因更容易触发长度/时间限制而显著降低解题率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍为科学智能体配置详细的职业专属系统提示，认为这能提升专业任务表现，但这类提示会显著增加token消耗与单次响应成本。论文希望验证：这种"专业角色提示"带来的准确率收益是否真实存在，以判断其成本是否值得。

Method: 构建并开源Scientific Agents语料库，包含503个职业专属AGENTS.md配置；使用Gemini 3.8 Flash经OpenRouter在Pi智能体框架中运行。设置四类对照：极简基线（"You are a helpful assistant"）、配置的开场角色句、通用科学严谨性指南、以及来自无关领域的配置。在9个文本科学基准（4,531题、100组配对配置）和60道BioMysteryBench生物信息学工具使用题（每条件三次运行）上评测，采用自动化规则评分，并做固定任务上的bootstrap区间估计。

Result: 文本基准上：配置文件与基线的平均准确率差为-0.6个百分点（95% bootstrap区间[-1.5, +0.2]），无任何基准显示统计上明确的提升；配置文件产生1.5-2.3倍输出token，单次成功调用成本高2.2-4.5倍。工具类任务上：BioMysteryBench平均解题率46.7%（配置）对56.7%（基线），差-10.0个百分点（区间[-16.7, -3.3]），主因是配置条件下更频繁触发token与时间上限停止。意外发现：在SuperGPQA上，频繁的供应商API中断使短基线首轮正确率仅54.0%，而配置为71.6%；通用与错配提示可靠性相近，说明该优势来自提示长度或格式而非领域专业性。

Conclusion: 在所测模型与任务上，默认加载完整职业配置并不能提升准确率，且成本明显更高，因此不建议无条件使用长职业提示。提示长度/格式可能在API不稳定场景下带来鲁棒性优势。未来需验证按需检索配置片段或开放式科学任务是否会改变这一结论。

Abstract: Detailed profession-specific system prompts raise token use and estimated cost per response without a consistent accuracy gain. We evaluate Scientific Agents, an open-source corpus of 503 profession-specific AGENTS.md profiles, with Gemini 3.8 Flash via OpenRouter in the Pi agent harness. We compare matched profiles with four controls: a minimal baseline ("You are a helpful assistant"), the profile's opening role sentence, a generic scientific rigor guide, and a profile from an unrelated domain. Across nine text-based science benchmarks (4,531 sampled questions, 100 matched profiles), 4,488 items completed all five conditions after API-error retries, scored with automated, rule-based grading. The average profile-baseline accuracy difference is -0.6 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval [-1.5, +0.2] across fixed tasks), and no benchmark shows a statistically clear improvement. Matched profiles produced 1.5-2.3 times as many output tokens and cost 2.2-4.5 times more per successful call. On 60 tool-using BioMysteryBench bioinformatics problems (three runs each for baseline and profile), mean solve rates were 46.7% with the profile and 56.7% at baseline, a difference of -10.0 percentage points (95% interval [-16.7, -3.3]) driven by more frequent token- and time-limit stops under the profile. Longer prompts had one unexpected operational advantage: on SuperGPQA, frequent provider API drops left the short baseline with a correct first-pass answer on only 54.0% of items, against 71.6% with the profile. Generic and mismatched prompts were about as reliable, so this gain comes from prompt length or formatting rather than domain expertise. For the tested model and tasks, loading full profession profiles by default does not improve accuracy and costs considerably more; whether selective retrieval of profile sections or open-ended scientific tasks would change this remains to be tested.

</details>


### [10] [Comedic Fool's Gold: Reward Exploits and Countermeasures in Conversational Humor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00197)
*Sam Larson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对话幽默训练中的自动化奖励设计，揭示两类奖励的利用漏洞及对策，并指出奖励设计的核心挑战在于既要阻断漏洞又要保留目标行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语言模型训练设计自动化奖励时，奖励可能被模型"钻空子"（reward exploit）。作者希望探究在对话幽默任务中，基于"可理解的意外感"和"预期观众愉悦度"的两类奖励是否可靠，以及如何设计对策。

Method: 针对两类奖励分别测试：一是基于嵌入的"意外感"奖励，并尝试用流畅度过滤器作为对策；二是基于观众模型的"预测笑声"奖励，尝试通过跨说话人归一化笑声线索作为对策。随后进行三轮强化学习训练，逐步修订奖励。

Result: 嵌入式意外感奖励会把打乱顺序的回复与机智回复同等接受；流畅度过滤器虽能识别打乱回复，但联合奖励会误拒部分机智回复且未通过后续验证。观众模型奖励易受任一方消息中的笑声线索影响，跨说话人归一化可阻断已知攻击，但未匹配的表达仍可被利用。最终一轮训练使综合评分提升0.0903、零分会话减少40%，但幽默专项提升仍低于预注册目标。

Conclusion: 自动化奖励设计的普遍难题在于：对策必须阻断可被利用的捷径，同时保留奖励本意鼓励的行为。幽默奖励尤其难以平衡此二者。

Abstract: We investigate automated rewards for training language models in conversational humor, focusing on reward exploits and countermeasures. Two approaches aim to capture understandable surprise and predicted audience amusement. Controlled tests show that an embedding-based surprise reward accepts word-shuffled replies as readily as witty ones. A fluency filter detects the shuffles, but the combined reward also rejects some witty replies and fails further validation. An audience model's predicted laughter is instead vulnerable to laughter cues in either speaker's messages. Normalizing these cues across speakers blocks the covered attacks, although unmatched expressions remain exploitable. Three reinforcement-learning runs evaluate training with successive reward revisions. The final run improves the combined evaluation score by 0.0903 and reduces zero-score sessions by 40%, but its humor-specific improvement remains below our preregistered target. These findings illustrate a broader challenge for automated reward design: countermeasures must block exploitable shortcuts while preserving the behavior the reward was intended to encourage.

</details>


### [11] [ABDA-NL: A Natural-Language Scenario Explorer for Argument-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00947)
*Shawn Bowers,Martin Caminada,Haoyang Liu,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABDA-NL 为基于 ASPIC- 知识库的论证讨论系统 ABDA 增加了自然语言接口，用户可查看结论状态、展开论证博弈、进行假设/规则的 what-if 探索、基于文档问答，并用英文编写新事实、假设和规则，LLM 仅负责语言与形式化之间的转换，所有编辑需用户确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让不具备形式化背景的用户也能使用基于 ASPIC- 的论证系统，降低操作与理解门槛，同时保留确定性引擎的可靠性。

Method: 在 ABDA 上叠加自然语言层：LLM 负责从参考文档和场景状态回答问题，并将英文编辑转换为候选形式化语句；ABDA 引擎在 grounded 语义下独立计算论证、攻击与接受标签；模型输出经验证并由用户确认后才生效。

Result: 用户能够查看结论的接受、拒绝或未定状态，打开 grounded 讨论博弈的交互式呈现以了解原因，通过暂停假设/规则或更改偏好探索 what-if 方案，并基于文档问答及用英文撰写新事实、假设和规则。

Conclusion: 该系统通过 LLM 充当语言与形式化之间的桥梁，在保持 ABDA 引擎为论证与接受标签唯一来源的前提下，提供了更易用的自然语言交互界面。

Abstract: ABDA-NL adds a natural-language interface to ABDA, a system for argument-based discussion using ASPIC- knowledge bases under grounded semantics. Users see which conclusions are accepted, rejected, or undecided, open an interactive rendering of the grounded discussion game to learn why, explore what-if alternatives by suspending assumptions and rules or changing preferences, ask questions that are answered from a scenario's reference documents, and author new facts, assumptions, and rules in plain English. A large language model provides the bridge between language and formalism: it answers questions from the documents and the current state of the scenario, and it translates plain-English edits into candidate formal statements. The deterministic ABDA engine remains the sole source of arguments, attacks, and acceptance labels, and every proposal of the model is validated and confirmed by the user before it takes effect.

</details>


### [12] [EviGraph: Proof-Carrying Selective Recommendation over Temporal Public-Service Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00212)
*Yixi Zhou,Sikun Wang,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出EviGraph方法，区分"关键决策需求"与"可保留未解决的信息"，以语言智能体将关键需求链接到时序知识图谱中的证据，并用确定性检查器判定推荐是否有据，从而减少不必要的弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务推荐需要与服务类型、范围和日期相匹配的证据，但若把每个缺失细节都视为决定性因素，会导致过度弃答，从而错失有用推荐。因此需要区分哪些信息是决策所必需的，哪些可以暂不解决。

Method: EviGraph由两部分组成：一是语言智能体，将关键决策需求链接到时序知识图谱中的证据；二是确定性检查器，用于判定某项推荐是否得到支持。作者在香港双语公共服务基准上（含可执行的政策引用）进行评估。

Result: 该区分显著减少了不必要的弃答。但额外的验证步骤可能撤回本已被支持的推荐，且并未提升决策质量。

Conclusion: 可靠的基于证据的导航，关键在于明确"一个决策必须确立什么"，而非简单地增加更多验证。

Abstract: Public-service recommendations require evidence that matches the requested service, scope, and date. Yet treating every missing detail as decisive can withhold useful recommendations. We introduce EviGraph, which distinguishes critical decision requirements from information that can remain unresolved. A language agent links these requirements to evidence in a temporal knowledge graph, while a deterministic checker establishes whether a recommendation is supported. Evaluation on a bilingual Hong Kong public-service benchmark with executable policy references shows that this distinction reduces unnecessary abstention. Additional verification, however, can withdraw supported recommendations without improving decision quality. These findings suggest that reliable evidence-based navigation depends on specifying what must be established for a decision, rather than simply adding more verification.

</details>


### [13] [Cybernetic and Epistemic: A Missing Vocabulary for Trustworthy Agentic Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00961)
*Jérémie Lumbroso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出AI代码生成时代监督瓶颈在于"词汇缺口"：混淆了语言的协调行动功能（控制论）与协调理解功能（认识论），提出以"反事实条件"为核心的智能体治理准则及可操作的"两部分重构测试"与ORRCF审议记录规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代码生成日益交给AI系统，瓶颈从"写代码"转向"监督写代码的系统"。然而领域内呼吁"人类监督"时缺乏有效区分：语言在委托通道中既用于协调行动（控制论式，词语成功与否取决于世界是否与之匹配），又用于协调理解（认识论式，成功取决于是否对世界负责且听者能核查）。当前的失败并非控制论语言本身，而是"认识论形态的语言在做控制论的工作"——以解释为外形、却为获得认可而非求真而校准的输出。仅检查输出是否被批准的监督可被"橡皮图章"式通过，而真正问责需要推理过程可检索、可核查。

Method: 作者呈现三个委托案例（作为示例而非受控证据），展示认识论参与既可行又可审计：其中包括一个公共记录中建议按其自身所述条件被撤回，以及一个失败案例，说明监督无需为自由裁量选择提供理由。在此基础上，提出面向智能体系统治理的准则：每个重要选择都应附带"若情况不同将会如何"的条件，且以第三方可测试的形式呈现。并给出该准则的可操作形式——两部分重构测试（由第二位读者预测智能体在扰动下会做什么来给委托记录打分），以及一种审议记录规范ORRCF，将上述条件设为每条记录选择的必备组成部分。

Result: 通过三个委托案例（含一个建议按自身条件撤回的公共记录、一个揭示无理由监督缺陷的失败案例）验证了认识论参与的可实践性与可审计性；提出并形式化了"反事实条件"治理准则、两部分重构测试和ORRCF记录规范。

Conclusion: 在现有技术信任属性之外，应新增一项治理准则：每个重要选择都应携带其在什么条件下会走向不同结果的信息，并以第三方可测试的形式呈现。若缺乏此类条件，第三方就无法区分一个真正的决策与一次橡皮图章式批准。ORRCF规范与两部分重构测试为该准则提供了可操作的落地形式。

Abstract: As code generation is increasingly delegated to AI systems, the bottleneck is shifting from writing code to supervising the systems that write it --- a shift CS-education researchers have begun to name. This shift exposes a vocabulary gap: the field asks for "human oversight" without a working distinction between the two things language does in a delegation channel --- coordinate action (cybernetic: words succeed when the world comes to match them) and coordinate understanding (epistemic: they succeed when they answer to the world and a hearer can check that they do). The failure this names is not cybernetic language but epistemic-form language doing cybernetic work: explanation-shaped output calibrated for approval rather than truth. Oversight that checks only whether an output was approved is satisfiable by rubber-stamping; oversight that holds an agent accountable requires the reasoning behind its work be retrievable and checkable. We present three delegation episodes --- illustrations, not controlled evidence --- in which epistemic engagement proved practicable while remaining auditable, one public record where a recommendation was withdrawn on its own stated terms, and one failure case illustrating oversight that requires no reasons for its discretionary choices. We propose a criterion for agentic-system governance, alongside existing technical trust properties: every consequential choice should carry the condition under which it would have gone otherwise, in a form a third party can test. Without such a condition, a third party cannot distinguish a decision from a rubber stamp. We give the criterion an operational form --- a two-part reconstruction test scoring a delegation record by whether a second reader can predict what the agent does under a perturbation --- and a deliberation-recording convention, ORRCF, that makes the condition a required component of every recorded choice.

</details>


### [14] [Build2SPARQL: A Large-Scale Text-to-SPARQL Benchmark Dataset for Building Knowledge Graph Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00224)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Build2SPARQL，一个面向建筑语义知识图谱的大规模text-to-SPARQL基准，通过图遍历代码生成并验证SPARQL、由大模型生成自然语言问题，构建了6,136条可执行查询和30,680个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统正以Brick、ASHRAE 223P等本体表示为语义知识图谱，text-to-SPARQL可让运维人员用自然语言查询，但缺乏大规模自然语言/SPARQL基准，制约了进展。

Method: 采用知识图谱驱动的流水线：SPARQL查询完全由图遍历代码生成并验证，大模型仅负责生成自然语言问题，从而保证查询正确性独立于模型行为；挖掘六类查询模式（线性链、分支、UNION、聚合、OPTIONAL、属性过滤），每种查询以五种词汇语域表述。

Result: 在201个建筑知识图谱（180个Brick、21个ASHRAE 223P）上生成6,136条可执行SPARQL查询和30,680个问题；300个问题的双人人工验证显示语义保真度98.8%、自然度98.8%、操作合理性84.0%;在三个开放权重语言模型上的检索增强评估将精确匹配准确率从零样本的0.2-20%提升至三样本检索的56-65%。

Conclusion: Build2SPARQL为建筑知识图谱的text-to-SPARQL提供了大规模、可执行且经人工验证的基准，检索增强可显著提升开放权重模型的查询准确率。

Abstract: Building automation systems are increasingly represented as semantic knowledge graphs (KGs) using ontologies such as Brick and ASHRAE 223P, creating a machine-readable substrate for artificial-intelligence applications. One promising application is translating natural-language questions into SPARQL (text-to-SPARQL), which would let building operators query these graphs through language agents, but progress is limited by the scarcity of large natural-language/SPARQL benchmarks. This paper presents Build2SPARQL, a large-scale benchmark for building KGs generated by a KG-grounded pipeline: SPARQL queries are produced and validated entirely by graph-traversal code, while large language models generate only the natural-language questions, keeping query correctness independent of model behavior. The pipeline mines six query-pattern families -- linear chains, branching, UNION, aggregation, OPTIONAL, and attribute-filtered -- and phrases each query across five vocabulary registers. Applied to 201 building KGs (180 Brick, 21 ASHRAE 223P), it yields 6,136 executable SPARQL queries and 30,680 questions. A two-rater human validation of 300 questions found 98.8% semantic fidelity, 98.8% naturalness, and 84.0% operational plausibility. A retrieval-augmented evaluation across three open-weight language models raised exact-match accuracy from 0.2-20% (zero-shot) to 56-65% (three-shot retrieved).

</details>


### [15] [Robust Is Salient: An Informed Adversary Moves the Optimal Signal onto the Salience Pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00233)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在受约束信号通道中引入有说服预算的对抗者后，最优信号从后验最大化转向边际最大化，且该对抗鲁棒最优与既有研究中的"显著性极点"在绝大多数项目上完全重合，从而在结构上限制了对抗意识的实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当知情对手与受众共享一个受约束的信号通道时，什么样的信号最能保护真相？作者希望形式化"对抗鲁棒"的最优信号，并检验其与既有显著性理论中"显著性极点"的关系，同时揭示当前评估方法可能存在的结构性局限。

Method: 从《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》抽象出一个强迫选择任务，引入一个知情对抗者：它知道目标、观察信号，并用说服预算 β 为最强的错误答案辩护。分析 β 从 0 增长时最优信号的移动，并在 108 个确认性项目以及一个 20 万项目池上对比对抗鲁棒最优与显著性极点。另用两种对抗框架测试七个语言模型在 108 个项目上的选项变化。

Result: 在 108 个确认性项目上，对抗鲁棒最优与既有显著性极点完全一致；20 万项目池中两者仅在 2,748 个项目上有差异，且恰好落在显著性—贝叶斯坐标未定义处。β 增大时最优信号从后验最大化转向边际最大化，β=0 时复现原始 oracle 模型（听众温度 τ=1）。池中 18.2% 的项目在有限预算下最优解发生移动，每个项目的临界预算可精确计算。两种对抗框架使七个语言模型在 108 个项目中的 30–77 个上改变选项。

Conclusion: 由于对抗鲁棒最优与显著性启发式目标在评估项目上常常完全相同，无法判断语言模型的选项移动是朝向对抗感知最优还是朝向显著性，这是结构性限制而非零结果。作者建议在评估对抗意识之前，先做廉价诊断：检查鲁棒目标是否与启发式目标在评估项目上重合。

Abstract: When an informed adversary shares the audience of a constrained signalling channel, the signal that best protects the truth is the signal that best describes it. On 108 confirmatory items, the adversary-robust optimum aligns exactly with the salience pole from prior work. Across a 200,000-item pool, the two differ on only 2,748 items --- lying exactly where the prior salience-to-Bayes coordinate is undefined. Where defined, robustness is achieved by moving from Bayesian discrimination entirely to salience. We show this by introducing an adversary to a forced-choice task (abstracted from Deception: Murder in Hong Kong). The adversary knows the target, observes the signal, and argues for the strongest wrong answer using a persuasion budget, $β$. As $β$ grows, the optimal signal shifts from the posterior-maximizing option to the margin-maximizing one; at $β= 0$, the game reproduces the original oracle model with a listener temperature of $τ= 1$. This effect is real: 18.2 percent of the pool has an optimum that shifts under a finite budget, and each item's critical budget is exact. This coincidence structurally limits empirical evaluation. Two adversary framings change the chosen option of seven language models on 30 to 77 of 108 items against an exact no-effect rate. Yet, no measurement can determine whether this movement is toward the adversary-aware optimum or toward salience, because the two options are identical. This is a structural limit, not a null result. The diagnostic check is cheap: before evaluating adversary-awareness, verify whether the robust target coincides with a heuristic target on the evaluation items.

</details>


### [16] [SPHERE: Adaptive VR Indoor Scene Generation via LLM-Enhanced Spatial Preference Learning and Human-in-the-Loop RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02023)
*Hyeonmin Lee,Zheng Wei,Kyungmin Kwon,Jumin Seo,Jiwon Park,Hayoung Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPHERE 是一个自适应 VR 生成框架，通过从多模态交互中提取持久空间偏好，将孤立的 3D 室内场景合成转变为持续的人机协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的 3D 室内场景合成管线无法跨会话保留用户特定偏好，导致沉浸式创作重复且体力负担重。

Method: 从语音与手柄编辑等自然多模态交互中提取持久空间偏好，将原始编辑抽象为建模局部功能与全局拓扑上下文的层次化约束以保证几何鲁棒性，并采用人在回路强化学习机制根据用户最终编辑场景动态更新检索策略。

Result: 混合设计用户研究（N=42）与离线消融实验表明，SPHERE 显著减少修正编辑与体力需求，避免偏向浅层物体级特征，生成几何鲁棒且与用户画像对齐的布局。

Conclusion: SPHERE 展示了如何通过捕捉用户演示的空间逻辑实现可控的空间自适应，为沉浸式创作建立了可靠、可治理的人机协作框架。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) advance 3D indoor scene synthesis, current pipelines fail to retain user-specific preferences across sessions, making immersive authoring a repetitive and physically fatiguing process. We present SPHERE, an adaptive VR generation framework that transforms isolated synthesis into continuous human-AI co-creation. SPHERE extracts persistent spatial preferences from natural multimodal interactions (speech and controller edits). To ensure geometric resilience against spatial distortions, it abstracts these raw edits into hierarchical constraints modeling both local functional and global topological contexts. Furthermore, a human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning mechanism dynamically updates retrieval policies based on the user's final edited scenes. A mixed-design user study ($N=42$) and an offline ablation demonstrate that SPHERE significantly reduces corrective edits and physical demand, preventing bias toward shallow object-level traits to yield geometrically resilient, profile-aligned layouts. Ultimately, SPHERE demonstrates how capturing demonstrated spatial logic enables controlled spatial adaptation, establishing a reliable, governed human-AI collaboration framework for immersive authoring. Project page and source code will be available at: https://github.com/hyeonmin11/SPHERE

</details>


### [17] [Conflicting Supervision Moves Commitment, Not Capability: A 12.29σ arrangement effect that is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00234)
*Wenhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 学习率调度是决定数据排序效应大小的“平均算子”，排序与调度以相乘因子分离：衰减调度会削弱排序效应，恒定调度会放大它；且该效应在传统精确匹配基准上不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机是重新界定“数据顺序是否重要”这一问题：以往把学习率调度当作背景条件，而作者认为调度本身就是决定排序效应强弱的核心算子，并且排序效应可能被常规评测指标掩盖。

Method: 使用同一语料、同一预算、仅改变路径的十种排序，在只差 lr_scheduler_type 的两类调度家族下各运行两次；证明一个包含“块周期”与“单步最大权重”两个相乘因子的界，并进行受控测量。

Result: 恒定学习率下内部差异达 0.2221 分配、11.63 对比下限，并随块状程度单调；单一 cosine 下十个臂仅落入两个可区分状态（其分辨率本应允许约十个）。十二个臂中 acc_A+acc_B 在 9.7% 内保持恒定，而分配份额从 0.04 变到 0.87，说明 12.29-sigma 的排序切换在约定无关指标下恰好为零。

Conclusion: “顺序重要”与“顺序不重要”是同一旋钮的两端，路径写入的是模型承诺哪种约定，而精确匹配基准看不到这一点；在提示中标注约定可消除该切换，并达到并集上限的 87.5%。

Abstract: "Train a model on the same problems written under two incompatible conventions, both correct, and ask what the ordering of that data writes into the parameters. The learning-rate schedule is not a background condition for that question. It is the averaging operator, and it decides the answer. We prove a bound in which the arrangement and the schedule enter the ordering effect as separate multiplied factors: the arrangement only as a block period, the schedule only as how much weight the endpoint can place on any one moment of the run. A decaying schedule cannot put a large step size and an uncontracted remainder at the same moment; a constant one does exactly that at the last step. That decay moderates ordering effects has been reported in pretraining; the mechanism, the separation, and a controlled measurement of both halves are ours. Ten orderings of one corpus, one budget, everything but the path held fixed, run twice under families differing in lr_scheduler_type and nothing else: at a constant rate the interior spans 0.2221 in allocation, 11.63 contrast floors, monotone in how blocked the arrangement is. Under the single cosine every published arm uses, the same ten arms occupy two distinguishable states where their own resolution would allow about ten. "Order matters" and "order does not matter" are the two ends of one knob. What the path writes is which convention the model commits to, and no exact-match benchmark can see it. Across twelve arms acc_A+acc_B is constant to within 9.7% while the allocation share runs 0.04 to 0.87, so the 12.29-sigma arrangement switch this paper measures is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic metric. That conservation is quoted from the decayed family throughout, the constant-rate one being a noisier place to read it. Marking the convention in the prompt collapses the switch and reaches 87.5% of the union ceiling."

</details>


### [18] [Knowing When to Yield: Grounded Arbitration of User Corrections in Text-Based Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00282)
*Yezhou Cheng,Runjia Du,Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"有依据的纠正仲裁"（grounded correction arbitration）问题，并开发GAVA框架，让具身智能体在人类纠正可能有误时，选择接受、拒绝、检查世界或询问说话者。文本ALFWorld实验显示：完整本地检查可达100%纠正准确率；语义先验能显著降低交互成本与联合成本，并在独立检查点上复现，但未证明环境信息价值普遍优于澄清询问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当具身智能体收到人类纠正时，纠正本身可能是错误的。现有工作通常默认纠正正确或直接接受，缺乏对"该不该信、该不该查、该不该问"的系统化决策机制。作者希望将这一问题形式化为在不同证据获取方式（接受、拒绝、检查环境、询问说话者）之间的仲裁选择，并在受控条件下检验信息收集策略的价值。

Method: 将仲裁建模为在四类动作间的选择，GAVA采用"观测受限证据"（observation-bounded evidence）、合法探针（legal probes）与一步期望损失规则（one-step expected-loss rule）实现该接口。实验在纯文本ALFWorld中进行：162个检查点生成972对真假干预配对；设置same-episode执行、总是验证（always verify）等基线；引入仅训练用的物体位置先验（object-location prior）；冻结策略、成本、基线与多重性方案后，在77个非重叠可见检查点（308个场景）上复现，并使用95%检查点自助法置信区间。

Result: 完整本地检查使GAVA与always verify均达到100%纠正准确率（确立证据契约而非相对优势）；same-episode执行中GAVA相对always verify降低交互成本，但在完美说话者下与成本阈值打平。训练先验在340个未见场景上相对均匀GAVA降低交互成本0.490、声明联合成本0.420；冻结后在308个场景上复现为0.595与0.517，两个95%置信区间均不含零。联合成本也优于同先验固定策略，但匹配校准的无VOI比较尚无定论。语义GAVA在两个队列中各出现4个事实错误，准确率分别为98.8%与98.7%，所有方法均完成任务。

Conclusion: 结果支持在声明的成本结构下，结合语义先验进行选择性信息收集；但并未证明环境信息价值（VOI）相对澄清询问具有普遍优势。研究局限在于使用归一化声明、完整符号观测与受控说话者，未评估真实人类参与者、视觉输入或物理机器人，因此结论限于受控文本符号环境。

Abstract: How should an embodied agent respond when a person's correction may be wrong? We formulate grounded correction arbitration as a choice among accepting, rejecting, inspecting the world, and asking the speaker. GAVA implements this interface with observation-bounded evidence, legal probes, and a one-step expected-loss rule. In text-only ALFWorld, 162 checkpoints produce 972 paired true and false interventions. Complete local inspections give GAVA and always verify 100 percent correction accuracy, establishing the evidence contract rather than a comparative advantage. In same-episode execution, GAVA reduces interaction cost against always verify but ties a cost threshold under a perfect speaker. An exploratory training-only object-location prior lowers interaction and declared joint cost on 340 unseen scenarios by 0.490 and 0.420 relative to uniform GAVA. After freezing the policy, costs, baselines, and multiplicity plan, the gains replicate on 77 non-overlapping seen checkpoints, covering 308 scenarios: 0.595 and 0.517, with both 95 percent checkpoint-bootstrap confidence intervals excluding zero. Joint cost also improves over an identical-prior fixed policy, while the matched calibrated no-VOI comparison remains inconclusive. Semantic GAVA makes four factual errors in each cohort, corresponding to 98.8 percent and 98.7 percent accuracy, and all methods complete every task. Results support selective information gathering with semantic priors under declared costs, but do not establish a general advantage of environmental value of information over clarification. The study uses normalized claims, complete symbolic observations, and controlled speakers; it evaluates neither human participants, visual input, nor physical robots.

</details>


### [19] [Rules to Tools: Executable Checks for LLM Agents in Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00313)
*Jingjie Ning,Guojiang Zhao,Chen Xu,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"规则到工具"（Rules to Tools, R2T），将有文字的公开科学需求转化为可执行检查，以评估科学编码智能体在修复任务中的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体接收书面形式的方程、边界条件和输出要求，并需评估其修改的程序。作者想检验：如果将书面需求转换为准备好的可执行检查（工具），智能体的修复表现和成本会如何变化。

Method: 在SciCode修复任务上构造匹配组（起始程序、模型、预算相同），一组仅获得文字检查，另一组额外获得可调用的工具实现；跨两个任务ID队列比较（30个与16个任务ID），并做任务簇自助法区间估计。另设"源码经Python"新臂和匹配的PDE对比实验，同时记录模型输出量与公共CPU使用。

Result: 完整修复：文字组26/30，工具组29/30；三个任务ID偏好工具，一个偏好文字，十一个持平。8-ID队列为13/16对15/16，差值95%自助区间[-12.5, 43.75]个百分点。更大共享定义队列两组同为13/24。五个开发暴露任务为3/10对7/10。工具组偏好任务17、77、11，初始检查标记17、对77和11无违规报告；任务37偏好文字且初始无违规。新臂源码经Python达15/16。PDE对比中详细文字23/24、检查24/24，检查组模型输出低31.2%。智能体侧输出节省随队列变化，公共CPU使用在两队列均上升。

Conclusion: 准备好的可执行检查能带来任务依赖的修复收益，并改变智能体侧成本与公共CPU使用；差异幅度受任务构成影响，整体上工具方式略有优势但不普遍。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents receive equations, boundary conditions, and output requirements in writing, then must assess the programs they revise. Rules to Tools (R2T) supplies prepared executable checks of public scientific requirements. Matched SciCode repair groups share written checks, starting programs, model, and budgets; the tool group receives a callable implementation. Across two task-ID cohorts, complete repair is 26/30 with text and 29/30 with the prepared checks. Three task IDs favor tools, one favors text, and eleven tie. The eight-ID cohort scores 13/16 versus 15/16, with a task-cluster bootstrap 95% interval of [-12.5, 43.75] percentage points for the difference. The larger shared-definition SciCode cohort ties at 13/24 per group. Five development-exposed tasks with alternate starting programs score 3/10 versus 7/10. The tool group favors tasks 17, 77, and 11; initial checks flag task 17 and report no violation for tasks 77 and 11. Task 37 favors text and has no initial reported violation. A fresh source-through-Python arm also reaches 15/16, matching the dedicated command's aggregate. In a matched PDE comparison, detailed text scores 23/24 and checks score 24/24, with 31.2% lower reported model output for checks. Agent-side output savings vary by cohort, while public CPU use rises in both task-ID cohorts. These results measure task-dependent repair outcomes and agent-side costs with prepared checks.

</details>


### [20] [Predictive Credit: Measuring What Scientific Explanations Add to Experimental Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00314)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用配对预测来衡量研究智能体"预测信用"的协议，通过五项检查在336个前瞻状态、12个Tox21端点及24个OpenML任务上评估。结果显示v5冻结信用决策不确定，Tox21与OpenML的关键假设均未满足，匹配解释带来的点准确率增益未被确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究智能体会解释其计划的实验，但这类解释是否真正带来可验证的预测信用尚无可靠度量方法。作者希望建立一套可控、可重复的协议来量化解释与上下文对预测表现的贡献。

Method: 采用配对预测设计：共享干预、预测者与结果，仅变化描述、匹配解释与捐赠者上下文。设置五项检查（承诺、交付、预测增益、对齐、已知信号吸收）。在336个前瞻状态、12个Tox21端点、24个OpenML任务上开展受控学习，并包含种子捐赠区间、Flash回放、直接文本传递及机制阳性对照等实验。

Result: v5冻结信用决策不确定；Tox21预注册ROC AUC区间评分损害检验未满足（D-M=-.0026，95%区间[-.0174,.0104]）；OpenML联合形成、点等价与可重复性规则未满足；匹配点准确率增益未确认，Tox21/OpenML种子捐赠区间跨零。DeepSeek V4 Pro下匹配与捐赠卡使Tox21二次漂移降低64.5%和59.1%；Flash回放将匹配点MAE从.01823升至.02020。OpenML全卡分配使名义80%区间扩大21%，覆盖率49.3%对51.4%。直接文本Flash交付全部144条笔记但无可检测增益。研究者机制阳性对照使点MAE相对描述降低2.60个百分点。

Conclusion: 该协议可用于衡量研究智能体基准与科学预测中的预测信用，但在所测试的捐赠分辨率下，自然解释的信用仍未得到确认。

Abstract: Research agents explain planned experiments. We measure predictive credit with paired forecasts sharing an intervention, forecaster, and outcome while varying description, matched explanation, and donor context. Five checks track commitment, delivery, predictive gain, alignment, and known-signal uptake. Across 336 prospective states in controlled learning, 12 Tox21 endpoints, and 24 OpenML tasks, v5's frozen credit decision was inconclusive. Tox21's preregistered ROC AUC interval-score harm test was unmet ($D-M=-.0026$, 95 percent interval [$-.0174$, .0104]); OpenML's joint formation, point-equivalence, and repeatability rule was unmet. Matched point-accuracy gains over description remained unconfirmed, and Tox21/OpenML seed-donor intervals spanned zero. Under requested DeepSeek V4 Pro, matched and donor cards reduced secondary Tox21 drift by 64.5 and 59.1 percent. A DeepSeek V4 Flash replay raised matched point MAE from .01823 to .02020 and missed matched-donor interval-score equivalence. OpenML full-card assignment widened nominal 80 percent intervals by 21 percent, with 49.3 percent coverage versus 51.4 percent for description and content in 66/144 cards. Direct-text Flash delivered all 144 notes without detectable matched point-accuracy gain. A researcher-authored mechanism positive control lowered point MAE by 2.60 percentage points versus description. The protocol measures predictive credit for research-agent benchmarks and scientific forecasting; natural-explanation credit remained unconfirmed at the tested donor resolutions.

</details>


### [21] [ContractRL: Shielded Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Auditable Tool-Call Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00328)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Yina Sa,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContractRL，将验证器引导的 JSON 修复建模为有界决策过程，通过合约约束的序列修复协议显著提升语义成功率并降低生成 token 数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工具调用中，少量字段违反 schema 或执行合约即会导致整体失败；重新生成完整对象会扩大动作空间，且多次修复难以审计，因此需要一种受合约约束、可逐步修复且可审计的机制。

Method: ContractRL 将有界决策过程用于修复：每步策略观察候选对象、带类型的验证器反馈、JSON Pointer、不可变修复历史和剩余预算；合约推导的动作掩码在确定性验证器执行转移前过滤非法或禁止的 RFC-6902 操作；并设计合约约束的组相对目标用于 patch、重试和弃权决策，且在线状态不暴露规范目标与语义标签直到轨迹冻结。

Result: 在相同验证器信息下，ContractRL 在每种子 192 个案例、五个种子上取得 0.9362 语义成功率和 34.4 生成 token，优于 Patch-SFT（0.9076，44.9 token）和全量重生成（0.9148，137.2 token）。策略优化将语义成功率从监督 ContractRL 的 0.9186 提升到 0.9375；三种子配对评估相对 Patch-SFT 的语义差为 +0.0396（95% CI [+0.0137,+0.0662]，p=0.0039）。反馈、动作掩码、预算和 schema 漂移分析将增益归因于局部纠错，对抗与多轮评估刻画了剩余失败模式。

Conclusion: ContractRL 通过合约约束的序列化修复与局部纠错，在不增加生成开销的情况下提升结构化工具调用的语义成功率，并优于全量重生成与 SFT 基线。

Abstract: Structured tool calls often fail after only a small number of fields violate a schema or an execution contract. Regenerating the complete object enlarges the action surface and makes repeated repair difficult to audit. We introduce ContractRL, a contract-constrained sequential repair protocol that models verifier-guided JSON repair as a bounded decision process. At each step the policy observes the candidate, typed verifier feedback, JSON Pointer, immutable repair history, and remaining budget; a contract-derived action mask filters malformed or prohibited RFC-6902 operations before a deterministic validator performs the transition. We specify a contract-constrained group-relative objective for patch, retry, and abstention decisions while keeping canonical targets and semantic labels outside the online state until trace freeze. Under identical verifier information, ContractRL attains 0.9362 semantic success with 34.4 generated tokens, compared with 0.9076 and 44.9 tokens for Patch-SFT and 0.9148 and 137.2 tokens for full regeneration over 192 cases per seed and five seeds. Policy optimization improves semantic success from 0.9186 for supervised ContractRL to 0.9375. A separate three-seed paired evaluation against Patch-SFT yields a semantic difference of +0.0396 (95% CI $[+0.0137,+0.0662], p=0.0039$). Feedback, action-mask, budget, and schema-shift analyses connect these gains to localized correction, while adversarial and multi-turn evaluations characterize the remaining failure modes.

</details>


### [22] [Mathematical Transfer in LLMs Follows Reasoning Approach More Than Topic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00331)
*Sajad Goudarzi,Samaneh Zamanifard,Seyed Amin Seyed Haeri,Moloud Nasiri,Hamed Rahimian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在数学训练数据选择中，按"推理方法"匹配比按"主题"匹配能带来更强的迁移效果：跨五个基座模型、三种训练种子、两个2×2设计，共40个模型-目标对比中，同方法（SA）源全部优于同主题（ST）源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择LLM数学训练数据时，常见原则是按主题组织（如概率题用于概率目标）。但作者提出另一种可能性：按推理方法组织（共享解题方法，即使数学领域不同）。核心问题是：微调后哪种关系产生更大的迁移？

Method: 采用两个平衡的2×2设计：设计一为概率与组合数学 × 不变量推理与双重计数（2000题）；设计二为数论与几何 × 补集与鸽巢原理（800题）。每个单元格轮流作为留出目标：同方法（SA）源共享目标方法但换主题，同主题（ST）源共享主题但换方法。每个源在各角色中出现一次，使加性源质量效应在等权聚合对比中抵消。跨五个基座模型、三种训练种子评估。

Result: 全部40个种子池化的模型-目标对比中SA均优于ST。模型级优势在设计一中为8.2–16.2个百分点（均值10.8），设计二为12.0–16.0（均值14.3）；十个模型级95%置信区间均不包含零。两个设计中ST源在嵌入和词汇度量上都与目标更相似，说明SA优势与语句级相似度的测量排序相反。

Conclusion: 在所评估的主题-方法组合中，推理方法比主题是更有效的数学迁移匹配标准。

Abstract: When selecting mathematical training data for LLMs, a natural organizing principle is topic: probability examples for probability targets. An alternative is reasoning approach: worked solutions that share a solution method with the target, even when the mathematical domain differs. We ask which relation produces greater transfer after fine-tuning. We evaluate two counterbalanced $2\times2$ designs: probability and combinatorics crossed with invariant reasoning and double counting (2,000 problems), and number theory and geometry crossed with complement and pigeonhole reasoning (800 problems). In each design, every cell serves as the held-out target in turn: same-approach (SA) sources share the target's method but change the topic, while same-topic (ST) sources share the topic but change the method. Every source appears once in each role, so additive source-quality effects cancel from the equally weighted aggregate contrast. Across five base models and three training seeds per design, SA outperforms ST in all 40 seed-pooled model--target comparisons. Model-level advantages range from 8.2 to 16.2 percentage points in the primary design (mean: 10.8) and from 12.0 to 16.0 in the second design (mean: 14.3); all ten model-level 95% confidence intervals exclude zero. In both designs, ST sources are more similar to targets under embedding and lexical measures, so the SA advantage runs opposite to the measured ordering of statement-level resemblance. These findings identify reasoning approach as a more effective matching criterion than topic for mathematical transfer across the evaluated topic--approach combinations.

</details>


### [23] [Fault-Tolerant Budget Conservation in Distributed Multi-Agent Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00349)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文为分布式多智能体委派中的预算守恒建立容错形式化框架：以独占托管信用（配额化资源向量）在委派DAG中流转，通过绑定谱系、纪元、规范化效应、最大扣费、接收方与幂等键的操作预留，以及带隔离的签名派遣许可与网关校验，确保在消息丢失、超时后完成、重复、分区与DAG汇合别名等故障下不超支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源限制正成为AI智能体跨并发且易故障工作节点委派任务的授权边界。现有手段（父子分配约束、仿射对象、分布式托管）在回复丢失、超时后效应完成、消息重复、分支分区或DAG汇合别名同一谱系时，本身无法防止超支，因此需要面向故障的预算守恒形式化。

Method: 将预算建模为由独占托管信用表示的配额化资源向量，在委派DAG中流动。派遣前，分支把信用转换为绑定谱系、纪元、规范化效应、最大扣费、接收方与幂等键的操作预留；持久化带隔离的签名派遣许可，网关在首次接受前验证该许可。不确定效应在经认证结算、带栅栏的权威无效应证明或永久退役前保持扣费状态。并在显式中介、持久性、认证、规范化与网关假设下进行证明与验证（受限TLA+检查、独立JavaScript探索器、崩溃注入的双进程SQLite实验）。

Result: 证明了所有权划分、账本与效应守恒、后代非放大、至多一次结算、迟到完成安全性与分区隔离等性质；一个不可区分性结果表明分区局部可用性需要独占预分配。受限TLA+检查、独立JavaScript探索器与崩溃注入的双进程SQLite实验覆盖了声明范围，并检测出超时退款与历史证书验证两类变异体。

Conclusion: 在评估的崩溃、重试、重复、分区、汇合与迟到完成调度下，该机制始终维持已发放预算上界，为故障容忍的分布式多智能体委派提供了可靠的预算守恒保障。

Abstract: Resource limits are becoming an authorization boundary for AI agents that delegate work across concurrent and failure-prone workers. Parent-child allocation constraints, affine objects, and distributed escrow do not by themselves prevent overspend when replies are lost, effects complete after timeout, messages repeat, branches partition, or DAG joins alias one lineage. We formalize fault-tolerant budget conservation for distributed multi-agent delegation. Budgets are quantized resource vectors represented by exclusive escrow credits that move through a delegation DAG. Before dispatch, a branch converts credit into an operation reservation bound to lineage, epoch, normalized effect, maximum charge, receiver, and idempotency key. It persists a signed dispatch permit with quarantine; the gateway verifies that permit before first acceptance. Uncertain effects remain charged until authenticated settlement, a fenced authoritative no-effect proof, or permanent retirement. We prove ownership partition, ledger and effect conservation, descendant non-amplification, at-most-once settlement, late-completion safety, and partition confinement under explicit mediation, durability, authentication, normalization, and gateway assumptions. An indistinguishability result shows that partition-local availability requires exclusive preallocation. Bounded TLA+ checking, an independent JavaScript explorer, and crash-injected two-process SQLite experiments exercise the declared scope and detect timeout-refund and historical-certificate-validation mutants. The mechanism preserves the issued budget bound across the evaluated crash, retry, duplicate, partition, join, and late-completion schedules.

</details>


### [24] [JusticeAxis: Benchmarking Legal Judgment between Rigid Rule Application and Ungrounded Discretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00353)
*Zhengkai Tu,Mingda Zhang,Zijia Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Jimmy Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将法律判决形式化为"参照锚定"任务，提出 JusticeAxis 基准（18国256个刑事案件的音图文本证据与三份律师判决）和 JusticeAgent 框架，实现法律与情形的双重锚定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律判决方法在僵化的法条匹配与无依据的自由裁量之间摇摆，基准仅评分标签或评分表，而能够提供平衡的实践经验缺乏验证。

Method: 构建 JusticeAxis：256个真实刑事案件，涵盖18个国家，含音频、图像、文本证据，每案配三份律师撰写的判决（记录版及两种失败情形版）；提出 JusticeAgent：要素智能体确立事实，法官智能体在承载情形经验的技能下适用法律；技能从执行轨迹中蒸馏，仅在贝叶斯可信界下被采纳。

Result: 实验表明失败方向随规模转变：开放权重主干模型偏向无根据的理由，前沿模型偏向法条默认。JusticeAgent 作为简单有效的插件，可将冻结的开放权重主干提升至商业级水平。

Conclusion: 法律判决可通过参照锚定任务与经验蒸馏技能得到有效建模，JusticeAgent 能显著提升开放权重模型表现，资源已开源。

Abstract: A sound judgment applies the law to established facts and weighs the circumstances in which they arose. However, existing methods swing between rigid statute matching and ungrounded discretion, benchmarks score a label or a rubric, and the experience that would supply the balance stays unverified. We formalize legal judgment as a reference-anchored task, whose object is a single decision that stays tied to the statute and to the circumstances at once. We introduce JusticeAxis, 256 real-world criminal cases from 18 countries with audio, image, and text evidence, and three lawyer-written judgments for every case: the recorded one and one for each failure. We further propose JusticeAgent, a harness whose element agents establish the facts and whose judge agent applies the law under skills carrying experience of the circumstances. Skills are distilled from execution trajectories and admitted only under Bayesian credible bounds. Experiments show that failure turns direction with scale: open-weight backbones drift to unsupported grounds, frontier models to the statutory default. We further verify that JusticeAgent, as a simple yet effective plugin, carries a frozen open-weight backbone to commercial level. Project resources are available at https://github.com/beita6969/JusticeAxis.

</details>


### [25] [What Should an Agent Remember? Disentangling Retention from Retrieval in Bounded-Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00366)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建流式召回基准，交叉保留与选择规则，在同一批300个种子情节上评估，证明有界记忆评估应固定访问方式并分别报告保留与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久智能体需同时决定保留哪些信息和在查询时呈现哪些信息，但现有记忆评估常混淆保留与选择两个决策，因为比较的方法在这两方面同时存在差异，导致归因不清。

Method: 构建流式召回基准，交叉组合保留规则与选择规则，在所有条件下使用相同的300个种子情节进行评估；在固定访问方式下比较查询感知选择；并区分混合比较中访问因素所占优势；在有界保留下测试查询感知、稠密与oracle选择；最后在SQuAD上重复评估。

Result: 固定访问时，查询感知选择将所需事实召回提升15.5个百分点（95% CI: 12.8–18.2）；混合比较报告68.7个百分点优势，其中53.2个百分点归因于访问。有界保留下，查询感知、稠密与oracle选择均达到保留上限，而bounded recency条件下全部319个失败均由驱逐而非排序错误导致。目标足够久远时召回降至0%。SQuAD结果保持保留上限，并显示稠密检索在自然文本上可优于词汇检索。

Conclusion: 有界记忆评估应固定访问方式，并分别报告保留与选择指标，以避免混淆两者的贡献。

Abstract: A persistent agent must decide both what to retain as information arrives and what to surface once a query appears, yet memory evaluations can confound these decisions by comparing methods that differ in both retention and selection. We build a streaming-recall benchmark crossing retention and selection rules and evaluate every condition on the same 300 seeded episodes. Holding access fixed, query-aware selection improves required-fact recall by 15.5 percentage points (95% CI: 12.8 to 18.2), whereas a mixed comparison that also changes history access reports a 68.7-point advantage, of which 53.2 points are attributable to access. Under bounded retention, query-aware, dense, and oracle selection reach the retention ceiling, and all 319 observed failures in the bounded recency condition are caused by eviction rather than ranking errors. Recall falls to 0% as targets recede sufficiently far into the past. Repeating the evaluation on SQuAD preserves the retention ceiling while showing that dense retrieval can outperform lexical retrieval on natural text. These results show that bounded-memory evaluations should hold access fixed and report retention and selection separately.

</details>


### [26] [When Harnesses Lose the Signal: Causal Evaluation of Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00372)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Xuan Chen,Ke Chao,Ming Cui,Feifei Qian,Chaoyang Mei,Fanlin Meng,Ziming Yu,Junxi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将语言模型智能体的错误恢复重新定义为因果决策问题，提出因果干预路由器（CIR），在长时程ALFWorld任务上把成功率从70.33%提升到73.33%（+3.00个百分点），且不干扰已有正确观测的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体依赖外部执行框架在模型与环境之间传递信息并从执行错误中恢复，但现有评估通常只用平均任务成功率来衡量恢复。这掩盖了一个重要矛盾：同一恢复操作有时能挽救失败轨迹，有时却会破坏本可成功的轨迹。因此需要区分恢复的"挽救"与"伤害"，判断何时干预才真正有价值。

Method: 论文把恢复建模为因果决策问题：从同一执行状态出发，对比有无恢复两种情况，从而分离出"挽救"与"伤害"，并研究恢复价值随时间的变化。基于此提出因果干预路由器（CIR），一种轻量级策略，仅利用恢复前可得的信息来决定是否值得进行干预。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上的长时程ALFWorld任务中，CIR将成功率从70.33%提升到73.33%，增益3.00个百分点；同时它对所有观测正确的轨迹保持不变。额外的对照实验表明，恢复带来的收益不能仅由环境返回的新观测来解释。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了评估恢复并选择性应用恢复的实用方法，说明恢复在关键时刻能带来正向收益，而其选择性应用对性能提升至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model agents rely on external harnesses to pass information between the model and its environment and to recover from execution errors. Yet recovery is usually judged only by average task success. This hides an important tension. The same operation can rescue a failing trajectory or disrupt one that would otherwise succeed. We frame recovery as a causal decision problem. Starting from the same execution state, we compare what happens with and without recovery, separate rescue from harm, and study how the value of recovery changes over time. We then introduce the Causal Intervention Router (CIR), a lightweight policy that uses information available before recovery to decide when intervention is worthwhile. On long-horizon ALFWorld tasks with Qwen3-14B, CIR raises success from 70.33% to 73.33%, a gain of 3.00 percentage points. It leaves all evaluated trajectories with correct observations untouched. Additional controls show that the benefit of recovery cannot be explained solely by the new observation returned by the environment. These results provide a practical way to evaluate recovery and apply it selectively.

</details>


### [27] [Benchmarking Prompt Optimization of Large Language Models With Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00416)
*Timothée Lesort,Alejandra López de Aberasturi Gómez,Tristan Karch,Tom Veniat,Philippe Modard,Karl Tuyls,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于1,118道Lichess国际象棋谜题的基准，用于研究冻结大模型的自动提示优化（APO），兼具低评分成本、可再生性与跨模型/对局的迁移评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型能力提升使得评测日益困难：基准易饱和、公开测试集存在污染风险、更难任务需要昂贵评分或执行设施。这些问题在APO中因搜索过程中反复评估而被放大，因此需要一个评分廉价且确定、难度足够留有提升空间、并能随模型演进更新的基准。

Method: 构建由1,118道Lichess谜题组成的国际象棋基准，仅优化提示而不更新模型权重。利用精确匹配评分、引擎对备选着法的评估，以及难度可调的谜题供给；并在同一领域内将解题增益与简短对局rollout相关联。用该基准在8个目标模型上评估6种APO算法，测量基线强度、对优化的响应程度，以及优化提示的跨模型迁移与对局迁移。

Result: 最强被评模型Gemini 3.5 Flash（作元模型）仅解出约55%谜题；基准能区分不同方法与模型，表现出提升、无变化与退化；新谜题可降低污染风险、可调难度维持提升空间；完整研究成本约800美元。

Conclusion: 国际象棋是适合APO的基准：具有挑战性、具有区分度、可再生且成本可负担。作者公开谜题、优化与评测代码及数据集更新脚本。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models becomes increasingly challenging as their capabilities advance: benchmarks can saturate, public test sets risk contamination, and assessing harder tasks can require expensive grading or execution infrastructure. These challenges are amplified in automatic prompt optimization (APO), where evaluation is repeated throughout the search for better prompts. Studying APO therefore requires a benchmark that is cheap and deterministic to score, hard enough to leave room for improvement, and renewable as models evolve. We introduce a chess benchmark built from 1,118 Lichess puzzles to study APO for frozen LLMs: we optimize their prompts without updating their model weights. Chess combines inexpensive exact-match scoring, engine-based evaluation of alternative moves, and a renewable supply of problems with adjustable difficulty. Unlike evaluations that report only success on isolated test items, the benchmark also connects puzzle-solving gains to short game-play rollouts within the same domain. We use it to evaluate six APO algorithms on eight target models, measuring not only baseline strength but also how much each model responds to optimization and whether optimized prompts transfer across models and to game play. Chess is thus a well-suited benchmark for APO: it is (i) challenging, as even the strongest evaluated model, Gemini 3.5 Flash (used as the meta-model), solves only about 55\% of puzzles; (ii) discriminative, revealing gains, unchanged performance, and regressions across methods and models; (iii) renewable, with fresh puzzles to reduce contamination risk and adjustable difficulty to maintain headroom as models improve; and (iv) affordable, as the complete study runs for around \$800. We release the puzzles, optimization and evaluation code, and dataset-renewal scripts (https://github.com/imec-ailabs/Automatic-Prompt-Optimization-with-Chess).

</details>


### [28] [JevSpawn: Adaptive Agentic Inference through Compositional Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00437)
*Haoyang Su,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JevSpawn是一种组合式策略，将自然语言任务规范与有限概率探索相连接，通过并行动作生成与反馈驱动的分支选择，在无需额外训练的情况下提升任务性能并加快导航速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体逐token生成中间推理和动作，导致长交互过程缓慢且计算昂贵。Jev风格模型虽能提供快速概率预测，但需要预先指定有限动作域，这限制了需要从自然语言指令中推导并动态调整动作的自主任务求解。

Method: 提出JevSpawn组合式策略，连接自然语言任务规范与有限概率探索。采用并行动作生成，结合反馈驱动的分支选择、表示修订以及从保留备选方案中恢复。利用共享动作结构和模型前缀减少重复生成与上下文计算，无需额外训练。

Result: 在八个基准任务上，与七种智能体基线及一个TypeSafe Jev变体进行对比评估，JevSpawn在任务性能上有所提升，且导航速度更快。

Conclusion: JevSpawn是结构化智能体推理的一种有前景的方法，能够改善任务表现并加速导航。

Abstract: LLM agents generate intermediate reasoning and actions token by token, making extended interactions slow and computationally expensive. Jev-style models offer fast probabilistic predictions over finite fields, but require those fields to be specified in advance. This requirement limits autonomous task solving, where the available actions must be derived from natural language instructions and adapted through interaction. We introduce JevSpawn, a compositional policy that connects natural language task specifications to finite probabilistic exploration. Parallel action spawning is coupled with feedback driven branch selection, representation revision, and recovery from retained alternatives. Shared action structure and model prefixes reduce repeated generation and context computation without additional training. Evaluations on eight benchmark tasks against seven agent baselines and a TypeSafe Jev variant establish JevSpawn as a promising approach to structured agentic inference, with improved task performance and faster navigation.

</details>


### [29] [Frozen Scenes, Shifting Winners: Configuration Fragility in Text-to-3D Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00447)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了文本到3D排行榜在渲染图像评估中的可靠性：固定300个生成场景，变化8种渲染与描述因素，发现多数对齐评估器的配置方差超过生成器间方差，排名虽较稳定但获胜者会随配置改变，且无评估器在布局扰乱上超过67%的方向判别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D生成的排行榜通常依赖渲染图像评估，而相机设置与描述措辞会进入测量协议，可能导致排行榜结论随协议配置而变。作者希望厘清分数稳定性、决策不确定性与目标敏感性，检验排行榜在场景固定时是否仍然稳健。

Method: 从六个生成器取300个冻结场景，对19个对齐评估器加一个感知质量对照，变化8种渲染与描述因素，并进行四种针对性场景退化测试。分析配置方差与生成器间方差、提示自助下界、排名与获胜者变化、成对协议边际包络及同时推断下的反转检验，并单独评估对布局扰乱的敏感性。

Result: 17/19的对齐评估器其峰值配置方差超过生成器间方差，11/19的提示自助下界大于1。排名比分数更稳定，但18/19的评估器在某些配置下点估计获胜者会改变。成对协议边际包络显示哪些比较方向保持；部分配对点间区间相反，但在全搜索同时推断下无反转成立，故获胜者变化属描述性而非确认性。敏感性方面，包括无提示对照在内，无评估器在布局扰乱上超过67%的并列调整方向判别率，该结果属诊断性质而非人验证真值。

Conclusion: 该审计将分数稳定性、决策不确定性与目标敏感性加以区分，建议在报告中给出（生成器、分数、卡片ID），采用协议依赖的比较与考虑选择的不确定性。

Abstract: Can a text-to-3D leaderboard change when every generated scene stays fixed? We audit this question for rendered-image evaluation, where camera settings and caption wording become part of the measurement protocol. Across 300 frozen scenes from six generators, we vary eight render and caption factors for 19 alignment evaluators plus one perceptual-quality control, then test four targeted scene degradations. Peak configuration variance exceeds between-generator variance for 17/19 alignment evaluators, with prompt-bootstrap lower bounds above 1 for 11/19. Rankings are more stable than scores, yet 18/19 evaluators change their point-estimate winner under some configuration. Pairwise protocol margin envelopes show which comparisons keep their direction across the tested settings. Selected pairs have opposite pointwise intervals, but no reversal survives simultaneous inference over the full search. Thus the observed winner changes are descriptive, not confirmed changes in generator superiority. Sensitivity remains separate: no evaluator, even the prompt-free control, exceeds 67% tie-adjusted directional discrimination on layout scrambling, which is diagnostic rather than human-validated ground truth. The audit separates score stability, decision uncertainty, and targeted sensitivity, and recommends reporting (generator, score, card ID) with protocol-dependent comparisons and selection-aware uncertainty.

</details>


### [30] [Before Agents Decide: Epistemic Action in LLM-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00511)
*Yizhi Liu,Balaji Padmanabhan,Siva Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知科学中的"认知行动"概念引入LLM智能体设计，区分三种模式：获取缺失证据、转换现有证据、探测系统以生成揭示性响应，并用"认知脚手架"指代使这些行动可行且可审计的接口、工具与环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体能搜索和探索，但较少关注一个更早的问题：现有证据是否已为决策做好准备。证据可能缺失，或形式掩盖了关键信息，或用于判断的比较尚不存在。

Method: 概念性论述。借鉴认知科学中的"认知行动"理论，将其引入LLM智能体，划分三种行动模式，并提出"认知脚手架"作为支撑框架。

Result: 提出三种认知行动模式（获取、转换、探测）及认知脚手架概念，为智能体设计提供新的分析维度与设计方向。

Conclusion: 智能体设计必须解决"决策就绪证据"如何产生的问题，这是当前设计中被低估的关键环节。

Abstract: Before a difficult decision, people often act simply to understand the situation better. We turn an object to see another side, place alternatives next to each other, or change one condition and observe what happens. These actions may not complete the task, but they improve the evidence needed for the next choice. LLM-based agents can search and explore, yet agent design gives less attention to an earlier question: is the available evidence ready for the decision? Sometimes necessary evidence is missing. In other cases, the evidence is present but its form hides what matters, or the comparison needed to judge it does not yet exist. Cognitive science calls actions that improve the basis for a later choice epistemic actions. We bring this idea to LLM-based agents and distinguish three modes: acquiring missing evidence, transforming available evidence, and probing a system to create a revealing response. We use the term epistemic scaffolding for the interfaces, tools, and environments that make these actions possible and auditable. This paper argues that agent design must address how decision-ready evidence is produced.

</details>


### [31] [Ontology-Based Contextual AI Evaluations (OB-CAIE) Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00529)
*Julie Krugler Hollek,Michael Zargham,Mala Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于本体的情境化AI评估方法（OB-CAIE），通过领域本体与评估过程本体明确界定测试范围，平衡人工专业判断与自动化，并提升评估环境的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估存在测试覆盖不清、科学严谨性不足、人工与自动化失衡以及测试环境难以复现等问题。

Method: OB-CAIE方法采用两个本体刻画可处理问题空间：领域特定本体（DSO，回答"评什么"）和评估过程本体（EPO，回答"怎么评"）。该方法在科学方法首步明确定义测试内容，并在特定环节以科学化方式引入人工判断。

Result: 一个OB-CAIE问题空间可支持一项或多项AI评估；其关键优势在于失败点可在规范化的问题空间内被追踪、可视化与分析。

Conclusion: OB-CAIE通过本体驱动的规范化框架提升了AI评估的严谨性、可复现性与可解释性，为复杂领域中不可替代的人工反馈提供了科学化整合路径。

Abstract: The ontology-based contextual AI evaluation (OB-CAIE) methodology was developed to address a lack of scientific rigor that arises from unclear testing coverage, to balance human expertise and automations, and to address a lack of reproducibility of AI evaluation testing environments. OB-CAIE strengthens the current state of AI evaluations by addressing the first step in the scientific method by clearly defining what will be tested. Two ontologies represent the tractable problem space in the OB-CAIE methodology: the Domain-Specific Ontology (DSO) and the Evaluation Process Ontology (EPO). The DSO is the what; the EPO is the how. An OB-CAIE problem space can be used for one or multiple AI evaluations. The OB-CAIE methodology allows for human judgment at specific points, in scientifically grounded ways, and in complex subject areas where human feedback is genuinely irreducible or machine irreplaceable. A key advantage of the OB-CAIE methodology is that failure points can be traced, visualized and analyzed within the canonical OB-CAIE methodology problem space.

</details>


### [32] [Science or Slop?: Benchmarking and Mitigating Scientific Slop in AI-Generated Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00531)
*Yerim Oh,Young-Jun Lee,Jaewoo Ahn,Gunhee Kim,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"科学废料"(scientific slop)概念，通过结构、论证、制品三方面的六项指标构建SciSlopBench基准（390篇AI生成论文），以85.9%准确率区分AI与人类论文，并提出SciSlopHarness框架将残留差距降低63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成内容在学术界日益普遍，但AI生成科学论文的"废料"具有更复杂模式——各部分看似合理，但连接各部分的科学推理却断裂，现有基于词元的AI检测器难以识别，可能误导读者对工作的评估。

Method: 构建.sciSlopBench基准，包含390篇AI生成论文（主要计算机科学，涵盖生命、社会、自然科学），每篇配对一篇研究问题和贡献类型匹配的人类论文；从结构、论证、制品三方面设计六项度量指标；提出SciSlopHarness框架，引导固定LLM仅在实验记录支持的范围内修订单篇废料。

Result: 所提度量以85.9%准确率识别配对中的AI论文，优于Binoculars的68.7%；更高的科学废料伴随更低的ICLR评分，且在2017至2025年每年都能以超过随机的水平区分被拒与接收论文；直接优化度量无效，标准修订留有残余废料，直接废料感知提示会触发奖励黑客，而SciSlopHarness在无需人类参考目标的情况下将AI-人类差距降低63%。

Conclusion: AI生成科学论文在全局推理中留下根本性痕迹；负责任地缓解这一问题需要严格的证据基础，而非仅仅润色文字。

Abstract: AI-generated content, often called AI slop, is increasingly common everywhere, particularly in academia. Slop in AI-generated scientific papers, however, has more complex patterns that cannot be easily detected by existing token-based AI detectors. Each part of such a paper looks plausible while the scientific reasoning that connects the parts breaks down, which can mislead how readers assess the work. We benchmark these failures as scientific slop through six measures across Structure, Argument, and Artifacts. We construct SciSlopBench with 390 AI-generated papers, mostly in computer science but spanning the life, social, and natural sciences, each paired with a human-written paper matched by research problem and contribution type. Our measures identify the AI paper in each pair with 85.9% accuracy, compared with 68.7% for Binoculars. Higher scientific slop accompanies lower ICLR ratings and distinguishes rejected from accepted papers above chance in every year from 2017 to 2025. Reducing these patterns, however, is not as simple as directly optimizing the measures. We therefore propose SciSlopHarness, a harness-level framework that guides a fixed LLM to revise slop only where the experiment records support the change. While standard revisions leave residual slop and direct slop-aware prompting triggers reward hacking, SciSlopHarness reduces the remaining AI-human gap by 63% over the strongest revision baseline without requiring human reference targets. Overall, we demonstrate that AI-generated scientific papers leave fundamental traces in their global reasoning, and that responsible mitigation demands strict evidentiary grounding rather than mere prose refinement.

</details>


### [33] [Worse Together: How Performance Breaks Down in Multi-User Multi-Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00583)
*Sahan Paliskara,Nattaput Namchittai,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多用户多智能体协调：5个前沿模型、4类环境77个场景下，单体协调者普遍优于多智能体团队，且提出环境相关的缓解措施并发布MAMUBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中用户越来越多地将任务委派给AI智能体，且不同用户的智能体常共享代码库、日历、预算等资源。当各智能体服务目标不同的用户时，协调容易失败，群体结果可能比由单个智能体统一服务所有人更差。论文旨在系统研究这一多用户多智能体协调问题。

Method: 在4种环境（共享计算预算的API key、共享日历的诊所、共享团购/预订的个人助手、共享发布截止时间的合并队列）中的77个场景下，对5个前沿模型进行对比实验。每场景比较：服务所有用户的单体协调者 vs. 每个智能体服务一个用户的团队，并区分有无通信频道。进一步识别导致群体表现差的行为，并测试缓解手段（团队领导、显式流程指令、平台强制先阅读同伴消息的检查）。

Result: 在所有环境中，团队群体结果均差于协调者。无通信时团队在两个环境中完全崩溃；即便有通信，协调开销仍造成显著差距。例如个人助手环境中，协调者完成目标用户请求的频率约为团队的两倍。识别出团队增长时的停滞、相互覆盖对方操作、捏造声明等行为。有效的缓解措施具有环境特异性。

Conclusion: 多用户多智能体协调存在系统性缺陷，单体协调者在多种场景下更优，团队协作需依赖环境特定的干预手段。论文将发布包含API key、诊所和个人助手环境的MAMUBench，共74个场景，用于评估多用户多智能体协调。

Abstract: People are increasingly delegating tasks to AI agents, and those agents are increasingly encountering other people's agents over shared resources such as a codebase, a calendar, or a budget. When each agent acts for a different user with different goals, coordination often fails, and the group ends up worse off than if a single agent had acted for everyone. We study this multi-user, multi-agent setting across five frontier models and 77 scenarios in four environments: an API key environment in which agents share a compute budget, a clinic in which they share a calendar, a personal assistant environment in which they share a group order or booking, and a merge queue in which they share a release cutoff. In each scenario, we compare a single agent that serves every user (a coordinator) to a team in which each agent serves one user, with and without a communication channel between the agents. Teams deliver worse group outcomes than the coordinator in every environment: without a channel, they completely collapse in two environments, and even with one, coordination overhead creates substantial gaps. For example, in the personal assistant environment, the coordinator fulfills a targeted user request about twice as often as teams. We identify distinct behaviors associated with this poor group-level performance, including stalling as teams grow, overriding each other's actions, and fabricating claims. We find effective but environment-specific mitigations, such as a team lead, explicit procedural instructions, and a platform check that makes an agent read its peers' messages before committing.
