Results show consistent downward shifts in feasibility profiles under strengthened observable controls, while worst case residual feasibility persists when technique specific core controls remain absent from the evidence scope.

</details>


### [104] [DRelay: Global Draft Context for Prefix-Aware Parallel Speculative Decoding Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01439)
*Zhuoyu Wang,Junnan Huang,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRelay 提出基于全局信息的"前缀感知选择性修复"机制，在目标模型验证前修正并行草稿中的早期选择错误，从而延长接受前缀，显著提升推测解码的接受长度与端到端速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行草稿（parallel drafting）虽降低了推测解码的草稿开销，但其收益受限于"被接受前缀长度"。即使正确 token 已在候选池中，一旦某位置出现过早的选择错误，后续预测便无法被利用，导致接受前缀过早截断。

Method: DRelay 在目标模型验证前，利用整个草稿块的全局信息对候选选择进行前缀感知的选择性修复。其决策同时依赖候选相关性（candidate correlations）与已选路径：全局读取器（global reader）为每个候选项跨位置提取预测信息；因果选择器（causal selector）融合该候选级信息与前置位置已选 token，判断当前位置的原生选择是否与全局证据及已选前缀一致，进而决定保留或替换该 token。训练上联合训练草稿骨干与选择器，将候选支撑学习与修复目标结合，并依据每个块位置对连续接受前缀的潜在贡献对修复损失加权。

Result: 在 H800 GPU 上的八个多样基准测试中，DRelay 在平均接受长度和端到端解码性能上均稳定优于 DFlash、Domino 与 DSpark。在 SGLang 服务下，DRelay 相对 DFlash、Domino、DSpark 的平均端到端加速分别提升 14.7%–16.8%、8.7%–9.3% 和 8.1%–9.3%。

Conclusion: 通过全局信息驱动的选择性修复与针对性联合训练，DRelay 有效弥补了并行草稿中早期选择错误的短板，延长了接受前缀，并在多种基准与真实服务场景下取得一致的加速收益。

Abstract: Parallel drafting reduces the drafting overhead of speculative decoding for large language models (LLMs), but its gains remain limited by the accepted prefix length. Even when the correct token is present in the candidate pool, a single early selection error prevents subsequent predictions from being used. We propose DRelay, which uses global information from the entire draft block to perform prefix-aware selective repair of candidate selections before target-model verification. DRelay bases its decisions on candidate correlations and the selected path: a global reader extracts predictive information across positions for each candidate. While a causal selector combines candidate-level information extracted by the global read with the tokens selected at preceding positions to determine whether the native choice at the current position is consistent with the global evidence and the selected prefix. It then decides whether to retain or replace the token, thereby repairing early errors and extending the accepted prefix. We further jointly train the draft backbone and the selector, combining candidate-support learning with a repair objective, while weighting the repair loss according to each block position's potential contribution to the consecutive accepted prefix. Across eight diverse benchmarks on an H800 GPU, DRelay consistently improves both average acceptance length and end-to-end decoding performance over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark. Under SGLang serving, DRelay improves average end-to-end speedup over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark by 14.7%-16.8%, 8.7%-9.3%, and 8.1%-9.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [105] [A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Personalized Health Checkup Interpretation and Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01451)
*HyungJun Kim,Taehan Lee,Soojin Cheon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体LLM系统用于个性化体检结果解读：识别多意图、分配给专用智能体并行执行并综合输出。在120条韩语复合查询上，多智能体在LLM评判得分（1.695→1.797, p=0.027）和人工偏好上均优于单智能体，但延迟与成本分别增加1.31倍和2.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化解读体检结果需同时处理纵向记录、医学知识、生活方式指导与就医导航等多重需求，单一智能体难以兼顾复合查询中的全部要求，因此需要多智能体协作来识别并分别处理多意图。

Method: 构建多智能体LLM系统：识别多个意图，将每个意图映射到任务专用智能体，并行执行后综合各智能体输出。使用合成体检记录，构造120条包含2至4项需求的韩语复合查询，比较单智能体与多智能体设置，采用加权LLM评判分数及另外3个LLM评判器评估，并辅以两名人工评估者的成对比较。

Result: 多智能体将加权LLM评判分数从1.695提升至1.797（p=0.027），另外3个LLM评判器结果一致改善（Δ=+0.111至+0.186，均p<0.05）。提升主要来自有用性、一致性和对复合查询中每项需求的处理；数值准确性与依据性仅在4个评判器中的1个下显著改善，医学安全性无差异，关键失误率相近（单智能体15.0% vs 多智能体13.3%）。两名人工评估者分别在66.7%和68.3%的成对比较中偏好多智能体。多智能体使延迟和成本分别增至1.31倍和2.02倍。探索性亚组分析显示，改善集中于涉及个人记录查询的题目。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统在复合体检解读查询中优于单智能体，主要体现在需求覆盖度与一致性，但代价是更高的延迟与成本，且安全性与数值准确性方面未见明显增益；其收益主要限于涉及个人记录查询的场景。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of health checkup results requires reasoning across longitudinal records, medical knowledge, lifestyle guidance, and healthcare navigation. We present a multi-agent large language model (LLM) system that identifies multiple intents, maps each to a task-specific agent, executes them in parallel, and synthesizes their outputs. We compared answers generated in Single Agent and Multi Agent settings on 120 Korean compound queries combining two to four requirements, using synthetic health checkup records. The Multi Agent improved the weighted LLM-judge score from 1.695 to 1.797 (p = 0.027), and three additional LLM judges showed consistent improvements ($Δ$ = +0.111 to +0.186, all p < 0.05). The gains came from usefulness, consistency, and the handling of every requirement in compound queries, whereas numerical accuracy and grounding improved significantly under only one of the four judges and medical safety did not differ, and critical failures occurred at similar rates (Single Agent 15.0% vs. Multi Agent 13.3%). Two human evaluators preferred Multi Agent in 66.7% and 68.3% of pairwise comparisons. Multi Agent execution increased latency and cost by 1.31$\times$ and 2.02$\times$, respectively. In exploratory subgroup analyses, the improvement was concentrated in queries involving personal-record lookup.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking Probability-Based Reinforcement Learning From Posterior Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01458)
*Shiu-Hong Kao,Yubo Zhao,Zhenyu Tian,Pengzhan Sun,Yicong Li,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了概率奖励在长推理链下的一种失败模式——后验集中现象（PCP），并提出无验证器的强化学习框架 RLCPR，通过不确定性感知采样与集中感知正则化稳定优化、提升 token 效率，在 7 个基准中的 6 个上超越最强无验证器基线至多 4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无验证器强化学习借助基于概率的奖励，可在缺乏外部验证器的通用推理任务上训练大语言模型，但这类奖励的可靠性，尤其是长程推理场景下的可靠性，尚未被充分研究。作者发现，随着推理轨迹变长，参考答案在给定轨迹下的条件概率常坍缩到一个低方差区间，导致奖励几乎无法区分，进而使基于 GRPO 的策略优化不稳定、低效。这一长度依赖的失败模式被命名为后验集中现象（PCP）。

Method: 提出 RLCPR（Reinforcement Learning with Concentration-aware Posterior Rewards）无验证器强化学习框架，显式建模 PCP，包含两个组件：1）不确定性感知数据采样，在生成前减少易发生集中的 rollout；2）集中感知正则化，当后验奖励坍缩时惩罚不必要的过长推理轨迹。

Result: 在 7 个基准（含通用领域与数学推理任务）中，RLCPR 在 6 个上超越当前最优的无验证器强化学习基线，提升最高达 4.0%，同时具备更高的 token 效率。

Conclusion: 后验集中现象是概率奖励在长程推理中的关键可靠性瓶颈，显式针对该现象设计的采样与正则化机制可有效提升无验证器强化学习的优化稳定性与 token 效率，并带来一致的性能收益。

Abstract: Verifier-free reinforcement learning with probability-based rewards offers a promising way to train LLMs on general reasoning tasks where external verifiers are unavailable. Yet the reliability of these rewards, especially in long-horizon reasoning, remains underexplored. This work identifies a length-dependent failure mode of probability rewards, which we call the Posterior Concentration Phenomenon (PCP). We show that the probability of a reference answer conditioned on a reasoning trace often collapses to a low-variance interval as the trace becomes lengthy. This phenomenon results in nearly indistinguishable rewards, which, under GRPO-based settings, makes probability-based policy optimization unstable and inefficient. Motivated by this, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Concentration-aware Posterior Rewards (RLCPR), a verifier-free RL framework to explicitly account for PCP for better optimization stability and token efficiency. It has two components: uncertainty-aware data sampling, which reduces concentration-prone rollouts before generation, and concentration-aware regularization, which penalizes unnecessarily long traces when posterior rewards collapse. Extensive experiments show that, alongside higher token efficiency, RLCPR outperforms the state-of-the-art verifier-free RL baseline by up to 4.0% on six of seven benchmarks, including general-domain and mathematical reasoning challenges.

</details>


### [107] [NextMe-800: Anticipating Personal Behavior from Months of Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01461)
*Zhaoxu Meng,Yiming Sun,Mingyuan Gao,Jiachang Zhang,Zhuhan Dai,Yipeng Du,Zheng Lian,Jian-Qiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NextMe-800——一个约800小时、126天、单人第一视角数据集（含1Hz图像、注视、音频，五级层次化标注），并将个性化动作预判定义为开放词汇K步序列预测，构建NextAct基准（1,500个评测点），用基于嵌入的软编辑距离评估模型跨抽象层次与预测时域的行为预判能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们常雄心勃勃地计划却按习惯行事，事后才反思若能预知实际行为是否会做出不同规划。事后之明对过去决策有价值，但人们希望在抉择当下就能"模拟事后之明"。若系统能从个人历史生成合理的行为轨迹，这种预览或可帮助人们制定更现实的计划、做出更明智的决策。为此需要长时程的个性化第一视角数据与评测框架。

Method: 构建NextMe-800数据集：来自一名志愿者、跨越126天、约800小时的第一视角记录，包含1Hz图像、注视与音频，并在从原子动作到重大活动的五个层次化抽象级别上进行文本标注。将个性化动作预判形式化为开放词汇的K步序列预测任务。构建NextAct基准，包含1,500个评测点，融合NextMe-800与多人EgoLife数据集。采用基于嵌入的软编辑距离（soft edit distance）作为评价指标。

Result: 通过软编辑距离指标，评测了不同模型在不同抽象层次和不同预测时域下对个人行为的预判表现。

Conclusion: NextMe-800与NextAct提供了一个长达数月的数据资源与评测框架，用于研究从第一视角观察中能提前多久预判个人行为。

Abstract: We often plan ambitiously yet act habitually and wonder, in retrospect, whether we would have planned differently had we known what we would actually do. Hindsight offers a valuable perspective on past decisions, although we often wish we could have simulated hindsight at the moment of choosing. If a system could generate plausible trajectories from one's personal history, such previews might help people formulate more realistic plans and make better informed decisions. We introduce NextMe-800, an approximately 800-hour first-person dataset from one volunteer over 126 days with 1 Hz images, gaze, and audio, captioned at five hierarchical abstraction levels from atomic actions to major activities. We formulate personalized action anticipation as open-vocabulary K-step sequence prediction and construct NextAct, a 1,500-point benchmark combining NextMe-800 with the multi-person EgoLife dataset. Using an embedding-based soft edit distance as the metric, we evaluate how well different models can anticipate personal behavior across abstraction levels and prediction horizons. NextMe-800 and NextAct provide a months-long resource and evaluation framework for studying how far ahead personal behavior can be anticipated from egocentric observation.

</details>


### [108] [Auditing Routing Entropy as an Uncertainty Signal in Attention-Residual Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01495)
*Wenhao Liang,Lin Yue,Wei Emma Zhang,Mingyu Guo,Olaf Maennel,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 审计AR变体（Swin-Tiny、DeiT-Small）的路由熵是否携带超出模型置信度的正确性信息，发现固定30次二值检验无一通过多重校正，种子间不复现，标量路由探针无合并改进，熵剖面探针优于乱序迹但不如仅置信度预测器；敏感性审计表明估计器恢复不完整，故结论是控制依赖的增益与不完整的估计器恢复，而非不存在条件路由信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态架构在每次预测旁留下一条例样例路由迹，弥散路由容易被解读为预测不可靠的标志。作者要审计这一解读，检验路由熵迹是否在模型自身置信度之外还携带关于正确性的信息。

Method: 在CIFAR-10/100上从零训练Attention-Residual（AR）变体的Swin-Tiny与DeiT-Small，使用软分箱校准辅助损失；三项检验：固定置信度下是否出现路由信号、是否跨训练种子复现、留出预测器能否在仅输出与乱序迹对照下利用该信号。随后进行敏感性审计，注入已知大小的效应并测量各探针恢复的比例。

Result: 固定30次二值检验无一通过多重校正，名义命中与边缘结果在其兄弟种子中均不复现。24对配对运行中，标量路由探针在路由分层校准上无合并改进；熵剖面探针优于乱序剖面，却不如仅置信度预测器（二值对数损失与Brier分数）。对完整logit向量条件化后该比较仍无定论。敏感性审计：注入0.010 nats效应时剖面探针恢复24-59%的oracle增益，参考保持校正探针在两个CIFAR-100设置中分别恢复8%和23%，低于应用于真实标签所设阈值。

Conclusion: 结果确立了控制依赖的增益与不完整的估计器恢复，而非条件路由信息的缺失；非检测不能被过度解读为路由迹无信息。

Abstract: Dynamic architectures leave a per-example routing trace beside each prediction, and diffuse routing is easy to read as a sign that the prediction is unreliable. We audit that reading for routing entropy in Attention-Residual (AR) variants of Swin-Tiny and DeiT-Small, trained from scratch on CIFAR-10/100 with a soft-binned calibration auxiliary loss, asking whether the trace carries information about correctness beyond what the model's own confidence already reveals. Three checks probe this increment: does a routing signal appear at fixed confidence, does it replicate across training seeds, and can a held-out predictor exploit it against output-only and shuffled-trace controls? A sensitivity audit then injects effects of known size and measures the fraction of each that the probes recover. No test in the fixed 30-test binned family survives multiplicity correction, and neither the nominal hit nor a borderline result recurs in its sibling seeds. Across 24 paired runs a scalar routing probe yields no pooled improvement in routing-stratified calibration, and an entropy-profile probe predicts correctness better than the same probe given shuffled profiles yet worse than a confidence-only predictor in both binary log-loss and Brier score: a gain over shuffled traces does not become a gain over the output. Conditioning on the complete logit vector leaves the corresponding comparison unresolved. The audit bounds how far these non-detections can be read: at an injected effect of 0.010 nats the profile probe recovers 24-59% of the oracle gain, and a reference-preserving correction probe recovers 8% and 23% in the two CIFAR-100 settings, below the threshold we fixed for applying it to real labels. The results establish control-dependent gains and incomplete estimator recovery, not the absence of conditional routing information.

</details>


### [109] [OpenMTB-Audit: Exposing Over-Refusal and Clinical Expert Perspectives in LLM-Based Molecular Tumor Board Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01497)
*Negin Ashrafi,Jia Luo,Stacey M. Frumm,Roxana Daneshjou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenMTB-Audit基准（500例合成NSCLC病例，5类对抗性错误、4种安全标签），发现8种LLM配置普遍存在"过度拒绝"（83.3-100%的Partially Supported病例被错误合并标签）；为此开发确定性七模块框架MTB-AuditAgent，将过度拒绝降至6.7%，准确率达91.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子肿瘤委员会需整合基因组、临床与治疗证据以支持精准肿瘤学。AI介入该流程的核心安全挑战在于：区分"真正无证据支持的建议"与"有证据支持但因信息不全、ECOG体能状态差等临床因素仍需肿瘤医生复核的选项"。现有评估缺乏能刻画这种细微差别的基准。

Method: 构建开源基准OpenMTB-Audit：500例合成非小细胞肺癌病例，覆盖五类对抗性错误、四种安全标签（Supported、Partially Supported、Unsupported、Insufficient Information）。评测八种大语言模型配置；并开发MTB-AuditAgent——确定性七模块框架，将证据核验、缺失信息检测、安全分类与弃权（abstention）分离处理。另进行双肿瘤医生标注研究。

Result: 所有LLM配置在真实的Partially Supported病例中有83.3-100%未能保留该标签，通过"标签坍缩"获得高总体安全分，而非临床校准的推理；MTB-AuditAgent将过度拒绝降至6.7%，准确率91.2%（95% CI: 88.6-93.6%）。双肿瘤医生标注的分歧集中于"信息充分性"与"治疗优化"的边界。

Conclusion: LLM在MTB安全分类中存在系统性过度拒绝与标签坍缩，掩盖了真实推理缺陷；确定性的模块化框架可显著缓解该问题。临床标注分歧表明须保留具有临床意义的标签区分，避免为追求聚合安全指标而牺牲细粒度。

Abstract: Molecular tumor boards integrate genomic findings, clinical context, and therapeutic evidence to support precision oncology. As AI enters this workflow, a key safety challenge is distinguishing truly unsupported recommendations from evidence-supported options that still require oncologist review because of incomplete information, poor ECOG performance status, or other clinical caveats. We introduce OpenMTB-Audit, an open-source benchmark of 500 synthetic non-small cell lung cancer cases spanning five adversarial error categories and four safety labels: Supported, Partially Supported, Unsupported, and Insufficient Information. Across eight large language model configurations, we identify pervasive over-refusal: all LLM configurations failed to retain the Partially Supported label in 83.3-100% of true Partially Supported cases, achieving high aggregate safety scores through label collapse rather than clinically calibrated reasoning. To address this limitation, we developed MTB-AuditAgent, a deterministic seven-module framework separating evidence verification, missing-information detection, safety classification, and abstention. It reduces over-refusal to 6.7% and achieves 91.2% accuracy (95% CI: 88.6-93.6%). A two-oncologist annotation study found disagreement concentrated at the boundary between information sufficiency and treatment optimization, underscoring the need to preserve clinically meaningful distinctions.

</details>


### [110] [MCRI: A Four-Dimensional Framework for Analyzing and Evaluating Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01506)
*Zongrui Yang,Li Xintong,Runchen Xu,Zhongsheng Wang,Zhedong Lin,Haoyuan Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出四维MCRI框架及基于大模型的评测方法MCRI-Eval，用于系统分析和评估Agent技能，并在大规模公开技能与多基准执行上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agent从单工具系统演变为模块化复合架构，技能成为能力开发与分发的重要机制，但学术界缺乏系统化分析和评估技能的结构化框架。

Method: 借鉴信息增益与行为约束，提出四维MCRI框架，并将其操作化为基于大语言模型的评测方法MCRI-Eval；使用OpenClaw技能库中的63,812个公开技能，在BigCodeBench、BFCL-Fundamental和Mind2Web上完成58,275次技能条件模型执行进行验证。

Result: MCRI-Eval得分与社区流行度信号正相关，在评估方法中取得最高的下游排序一致性；在三项基准上均提升top-1技能选择，相较各基准最强基线，下游性能排名分别提升17.7、22.8和19.6个百分点。

Conclusion: MCRI-Eval可在高成本执行式评估之前提供有用的预执行信号，用于优先筛选有潜力的技能。

Abstract: As agents evolve from single-tool systems into modular, composite architectures, skills are becoming an important mechanism for capability development and distribution. However, the academic community lacks a structured framework for systematically analyzing and evaluating skills. Drawing on information gain and behavioral constraint, we propose the four-dimensional MCRI Framework and operationalize it as MCRI-Eval, a large language model-based evaluation method. We evaluate MCRI-Eval using 63,812 public skills from the OpenClaw skill Hub, with 58,275 skill-conditioned model executions across BigCodeBench, BFCL-Fundamental, and Mind2Web. MCRI-Eval scores are positively associated with community popularity signals and achieve the highest downstream ranking agreement among the evaluated methods. MCRI-Eval also improves top-1 skill selection across all three benchmarks: compared with the strongest baseline on each benchmark, the skills selected by MCRI-Eval advance by 17.7, 22.8, and 19.6 percentile points in downstream performance rank on BigCodeBench, BFCL-Fundamental, and Mind2Web, respectively. These results indicate that MCRI-Eval provides a useful pre-execution signal for prioritizing promising skills before costly execution-based evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Sharpening Tax in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01509)
*Changdae Oh,Qi Zeng,Qi Qi,Andrey Zhmoginov,Deren Lei,Yun He,Hoang Phan,Hangoo Kang,Azalia Mirhoseini,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 预训练LLM配轻量推理框架即可胜任智能体任务；RL后训练会“锐化”行为，提升单次准确率却牺牲解覆盖度，作者提出度量该代价的指标Sharpening Tax及缓解方法PTGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观点认为RL后训练只是强化基座模型已有行为，以解覆盖度换取单次准确率，但该权衡在数学、代码任务中被观察到，未必适用于需要多轮工具使用与交互的智能体任务。作者希望检验后训练在智能体场景下是否同样"锐化"行为，并量化其对测试时扩展性的代价。

Method: 1) 用预训练模型加轻量推理harness与对应后训练模型对比，在充足测试时预算下评估pass@1与pass@K；2) 分析机制，指出后训练把任务推向恒解或恒不解两极；3) 提出诊断指标Sharpening Tax，量化后训练带来的测试时可扩展性损失，并在14对基座/后训练模型、4个模型家族、3个智能体基准（共42例）上验证；4) 提出posterior-tempered group sampling (PTGS)，一种按估计难度自适应调整采样温度的贝叶斯采样器，作为即插即用模块在两个智能体环境的RL训练中使用。

Result: 预训练模型虽pass@1远低，但在足够测试时预算下pass@K常超过后训练版本；Sharpening Tax在多数设置中普遍存在，可由少量rollout估计，并与其他指标良好相关；PTGS在重复采样下解出更多任务，同时提升单次准确率，且比固定温度基线付出的tax更小。

Conclusion: RL后训练在智能体任务中并非单纯增强能力，而是以解覆盖度和测试时可扩展性为代价换取采样效率与一致性；这种代价普遍存在且可度量、可缓解，PTGS为降低该代价提供了简单有效的方案。

Abstract: An emerging hypothesis about reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) is that it merely sharpens existing behaviors of a base model, improving single-shot accuracy at the cost of solution coverage. Although this trade-off has been observed in math and coding tasks, it need not extend to agentic tasks, where multi-turn tool use and interaction may require capabilities newly acquired during post-training. Our surprising finding is that pre-trained LLMs, equipped with a light inference harness, can serve as capable agents. Despite far lower accuracy (pass@1), they often surpass their post-trained counterparts in solution coverage (pass@K) given a sufficient test-time budget. We further analyze the underlying mechanism and show that post-training pushes tasks toward two extremes, always solved or never solved, and thereby improves sampling efficiency and consistency at the cost of solution coverage. To measure this cost, we propose Sharpening Tax, a diagnostic metric that quantifies the loss in test-time scalability after post-training. Across 14 base/post-trained model pairs from four families and three agentic benchmarks (42 cases in total), the tax is prevalent in most settings, can be estimated from a few rollouts, and correlates well with other metrics. Finally, we present posterior-tempered group sampling (PTGS), a simple plug-and-play Bayesian sampler that adapts the sampling temperature per prompt to its estimated difficulty. Applied during RL training in two agentic environments, PTGS pays a smaller tax than the fixed-temperature baseline, solving more tasks under repeated sampling while also improving single-shot accuracy.

</details>


### [112] [Decision Titan: Test-Time Training for Long-Term Memory in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01513)
*Jude Waide,Robert Lieck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将TTT框架引入离线强化学习，提出Decision Titan（在Decision Transformer中加入TTT层），在X-Maze环境中验证其长时依赖建模能力并可视化分析记忆机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时依赖是AI序列决策的核心难题：RNN存在梯度消失与隐藏状态表达能力有限的问题，Transformer受限于注意力二次复杂度。TTT框架通过在训练和测试时用梯度下降将情景记忆存入网络参数，已在NLP中成功，但尚未应用于强化学习，也缺乏对其记忆机制的实证分析。

Method: 用TTT层增强Decision Transformer，构建Decision Titan模型，应用于离线RL；在T-Maze扩展环境X-Maze上评估其性能与性质，并通过可视化门控值随时间的演化来考察记忆机制的学习过程。

Result: Decision Titan能学习比上下文窗口长20倍的长时依赖；可泛化到训练数据1.7倍的序列长度；但时间泛化能力取决于所采用的时间嵌入方式，而长时依赖学习能力取决于相关信息被编码的方式。

Conclusion: TTT框架在离线强化学习中具有潜力，其长时记忆能力显著，但效果高度依赖于时间嵌入和信息的编码方式，这为后续设计提供了关键指导。

Abstract: Long-term dependencies remain a major challenge for sequential decision-making in the field of AI: RNNs suffer from vanishing gradients and the limited expressivity of vector-based hidden states, whilst Transformer-based models are limited by the quadratic scaling of attention. Recent work has proposed tackling this problem with the Test-Time Training (TTT) framework, which stores episodic memories in the parameters of a neural network through gradient descent at both train and test-time. This approach has seen success in the domain of Natural Language Processing, however, to the best of our knowledge it has not yet been applied to the domain of Reinforcement Learning (RL), nor has there been a study analysing how this memory practically functions. In this paper, we study the potential of the TTT framework for offline RL by augmenting a Decision Transformer with TTT layers, dubbed the Decision Titan. We analyse performance and properties of the model in the X-Maze environment, an extension of T-Maze designed to test sequential memory, and investigate how the memory mechanism learns by visualising gate values over time. Our key findings are that Decision Titan can learn long-term dependencies with ranges 20x longer than the context window, generalises to lengths 1.7x the training data, but crucially temporal generalisation depends on the time embeddings used, and the ability to learn long-term dependencies depends on how the relevant information is encoded.

</details>


### [113] [Towards Reliable Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01531)
*Manasa Mariam Mammen,Priyanka Mary Mammen,Zafer Kayatas,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种视觉语言模型在四类驾驶相关问答数据集及多种视觉输入设置下，面对视觉退化的鲁棒性与置信度可靠性，并检验了视觉证据增强（VEA）方法的改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型在自动驾驶中承担场景理解、驾驶推理、决策与端到端驾驶等任务，其鲁棒性和可靠性愈发重要。真实条件下视觉输入可能因传感器缺陷和环境因素而退化，影响模型预测及其置信度；在安全攸关的自动驾驶中，模型既要预测准确，也要能识别自身预测何时不可靠。

Method: 在四个驾驶相关问答数据集上、采用单帧、多视角、多帧和单目等不同视觉输入设置，评估五种视觉语言模型（Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma4-E4B、LLaVA-OneVision-7B、DriveFusion/DriveFusionQA-4B、NVIDIA Alpamayo-1.5-10B）；随后应用推理时方法"视觉证据增强"（VEA），考察其能否在视觉退化条件下提升模型可靠性。

Result: 视觉损坏的影响因模型、数据集和输入设置而异，准确率与置信度可靠性的变化在不同条件下也各不相同。VEA 对部分模型和数据集有改进，但增益并非在所有设置下都一致。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在视觉退化条件下的可靠性表现存在显著差异，VEA 只能带来部分且不一致的改进，说明在自动驾驶中提升此类模型的鲁棒性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) are increasingly being explored in autonomous driving for tasks such as scene understanding, driving reasoning, decision-making, and end-to-end driving. As their role becomes more prominent, ensuring their robustness and reliability is increasingly important. In real-world conditions, visual inputs may be degraded by sensor imperfections and environmental conditions, potentially affecting both model predictions and their associated confidence. Such degradation is especially concerning in autonomous driving, where safety-critical decisions require models to make accurate predictions and recognize when their predictions may be unreliable. In this work, we evaluate five VLMs (Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma4-E4B, LLaVA-OneVision-7B, DriveFusion/DriveFusionQA-4B, and NVIDIA Alpamayo-1.5-10B) across four driving-related QA datasets with different visual input settings, including single-frame, multi-view, multi-frame, and monocular inputs. Our results show that the effects of visual corruption vary across models, datasets, and input settings, with changes in accuracy and confidence reliability and also differing across conditions. We then apply Visual Evidence Augmentation ($\mathrm{V}{\scriptstyle \mathrm{EA}}$), a recent inference-time method to examine whether it can improve model reliability under degraded visual conditions. We find that $\mathrm{V}{\scriptstyle \mathrm{EA}}$ improves performance for some models and datasets, although the gains are not consistent across all settings.

</details>


### [114] [Neither Black nor White: Balancing Semantic and Collaborative Signals with Graph-Informed Semantic IDs (GrIS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01533)
*Aleksei Medvedev,Alejandro Ariza-Casabona,Steven Derby,Gonzalo Fiz Pontiveros,Xinyang Shao,Florian Spiess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将语义ID（SID）构建重新定义为递归聚类问题，提出分层图划分的统一框架GrIS，把RQ-VAE/RQ-KMeans作为空图的特例，并实例化出RecDMoN与RQ-GAE两种方法，在多数据集上最高提升52% Hit@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有生成式推荐的SID工作把SID构建当作表示学习问题（将物品编码到量化隐空间再读取码本），作者认为这一视角是偶然的；真正本质在于递归聚类，且应当对同时承载语义内容与协同信号的图进行聚类，从而统一并拓展已有方法。

Method: 提出GrIS统一框架：把SID分配视为分层图划分，图的节点带语义内容、边带协同信号；框架沿两个此前被合并的维度展开——图构建与递归划分算法。给出两种实例：RecDMoN（借助可微图池化进行分层分配）与RQ-GAE（在RQ-VAE基础上加入图感知物品表示与图重建目标）。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上，GrIS持续优于协同过滤感知的SOTA方法，Hit@10最高提升52%。由于图构建与划分是可显式、可分别配置的组件，两条轴上的改进可以组合并系统评估。

Conclusion: SID构建的本质是递归图聚类；该重构提供了一个统一框架，将RQ-VAE与RQ-KMeans纳入为图为空时的特例，揭示内容-only量化只是更大设计空间的一角，并为未来方法设计提供可分解、可组合的模块化方案。

Abstract: Existing work on Semantic IDs (SIDs) for generative recommendation treats SID construction as a representation learning problem: encode items into a quantised latent space and read off codes. We argue this view is incidental. SID construction is, at heart, a recursive clustering problem, and once stated this way the natural object to cluster is a graph whose nodes carry semantic content and whose edges carry collaborative signal; SID assignment becomes a hierarchical graph partition. This reframing yields a unified framework, Graph-Informed Semantic IDs (GrIS), that subsumes prior approaches rather than displacing them. RQ-VAE and RQ-KMeans are recovered as the special case where the graph is empty, exposing content-only quantisation as one corner of a larger design space along two so-far-collapsed axes: graph construction and recursive partition algorithm. We explore two contrasting instantiations: RecDMoN, which performs hierarchical assignment via differentiable graph pooling, and RQ-GAE, which extends RQ-VAE with graph-aware item representations and a graph reconstruction objective. On multiple real-world datasets, GrIS consistently improves over CF-aware SOTA, with gains of up to +52\% Hit@10. Because graph construction and partition are explicit, separately configurable components, improvements on either axis can be combined and evaluated systematically.

</details>


### [115] [The AI Assessment Sandbox Configurator: A Framework to Support Technical Assessment in AI Regulatory Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01539)
*Alessio Buscemi,German Castignani,Daniele Pagani,Maxime Cordy,Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧盟《人工智能法案》要求各成员国在2027年8月前建立AI监管沙箱（AIRS）的背景，提出面向AIRS技术测试基础设施的11项架构与治理需求，并推出开源框架AI Assessment Sandbox Configurator以支撑测试编排、输出统一与报告生成，并通过早期试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案要求成员国建立AI监管沙箱，规模化技术测试需要专用基础设施，但现有工具生态高度碎片化，输出难以比较、追溯和复用。

Method: 从AIRS参与的程序条件与高风险系统义务中推导出11项架构与治理需求；据此设计开源框架，包含经策展的测试与控制目录（通过稳定插件API访问）、统一异构输出的共享数据模型、面向多学科的角色化仪表盘以及分受众报告。

Result: 介绍了框架架构与当前版本，并报告了在真实AIRS参与中检验统一化与报告层的早期试点，其成果贡献于一份官方Exit Report。

Conclusion: 讨论了路线图、目录分层贡献模型引发的治理问题，以及开源评估生态在成员国间形成的制度路径。

Abstract: The EU's Artificial Intelligence Act requires all Member States to establish AI Regulatory Sandboxes (AIRS) by August 2027: supervised environments bringing together national Competent Authorities, technical experts, and the organisations under assessment. When AIRS engagements include structured technical testing, running such testing at scale demands dedicated infrastructure, yet the tooling ecosystem remains structurally fragmented, with heterogeneous tools producing outputs that are difficult to compare, trace, and reuse. From the procedural conditions of AIRS engagements and the AI Act obligations for high-risk systems, we derive 11 architectural and governance requirements for the infrastructure that operationalises technical testing within an AIRS. In response to these requirements, we introduce the AI Assessment Sandbox Configurator, an open-source framework combining a curated Catalogue of tests and controls accessed through a stable plug-in API, a shared data model that harmonises heterogeneous outputs, role-specific dashboards for multi-disciplinary interpretation, and audience-segmented reporting. We describe the architecture and current release, and report an early-stage pilot that exercised the harmonisation and reporting layers within a live AIRS engagement and contributed to an official Exit Report. We discuss the roadmap, the governance questions raised by the Catalogue's tiered contribution model, and the institutional pathways through which an open-source assessment ecosystem could emerge across Member States.

</details>


### [116] [Evaluating Physical Consistency and Plausibility in Generative Scenario Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01581)
*Manasa Mariam Mammen,Zafer Kayatas,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自动驾驶场景生成的分层评估协议，从内部表征、网络层到输出动力学约束共五层进行评估，并将其应用于VAE等生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI用于自动驾驶场景生成时，虽能生成看似逼真的场景，但学习到的表征缺乏透明度，且与真实车辆动力学的 consistency 不足，缺少形式化保障，限制了其在安全关键的验证与认证流程中的应用。

Method: 提出一种分层评估协议，作为现有方法的补充，从五个层面评估模型：前四层通过运动学对齐、统计基线比较、潜在空间可控性、激活分析来检查内部表征与网络层；第五层则依据横向 jerk 阈值等车辆动力学约束来评估模型输出。

Result: 在一个基于VAE的场景生成器上进行验证：尽管标准输出层指标与可视化显示生成场景逼真，该协议仍揭示了潜在空间与运动学特征的对齐程度，以及视觉上合理的轨迹是否满足车辆动力学约束。此外，将该协议应用于其他生成模型，证明其可扩展至VAE架构之外。

Conclusion: 该分层评估协议能为自动驾驶生成模型的内部表征与动力学一致性提供更深入的评估，提升其在安全关键验证与认证工作流中的可用性与可信度。

Abstract: Generative AI models are increasingly used for scenario generation in autonomous driving. While they can generate realistic-looking scenarios, they often provide limited transparency into learned representations and consistency with real-world vehicle dynamics. This lack of formal assurance limits their use in safety-critical validation and certification workflows. To address this aspect, we introduce a layered evaluation protocol that complements existing methods by assessing models across five layers. The first four layers inspect internal representations and network layers through kinematic alignment, statistical baseline comparison, latent controllability, and activation analysis. The fifth layer evaluates model outputs against vehicle dynamics constraints such as lateral jerk thresholds. We demonstrate the protocol on a Variational Autoencoder (VAE)-based scenario generator. Although standard output-level metrics and visualizations suggest that the generated scenarios are realistic, our protocol provides deeper insight into the extent to which the model's latent space aligns with kinematic features and whether visually plausible trajectories satisfy vehicle-dynamics constraints. We further apply the protocol to additional generative models, demonstrating its applicability beyond the VAE architecture.

</details>


### [117] [Agents Are Systems, Not Models: Rethinking Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01618)
*Luis Wiedmann,Leander Girrbach,Cordelia Schmid,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将编码智能体视为可配置系统进行评测，通过四个科学任务的新基准考察任务信息、推理、自验证、时间预算和骨干模型五个配置维度，发现配置选择显著影响性能与稳定性，且配置间存在交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测多聚焦单一成功率指标（如成本、一致性、鲁棒性），但通常将智能体本身视为固定不变。实际上智能体是可配置系统——用户决定告知其什么信息、让其运行多久、使用哪个模型，这些选择都会影响表现和稳定性，因此需要将智能体作为可配置系统来评测。

Method: 构建包含四个科学任务的新基准，要求编码智能体找到并正确操作已发表的专用模型。系统考察智能体配置的五个方面：任务信息、推理、自验证、时间预算和骨干模型。基于轨迹对智能体行为进行分类分析，并发布基准及超过18,000条智能体轨迹。

Result: 存在显著的运行间变异性，约54%的结果方差来自重复同一配置而非改变配置。跨配置比较中，提供给智能体的信息影响最大，超过时间预算和模型规模，同时降低成本并改善校准度。配置选择存在交互：额外时间仅在智能体有足够信息或足够强的模型时才有帮助。基于轨迹的行为分类显示，提示智能体验证答案对其验证行为影响很小，而提供专门的验证工具则显著改变该行为。

Conclusion: 智能体本身应作为可配置系统来评测；某些期望行为在系统中实现比通过提示请求更有效。研究发布了基准和超过18,000条智能体轨迹。

Abstract: Agent evaluations increasingly go beyond a single success rate, reporting metrics such as cost, consistency, and robustness. Yet they typically treat the agent itself as fixed. In practice, an agent is a configurable system: users decide what to tell it, how long to let it run, and which model to use, and each of these choices can change how well and how consistently it performs. We study these choices on a new benchmark of four scientific tasks, where a coding agent must find and correctly operate a published specialist model. We investigate five parts of the agent's configuration: task information, reasoning, self-verification, time budget, and backbone model. We find substantial run-to-run variability, with approximately 54% of the outcome variance coming from repeating the same configuration rather than changing it. Across configurations, the information provided to the agent has the largest effect, exceeding both time budget and model size, while also reducing cost and improving calibration. Configuration choices also interact: additional time helps only when the agent has sufficient information or a capable enough model to use it. Finally, a trajectory-based taxonomy of agent behavior reveals that prompting an agent to verify its answer has little effect on its verification behavior, whereas providing a dedicated verification tool changes that behavior substantially. These results suggest that agents should be evaluated as configurable systems themselves, and that some desired behaviors are more effectively implemented in the system than requested through prompting. We release the benchmark and more than 18,000 agent trajectories.

</details>


### [118] [FedLore: Communication and Memory Efficient Federated Learning via Shared Gradient Low-Rank Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01620)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FedLore，通过每轮共享并跨轮刷新低秩优化基，解决联邦基础模型训练中的子空间碎片化问题，实现低秩坐标下的精确聚合，并降低通信与优化器状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦基础模型训练受客户端内存与通信成本限制。LoRA 等低秩适配方法虽降低成本，但固定秩预算限制适配能力；梯度低秩优化更灵活，却因各客户端独立选择子空间而带来“子空间碎片化”：局部投影与数据异质性相互作用使聚合方向产生偏差，且聚合会增加更新秩与通信成本。因此，准确的局部梯度压缩并不必然保持全局下降。

Method: FedLore 在每一轮内共享一个低秩优化基，并在轮间刷新该基。共享基使低秩坐标下可精确聚合，消除投影偏差；子空间刷新使累计模型更新能够超过每轮秩预算。作者刻画了聚合偏差，并在全局梯度覆盖条件及标准光滑性与方差假设（含有限梯度异质性）下，为投影 SGD 变体建立了 O(T^{-1/2}) 的平稳性界。

Result: 在视觉与语言任务（包括联邦预训练）上，FedLore 优于所评估的低秩适配器基线，并匹配或超过全参数训练，同时减少通信与优化器状态内存。

Conclusion: 共享低秩优化基与跨轮刷新可同时解决子空间碎片化与秩预算限制，在保证理论收敛性的同时，实现更优的联邦基础模型训练效率与性能。

Abstract: Federated training of foundation models is constrained by client memory and communication costs. LoRA-based methods reduce these costs through low-rank adapters, but their fixed rank budget can limit adaptation. Gradient low-rank optimization offers greater flexibility, yet independently chosen client subspaces create a problem we term \emph{subspace fragmentation}: local projections interact with data heterogeneity to bias aggregated directions, while aggregation can increase update rank and communication cost. Thus, accurate local gradient compression need not preserve global descent. We propose \texttt{FedLore}, which shares a low-rank optimization basis within each round and refreshes it across rounds. The shared basis enables exact aggregation in low-rank coordinates and eliminates the identified projection bias. Subspace refresh allows the accumulated model update to exceed the per-round rank budget. We characterize the aggregation bias and establish an $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound for the projected-SGD variant under a global-gradient coverage condition and standard smoothness and variance assumptions, with bounded gradient heterogeneity. Experiments on vision and language tasks, including federated pre-training, show that \texttt{FedLore} outperforms the evaluated low-rank adapter baselines and matches or exceeds full-parameter training, while reducing communication and optimizer-state memory.

</details>


### [119] [Measuring the Stability Assumption Behind Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01626)
*Aryan Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过注入微小动作误差并测量其在开环与闭环执行下的传播速率，发现稳定收缩状态罕见、误差放大普遍，且误差放大具有前置性，进而指出闭环反应性应被显式训练而非依赖模仿学习自然涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于动作分块（action chunking）为何提升行为克隆性能的解释包括时间一致性、缩短规划视野、表征学习和减少误差累积等，但缺乏对"动作误差进入系统后如何演化"这一机制的深入探讨。作者希望从误差传播动力学角度重新审视这些解释是否完备。

Method: 在每个状态注入微小动作误差，分别在开环（不重规划、重放剩余分块）和闭环（扰动后重新规划）两种执行模式下测量误差的增长率或收缩率，并将状态标注为收缩、扩张或未解析。在来自三个基准套件的十二个操作任务上进行实验，同时研究拟合视野对传播速率的影响，并训练预测器从相机帧和本体感受信号中恢复这些标签。

Result: 在十二个操作任务中，稳定收缩的状态稀少，而误差放大在可解析传播速率的状态中普遍存在。传播速率强烈依赖拟合视野：放大通常前置，短窗口会高估长视野传播。预测器可从相机帧和本体感受恢复开环传播情况，但闭环传播只能部分恢复，因为它还取决于扰动后策略如何行动。

Conclusion: 单独的误差累积论证不足以完整解释动作分块的作用：无论被动的开环动力学还是策略重规划，都不能持续收缩注入误差，重规划也很少将开环放大转为稳定收缩。因此闭环反应性应通过扰动导向和树覆盖导向的训练显式习得，而非期望其从标准模仿学习中可靠涌现。

Abstract: Action chunking improves the performance of policies learned by behavioural cloning, and several mechanisms have been proposed to explain why, including temporal consistency, horizon reduction, representation learning, and reduced error compounding. We instead study what happens to an action error once it enters the system. At each state, we inject a small action error and measure how fast it grows or shrinks under two execution regimes: open-loop, where the rest of the chunk is replayed without replanning, and closed-loop, where the policy replans after the perturbation. The fitted rate labels each state as contracting, expanding, or unresolved. Across twelve manipulation tasks from three benchmark suites, we find that confidently stable states are rare, while error amplification is common among states whose propagation rate can be resolved. We further find that the measured propagation rate depends strongly on the fitting horizon: amplification is typically front-loaded, so short windows can overestimate longer-horizon propagation. Finally, we train predictors on these labels and find that a state's open-loop regime can be recovered from camera frames and proprioception alone, while its closed-loop propagation is only partially recoverable because it also depends on how the policy acts after the perturbation. These results suggest that error-compounding arguments alone do not provide a complete account of action chunking: neither passive open-loop dynamics nor policy replanning consistently contracts an injected error, and replanning rarely turns open-loop amplification into confident contraction. This suggests that closed-loop reactivity should be trained explicitly, using perturbation- and tree-coverage-oriented training to expose policies to deviations they must recover from, rather than expected to emerge reliably from standard imitation learning.

</details>


### [120] [CoEvolve: Construct-to-Edit Visual Grounding with Bidirectional State Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01710)
*Dongwei Sun,Yujie Zhang,Bowen Yao,Pei Liu,Jing Yao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoEvolve框架，将视觉定位拆分为显式状态构建与状态编辑两个阶段，在9B骨干下达到241B参数模型的定位精度，并具备强错误恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数多模态定位模型把目标识别、空间推理与边界估计压缩为一个终端预测；自由形式的推理链虽使推理语言化，却未暴露可测量、可编辑的空间状态，导致中间定位错误难以诊断与纠正，错误区域选择和不精确边界会残留到最终框。

Method: CoEvolve包含两部分：Region-Evolution Reinforcement（RER）将定位分析组织为递进的语义—空间轨迹，每步推理都承诺一个显式候选区域；Bidirectional Denoising Refiner（BDR）将推理文本视为固定语义上下文，通过双向同位置重建精炼轨迹的坐标字段。另设几何层与行为层目标，提供目标几何与编辑偏好信号，用于巩固可靠候选、保留准确输入或向标注纠正。

Result: 评估覆盖自然图像与遥感定位。9B骨干的CoEvolve在定位精度上可与高达241B参数的模型匹敌；在受控损坏下，单次BDR处理使平均框重叠提升超过27个百分点，表明能从显著定位错误中强恢复；状态来源对比进一步支持显式状态构建与来源匹配编辑的互补性。

Conclusion: 通过将定位显式分解为可测量的状态构建与可编辑的状态精炼，CoEvolve提升了定位精度与错误可诊断/可恢复性，证明了显式空间状态与源匹配编辑设计的有效性。

Abstract: Visual grounding localizes an object described by language with a bounding box. Most multimodal grounding models compress target identification, spatial reasoning, and boundary estimation into one terminal prediction. Free-form rationales make reasoning linguistically explicit but do not necessarily expose measurable, editable spatial states. Intermediate localization errors are therefore difficult to diagnose and correct, allowing incorrect region choices and imprecise boundaries to persist in the final box. We introduce CoEvolve, a construct-to-edit framework that separates grounding into explicit state construction and state editing. Region-Evolution Reinforcement (RER) organizes grounding analysis into a progressive semantic--spatial trajectory, with each reasoning step committing to an explicit candidate region. Bidirectional Denoising Refiner (BDR) treats the reasoning text as fixed semantic context and refines the trajectory's coordinate fields through bidirectional same-position reconstruction. Geometry- and behavior-level objectives provide target geometry and edit-preference signals for consolidating reliable candidates, preserving accurate inputs, or correcting toward annotations. Evaluations cover natural-image and remote-sensing grounding. With a 9B backbone, CoEvolve rivals models up to 241B parameters in grounding accuracy. Under controlled corruption, a single BDR pass improves mean box overlap by over 27 percentage points, demonstrating strong recovery from substantial localization errors. State-source comparisons further support the complementarity of explicit state construction and source-matched editing. The project is at https://sundongwei.github.io/CoEvolve_Project/.

</details>


### [121] [vFedProtoQNAS: Prototype-Guided Personalized Quantum Neural Architecture Search for Virtual Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01718)
*Seok Bin Son,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Soohyun Park,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出vFedProtoQNAS，通过原型共享实现个性化量子神经架构搜索，避免异构QNN参数聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习中设备能力差异导致单一共享QNN架构难以适配所有客户端，而个性化QNAS在结构不同的QNN间平均参数会混合语义不一致的电路操作。

Method: 模型参数从不在客户端间聚合，联邦协作通过类别原型共享实现。每个客户端独立搜索和训练专属QNN，从潜在表示计算类别局部原型，并用服务器全局原型作为联邦语义锚进行精炼。

Result: 实验表明vFedProtoQNAS相比FedAvg准确率提升3.70%，并增强了类别一致的表示对齐。

Conclusion: 基于原型引导的个性化QNAS可有效解决异构量子架构下的联邦协作问题，在不聚合参数的情况下实现性能提升与语义对齐。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning (QFL) has emerged as a promising approach for collaboratively training compact quantum neural networks (QNNs) over distributed private data on resource-constrained devices. However, differences in device capabilities make a single shared QNN architecture unsuitable for all clients. While personalized quantum neural architecture search (QNAS) allows each client to select a device-specific QNN, averaging parameters across structurally different QNN architectures mixes semantically inconsistent circuit operations. To address this, prototype-guided personalized QNAS for virtual FL (vFedProtoQNAS) is proposed, where model parameters are never aggregated across clients and federated collaboration is achieved through class-wise prototype sharing. Each client independently searches and trains a client-specific QNN, computes class-wise local prototypes from latent representations, and refines them using global prototypes from the server as federated semantic anchors. Experiments demonstrate that vFedProtoQNAS improves accuracy by 3.70\% over FedAvg and enhances class-consistent representation alignment.

</details>


### [122] [TopK-Guided: Adaptive, Budget-Aware Activation Sparsity for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01763)
*Mukund Agarwalla,Chih-Jen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopK-Guided是一种免训练激活稀疏方法，通过token级自适应稀疏与块级敏感度分配预算，在Llama模型上超越TEAL和WINA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练激活稀疏方法存在矛盾：阈值法（如TEAL）逐token自适应但无法严格控制实际稀疏率；TopK法（如WINA）保证固定稀疏率但每token用相同预算。两者都对所有transformer块使用同一预算，忽视块间敏感度差异。

Method: 提出TopK-Guided免训练方法，结合有界的token级稀疏自适应与敏感度感知的块级预算分配，融合两种机制。

Result: 在Llama-2和Llama-3上，困惑度和下游准确率均优于TEAL和WINA，同时保持与WINA基本相同的稀疏相关投影计算量，高稀疏率下增益最大。消融实验显示两组件互补。

Conclusion: TopK-Guided有效缓解了现有方法在稀疏控制和预算分配上的局限，在不增加计算的前提下提升LLM推理质量。

Abstract: Activation sparsity speeds up large language model (LLM) inference by setting unimportant activations to zero so that the corresponding computations can be skipped. Existing training-free methods, however, make different trade-offs: threshold-based methods such as TEAL adapt the sparsity level to each token but do not tightly control the realised sparsity, while TopK-based methods such as WINA enforce a fixed sparsity level but use the same sparsity budget for every token. Both also apply the same budget across transformer blocks, despite large differences in block sensitivity. We introduce TopK-Guided, a training-free method that addresses both limitations by combining bounded token-level sparsity adaptation with sensitivity-aware block-level budget allocation. Across Llama-2 and Llama-3 models, TopK-Guided consistently improves perplexity and downstream accuracy over TEAL and WINA while preserving essentially the same sparsitydependent projection compute as WINA, with the largest gains at high sparsity. Ablations show that both components provide complementary improvements.

</details>


### [123] [VideoEvolve: Evolving Agent Harnesses for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01766)
*Bingjun Luo,Yuhuan Fan,Jialin Guo,Siqi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoEvolve，一个自动演化 agent harness 的框架，用于提升视频时序定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于冻结的视频-语言模型构建的 agent，其 harness 决定查询如何引导时序预测及预测如何被细化，但人工改进 harness 需要诊断失败并协调 agent 工作流与指令的改动，成本高。

Method: 提出 VideoEvolve，包含：Cloze-Structured Harness Representation（保持阶段接口不变，让工作流与指令可演化）；Branch-Guided Harness Evolution（保留有潜力的代码分支继续细化，利用执行反馈指导局部编辑，并通过验证决定保留哪些改进）。

Result: 在多个 benchmark 上提升了 grounding 性能。组件分析显示指令细化是一致性增益来源，而演化代码的收益在不同评测设置下存在差异。

Conclusion: 结果表明自动 harness 演化是改进视频时序定位的有效途径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Video temporal grounding aims to localize events in videos from natural-language queries. For agents built around frozen video-language models, the harness determines how queries guide temporal predictions and how those predictions are refined. Manually refining these harnesses requires diagnosing grounding failures and coordinating changes to both agent workflows and instructions. We introduce VideoEvolve, a framework that automatically evolves agent harnesses for video temporal grounding. VideoEvolve uses a Cloze-Structured Harness Representation that preserves stage interfaces while leaving agent workflows and instructions open to evolution. Branch-Guided Harness Evolution preserves promising code branches for continued refinement, using execution feedback to guide local edits and validation to determine which improvements are carried forward. Experiments demonstrate improved grounding performance across multiple benchmarks. Component analyses identify instruction refinement as a consistent source of gains, while the benefits of evolved code vary across evaluation settings. Together, these results support automated harness evolution as an effective approach to improving video temporal grounding. Code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/VideoEvolve .

</details>


### [124] [RealCompanion: Benchmarking Human Understanding from Reasoning over Longitudinal Real-World Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01780)
*Arman Behnam,Sunglyoung Kim,Liangwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发布 \bench 基准：包含10段真实人机伴侣关系、共27,218条消息、最长120天的对话记录，并衍生出档案、人设、对话真值与问题集四个文件，每个标签均附有逐阶段校验的推理链。三项核心发现揭示了长期记忆评估的失真、记忆需求检测的困难以及智能体成本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期陪伴型AI应能记住用户所言、推断其人设，并判断历史信息何时与当前消息相关。但真实个人记录涉及隐私，现有基准只能凭空生成"人"和问题、预先设定什么重要，因而无法反映真实对话中的记忆需求。

Method: 构建 \bench 基准：采集10段真实人机伴侣关系，覆盖最长120天、27,218条消息；将对话本身与四个衍生文件（profile、persona、chat ground truth、question set）一并发布，每个条目均标注其所依据的消息。所有对话标签附带产生该标签的推理痕迹，并逐阶段与对话进行核验。

Result: 第一，过去信息很少被需要且距离遥远：汇总指标具有误导性——近期窗口对95.9%的探针能找到所需消息，但对真正需要记忆的探针仅占2.2%；在自然频率下，提供记录证据所带来增益的96%来自本不需要记忆的消息。第二，所尝试的检测器均无法在真实消息上判断何时需要记忆；由同一批历史记录撰写的提问会泄露线索，而将同样的消息标注为"记忆"可使其使用率提升10至14个百分点。第三，三种智能体系统重建人设的F1相同，但成本相差31倍。

Conclusion: 长期记忆基准应基于真实关系而非生成式设定，并以推理链与出处引用保证可验证性；评估需区分"是否需要记忆"与"是否用上记忆"，否则汇总指标会严重高估记忆的作用；同时记忆系统的成本效率差异巨大，需纳入评估维度。

Abstract: A companion that talks with a person for months should come to understand them. It should remember what they said, infer who they are, and know when the past bears on the message in front of it. Testing this requires a real person's record, and such records are private, so benchmarks generate the person and the questions and settle in advance what matters. We release \bench, ten real relationships with an AI companion: 27,218 messages over up to 120 days, released as the conversation and four files derived from it, a profile, a persona, a chat ground truth and a question set, each citing the messages it rests on. Every chat label carries the reasoning trace that produced it, checked stage by stage against the conversation. Three findings follow. First, the past is rarely needed and far away. Pooled measures mislead: a recency window finds the required message for 95.9\% of probes and 2.2\% of those that need memory, and at the natural rate 96\% of the gain from supplying recorded evidence comes from messages that need none. Second, no detector we tried can tell when memory is needed on real messages, authored questions over the same histories leak the cue, and labeling the same messages as memories raises their use by ten to fourteen points. Third, three agent systems reconstruct the persona with the same F1 at a 31-fold difference in cost.

</details>


### [125] [Q-Learning for Reachability in MEC-Free MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01781)
*Lu-Chin Chang,Suguman Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Quasar是首个在无非终止极大端分量(MEC)的MDP片段上具有渐近可达性保证的无模型算法,通过时序差分Q学习在不估计转移概率的情况下收敛到最优策略,内存占用从O(|S|^2|A|)降至O(|S||A|),样本效率较此前基于模型的最优方法提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达性规约的强化学习是序贯决策的基础。已有工作虽建立了最优策略的渐近收敛,但仅限于基于模型的方法,必须显式估计底层MDP的转移概率,这带来巨大的内存与样本开销,限制了实际部署。

Method: 作者提出Quasar算法,针对无非终止MEC的MDP片段(任何MDP均可通过标准MEC商约简到该片段)。算法遵循经典Q学习框架,利用时序差分更新直接收敛到最优策略,全程无需学习转移概率,属于无模型方法。

Result: 内存占用由基于模型方法所需的O(|S|^2|A|)降至O(|S||A|)。在标准量化验证基准集(Quantitative Verification Benchmark Set)上,算法以比此前基于模型的最优方法少数个数量级的样本量收敛到最优策略。

Conclusion: 该工作为可达性学习的实际部署以及随之而来的规约引导强化学习迈出了具体而坚实的一步。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for reachability specifications is fundamental to sequential decision-making. Prior work establishes asymptotic convergence to optimal policies, but only through model-based methods that must explicitly estimate the transition probabilities of the underlying Markov Decision Process (MDP). We present Quasar, the first model-free algorithm with asymptotic guarantees for reachability on the fragment of MDPs free of non-terminal maximal end components (MECs), a building block to which every MDP reduces by the standard MEC quotient. Our algorithm follows the classical Q-learning approach, using temporal-difference updates to converge to an optimal policy without ever learning the transition probabilities. The resulting learner reduces the memory footprint from the O(|S|^2|A|) that model-based methods require to O(|S||A|). On the standardized Quantitative Verification Benchmark Set, our algorithm converges to the optimal policy with orders of magnitude fewer samples than the previous model-based state-of-the-art. Together these results are a concrete step toward the practical deployment of reachability learning and, with it, of specification-guided RL.

</details>


### [126] [Not All Experience Belongs in the Weights: Component Routing for Self-Improving GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01787)
*Beining Wu,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出“组件路由”：将GUI智能体经验拆分为定位器、流程、状态事实与经验教训四类组件，分别决定送入上下文还是写入权重，并用两条可预先测量的属性（复现性、状态条件性）预测最佳去处，在24/24单元中验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进GUI智能体把产生的轨迹回馈给自身时，可选微调（写入权重）或检索进提示（放入上下文），但既有研究对两种去向的比较结论相互矛盾。作者认为矛盾源于“经验单元”的粒度：一条轨迹把性质不同的内容捆绑在一起，因此关于整体的结论取决于其成分组合。

Method: (i) 提出组件路由，把经验拆为定位器、流程、状态事实、经验教训，在相同项目上跨三个骨干模型家族、两个环境、三个随机种子比较送上下文与写权重。(ii) 用训练前即可测量的复现性与状态条件性拟合规则预测去向，并做两种干预检验边界。(iii) 分析训练与生产者-消费者差异如何改变两种去向的价值。

Result: 同一经验池存在两种最优去向：定位器与经验教训在权重中更优，流程与状态事实在上下文中更优。该规则在留出的骨干家族上24/24单元全部命中；按规则路由优于所有整条轨迹基线，并平均比每个骨干各自最优的单一去向高出3.5个点。此外，同一组件写入权重后其读取价值下降（复现最多者下降最明显），上下文收益随信息差距增大，权重收益随策略差距增大而减小。

Conclusion: 经验的有效去向取决于其组件属性而非整条轨迹，组件路由可作为解决微调与检索之争的统一框架；用复现性和状态条件性即可在训练前预测去向，为GUI智能体的自改进设计提供可操作的准则。

Abstract: Self-improving GUI agents keep the trajectories they produce and return them to the agent, by fine-tuning or by retrieval into the prompt, and studies that compare the two destinations disagree. We attribute this to the unit of experience: a trajectory bundles items with different properties, so a conclusion about the bundle depends on its mix. To address this, (i) we introduce component routing, which splits the experience into locators, procedures, state facts and lessons and sends each component to the context or to the weights, compared on the same items across three backbone families, two environments and three seeds. One pool has two destinations: locators and lessons win in the weights, procedures and state facts in the context. (ii) We fit a rule in two properties measured before any training, recurrence and state-conditionality; it recovers the destination of a held-out backbone family in 24 of 24 cells, two interventions move a component toward the boundary, and routing by the rule beats every whole-trajectory baseline and, by +3.5 points on average, the better single destination of each backbone. (iii) We identify how training and producer-consumer differences change the value of the two destinations: note readout decreases after the same component is written into the weights, most for the items that recur most, context gains increase with the information gap, and weights gains decrease with the policy gap. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [127] [LineupRL: Verifiable Reinforcement Learning for Time Series Captioning via Caption-to-Series Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01800)
*Haochen Zhang,Laura Yao,Zachary Plotkin,Gengwei Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LineupRL，用"图注-序列识别"作为可验证奖励的RLVR流程训练时序图注模型，性能超越SFT与RL基线，3B模型胜过72B教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图注（time series captioning）是连接信号与自然语言的关键任务。监督微调（SFT）依赖更大模型生成的图注，质量难以超越教师；而强化学习（RL）的奖励函数原本为其他模态和任务设计，迁移到时序领域的开放式生成时效果差。

Method: 提出LineupRL，一种带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）流程，奖励为"图注到序列的识别"任务：冻结的LLM验证器只读取生成的图注与候选时序的原始数值（不看图表），需从多个干扰项中选出被描述的序列。匹配远比写问题或评判图注轻松，因此现成LLM即可提供奖励信号。

Result: 在两个图注基准上，以及在预测和重建任务（预测器仅看图注）中，LineupRL在所有指标上均优于SFT和RL基线。经其训练的3B VLM以1/24的参数量超过SFT基线所蒸馏的72B VLM。案例分析显示LineupRL能抵抗奖励黑客，所训图注模型既能刻画趋势又能标出关键点数值。

Conclusion: 以"图注-序列识别"作为可验证奖励，是一种轻量有效、抗奖励黑客的时序图注RLVR方案，可显著提升时序理解与语言桥接能力，并大幅降低模型规模需求。

Abstract: Time series captioning is a fundamental step in time series understanding and can also serve as the bridge between signal and natural language. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) relies on a larger model's captions and cannot exceed their quality. Reinforcement learning (RL) can, but its rewards were designed for other modalities and other tasks, and they transfer poorly to open-ended generation in the time series domain. We address this by proposing LineupRL, a reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) pipeline whose reward is caption-to-series identification. The reward model is a frozen large language model (LLM) verifier that reads the generated caption and the candidate time series as raw values, never the chart, and must pick the described time series from multiple distractors. Matching is a far lighter demand on the verifier than writing questions or judging a caption, so an off-the-shelf LLM can supply the reward. Across two captioning benchmarks, and on forecasting and reconstruction where the predictor sees only the caption, LineupRL outperforms SFT and RL baselines on every metric. The 3B vision language model (VLM) trained by LineupRL also outperforms, at 1/24 of the parameters, the 72B VLM whose captions the SFT baseline is distilled from. Our case study shows that LineupRL resists reward hacking, and that the captioner it trains both traces the trend and names the values at key points.

</details>


### [128] [AI-assisted mitotic counting improves reproducibility and efficiency across multiple tumour types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01813)
*Simon Graham,Mostafa Jahanifar,Quoc Dang Vu,Vygante Maskoliunaite,Donatas Petroska,Ruta Barbora Valkiuniene,Ayat Gamal Lashen,Jen Hong Ong,Amede Ogechi Nnorom,Sinclair Couper,Natasha Kardasz,Reshma Agrawal,Brinder Singh Chohan,Jose Luis Solorzano Rendon,Shonali Natu,Arvydas Laurinavicius,Nasir Rajpoot,David Snead*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MitPro是一种AI辅助有丝分裂计数工具，在385张全切片、7种肿瘤类型的配对阅片研究中，将评分者间一致性（ICC）从0.589提升至0.949，平均阅片时间从286.4秒降至127.8秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有丝分裂计数是肿瘤分级、诊断和预后评估的重要环节，但人工评估耗时且存在病理医生之间的判读差异。研究旨在开发一种AI工具以提升计数的一致性与效率，同时保留病理医生对区域选择和最终计数的控制权。

Method: 开发MitPro，引导病理医生关注预测有丝分裂活性最高的区域，并标出有丝分裂象供复核。采用回顾性、非干预性、配对阅片研究设计：385张全切片图像，来自3个国家3个中心、3种扫描仪、7种肿瘤类型；13位病理医生参与，每张切片由3位病理医生独立评估，先无AI辅助，经至少2周洗脱期后再在AI辅助下评估。

Result: AI辅助将有丝分裂计数的组内相关系数（ICC）从0.589提高到0.949；病理医生层面的中位评估时间从286.4秒降至127.8秒，平均每次评估节省151.8秒。在HALO AP和Sectra图像管理系统及另外2种肿瘤类型的支持性分析中，一致性与效率也均有改善。AI辅助与略偏高的有丝分裂计数和评分相关，符合识别出更活跃的热点区域并减少漏检的情况。分数在有无AI辅助之间的变化频率与常规计数中的病理医生间差异相当。

Conclusion: 研究支持将MitPro作为辅助工具，用于常规实践中更一致、更高效的有丝分裂评估。

Abstract: Mitotic counting is an important component of tumour grading, diagnosis and prognostic assessment across several tumour types, but manual assessment is time-consuming and subject to inter-pathologist variability. To help address these challenges, we developed MitPro, an AI tool designed to improve consistency and efficiency by directing pathologists towards regions with the highest predicted mitotic activity and highlighting mitotic figures for review, while retaining pathologist control over region selection and the final count. We evaluated its effect on the reproducibility and efficiency of mitotic counting in a retrospective, non-interventional, paired reader study comprising 385 whole-slide images from 3 centres in 3 countries and 7 tumour types using 3 different scanners. 13 pathologists participated, with each slide assessed independently by 3 pathologists without AI assistance and again with AI assistance after a minimum 2 week washout period. Across all slides, AI-assisted counting increased the intraclass correlation coefficient from 0.589 to 0.949. Mean pathologist-level median assessment time decreased from 286.4 to 127.8 seconds, corresponding to an average saving of 151.8 seconds per assessment. Improvements in agreement and efficiency were also observed in supporting analyses using HALO AP and Sectra image management systems and in 2 additional tumour types outside the main study population. AI-assisted assessment was associated with a subtle shift towards higher mitotic counts and scores, consistent with identification of more active mitotic hotspots and fewer missed mitotic figures. The frequency of score change between unassisted and AI-assisted assessment was comparable with inter-pathologist variation during routine counting. These findings support the use of MitPro as an assistive tool for more consistent and efficient mitotic assessment in routine practice.

</details>


### [129] [Continuous Process-Level Evaluation for Evolving Enterprise AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01833)
*Ngoc Phuoc An Vo,Aarya Doshi,Vadim Sheinin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合结果层与过程层的持续评估框架，发现即使最终输出通过，绝大多数企业AI agent运行仍存在轨迹偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI agent技能随工具API、模型和规格变化而演化，仅靠最终输出评估会遗漏过程级的行为漂移。

Method: 在Value Aware Resiliency系统的Business Value Determination技能（Revenue与Productivity变体）上，独立计算每次运行的ground truth，生成可复用模板测试，并通过程序化检查与范围受限的LLM judge评估工具选择、参数、执行顺序和数据库完整性。共评估240次试验，覆盖2个技能、2个规格变体、2个agent harness和3个模型。

Result: 在175次通过所有适用最终数值检查的试验中，162次（92.6%，Wilson 95% CI：87.7-95.6%）存在另一项评估器检测到的偏差；在七项最终状态检查定义下，164次通过运行中151次（92.1%，95% CI：86.9-95.3%）仍违反某轨迹检查。依赖归因将每次运行平均6.34个失败检查降至2.65个根因；规格敏感性因模型与harness而异，Revenue三项比较的探索性bootstrap交互区间均不含零，Productivity三项中有一项如此。

Conclusion: 运行时解析模板在评估配置中提供了可复用的回归覆盖；在真实API演化下的纵向验证仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agent skills evolve as tool APIs, models, and specifications change, yet final-output evaluation can miss process-level behavioral drift. We present a continuous evaluation framework combining outcome-level and process-level checks, applied to Revenue and Productivity variants of a Business Value Determination skill in an enterprise Value Aware Resiliency system. The framework independently computes per-run ground truth, materializes reusable template tests, and evaluates tool selection, arguments, execution order, and database integrity through programmatic checks and a narrowly scoped LLM judge. We evaluate 240 trials across two skills, two specification variants, two agent harnesses, and three models. Of 175 trials passing all applicable final numerical checks, 162 (92.6 percent; Wilson 95 percent CI: 87.7-95.6 percent) contained another evaluator-detected deviation. Under a broader seven-check final-state definition, 151 of 164 passing runs (92.1 percent; 95 percent CI: 86.9-95.3 percent) still violated a trajectory check. Dependency attribution reduced a mean of 6.34 failed checks per run to 2.65 roots. Specification sensitivity varied by model and harness, with exploratory bootstrap interaction intervals excluding zero for all three Revenue comparisons and one of three Productivity comparisons. Runtime-resolved templates provided reusable regression coverage across the evaluated configurations; longitudinal validation under actual API evolution remains future work.

</details>


### [130] [Code Owns the Simulation, Jev Owns the Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01834)
*Yaodong Yang,Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Xingyu Fan,Weixun Wang,Jinpeng Li,Tianpei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了判断模型 JE 在多种决策任务中的表现，发现其存在"评估"与"模拟"的清晰边界，并建议用代码承担模拟、判定模型负责评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断模型（如 JE）单次调用、无推理文本即可为每个选项返回概率，作为智能体动作选择层很有吸引力，但尚不清楚哪些决策可以信任它。

Method: 在反思测试（CRT）、一次性矩阵博弈、文本游戏 ALFWorld 和机器人控制上对 JE 进行测试。

Result: JE 在"评估"（可从输入描述直接判断正确选项）时成功，如解决 99% 的反直觉 CRT 问题；但在"模拟"（需预测输入中不存在的信息，如对手动作或前置子目标）时失败，例如在 ALFWorld 中把未洗的刀直接放进抽屉。许多失败并非知识不足：单独询问对手动作时通常答对，且给定对手动作后表现良好，失败发生在一次调用同时完成模拟与评估时。

Conclusion: 应让代码负责预测或模拟。当代码提供模拟（如 ALFWorld 的前瞻、机器人控制中的物理模拟）时，JE 凭借其通用评估能力成为专家级控制器。

Abstract: Judgment models such as \jev{} return, in a single call and without reasoning text, a probability for each described option. This makes them attractive as an agent's action-selection layer, but it is unclear which decisions they can be trusted with. We test \jev{} on reflection tests, one-shot matrix games, the text game ALFWorld and robot control, and find a sharp boundary. \jev{} succeeds when the right option can be judged from what the input describes, which we call \emph{evaluation}. Specifically, it solves 99\% of the counterintuitive Cognitive Reflection Test questions. However, it fails when the right option depends on \emph{simulation} (i.e., predicting something not in the input), such as the opponent's action or the subgoal that must come first. In games, \jev{} plays suboptimally as if its rational opponent acted at random, because the opponent's action is not given. In ALFWorld, \jev{} favors commands that mention an object or place named in the task description. For example, given the task ``put a clean knife in the drawer'', \jev{} carries an unwashed knife straight to the drawer instead of first washing it at the sink. Surprisingly, many of these failures are not due to a lack of knowledge. Asked separately what the opponent will do, \jev{} usually answers correctly, and it responds well given the opponent's action. It fails when one call must both perform the simulation and evaluate based on it. This suggests letting code make the prediction or simulation. When code supplies it, such as a lookahead in ALFWorld and physics simulation in robot control, \jev{} becomes an expert controller through its general evaluation ability.

</details>


### [131] [On the Divergence of Accuracy and Mechanism Consistency in Time Series World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01842)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Zhen Xu,Zachary Plotkin,Nicholas Konz,Zhen Tan,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时间序列世界模型(TSWM)的形式化框架与基准，区分状态、动作与外生输入，并引入"机制一致性"指标衡量模型对动作变化的响应方向是否正确，发现低预测误差与机制一致性并不一致，并提出方向性监督损失来改善一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列世界模型多以动作作为协变量，仅在已执行方案下的预测误差上进行训练与评估。然而世界模型的用途在于比较未执行的方案，其对改变方案（更改动作）的响应能力从未被检验。作者希望回答：哪些设计选择真正重要，以及预测准确的模型是否能像真实系统一样对改变的计划做出响应。

Method: 作者提出形式化描述与基准。形式化分离状态、动作和外生输入，区分连续动作、模式动作和事件动作，并引入"机制一致性"指标——基于已知方向的、声明过的动作-状态关系（如升压药升高血压），检查改变动作是否使预测沿声明方向移动。基准整合八个来自工程基础设施和临床护理、含真实动作的公开数据集，在七种骨干网络和五个随机种子上变化预测空间、计划融合与计划编码方式。此外提出方向性监督损失，惩罚对改变动作响应中符号错误的部分。

Result: 冻结的潜空间预测相比观测空间平均降低MAE 9.9%，门控输出融合相比输入拼接平均降低12.7%，二者在全部八个数据集上均有改善；时间计划编码对平均MAE的影响最多2.2%。预测误差与机制一致性出现分歧：误差最低的配置在五个有声明机制的数据集中的四个上，其一致性仅处于或低于随机水平，且没有任何设计选择能避免这一点。方向性监督在受惩罚机制上显著提升一致性，且MAE无变化。

Conclusion: 研究给出了TSWM的设计配方：采用冻结潜空间与输出端融合以提升准确性，并引入训练目标来保证机制一致性。同时表明预测误差低并不代表模型能正确响应改变的计划，机制一致性需要专门的设计与监督。

Abstract: A time series world model (TSWM) predicts a controlled system's state from its observed history and planned actions and exogenous inputs. Current approaches build forecasters with actions as covariates, trained and evaluated on prediction error under the executed plan. Yet world models compare unexecuted plans, but their responses to changed plans remain untested. We ask which design choices matter and whether accurate forecasters respond to changed plans as real systems do. We address both with a formalization and benchmark. The formalization separates state, actions and exogenous inputs, distinguishes continuous, mode and event actions, and introduces mechanism consistency, a metric built on declared action-state relations with known directions, such as a vasopressor raising blood pressure: it checks whether shifting an action moves the forecast in the declared direction. The benchmark consolidates eight public datasets with real actions from engineered infrastructure and clinical care, varying prediction space, plan fusion and plan encoding across seven backbones and five seeds. First, a frozen latent prediction space lowers MAE by 9.9% over observation space and gated output fusion lowers it by 12.7% over input concatenation on average, with both improving all eight datasets; temporal plan encoding changes average MAE by at most 2.2%. Second, prediction error and mechanism consistency diverge: the lowest-error configuration is at or below chance in consistency on four of five datasets with declared mechanisms, and no design choice avoids this. Finally, directional supervision, a loss penalizing the wrong-signed part of the response to a shifted action, significantly raises consistency on penalized mechanisms with no change in MAE. Together they give TSWMs a recipe: a frozen latent space and output-side fusion for accuracy, and a training objective for mechanism consistency.

</details>


### [132] [Temporal-Difference Learning for Dragonchess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01845)
*Jim O'Connor,Annika Hoag,Sarah Goyette,Gary B. Parker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在三维国际象棋变体Dragonchess中比较了进化迁移学习与TD(λ)两种自适应评估方法，通过将引擎从PyGame重写为C++以支持大规模实验，发现两种方法均优于其他智能体，且二者性能无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dragonchess具有独特的棋盘结构和较高的计算复杂度，是研究自适应方法在新型环境中更新评估启发式的理想场景，但原有PyGame引擎效率低，难以进行大规模、有统计意义的实验。

Method: 将Dragonchess引擎从PyGame重新实现为C++以提升运行速度，从而支持运行10000局对局，并引入置信区间与显著性检验；随后在循环赛（round-robin tournament）中对比进化迁移学习与TD(λ)两种自适应方法及其他智能体。

Result: 两种自适应方法在循环赛中均优于所有其他智能体；进化得到的评估与学习得到的评估在性能上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了自适应方法在结构复杂、新颖的游戏领域中的有效性，并表明进化与学习两种途径可获得相当的性能表现。

Abstract: Our research investigates how two adaptive AI methods, evolutionary transfer learning and TD(lambda), perform in the three-dimensional chess environment Dragonchess. The game challenges players with its unique board structure and computational load, making it an ideal setting to study how adaptive methods can update evaluation heuristics in novel environments. In this work we re-implement the Dragonchess engine, changing it from a PyGame engine to C++. This enables faster gameplay, allowing us to run 10,000 games with confidence intervals and significance tests, rather than a single small tournament. Both adaptive methods outperform all other agents in the round-robin tournament. Our results showed that there is no significant difference in the performance between the evolved and learned evaluations. This research establishes the efficacy of adaptive methods in structurally complex, novel game domains.

</details>


### [133] [AVSD-Scenes: A Dataset for Audio-Visual Description of Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01861)
*Dhanunjaya Varma Devalraju,Arshdeep Singh,Mark D. Plumbley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AVSD-Scenes，一个面向城市环境的成对音视频场景描述数据集，包含 12,291 条音视频场景描述，并通过多任务基准验证其语义与判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言能为音视频城市场景提供丰富语义表示，但联合描述听觉与视觉信息的数据集仍十分有限，因此需要构建此类数据集以支撑跨模态研究。

Method: 基于 TAU Urban Audio-Visual Scenes 数据集，先用 Qwen2-Audio-7B 与 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 分别生成音频与视觉单模态描述，再用 Qwen3-14B、Mistral-Small-3.2-24B-Instruct-2506、Gemma-3-27B-it 等大语言模型融合为多模态描述；随后用语义对齐、跨模态检索、场景分类、LLM-as-a-judge 与人工主观评估进行基准测试。

Result: 多模态描述在语义对齐与跨模态检索上优于单模态描述，同时保留强场景判别信息；生成描述在城市场景分类中最高达 94.5% 准确率，融合音频、视觉与描述嵌入后提升至 95.4%；即使去除提示中的场景标签，描述仍具高判别性。

Conclusion: AVSD-Scenes 有效融合音视频语义，所生成描述反映的是来自音视频内容的语义信息而非仅标签信息，为音视频城市场景理解提供了有价值的数据资源与基准。

Abstract: Natural language descriptions can provide rich semantic representations of audio-visual urban scenes, yet datasets that jointly describe both auditory and visual information remain limited. In this paper, we introduce AVSD-Scenes, a paired audio-visual scene description dataset for urban environments. The dataset contains 12,291 audio-visual scene descriptions generated from the TAU Urban Audio-Visual Scenes dataset. To construct the dataset, we first generate audio- and visual-based descriptions using Qwen2-Audio-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B, respectively. These modality-specific descriptions are then combined using large language models, namely Qwen3-14B, Mistral-Small-3.2-24B-Instruct-2506, and Gemma-3-27B-it, to produce multimodal descriptions that capture complementary information from both modalities. We benchmark AVSD-Scenes using semantic alignment, cross-modal retrieval, scene classification, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, and human subjective assessment. Results show that multimodal descriptions improve semantic alignment and cross-modal retrieval performance compared with modality-specific descriptions while preserving strong scene-discriminative information. The generated descriptions achieve up to 94.5% accuracy in urban scene classification, while combining audio, visual, and description embeddings further improves accuracy to 95.4%. Furthermore, the descriptions remain highly scene-discriminative even when scene labels are removed from the prompting instructions, indicating that they capture semantic information derived from the audio-visual content rather than merely reflecting label information.

</details>


### [134] [Mapping the RAG Landscape: A Four Axis Taxonomy of Efficiency, Defense, Interactivity, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01936)
*Meghana Sunil,Shravya V,Shravan Venkatraman,Joe Dhanith PR*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了检索增强生成（RAG）的最新进展，提出四轴分类法：提升检索效率、增强鲁棒性与安全性、支持用户驱动交互流程、实现多步复杂推理，并总结了评估实践、应用与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型受限于静态的参数化知识，且容易产生幻觉。RAG通过引入外部检索，将输出锚定在可验证、最新的来源上以缓解这些问题。但既有综述多聚焦于核心架构与标准流程，未充分覆盖近期在更广泛挑战与能力上的探索，因此有必要对当代RAG发展进行系统梳理。

Method: 本文采用结构化综述方法，将RAG领域归纳为四轴分类体系，并形式化RAG框架的关键组件；在此基础上回顾稠密与稀疏检索、融合策略、嵌入优化、基于强化学习的检索策略等方法，同时综合评估实践、领域应用及架构变体（Naive、Advanced、Modular RAG）。

Result: 综述系统地展示了上述方法如何影响实际部署与系统设计，并整合了评估实践与领域特定应用；同时归纳了RAG的多种架构变体，形成对当代RAG发展的统一视图。

Conclusion: 论文指出检索质量、可靠性、领域适配、可扩展性与可解释性等方面仍存在持续挑战，并展望了构建更可靠、可适应、透明RAG系统的机会。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable fluency across many tasks but remain limited by their static, parameter bound knowledge and their susceptibility to hallucinating information. Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) addresses these issues by incorporating external retrieval into the generation process, grounding model outputs in verifiable and up to date sources. While prior surveys primarily focus on core RAG architectures and standard pipelines, recent research explores broader challenges and capabilities that extend beyond these foundational designs. This survey provides a consolidated and structured examination of contemporary RAG developments, organizing the field into a four axis taxonomy: improving retrieval efficiency, strengthening robustness and security, supporting user driven and interactive workflows, and enabling multi step or complex reasoning. We formalize key components of the RAG framework and review methods spanning dense and sparse retrieval, fusion strategies, embedding optimizations, and reinforcement learning based retrieval policies, highlighting how these advances influence practical deployment and system design. We also synthesize evaluation practices, domain specific applications, and architectural variants such as Naive, Advanced, and Modular RAG. Finally, we outline persistent challenges related to retrieval quality, reliability, domain adaptation, scalability, and explainability, and identify opportunities for building RAG systems that are more reliable, adaptable, and transparent.

</details>


### [135] [Counterfactual Auditing of Bias in Open-Source Large Language Models for Clinical Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01963)
*Manar Aljohani,Brandon Ho,Kenneth McKinley,Dennis Ren,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对十个开源大语言模型进行儿科急诊分诊（ESI）反事实审计，发现模型的反事实偏见敏感性不随模型规模或医学预训练一致降低，微调后的小模型表现最好，反事实审计可作为临床部署前的公平性评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊是高风险的优先级排序任务，人口统计学、社会经济和系统背景信息可能不当影响分诊等级判定。尽管开源大模型越来越多被考虑用于本地化、隐私保护的临床决策支持，但反事实偏见在不同模型家族、规模、医学领域模型和领域适配模型间的差异尚不清楚，需要系统比较其公平性风险。

Method: 构建对比式反事实审计，对十个开源大模型进行儿科ESI预测。基于真实和手册式临床案例，构造配对反事实变体，仅改变一个人口统计、社会经济、医疗可及性、行为、社会或系统背景变量而保持临床表现不变。模型包括Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct、QLoRA微调的Qwen2.5-7B、MedGemma变体、MedLLaMA2-7B、GPT-OSS-20B和GPT-OSS-120B。测量任何反事实偏移、分诊不足、过度分诊、偏移超过一个ESI等级、平均偏移和平均绝对偏移。

Result: 反事实敏感性差异显著，且不随模型规模增大或医学领域预训练而一致降低。微调的Qwen2.5-7B总体敏感性最低，任何偏移率为5.27%、平均绝对偏移0.0534，而基础模型为16.02%和0.1706。若干更大或医学领域模型表现出更显著的偏移。分层和相关性分析进一步揭示出聚合率掩盖的临床重要方向性和共同失败模式。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持将反事实审计作为一种轻量、临床可解释的框架，用于在临床部署前比较开源大模型的公平性风险。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) triage is a high-stakes prioritization task in which demographic, socioeconomic, and system-context information may improperly influence acuity assignment. Although open-source large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for local and privacy-preserving clinical decision support, it remains unclear how counterfactual bias varies across model families, sizes, medical-domain models, and domain-adapted models. We present a comparative counterfactual audit of ten open-source LLMs for pediatric Emergency Severity Index (ESI) prediction. Starting from real and handbook-style clinical vignettes, we construct paired counterfactual variants that change only one injected demographic, socioeconomic, healthcare-access, behavioral, social, or system-context variable while holding the clinical presentation fixed. Models include Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, a QLoRA fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B, MedGemma variants, MedLLaMA2-7B, GPT-OSS-20B, and GPT-OSS-120B. We measure any counterfactual shift, undertriage, overtriage, shifts greater than one ESI level, mean shift, and mean absolute shift. Counterfactual sensitivity varied substantially and did not consistently decrease with larger model size or medical-domain pretraining. The fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B showed the lowest overall sensitivity, with a 5.27% any-shift rate and mean absolute shift of 0.0534, versus 16.02% and 0.1706 for the base model. Several larger or medical-domain models showed more significant shifts. Stratified and correlation analyses further revealed clinically important directionality and shared failure patterns hidden by aggregate rates. These findings support counterfactual auditing as a lightweight, clinically interpretable framework for comparing fairness risks in open-source LLMs before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [136] [Can AI Oversight Be Zero Knowledge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01995)
*Alessandro Chiesa,Ziyi Guan,Burcu Yildiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究带预言机计算的零知识交互论证的可行性：一般情形下不可行（含辩论模型），但在预言机对答案签名时可在弱假设下实现零知识验证，并给出可扩展监督的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统常基于机密数据（如医疗记录、药物分子结构）产生输出，需要在验证输出正确性的同时不泄露底层数据。已有关于预言机辅助计算验证的工作（交互证明、辩论）主要关注验证者远快于计算的效率，为此依赖额外假设；本文转而关注隐私，允许验证者以计算的多项式时间运行，探究此类交互论证能否做到零知识。

Method: 通过理论证明与构造：在随机预言机模型下给出不存在零知识证明的不可能性结论，并将该不可能性推广到辩论（debate）模型；随后在预言机对每个答案附带密码学签名的设定下，构造零知识验证协议，仅假设抗碰撞哈希函数。

Result: 证明了一般情况下不存在覆盖所有预言机辅助计算的零知识证明，即使证明者与验证者被允许运行远超计算本身的时间；该不可能性同样适用于辩论这一可扩展监督的典型模型。正向方面，若预言机对答案进行密码学签名，则每个预言机辅助计算都可在高效证明者与验证者下实现零知识验证。

Conclusion: 隐私与可验证性在一般预言机辅助计算中存在根本张力，但通过给预言机答案加上密码学签名可以绕开该限制；该方法不仅保护隐私，还提供了一种既不依赖辩论中的诚实对手、也不依赖单证明者协议中计算鲁棒性的可扩展监督替代方案。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly produce outputs from confidential data, such as a fitness-for-duty assessment from medical records or the predicted properties of a drug candidate from its secret structure. It is important to verify that such outputs are correct without revealing the underlying data. A recent line of work studies verification of AI outputs via interactive proofs and debate for oracle-aided computation, where correctness may depend on an oracle such as human judgment, a physical experiment, or the web. These works focus on verification by a verifier that runs much faster than the computation. However, such efficient verification is impossible for general oracle-aided computation, and these works therefore rely on additional assumptions. We focus instead on privacy: allowing the verifier to run in time polynomial in the computation, we ask whether interactive arguments for oracle-aided computation can be zero knowledge, so that the verifier learns nothing about the confidential data beyond the correctness of the output.
  We prove that, in general, they cannot. In the random oracle model, there are no zero-knowledge proofs for all oracle-aided computations, even if both the prover and the verifier are allowed to run much longer than the computation itself. The impossibility extends to debate, a canonical model for scalable oversight.
  On the positive side, we show that if the oracle attaches a cryptographic signature to each of its answers, then every oracle-aided computation can be verified in zero knowledge with an efficient prover and verifier, assuming only collision-resistant hash functions. Beyond privacy, this also gives an alternative approach to scalable oversight that relies neither on an honest opponent, as in debate, nor on the robustness of the computation, as in prior single-prover protocols.

</details>


### [137] [Mingbird: A Local-First Agent Harness Enabling Small Open Models to Complete Real Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02001)
*Hao Wang,Ting Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出小参量开源模型（2-9B）在云端 agent 框架下难以完成真实任务，其失败很大程度归因于框架（harness）而非模型本身；作者提出本地优先的 agent 框架 Mingbird，通过十项机制针对性弥补小模型失败模式，并在自建基准 LRAB 及第三方基准 τ²-bench 上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小参量开源模型（2-9B）虽可在普通笔记本上运行，但在云端规模的 agent harness 下几乎无法完成真实任务，表现为工具预填充溢出上下文、自我纠正发散、工具演示循环、任务被静默放弃等。作者希望证明相当一部分失败源于框架而非模型，并据此设计适配小模型的本地框架。
