Method: 作者在受控的单机对比及一个第三方基准上提供证据归因。提出 Mingbird——面向 Windows 与 Ollama 的本地优先 agent harness，含十项机制，逐点补偿小模型失败形式，其中三项具代表性：字节级净零预填充预算、在确认完成前重读任务的完成闸门（finish gate）、签名级循环检测。在 LRAB 上进行受控比较（4 harnesses × 4 开源模型(2B-35B) × 18 真实任务，确定性产物评分，固定机器、模型、预算与评分）；并在 τ²-bench（278 任务、三臂、单一协议）上评估，另做前沿模型探针与留一机制消融。

Result: LRAB 上 Mingbird 总分 0.886，对比 goose 0.631、opencode 0.479、agent-mini 0.405，全部 288 个单元格已公开；τ²-bench 上总计 0.856，对比 0.791 与 0.737；同一 18 任务的前沿模型探针在不同 harness 下跨度 0.997 至 0.478，而结构良好的 scaffold 之间差距在 0.072 以内。留一机制消融仅作方向性报告：同夜复现使同一臂均值变动最高 0.069，与所有单次试验标称差值相当；唯一批次匹配比较（完整机制栈 vs 仅文本重读）给出可执行完成守卫的配对 +0.10（三次复现）。

Conclusion: 小模型 agent 失败很大程度可通过框架设计来缓解，Mingbird 的机制化补偿在多项基准上显著优于现有 harness。但证据存在明确局限：自建基准、单一机器、单次试验评分，消融结果仅为方向性，因此结论需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Small open-weight models (2-9B) run on ordinary laptops, but under cloud-scale agent harnesses they rarely complete real tasks: tool prefill overflows the context, self-correction diverges, tool demonstrations loop, and tasks are silently abandoned. We present evidence, from a controlled single-machine comparison and one third-party benchmark, that a substantial share of these failures is attributable to the harness rather than the model. We introduce Mingbird, a local-first agent harness for Windows and Ollama whose ten mechanisms compensate point-by-point for small-model failure forms, three of them representative: a byte-level net-zero prefill budget, a finish gate that re-reads the task before accepting completion, and signature-level loop detection. On LRAB, a controlled comparison holding machine, models, budgets, and scoring fixed (4 harnesses $\times$ 4 open models (2B-35B) $\times$ 18 real tasks, deterministic artifact scoring), Mingbird reaches 0.886 overall against 0.631 (goose), 0.479 (opencode), and 0.405 (agent-mini), with all 288 cells published; on $τ^2$-bench (278 tasks, three arms, one protocol) it totals 0.856 against 0.791 and 0.737; and a frontier-model probe on the same 18 tasks spans 0.997 to 0.478 across harnesses, with well-formed scaffolds staying within 0.072 of each other. A leave-one-mechanism-out ablation is reported as directional only: same-night replications of the same arm move its mean by up to 0.069, the size of every nominal single-trial delta, and the one batch-matched comparison (full mechanism stack versus text re-read alone) gives the executable completion guards a paired +0.10 across three replications. The evidence carries stated limits: a self-built benchmark, a single machine, and single-trial scoring.

</details>


### [138] [Counting Moves, Weighing Voices: Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation for Calibrated Multi-LLM Councils under Persistent Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02005)
*Ionel Eduard Stan,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯辩证论证（BDA），将多LLM委员会的辩论轨迹建模为带有每智能体可靠度的经典标注者模型，从而在无需额外LLM调用的情况下获得校准的正确答案概率，并能识别和反转持续不可靠的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多LLM委员会系统被越来越多地用于推理任务，其置信度应代表校准的"正确概率"，且在部分智能体持续不可靠时决策仍应稳健。然而现有委员会聚合方法在两方面均失败：其置信度度量的是"决断性"而非"正确性"，且无法识别或折扣持续不可靠的智能体。

Method: BDA把委员会的"带类型"动作（谁提出、质疑或让渡了哪个答案）视为经典标注者模型的观测，并为每个智能体设置可靠度参数。该形式化将多智能体审议重构为可靠度估计问题，利用审议轨迹在持续对抗行为下推断智能体可靠度；再依据推断出的可靠度加权证据，得到候选答案的校准后验概率。

Result: 在二分类与多分类基准上，BDA在零额外成本的委员会聚合方法中取得最佳校准效果，且不需要额外的LLM调用；在持续对抗联盟下鲁棒性提升，同时在干净环境下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将委员会审议显式建模为带每智能体可靠度的标注者推断问题，既能提供校准的正确概率，又能对持续不可靠的智能体进行反转而非仅靠投票压制，为多LLM聚合提供了新的可靠性与鲁棒性框架。

Abstract: A multi-LLM \emph{council} lets several large language models (LLMs) deliberate on a question and return an answer together with a confidence estimate. As these systems become increasingly used for reasoning, that confidence should represent a calibrated \emph{probability of being correct}, and the decision should remain robust when some agents are persistently unreliable. Existing \emph{council aggregation} methods fail on both fronts: their confidence estimates measure decisiveness rather than correctness, and they cannot identify or discount persistently unreliable agents. We introduce Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation (BDA), which treats the council's \emph{typed} moves---who proposed, challenged, or conceded which answer---as observations of a classical annotator model with \emph{per-agent} reliabilities. This formulation recasts multi-agent deliberation as a reliability estimation problem, using the deliberation trace to infer agent reliability under persistent adversarial behavior. By weighting evidence according to inferred agent reliability, BDA yields calibrated posterior probabilities over candidate answers while allowing persistently unreliable agents to be inverted rather than merely outvoted. Across binary and multi-class benchmarks, BDA achieves the best calibration among zero-cost council aggregation methods, requiring no additional LLM calls, and improves robustness under persistent adversarial coalitions while remaining competitive in clean settings.

</details>


### [139] [Atoms to Processes: The Role of Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning in Chemical Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02014)
*Michael Baldea,Linda J. Broadbelt,Marianthi G. Ierapetritou,Akhilesh Jain,Ankur Kumar,Thomas A. Kwan,Fèlix Llovell,Andrew J. Medford,Ilias Mitrai,Joel Paulson,Junyi Qiao,Matthew P. Rivera,Kirti C. Sahu,Lev Sarkisov,Zachary P. Smith,Calvin Tsay,Ching-Mei Wen,Victor M. Zavala,Huacheng Zhang,Dan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文评述了AI/ML在化学工程中的应用进展，指出核心趋势是从纯黑箱模型转向融合守恒定律与热力学一致性的物理信息/混合建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与机器学习快速成熟，加上算力、数据与算法的进步，正在改变化学工程问题的建模与求解方式。作者希望梳理这一转变，并说明AI/ML与第一性原理结合如何应对预测精度、数据稀缺、外推、可解释性与模型生命周期管理等挑战。

Method: 采用观点性综述（perspective）方式，回顾近期方法学发展与代表性应用，覆盖原子尺度模拟、材料与催化剂发现、传递与热力学、分离、过程系统工程及工业运营等领域，重点分析AI/ML与第一性原理模型、结构约束的整合方式。

Result: AI/ML方法已在多个化学工程应用中产生实质影响；跨领域的共同趋势是摒弃纯黑箱方法，转向显式遵守守恒定律、热力学一致性和已知结构约束的混合与物理信息框架，从而提升鲁棒性与可靠性，并支持人在合适抽象层次上与AI协作。

Conclusion: AI与ML并未取代化学工程的核心原理，而是对其起到放大作用。在迈向更自主、自适应和可持续系统的过程中，将AI/ML与第一性原理理解及领域专业知识审慎结合，是释放其在科研与工业实践中全部潜力的关键。

Abstract: The rapid maturation of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) has catalyzed a profound shift in how chemical engineering problems are formulated, analyzed, and solved. Advances in computing, data availability, and learning algorithms have enabled AI/ML methods to impact applications spanning atomic-scale simulations, materials and catalyst discovery, transport and thermodynamics, separations, process systems engineering, and industrial operations. This article provides a perspective on recent methodological developments and representative applications, emphasizing how AI/ML tools are being integrated with first-principles models to address challenges of predictive accuracy, data scarcity, extrapolation, interpretability, and model lifecycle management. Across domains, a unifying trend is the move away from purely black-box approaches toward hybrid and physics-informed frameworks that explicitly respect conservation laws, thermodynamic consistency, and known structural constraints. These approaches not only improve robustness and reliability, but also enable meaningful human-AI collaboration by providing information at an appropriate level of abstraction for the task and decision context. We conclude that AI and ML are not replacing the core principles of chemical engineering; rather, they are amplifying them. As the field advances toward increasingly autonomous, adaptive, and sustainable systems, the thoughtful integration of AI/ML with first-principles understanding and domain expertise will be essential to realizing their full potential across both research and industrial practice.

</details>


### [140] [Global Coherence: When Every Agent Is Right and the Team Is Still Wrong - A Local-to-Global Semantic Foundation for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02036)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"全局一致性难题"，用观察混叠不可能性定理刻画局部智能无法弥补共享状态缺失的边界，并给出局部到全局的运行时语义框架 X=(H,C,G,F;D)，通过九项实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体各自做局部有效决策却共同产生无效结果，这一问题的根源是共享状态缺失而非模型智能不足，现有研究未给出精确边界与系统性解法。

Method: 提出 Observation-Aliasing Impossibility Theorem 给出可保证有效动作的精确边界（k 个不可区分世界需两两不相交动作时随机最坏成功率为 1/k）；构建局部到全局运行时语义 X=(H,C,G,F;D)，由拓扑 H、范畴 C、群胚 G、层 F、最小历史 D 组成，模型提议而 harness 掌握共享状态与提交权。九项研究涵盖受控修订基准、TeamBench、tau2-bench Telecom 等。

Result: 受控基准中决策事件可见时同一模型达 40/40，隐藏时为 12–17/40（接近 1/3 随机水平），恢复单一权威事实后回到 40/40；TeamBench 中普通团队 5/5 超支，可见实时计数仍 4/5 违规，提交强制执行后 0/5；tau2-bench Telecom 中静默回滚后当前状态检查得 0.07，harness 得 1.00；传统求解器已掌握完整状态时与 harness 相当。

Conclusion: 局部智能无法替代缺失的全局状态；更强的模型只能在其上下文内推理得更好，却无法看到上下文之外的信息，因此保证全局一致性需要 harness 层面拥有并治理共享状态。

Abstract: AI agents can each make locally valid decisions yet jointly produce an invalid result. We call this the global coherence problem: a failure of shared state, not merely of model intelligence.
  Our Observation-Aliasing Impossibility Theorem gives the exact boundary. A policy can guarantee a valid action exactly when all worlds producing the same observation share an admissible action. If k indistinguishable worlds require pairwise-disjoint actions, the best randomized worst-case success is 1/k; more reasoning, roles, messages, or samples cannot recover the missing distinction. A stronger model can reason better within its context, but it cannot see beyond it.
  We then give local-to-global runtime semantics X = (H, C, G, F; D): topology H records overlapping scopes; category C governs state-changing actions; groupoid G retains reversible translations; sheaf F tests whether local views glue into one world; and minimal history D keeps only distinctions that alter legal futures. Models propose; the harness owns shared state and governs commit.
  Nine studies test both the failure and its boundary. On a controlled revision benchmark, the same frontier model scores 40/40 when the deciding event is visible; when it is hidden, tested arms score 12--17/40, consistent with chance (1/3); restoring one authoritative fact returns 40/40. On TeamBench, ordinary teams exceed a shared budget in 5/5 runs, a visible live count leaves 4/5 violations, and commit enforcement leaves 0/5. In tau2-bench Telecom, current-state checks score 0.07 after silent reverts, while the harness scores 1.00. Where a conventional solver already owns the complete relevant state, it ties the harness as predicted. The counterintuitive conclusion is that local intelligence cannot substitute for missing global state.

</details>


### [141] [Mimir: Physics-Grounded LLM Agents for Long-Horizon Irrigation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02038)
*Yimeng Liu,Mi Zhang,Younsuk Dong,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mimir 是一个基于物理的 LLM 智能体，用于长周期灌溉控制，通过快/慢双时间尺度修复机制，在保持物理模型不可变的前提下实现低成本、省水的自主决策与持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体的证据多来自回合制、反馈即时且可重置失败的任务；而长周期物理控制具有动作影响未来状态、误差跨决策累积、且智能体须在不改写物理安全规则的前提下从经验中改进的特点，这一领域仍缺乏研究。

Method: 以灌溉为场景，提出 Mimir——围绕两个修复时间尺度组织的物理落地 LLM 智能体。快时间尺度：结构化物理接口与确定性模拟器将 LLM 输出转为候选方案，经数值检查、修正与有界确定性动作选择后执行。慢时间尺度：把反复出现的失败模式固化为持久上下文原则以约束未来提议，而物理模型、评估器与执行约束保持不可变。

Result: 在跨多站点、多作物、多年份的统一回溯评估下，Mimir 取得所评估基线中最低的总体控制成本，比历史灌溉计划重放减少约 51% 的灌溉量。消融实验显示，移除前向模拟、经验证修正或持久上下文都会提高控制成本；模型规模与模型家族研究显示，增大 LLM 规模并无单调收益。

Conclusion: 持久物理智能体可将语义推理与有界、证据驱动的自我改进相结合，同时把物理真相与执行器权限保留给显式数值机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly combine reasoning, tool use, and action, but most evidence comes from episodic tasks with relatively immediate feedback and reset failures. Long-running physical control operates in a different regime: actions alter future states, errors compound across decisions, and an agent must improve from experience without being allowed to rewrite the physical rules that make execution safe. We study this regime through irrigation, where daily decisions interact with soil-water dynamics over entire growing seasons. We present Mimir, a physics-grounded LLM agent organized around two repair timescales. At the fast timescale, a structured physical interface and deterministic simulator turn an LLM output into a proposal that we numerically check, revise, and subject to bounded deterministic action selection before execution. At the slow timescale, recurrent failure patterns are consolidated into persistent contextual principles that condition future proposals, while the physical model, evaluator, and execution constraints remain immutable. Under a common retrospective evaluator across multiple sites, crops, and years, Mimir attains the lowest reported aggregate control cost among the evaluated references and uses about 51% less irrigation than the historical schedule replay. The ablation study show higher control cost when forward simulation, verified revision, or persistent context is removed; model-scale and model-family studies show no monotonic gain from increasing LLM size. The resulting lesson show that persistent physical agents can combine semantic reasoning with bounded, evidence-driven self-improvement while reserving physical truth and actuator authority for explicit numerical mechanisms.

</details>


### [142] [HydroJEV: A one-second, training-free screen for cyber-attack and fault attribution in water distribution networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02048)
*Tianwei Mu,Shengyan Jiang,Mingzhe Yuan,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Wenhong Wang,Jun Li,Manhong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文测试了一个无需训练、约一秒出结果的模型 Jev 能否作为 SCADA 告警三元分诊的第一层，用于区分网络攻击、物理故障、正常瞬变与传感器故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCADA 告警触发后，操作员需快速判断其原因，但监督分类器缺乏标注事件，前沿大语言模型（LLM）单次决策耗时数十秒，因此需要一种快速、无需训练的初筛方案。

Method: 在基于 EPANET 的 C-Town 网络构建的四类原因归因基准上，于四个密封、预注册轮次中，用相同证据比较 Jev 与手写规则树、监督分类器及七个云端 LLM；并采用仅标签自由的先验校正，将 Jev 与规则树组合成门控级联。

Result: Jev 与规则树相当（分布内 macro-F1 0.62–0.64 对 0.56–0.61），在标签中缺失的事件子类型上超过监督分类器 0.36–0.42，在每类标注事件少于约四个时也优于监督分类器，且比前沿 LLM 快 20–40 倍。仅接受经规则树确认的良性 Jev 判定，可在新密封集上为 LLM 评审节省 35–38% 的窗口且不损失 macro-F1。迁移到另外两个网络后，该门控级联在全部四个集合上均处于评审者的非劣效边界内。

Conclusion: 快速、无需训练的分诊筛选可承担 SCADA 异常分诊中约三分之一的评审负荷，同时保持审慎评审的准确率。

Abstract: When a SCADA alarm is raised in a water distribution network, operators must decide quickly whether it reflects a cyberattack, a physical fault, a normal transient or a faulty sensor. Supervised classifiers need labelled incidents that utilities rarely have, and frontier large language models (LLMs) take tens of seconds per decision. We tested whether Jev, a training-free model that returns class probabilities in about one second, can serve as the first tier of this triage. On a four-class cause-attribution benchmark built on the C-Town network in EPANET, Jev was compared with a hand-written rule tree, a supervised classifier and seven cloud LLMs on identical evidence in four sealed, pre-registered rounds. With only a label-free prior correction, Jev matched the rule tree (macro-F1 0.62-0.64 against 0.56-0.61 in distribution) and exceeded the supervised classifier by 0.36-0.42 on event subtypes absent from its labels, in all four rounds, and it outperformed the classifier whenever fewer than about four labelled events per class were available. Jev also decided 20-40 times faster than frontier LLMs. Accepting only benign Jev verdicts confirmed by the rule tree spared an LLM reviewer 35-38% of windows on fresh sealed sets without loss of macro-F1. Transferred unchanged to two further networks, this gated cascade stayed within the non-inferiority margin of its reviewer on all four sets. A fast, training-free screen can therefore take over about a third of the review load in SCADA anomaly triage while preserving the accuracy of deliberate review.

</details>


### [143] [External Observers May See More Clearly: Cross-Model Span-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models via Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02066)
*Kingshuk Gupta,Davide Buscaldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于内部隐藏状态的细粒度、片段级幻觉检测框架，可定位幻觉起始与延续token，并进一步提出跨模型检测范式，发现外部观察者可匹配甚至超越生成模型的自我检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为推理引擎时幻觉仍是关键缺陷。现有内部状态探测方法多将幻觉检测简化为逐token二分类，无法刻画语义漂移的结构化、序列化边界。

Method: 通过检查逐层激活模式，构建内部隐藏状态框架进行片段级幻觉检测，识别生成中的幻觉起始与延续token；并提出跨模型检测框架，让一个模型观察另一个模型生成所诱发的内部表示。

Result: 成功隔离幻觉起始点，在极端类别不平衡下，Precision-Recall AUC相比随机基线有显著提升；外部观察者能匹配或超越生成者自身的幻觉起始检测，即使观察者是更小的模型。

Conclusion: 自我检测并非幻觉起始定位的上限，跨模型观察为细粒度幻觉检测提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as foundational reasoning engines, their tendency to hallucinate remains a critical vulnerability. While recent internal state probes offer a promising alternative to slow external retrieval systems, they largely reduce hallucination detection to a token-wise binary classification task, failing to capture the structured, sequential boundaries of semantic drift. Here, we introduce an internal hidden state framework for fine-grained, span-level hallucination detection. By inspecting layer-wise activation patterns, we attempt to detect the exact hallucination onset and continuation tokens in an LLM generation. Our experiments show that this approach successfully isolates hallucination onsets, achieving substantial improvements in Precision-Recall AUC over random baselines despite extreme class imbalance. Ultimately, we propose a novel cross-model detection framework in which one model observes the internal representations elicited by another model's generation. We find that an external observer can match or exceed a generator's self-detection of its own hallucination onsets, including when the observer is the smaller model, suggesting that self-detection is not the ceiling for onset localisation.

</details>


### [144] [Causal Memory Policy: Making Memory Utility Identifiable by Intervening on Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02070)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果记忆策略（CMP），通过对检索本身进行干预恢复记忆效用的可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强大模型通过估计记忆对任务性能的影响来决定保留哪些记忆，但这种估计完全依赖被检索到的记忆。若某记忆从未被检索，存储层面的干预结果相同，其效用无法被识别，这是一种检索层阳性违背，且仅检查记忆操作的诊断无法发现。

Method: CMP 通过干预检索过程恢复可识别性，预留固定数量的上下文槽位用于以已知倾向性采样的记忆，并在平衡分配设计下用自归一化逆倾向加权估计记忆效用。作者证明了记忆效用通过检索的因果分解、估计量的无偏性与精确方差，以及不可逆操作下的最优决策规则。

Result: 在 LongMemEval 上 54% 的必需记忆识别失败，在 LoCoMo 上为 67%，且该失败在已部署的记忆系统中依然存在。CMP 将必需与非必需记忆的区分度从 0.54 提升至 0.66 AUC。此外，仅靠可识别的记忆效用不足以支撑保留决策：单查询效用在被估计的查询上可达 0.78 AUC，但保留策略可用的任何聚合方式都无法预测记忆在未见查询上的价值。

Conclusion: 记忆效用的因果识别问题真实存在且普遍，CMP 能有效改善区分度，但识别出的效用本身仍不足以直接指导记忆保留决策，需要进一步研究跨查询的效用聚合与泛化。

Abstract: Memory-augmented large language models must decide which memories to retain, and recent systems do so by estimating each memory's effect on task performance. However, these estimates rely entirely on retrieved memories. When a memory is never retrieved, store-level interventions produce identical outcomes, leaving its utility unidentified. This is a retrieval-level positivity violation, invisible to diagnostics that examine only memory operations. We introduce Causal Memory Policy (CMP), a causal framework that restores identification by intervening on retrieval itself, reserving a fixed number of context slots for memories sampled with known propensities. CMP estimates memory utility by self-normalized inverse propensity weighting under a balanced assignment design. We prove the causal factorization of memory utility through retrieval, the unbiasedness and exact variance of the estimator, and the optimal decision rule under irreversible operations. Empirically, identification fails for 54% of required memories on LongMemEval and 67% on LoCoMo, and the failure persists in a deployed memory system. CMP improves discrimination between required and non-required memories from 0.54 to 0.66 AUC. Finally, we show that identified memory utility alone is insufficient for retention decisions: per-query utility reaches 0.78 AUC on the query for which it is estimated, yet no aggregation available to a retention policy predicts a memory's value on unseen queries. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cmp-release-D0C3/.

</details>


### [145] [PyPottery: an AI-powered end-to-end suite for pottery processing and publication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02072)
*Lorenzo Cardarelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出开源AI工具套件PyPottery，将陶瓷考古绘图文档流程半自动化，实测显示相比传统工作流可实现约40倍（中位数）的感知提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶瓷材料研究是考古学的基石，但陶器文档整理的后处理工作流程高度依赖人力，导致出版瓶颈。作者希望借助AI降低重复性劳动，把人力重新分配到更具认知价值的环节。

Method: PyPottery为开源AI驱动的工具套件，包含四个集成模块：PyPotteryScan（自动图像提取与手写识别）、PyPotteryInk（铅笔图自动上墨）、PyPotteryTrace（语义感知矢量化）、PyPotteryLayout（自动排版生成）。在意大利Terramara di Montale的50张手绘图纸、共240幅陶器绘图上进行评估，并结合可用性研究收集用户主观体验。

Result: 框架带来了显著的时间节省；可用性研究的参与者报告的中位感知提速为40倍（范围17.5倍至120倍）。

Conclusion: 结果表明AI辅助工具在考古文档工作中具有潜力，且论文进一步讨论了认知劳动的战略性再分配，强调"增强"而非"取代"人类工作者。

Abstract: The study of ceramic materials constitutes a cornerstone of archaeological research, yet the post-production workflow for pottery documentation remains labor-intensive and creates significant publication bottlenecks. This paper presents PyPottery, an open-source, AI-powered suite designed to semi-automate the complete ceramic documentation pipeline. The suite comprises four integrated modules: PyPotteryScan for automated image extraction and handwriting recognition; PyPotteryInk for automatic inking of pencil drawings; PyPotteryTrace for semantically-aware vectorization; and PyPotteryLayout for automated layout generation. Evaluated on 50 hand-drawn sheets containing 240 pottery drawings from the Terramara di Montale (Italy), the framework achieved substantial time savings confirmed by usability study participants, who reported a median perceived speedup of 40$\times$ over traditional workflows (range: 17.5$\times$--120$\times$). These results highlight the potential of AI-assisted tools in archaeological documentation, while the paper addresses the strategic redistribution of cognitive labor toward augmentation rather than automation.

</details>


### [146] [Homomorphic Advantage Operator: Stabilizing Reinforcement Learning Under Fully Homomorphic Encryption Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02074)
*Abid Mohamed Nadhir,Ahmad Al Hanbali,Beggas Mounir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Homomorphic Advantage Operator (HAO)，通过将零均值中心化投影应用于 TD 目标，抑制 FHE 深度强化学习中的多项式近似发散（Bellman drift），在无额外非线性深度与引导开销下实现稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云端智能系统中，全同态加密（FHE）虽能保障机密数据的计算隐私，但其只能支持多项式运算。将 FHE 用于强化学习需用多项式近似替代非线性操作，而这类近似会因递归误差现象"Bellman drift"发生灾难性发散，现有正则化手段难以有效约束。因此需要一个既能稳定多项式近似、又不增加计算开销的框架。

Method: 提出 HAO（Homomorphic Advantage Operator）稳定化框架，将基于优势函数价值估计中的零均值中心化投影直接迁移到时序差分（TD）目标上。该线性投影消除驱动 Bellman drift 的均匀状态价值基线，同时保持每个状态下的动作排序；其不引入任何额外的非线性乘法深度，也无需昂贵的密文自举（bootstrapping）。实验采用三层方法：表格型 MDP、使用真实 CKKS 加密操作的 CartPole 环境，以及含稠密连续特征的 20 节点物流路径规划基准。

Result: HAO 严格将网络预激活值限制在安全的多项式近似域内。在所有随机种子下，HAO 智能体实现 0% 边界越界；仅使用正则化（L2 权重衰减与梯度裁剪）在 5 个种子中有 3 个越界，未稳定化的基线在 83.8% 的回合中越界。此外，HAO 智能体在表格域中把最优策略准确率提升 18.0 个百分点，并在裁剪梯度上加入 DP-SGD 式高斯噪声时仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: HAO 通过零均值中心化投影有效消除 Bellman drift，在不增加非线性深度和自举成本的前提下，为基于 FHE 的深度强化学习提供了一种切实可行的稳定化方案，兼具安全边界保证、策略精度提升与噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving machine learning presents significant deployment challenges on the cloud for intelligent systems with confidential data. Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) offers a compelling solution for secure computation, preserving data confidentiality of cloud computations. However, applying FHE to reinforcement learning (RL) requires replacing non-linear operations with polynomial approximations, which diverge catastrophically due to a unique recursive error phenomenon known as the Bellman drift. This article introduces the Homomorphic Advantage Operator (HAO), a stabilization framework designed to prevent polynomial approximation divergence in FHE-based deep RL. HAO adapts the zero-mean centering projection from advantage-based value estimation directly to temporal-difference (TD) targets. This linear projection annihilates the uniform state-value baseline that drives the Bellman drift, maintaining per-state action rankings while requiring zero additional non-linear multiplicative depth and avoiding expensive ciphertext bootstrapping. The proposed HAO framework was evaluated using a three-tier experimental methodology, including a tabular Markov Decision Process (MDP), an encrypted CartPole environment using real CKKS cryptographic operations, and a 20-node logistics routing benchmark with dense continuous features. The results demonstrate that the proposed HAO strictly bounds network pre-activations within the safe polynomial approximation domain. The proposed HAO RL agents achieved 0% boundary breaches across all random seeds used, whereas regularization alone (L2 weight decay and gradient clipping) breached the bound on 3 of 5 seeds and the unstabilized baseline did so in 83.8% of episodes. Finally, HAO agents improve optimal policy accuracy by 18.0 percentage points in tabular domains and remain stable when DP-SGD-style Gaussian noise is added to the clipped gradients.

</details>


### [147] [A Comparative Explainability Framework for DeBERTa-v3 in Zero-Shot Medical Abstract Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02116)
*Javier Diaz Esteban-Herreros,David Muñoz-Valero,Raquel Martínez-España,Jose M. Juarez,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个比较性可解释性框架，用于审计DeBERTa-v3在医学摘要零样本分类中的表现，通过五种归因方法的一致性与失败机制分析，揭示解释稳定性与预测确定性的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决可解释人工智能中的"分歧问题"，即不同归因方法对同一输入和预测产生不同解释；同时为基于Transformer的医学文本分类模型提供审计工具。

Method: 在Medical Abstracts语料上实现自然语言推理引擎，每个诊断类别设五个增强假设，每类平衡采样一千篇文本。比较五种解释方法：模型无关的SHAP和LIME、深度学习专用的遮挡法和Input x Gradient、Transformer专用的Attention x Gradient。通过top-token归因标准化解释，并用Jaccard指数量化成对一致性。

Result: 在定义明确的临床领域取得高预测准确率，但在高度语义模糊时性能下降。解释稳定性直接反映预测确定性：单义类别中收敛强，诊断不确定性下明显下降。定性错误审计发现三种系统性失败机制：词汇过度敏感、语义重叠、归因一致性丧失。

Conclusion: 支持在审计基于Transformer的医学文本分类模型时组合使用多种解释方法和定量一致性指标，并建议优先采用特定临床本体而非宽泛的诊断标签。

Abstract: A comparative explainability framework is presented to audit DeBERTa-v3 under zero-shot classification of medical abstracts. The work addresses the disagreement problem in Explainable Artificial Intelligence, where different attribution methods produce divergent explanations for the same input and prediction. A natural language inference engine is implemented over the Medical Abstracts corpus with five enriched hypotheses per diagnostic category and a balanced sample of one thousand texts per class. Five explanation methods are compared: SHAP and LIME as model-agnostic approaches, occlusion and Input x Gradient as deep-learning-specific approaches, and Attention x Gradient as a transformer-specific approach. Explanations are standardized through top-token attribution, and pairwise agreement is quantified using the Jaccard index. High predictive accuracy is achieved across well-defined clinical domains, whereas performance degrades under high semantic ambiguity. Explanatory stability directly mirrors predictive certainty, exhibiting strong convergence in univalent categories and a marked drop under diagnostic uncertainty. Furthermore, qualitative error auditing uncovers three systemic failure mechanisms: lexical hypersensitivity, semantic overlap, and loss of attribution coherence. The results support the combined use of several explanation methods and quantitative agreement metrics when auditing transformer-based models in medical text classification, and suggest prioritizing specific clinical ontologies over broad diagnostic labels.

</details>


### [148] [VISTA: A Visual Harness for Reasoning in an Interactive World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02200)
*Qiushi Han,Keya Hu,Linlu Qiu,Cathy Wu,Kaiming He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA，一种通用视觉脚手架，使多模态模型获得长时程视觉能力，可无损保存并主动检索视觉记忆，在ARC-AGI-3等基准上大幅提升推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型具备较强推理能力，但缺少合适的外部框架来释放其在多样交互环境中的潜力；现有做法难以支撑长时程、原始形式的视觉观察与记忆。

Method: 提出VISTA视觉脚手架：让模型直接通过视觉观察感知环境；维护无损视觉记忆，以原始形式保存过去观察；模型可在推理过程中主动检索这些观察并重组视觉输入；设计简单，可低成本迁移到多种视觉环境。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3上，VISTA将Claude Opus 5.0的Relative Human Action Efficiency从40.68提升至满分100.00，完成全部25个公开游戏，且比首次参与的人类玩家少用57.4%的动作；在另外三个覆盖多种视觉游戏与谜题的基准上，相同底层模型配合VISTA显著优于使用简单脚手架的基线。

Conclusion: VISTA有望成为通用视觉脚手架，推动多模态智能体在复杂视觉环境中的发展。

Abstract: We show that multimodal models possess strong reasoning abilities and that an appropriate harness can unlock their potential to solve tasks across diverse interactive environments. We introduce VISTA, a visual harness that gives a general-purpose multimodal model long-horizon vision. VISTA allows the model to directly perceive the environment through visual observations and maintains a lossless visual memory that preserves past observations in their original form. The model can actively retrieve these observations and reorganize its visual input as it reasons. On ARC-AGI-3, VISTA improves Claude Opus 5.0's Relative Human Action Efficiency score from 40.68 to a perfect 100.00, with the model completing all 25 public games using 57.4% fewer actions than first-time human participants. VISTA's simple design also allows it to extend naturally to diverse visual environments with minimal adaptation. Across three additional benchmarks covering a diverse range of visual games and puzzles, it substantially outperforms baselines using the same underlying model with minimal harnesses. Our results highlight VISTA's potential as a general-purpose visual harness for advancing multimodal agents in complex visual environments.

</details>


### [149] [ScholarCatalyst: A Benchmark for Retrieving Papers That Inspire New Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02202)
*Sohyeon Kim,Yoonho Lee,Bo Liu,Dayoon Ko,Rulin Shao,Seungone Kim,Graham Neubig,Pang Wei Koh,Aakanksha Chowdhery,Akari Asai,Omar Khattab,Yejin Choi,Gunhee Kim,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ScholarCatalyst 数据集与任务，考察 AI 能否像科学家一样为"半成品"研究问题找到关键前置文献；结果显示当前检索与智能体方法均远不及专家直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伟大科学家的核心能力之一是能在浩如烟海的文献中，判断一个新问题真正需要哪条早先的想法。随着 AI 开始在开放问题上取得进展，这种"专家直觉式检索"能力成为科学智能体的关键短板，但此前缺乏带作者判断的评测资源。

Method: 作者设计可扩展的自动化标注流程，邀请 207 篇近期计算机科学论文的 184 位第一作者，标注哪些候选文献实际或本可以推进其项目，并附详细理由。基于此构建 ScholarCatalyst，并设计检索任务：给定初始研究问题，仅从项目开始时的可得文献中检索这些论文。

Result: 智能体式搜索并不优于向量检索（Recall@20 为 0.42 对 0.48），尽管它调用了同一检索器；即便使用可能已在训练中见过成品论文的 Claude Fable 5.1 构建的智能体，也仅达到 0.51 R@20。

Conclusion: 结果表明需要新的训练方法，使模型具备在广域语料中检索的专家直觉。ScholarCatalyst 是迈向能接收半成形想法并指出所需前置研究的科学智能体的一步。

Abstract: What makes great scientists great? Even as AI systems start to make progress on open problems, scientists remain far ahead of them at sensing which prior idea, buried in an ever-growing archive of research, a new problem needs. To study this skill, we draw on researchers who know firsthand which earlier work advanced their completed projects, with papers serving as pointers to the ideas within. Using our automated pipeline that makes author annotation scalable, we build ScholarCatalyst by having 184 lead authors of 207 recent computer science papers label which candidates did or could have advanced their project, each with a detailed rationale. We introduce a retrieval task with author-provided judgments: given an initial research question, retrieve these papers from only the literature available when the project began. Agentic search does no better than embedding retrieval (0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20) despite calling that same retriever as a tool. Even an agent built on Claude Fable 5.1, which may have seen the completed papers during training, reaches only 0.51 R@20. These results highlight the need for new training recipes that equip models with expert intuition for searching broad corpora. We envision ScholarCatalyst as a step toward scientific agents that can take a half-formed idea and point to the prior research it needs.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [When the AI Leaves the Tailorshop: Measuring What an LLM Advisor Leaves Behind in Complex Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00163)
*Robin Welsch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 两项预注册实验发现，LLM顾问能提升用户在模拟服装厂任务中的信心、理解与公司价值，但未必提升预测准确性；撤除AI后，曾获支持者只在部分评分口径下表现更好，且更频繁修改AI建议者后续独立表现与知识更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂问题解决既依赖有效行动，也依赖对系统运作的理解；AI建议可能对这两类结果产生不均衡的影响。作者希望检验AI支持是否能真正提升用户的理解与独立能力，而非仅在使用AI时提高表现，从而为应用型人机协作（HAI）评估提供依据。

Method: 开展两项预注册实验，参与者管理一个模拟服装厂，并随机分为有/无LLM顾问两组。研究一N=200，研究二N=198。测量指标包括信心、理解、努力程度、公司价值、预测准确性、破产情况与知识水平；并在撤除AI后评估其独立表现，同时分析AI支持组内“修改推荐建议的频率”与后续表现的关系。

Result: 两项研究中，AI支持组报告更高信心与理解，且付出更少努力。研究一：AI支持提高公司价值，但预测准确性无可检测差异；撤除支持后，曾获支持者在“重复先前选择”的评分口径下优于控制组，但在默认设置口径下没有优势。AI支持组内，更频繁修改推荐建议预测更好的无AI辅助表现。研究二：AI支持组更少破产，注册分析中显示小幅知识优势，且该优势主要与保持偿付能力相关；组内更频繁修改推荐建议预测更高知识水平。

Conclusion: AI建议可提升使用中的绩效、信心与主观理解并降低努力，但不必然带来预测准确性或独立能力的同步提升。应用型HAI评估应同时考察用户的理解与脱离AI后的独立能力，而不能只看AI支持下的绩效；用户对AI建议的主动修改可能是促进学习与独立表现的重要行为指标。

Abstract: Complex problem solving depends on acting effectively and understanding how a system works. AI advice may support these outcomes unequally. Two preregistered experiments compared participants managing a simulated clothing factory with and without an LLM advisor. Across studies, AI-supported participants reported greater confidence and understanding with less effort. In the first study (N=200), assistance increased company value but produced no detectable prediction-accuracy difference. After withdrawal, previously supported participants outperformed controls when decisions were scored against repeating previous choices, but not default settings. Within the AI-supported group, more frequent recommendation alterations predicted better unaided performance. In the second study (N=198), AI-supported participants went bankrupt less often and showed a small knowledge advantage in the registered analysis, largely associated with remaining solvent. More frequent recommendation alterations predicted higher knowledge within the AI-supported group. Applied HAI evaluation should assess users' understanding and independent capability alongside the performance achieved with AI support.

</details>


### [151] [Faithful Chart Generation for Multimodal Deep Research: Frame-Evidence Co-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00374)
*Yuxin Yue,Yingchen Zhang,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Maarten de Rijke,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出FECA框架，通过视觉框架与检索证据的双向迭代协同，实现多模态深度研究中图表的证据自适应可视化规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析图表编码定量主张，需保证可视化数值有证据支撑且保持原义与范围。但现有系统在未知可检索定量证据前就构建可视化方案，导致预定义方案所需的实体、时间范围或比较维度仅被证据部分支持；现有方法仅做事后验证，只能识别不支持的值却无法改变底层视觉框架。

Method: 受Data-Frame Theory中双向意义建构过程启发，FECA将图表生成建模为视觉框架与检索证据的迭代交互：框架引导证据获取，检索证据决定框架被接受、修订还是丢弃，再进行渲染。由此将图表生成从固定方案验证转变为自适应、证据驱动的可视化推理。

Result: 在100个真实研究主题上的实验表明，FECA显著提升数值保真度，同时保持报告质量与图表实用性。

Conclusion: 通过将可视化规划与证据可得性相耦合，FECA实现了证据落地的自适应视觉推理，是解决分析图表数值保真问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Analytical charts in multimodal deep research encode quantitative claims, requiring every visualized value to be faithfully grounded in supporting evidence. Unlike retrieved images that mainly provide contextual information, charts require numerical fidelity: visualized values should not only match retrieved evidence quantitatively but also preserve its original meaning and scope. However, achieving such fidelity remains challenging because current systems usually construct visualization plans before knowing what quantitative evidence can actually be retrieved from the web. As a result, predefined plans may require entities, temporal ranges, or comparison dimensions that the retrieved evidence only partially supports. Existing approaches mainly address this issue through post-hoc verification after chart plans are fixed, enabling unsupported values to be identified but leaving the underlying visual frames unchanged. To address this challenge, we propose Frame-Evidence Co-Adaptation (FECA), an evidence-adaptive visual planning framework for multimodal deep research. Inspired by the bidirectional sensemaking process in Data-Frame Theory, FECA models chart generation as an iterative interaction between visual frames and retrieved evidence. Each visual frame is adaptive: the frame guides evidence acquisition, while retrieved evidence determines whether the frame should be accepted, revised, or dropped before rendering. By coupling visualization planning with evidence availability, FECA shifts chart generation from fixed-plan verification to adaptive evidence-grounded visual reasoning. Experiments on 100 real-world research topics show that FECA substantially improves numerical fidelity while preserving report quality and chart utility.

</details>


### [152] [Preliminary Evaluation of Transition-Aware Controller Locomotion Adaptations for Supporting Postural Stability in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00628)
*Ramisa Fariha Joyee,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出三种运动适应方法（运动加速、转向加速、运动减速），通过检测VR中站立-行走、转向、行走-停止等状态转换并进行运动平滑处理，以改善姿势稳定性；初步评估显示可降低压力中心速度并提升平衡信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR中身体状态转换（如起步、转向、停止）时姿势稳定性容易受影响，用户可能失去平衡。作者希望通过对运动过程的适应化平滑处理来改善这些转换期间的平衡与导航体验。

Method: 提出三种适应方法：Motion Acceleration（起步时逐渐增加运动速度）、Turn Acceleration（转向时平滑旋转）、Motion Deceleration（停止前逐渐降低速度）。系统检测站立到行走、转向、行走到停止的转换并施加自适应运动平滑。在虚拟导航任务中通过客观与主观平衡指标进行评估。

Result: 初步结果显示压力中心（COP）速度降低，平衡信心提升。

Conclusion: 运动适应方法能够改善VR中的平衡与导航体验。

Abstract: We present three locomotion adaptation approaches: Motion Acceleration, Turn Acceleration, and Motion Deceleration to improve postural stability during body-state transitions in virtual reality (VR). The system detects standing-to-walking, turning, and walking-to-stopping transitions and applies adaptive locomotion smoothing. Motion Acceleration gradually increases locomotion speed when users begin walking, Turn Acceleration smooths rotation while turning, and Motion Deceleration gradually reduces movement speed before stopping. We evaluated these techniques in a virtual navigation task using objective and subjective balance measures. Preliminary results show reduced center of pressure (COP) velocity and improved balance confidence. These findings suggest that locomotion adaptations can improve balance and navigation experience.

</details>


### [153] [Sensing Instability, Adapting the Scene: A Real-Time Movement-Smoothing Design Framework for Stable VR Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00643)
*Ramisa Fariha Joyee,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实时运动平滑设计框架，按站立、行走与转身三类运动状态的平衡需求组织视觉调整策略，当前聚焦框架设计与实现，用户研究留待未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在VR中站立、行走和转身时面临不同的平衡挑战，但多数移动技术不考虑运动状态，统一采用相同的视觉行为，难以针对性地支持姿态稳定。

Method: 提出实时运动平滑设计框架，依据运动特定的平衡需求组织视觉适应，并引入三种分别面向站立、行走和转身的设计策略，展示状态感知的视觉调整如何支持移动中的姿态稳定性；论文侧重框架的设计与实现。

Result: 本文尚未开展用户研究，仅完成框架设计与实现及三种策略的阐述，实证验证计划作为未来工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来情境感知VR移动系统的开发提供指导，有助于实现更安全、更舒适的导航体验。

Abstract: Users experience different balance challenges while standing, walking, and turning in virtual reality (VR), yet most locomotion techniques apply the same visual behavior regardless of movement state. We present a real-time movement-smoothing design framework in this paper that organizes visual adaptations according to movement-specific balance demands. The framework introduces three design strategies targeting standing, walking, and turning, illustrating how state-aware visual adaptations can support postural stability during locomotion. This paper focuses on the framework design and implementation, while a user study is planned as future work. Our work guides the development of future context-aware VR locomotion systems that better support safe and comfortable navigation.

</details>


### [154] [From Images to Tasks: Characterizing Multimodal LLM Interactions in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00701)
*Jinyi Ye,Scott Counts,Gaurav Verma,Kate Lytvynets,Weiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过分析超4万条真实图像上传对话，构建十类多模态能力框架，揭示真实使用与现有基准测试之间的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型日益普及，但用户在自然场景中如何实际使用它们仍缺乏了解，本文旨在回答"用户上传图像时想完成什么任务"这一问题。

Method: 从Microsoft Copilot收集超过40,000条去标识化的图像上传对话，建立覆盖感知、认知、生成三大层面的十类层次化能力框架进行分析；并在独立的ChatGPT数据集上验证结论。

Result: 多数图像上传任务涉及多种能力；多模态使用比纯文本交互覆盖更广泛多样的任务空间，呈现不对称覆盖和对跨模态对齐的依赖；将实际能力需求映射到253个现有基准后发现，基准集中于感知和指向固定答案的推理，而涉及文本、代码和数据生成的常见工作流测试不足。

Conclusion: 本文提供了大规模实证刻画，说明用户使用多模态大模型的目标，并指出应基于观察到的真实用户需求来设计基准测试。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) increasingly integrate vision and text, yet how people use them in natural settings remains underexplored. We seek to answer the question: when users upload images, what tasks are they trying to accomplish? Analyzing over 40,000 de-identified image-upload conversations from Microsoft Copilot, we characterize real-world multimodal use through a hierarchical framework of ten capabilities, spanning perception, cognition, and generation. First, we characterize the distribution and composition of these capabilities, finding that the majority of image-upload tasks involve multiple capabilities. Second, we find that multimodal use spans a broader and more diverse task space than text-only interactions, with asymmetric coverage and task classes that rely on cross-modal grounding. Third, mapping observed capability demand onto 253 existing benchmarks reveals uneven alignment between benchmark coverage and real-world use: benchmarks concentrate on perception and reasoning toward fixed answers, while common workflows involving text, code, and data generation remain comparatively undertested. We validate our taxonomy and findings on an independent ChatGPT dataset. Our results provide a large-scale empirical characterization of what users seek to accomplish with multimodal LLMs and highlight opportunities for benchmark design grounded in observed user demand.

</details>


### [155] [LeanSide: A Formally Verified Co-Reasoning System for Natural-language Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00760)
*Chenjun Guo,Manooshree Patel,Arnav Mehta,Krishiv Kothari,Thomas Lu,Niels Voss,Rayna Bhattacharyya,Peter Donovan,Bjoern Hartmann,Gireeja Ranade*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索一种结合自然语言书写与形式化验证后端的"H共推理"界面，开发了LeanSide系统，用于本科数学教育，帮助学生书写和修改自然语言证明，并获得可理解的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在演绎推理中会幻觉或偏离用户意图，而形式化证明助手虽能机器验证但学习曲线陡峭、推理粒度过细。需要结合两者优势。

Method: 开发LeanSide——一个形式化验证的共推理系统，可将学生推理自动形式化为Lean，并将验证器输出非形式化为人能理解的反馈。在课堂部署中进行用户研究。

Result: 通过课堂部署用户研究，分析了哪些系统属性能帮助学生取得进展，哪些导致他们卡住。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了在人类-AI共推理系统中使用形式化验证后端的设计启示。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as collaborators on deductive-reasoning tasks, but their outputs can hallucinate or pull users away from intended reasoning. Formal proof assistants provide machine-checked verification, but have a steep learning curve and require more granular reasoning than human written proofs. We explore an interface that combines these strengths, allowing users to write and revise free-form natural-language proofs while a verified backend checks their reasoning and returns feedback at the user's granularity. We study this interface in the context of undergraduate mathematics education by developing LeanSide, a formally verified co-reasoning system, which auto-formalizes student reasoning into Lean and informalizes verifier output into understandable feedback. We conducted user studies through classroom deployment and analyzed which system properties helped students make progress and which caused them to get stuck. We use these findings to derive design implications for using a formally verified backend in human-AI co-reasoning systems.

</details>


### [156] [Scaling Peer Assessments: An Integrity Report from a Large Engineering Internship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01020)
*Jinal Gupta,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Prakash Hegade,Rohit Sharma,Sakshi Sharma,Meenakshi V,S. R. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文在一项大规模多机构实习项目中实施"同伴验证"评估模式：先由教师验证少数学生，再授权这些学生去验证同伴，从而逐级扩展到整个群体。基于238份问卷，研究考察了参与者的体验与评估诚信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大课堂中教师难以逐一评估每位学生的理解、参与和考核行为，同伴评估可将这一责任分散给学习者，从而降低对教师主导评估的依赖。作者希望探索同伴验证能否在保持评估诚信的前提下实现规模化。

Method: 在一个大规模、多机构实习项目中采用同伴验证模型：通过简短口头讨论进行评估。流程先从教师验证一小部分学生开始，被验证通过的学生获授权去验证其同伴，随后逐步扩展至整个群体，实现规模化运作。研究采用项目结束时的问卷调查，共238名同意参与的受访者，分析参与者的体验以及评估诚信的维持程度。

Result: 多数参与者认为该活动有价值，79.8%表示解决了此前无法解决的问题。但29.0%承认至少出现过一次降低投入、放宽验证标准或相互验证的情况，88.7%认为至少存在少量未经充分考核的验证。在被问及如何强化流程时，参与者选择"验证后讨论解答"的比例约为选择更严格审核或导师主导验证的两倍。

Conclusion: 研究提供了描述性证据，说明同伴验证作为大规模学习环境中可扩展的评估方式，既具潜力，也面临诚信方面的挑战。

Abstract: Assessing learning in large classrooms presents a significant challenge for individual instructors, who may have limited capacity to evaluate the understanding, participation, and assessment behaviour of every student. Peer assessments have been a way of distributing this responsibility among learners, allowing them to evaluate and provide feedback to one another while reducing dependence on instructor-led assessments. Building on this approach, we implemented a peer validation model within a large, multi-institutional internship programme in which students who demonstrated sufficient understanding were authorised to assess and validate their peers through short oral discussions. The assessment process began with the instructor validating a small group of students, who were then authorised to validate their peers, allowing the process to gradually expand across the cohort and operate at scale. This study examines how participants experienced the model and the extent to which assessment integrity was maintained, using an end-of-programme survey of 238 consenting respondents. Most participants regarded the activity as worthwhile, with 79.8% reporting that they solved problems they could not previously solve. However, 29.0% acknowledged at least one instance of reduced effort, a lowered validation standard, or reciprocal validation, while 88.7% believed that at least a little validation had occurred without proper examination. When asked how the process could be strengthened, participants selected post-validation discussion of solutions approximately twice as often as closer auditing or mentor-led validation. These findings provide descriptive evidence of both the potential and the integrity challenges of using peer validation as a scalable assessment approach in large learning environments.

</details>


### [157] [Who Thinks First? Designing Productive Friction with Engage-to-Unlock GenAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01518)
*Xiaotian Su,Laura Rimell,Jiazheng Li,Amal Rannen-Triki,Ulrich Paquet,Lisa Anne Hendricks,Rida Qadri,Daphne Ippolito,Piotr Mirowski*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"Engage-to-Unlock"机制：用户需先有意义地参与任务，才能解锁生成式AI功能，从而在保持AI使用的同时促进用户的早期投入。通过N=398的对照实验验证了该机制能重分配认知努力并提升评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽能辅助写作，但无摩擦的即时使用可能导致用户在形成自己的观点之前就发生认知卸载（cognitive offloading），削弱独立思考与深度参与。因此需要一种"生产性摩擦"（productive friction）机制，在利用AI能力的同时鼓励用户先自行参与任务。

Method: 采用受控实验（N=398），将被试随机分配到四种写作任务条件之一：仅人类（Human-Only）、标准聊天机器人（Standard Chatbot）、Engage-to-Unlock（先参与后解锁）、时间匹配解锁（Time-Matched Unlock，解锁时间与前者相同但不依赖用户参与程度）。之后被试对文章进行证据错误与推理错误的评估。

Result: Engage-to-Unlock条件重分配了任务努力：被试花更多时间写作、更少时间评估，但整体任务时长未增加。该条件下被试提交的提示（prompts）数量多于其他AI辅助条件，并在所有条件中表现出最高的"单位时间评估准确率"（accuracy-per-time evaluation efficiency）。

Conclusion: 将生成式AI的访问设计为鼓励早期人类参与，可提供一种生产性摩擦形式，同时保留AI的积极使用与高效的下游评估。这为GenAI工具的人机交互设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative AI can support writing, but frictionless access may cause cognitive offloading before users develop their own ideas. We introduce Engage-to-Unlock, a productive-friction mechanism that unlocks generative capabilities after users meaningfully engage with the task. In a controlled experiment (N = 398), participants completed a writing task under one of four conditions: Human-Only, Standard Chatbot, Engage-to-Unlock, or Time-Matched Unlock, which matched unlock timing to Engage-to-Unlock participants but independent of users' engagement, then evaluated passages for evidence and inferential errors. Results show that Engage-to-Unlock redistributed effort across tasks: participants spent more time writing and less time evaluating, without increasing overall task duration. They also submitted more prompts than in other AI-assisted conditions and showed the highest accuracy-per-time evaluation efficiency across conditions. These findings suggest that designing GenAI access to encourage early human engagement may provide a productive form of friction, while retaining active AI use and efficient downstream evaluation.

</details>


### [158] [Where LLMs Fail with Visualization DSLs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01873)
*Chang Han,Andrew McNutt,Katherine Isaacs*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨LLM在使用可视化领域特定语言（DSL）生成图表时的失败模式，评估10种JSON风格的可视化DSL在3个LLM上的表现，识别出4种反复出现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM逐渐承担起使用可视化DSL创作图表的角色，但塑造这些语言的"人类约束"可能不再适用——对人类容易的事对模型未必容易。因此需要理解LLM为何以及如何在与现有DSL设计配合时失败，以指导未来DSL设计。

Method: 评估10种JSON风格的可视化DSL、41个任务、3个LLM；对生成的规格进行JSON与渲染检查，并对失败案例进行定性编码分析。

Result: 识别出4种反复出现的失败模式，并将每种失败模式关联到具体的DSL特征。

Conclusion: 针对LLM与DSL失效的关联，提出了未来DSL设计的设计考量。

Abstract: As LLMs take up the role of authoring charts using visualization domain-specific languages (DSLs), the human constraints that shaped those languages may no longer apply, as what is easy for a person is not necessarily easy for a model. To understand how LLMs might work better with DSLs, we explore where and how they fail with current DSL designs. We evaluate 10 JSON-style visualization DSLs with 41 tasks across 3 LLMs, then assess the generated specifications with JSON and rendering checks, and qualitative coding of failed cases. Analyzing how this specification generation process fails, we identify four recurring failure patterns, link each to specific DSL features, and discuss design considerations for future DSL designs.

</details>


### [159] [XAI Evaluation Cards: A Practical Method for Designing Human-Centred XAI Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02011)
*Kristýna Sirka Kacafírková,Ivania Donoso-Guzmán,Denis Parra,Katrien Verbert,An Jacobs*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于更新版XAI评估框架的卡片分类方法（36张卡片），帮助研究者系统化、跨学科地优先确定XAI系统评估维度，并在两个研究组（n=13）的五个项目中进行了测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以人为中心的XAI评估需要研究者从众多评估维度和指标中选择，但当前选择方式往往是临时的、碎片化的，缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 基于对82项研究的分析，构建更新的XAI专用评估框架，并据此开发包含36张卡片的卡片分类方法，用于帮助研究者优先确定相关评估要素；在两个研究组（n=13）、五个项目中进行测试，并提供可打印附录和以往XAI研究方法的在线资源库。

Result: 测试表明，卡片分类方法能够组织和简化评估流程的设计，促进对XAI系统更全面、跨学科的评估。

Conclusion: 尽管该方法并非穷尽所有可能性，但XAI评估卡片有助于系统化、跨学科地开展XAI系统评估，适用于研究与开发实践。

Abstract: Evaluating explainable AI (XAI) systems from a human-centred approach requires researchers to select from numerous evaluation dimensions and measures, often in an ad hoc and fragmented manner. This paper introduces a method to help HCI, computer science, designers and social science researchers systematically evaluate XAI systems. The approach is based on an updated XAI-specific evaluation framework derived from an analysis of 82 studies. Using this framework, we developed a card-sorting method with 36 cards to help researchers prioritise relevant evaluation aspects. The process was tested with two research groups (n = 13) across five projects. The XAI Evaluation Cards are available as a printable appendix, along with an online repository of methods from previous XAI studies. Although not exhaustive, our findings indicate that the card-sorting approach can organise and streamline the design of the evaluation process, encouraging a more comprehensive and multidisciplinary assessment of XAI systems in research and development.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Reverse Item Response Theory for Sparsity-Robust Ranking in Fragmented Cancer Drug-Response Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00002)
*Jung Min Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将反向项目反应理论（IRT）引入药物基因组学药物反应分析，把癌症类型视为具"耐药能力"的潜在被试、药物视为具"逃避难度"的题目，在GDSC2的242,036条药物敏感性测量上进行建模，估计共享潜在尺度上的癌型级别体外耐药性与药物级别广谱活性。在四种缺失情形下，反向IRT均比简单平均更好地恢复全数据潜在排序，60%缺失时Delta-rho为+0.089至+0.095；五方法比较中Brier分数最优；28种癌症类型中19种分类稳定；PRISM跨平台复制方向一致率82%但秩相关弱（rho=0.25）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物基因组学中的药物反应数据高度碎片化，存在大量缺失，简单平均等传统聚合方法在缺失情形下难以稳健地恢复癌症类型的真实耐药排序，因此需要一种能在共享潜在尺度上同时刻画癌型耐药性与药物活性的稳健方法。

Method: 将反向IRT应用于药物反应分析：癌症类型作为具"耐药能力"的潜在"被试"，药物作为具"逃避难度"的"题目"。基于GDSC2数据库中242,036条药物敏感性测量，在共享潜在尺度上估计癌型级别体外耐药性和药物级别广谱活性。通过四种缺失情形进行验证，与简单平均比较潜在排序恢复能力，用Brier分数评估留出预测，并用Bootstrap置信区间评估分类稳定性，最后在PRISM跨平台数据上做复制检验。

Result: 在四种缺失情形下，反向IRT比简单平均更好地恢复全数据潜在排序，60%缺失时在MCAR、癌症偏置和药物偏置稀疏条件下Delta-rho为+0.089至+0.095。留出预测中IRT在五种方法里Brier分数最佳。Bootstrap置信区间显示28种癌症类型中19种具有稳定的耐药/敏感分类。PRISM跨平台复制显示方向一致率82%，但秩相关较弱（rho=0.25）。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于方法学的稳健性，即在碎片化评估条件下仍能较好恢复潜在排序，而非提供一个普适的临床耐药排行榜；跨平台复制表明其结论在方向性上可迁移，但排序一致性有限。

Abstract: We introduce reverse Item Response Theory (IRT) to pharmacogenomic drug-response analysis by treating cancer types as latent "subjects" with resistance ability and drugs as "items" with evasion difficulty. Applied to 242,036 drug sensitivity measurements from the Genomics of Drug Sensitivity in Cancer (GDSC2) database, the model estimates cancer-type-level in-vitro resistance and drug-level broad activity on a shared latent scale. Validation across four missingness regimes demonstrates that reverse IRT better recovers the full-data latent ranking than simple averaging, with advantages of Delta-rho = +0.089 to +0.095 at 60% missingness under MCAR, cancer-biased, and drug-biased sparsity. Held-out prediction confirms IRT achieves the best Brier score among five evaluated methods. Bootstrap confidence intervals show 19 of 28 cancer types have stable resistant/sensitive classifications. Cross-platform PRISM replication shows 82% directional agreement but weak rank-order correlation (rho = 0.25), indicating the contribution is methodological robustness under fragmented evaluation, not a universal clinical resistance leaderboard.

</details>


### [161] [How Far is Adam from Natural Gradient Descent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00004)
*Vihaan Paka-Hegde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用尺度不变的γ(Δθ)度量，将Adam完整更新规则（含动量）视为对角经验Fisher近似，在四类损失景观上测量其与真实自然梯度的几何偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam是深度学习标准优化器，但其与自然梯度下降（NGD）的几何关系仍存在未解问题，需系统量化Adam偏离真实NGD的程度及成因。

Method: 将Adam（含动量）建模为对角经验Fisher近似，涉及对角截断、经验标签替换与时间滞后；采用尺度不变的γ(Δθ)度量，在良态线性回归、病态线性回归、逻辑回归和非凸小型神经网络四类损失景观上测量几何偏差；同时比较改进经验Fisher（iEF）与标准经验Fisher（EF）的轨迹稳定性。

Result: Adam几何轨迹依赖具体情境：良态设置下偏差较低，病态条件下显著上升，神经网络中错位程度达约10³；几何漂移越大对应初期优化越慢，但不影响最终目标最小化，Adam均能达到低损失；iEF比EF路径更稳定，EF常振荡或发散。

Conclusion: Adam的实际优化能力可能源于结构性近似误差与动量平滑之间的平衡，而非紧密跟随自然梯度路径。

Abstract: Adam is the standard optimizer in deep learning, yet its geometric relationship to natural gradient descent (NGD) contains unresolved questions. We study Adam's full update rule, including momentum, as a diagonal empirical Fisher approximation subject to diagonal truncation, empirical label substitution, and temporal lag. Using the scale-invariant $γ(Δθ)$ metric, we measure Adam's geometric deviation from true NGD across four loss landscapes: well-conditioned linear regression, ill-conditioned linear regression, logistic regression, and a non-convex small neural network. Adam's geometric trajectory is context-dependent. Deviation remains low in well-conditioned settings but rises significantly under ill-conditioning, reaching misalignments of $\approx 10^3$ in the neural network. Higher geometric drift correlates with slower initial optimization but does not degrade final objective minimization; Adam consistently reaches low loss. Furthermore, the improved empirical Fisher (iEF) tracks more stable paths than the standard empirical Fisher (EF), which frequently oscillates or diverges. Our results suggest Adam's practical optimization power may stem from a balance of structural approximation errors and momentum smoothing rather than close tracking of the natural gradient path.

</details>


### [162] [FourierQK: Filter Shape, Admissibility and the Leakage-Coverage Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00009)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对照消融实验，研究频域注意力中滤波器形状对性能的影响，验证带通振荡结构（尤其DC/Nyquist抑制与段落尺度带宽）在双向注意力中的关键作用，并指出自回归生成需因果谱变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频域塌缩注意力（Frequency-collapse attention）用学习频率上的带通滤波内积替代Q/K点积，取得显著提升。作者希望进一步探究：哪种滤波器形状效果最佳、以及背后的原因，从而明确频域注意力的适用边界。

Method: 在字符级语言建模任务（TinyShakespeare，6层GPT）上进行受控消融，检验五类滤波器属性假设：DC抑制、Nyquist抑制、带宽、中心频率、多尺度覆盖。

Result: (1) DC与Nyquist分量有害（val≈2.0，等同相位随机化），说明振荡带通结构不可或缺；(2) 最优单尺度带宽σ≈2 bins、中心位于段落尺度（~70 tokens），较BASE-DOT提升Δ=+1.15 nats；(3) 可容许滤波器（零均值、Mexican Hat DOG m=2）在同尺度下优于非可容许高斯，并部分抵御双边FFT泄漏；(4) 双边FFT泄漏随谱覆盖单调增加，窄带（gap>+4）干净、宽带（gap<+2）泄漏；(5) 字符尺度因果时域Morlet无法超越BASE-DOT（K=128 taps仅覆盖T=256上下文的50%）。

Conclusion: 综合发现表明FourierQK适用于双向注意力场景（编码器式，如BERT），因训练与推理时均可用全序列上下文；自回归生成则需要因果谱变体，如MorletQK（解码器式，如GPT）。

Abstract: Frequency-collapse attention [Zeris, 2026e] achieves large gains over standard dot-product attention by replacing the Q/K dot product with a bandpass-filtered inner product at a learned frequency. A natural follow-up question is: which filter shape works best, and why? We test five hypotheses about filter properties -- DC suppression, Nyquist suppression, bandwidth, centre frequency, and multi-scale coverage -- using a controlled ablation on character-level language modelling (TinyShakespeare, 6-layer GPT). Our main findings are: (1) DC and Nyquist components are actively harmful (val ~= 2.0, equivalent to phase randomisation), confirming that oscillatory bandpass structure is essential, not just any low-dimensional spectral summary; (2) the optimal single-scale bandwidth is sigma ~= 2 bins centred at paragraph scale (~70 tokens), giving a clean gain of Delta = +1.15 nats over BASE-DOT; (3) admissible filters (zero-mean, Mexican Hat DOG m = 2) outperform non-admissible Gaussians at the same scale and provide partial protection against bilateral FFT leakage; (4) bilateral FFT leakage scales monotonically with spectral coverage -- narrowband filters (gap > +4) are clean, wideband filters (gap < +2) are leaky; and (5) causal time-domain Morlet at character scale cannot beat BASE-DOT (K=128 taps covers 50% of T=256 context), motivating word-level experiments in the companion MorletQK paper [Zeris, 2026f]. Together, findings (1)-(5) characterise FourierQK as effective in bidirectional attention settings (encoder-style, e.g. BERT), where full-sequence context is available at both training and inference time; autoregressive generation requires a causal spectral variant such as MorletQK [Zeris, 2026f] (decoder-style, e.g. GPT). Code available at: https://github.com/AthanasiosZeris/energy-gated-attention

</details>


### [163] [Integrating Fairness and Explainability in a Multiple Instance Reinforcement Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00035)
*Bente Hinkenhuis,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将强化学习多示例学习（RL-MIL）、对抗去偏与偏好条件超网络结合的多目标框架，用于学生风险预测，发现超网络扩展出现模式崩溃，偏好权重无法有效控制公平性-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从教育交互数据预测学生表现需要兼顾准确性与可解释性以支持有效干预，同时人口统计信息带来不公平预测风险，因此需要能同时处理公平性与性能的多目标建模方法。

Method: 提出多目标框架：用RL-MIL将每个学生表示为弱标注交互的包，由RL智能体选择信息量大的示例供下游分类；结合对抗去偏；并评估两种偏好条件超网络变体，检验用户定义的偏好标量能否连续控制预测性能与Equalized Odds之间的权衡。

Result: RL-MIL基线取得较强分类性能，但两种超网络扩展均出现模式崩溃：改变偏好权重几乎无法在预期的公平性-性能前沿上产生系统性移动。失败原因包括目标主导、条件机制中梯度传播微弱以及动态生成参数间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 公平目标可被纳入可解释的RL-MIL流程，但仅靠偏好条件化并不能保证可控的多目标行为；稳健的公平RL-MIL需要显式的梯度平衡、目标分离与稳定性分析机制。

Abstract: Predicting student performance from educational interaction data requires models that are both accurate and sufficiently transparent to support meaningful intervention, while demographic information introduces an additional risk of unfair predictions. This study investigates a multi-objective framework that combines reinforcement learning-based multiple instance learning (RL-MIL), adversarial debiasing, and preference-conditioned hypernetworks for student-at-risk prediction. MIL represents each student as a bag of weakly labeled interactions, while an RL agent selects informative instances for downstream classification. Two hypernetwork variants are evaluated to determine whether a user-defined preference scalar can continuously control the trade-off between predictive performance and Equalized Odds. The underlying RL-MIL baseline achieves strong classification performance, but both hypernetwork extensions exhibit mode collapse: changing the preference weight produces little systematic movement along the intended fairness-performance frontier. The failure is associated with objective dominance, weak gradient propagation through the conditioning mechanism, and interactions between dynamically generated parameters. The results show that fairness objectives can be incorporated into an interpretable RL-MIL pipeline, but preference conditioning alone does not guarantee controllable multi-objective behavior. Robust fair RL-MIL therefore requires explicit mechanisms for gradient balancing, objective separation, and stability analysis.

</details>


### [164] [Fast Polynomial Transcendentals for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00049)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试短多项式程序能否在LLM中加速SFU操作，在GB200上以BF16多项式替换sigmoid、tanh、SiLU，训练吞吐提升0.3%–8.0%，训练损失差异在−0.107至+0.079之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU代际间矩阵、特殊函数与内存管线扩展速率不同，导致瓶颈随硬件演进而迁移（如Blackwell上FlashAttention-4暴露的注意力内部失衡）。作者由此探究短多项式程序能否加速LLM中其他特殊函数单元（SFU）操作。

Method: 先在隔离的FP16扫描中对比原生PyTorch与打包融合乘加（FMA）程序，覆盖L2驻留与HBM驻留工作集；随后在四项GB200集成任务（稠密SiLU、tanh-softcapped注意力、sigmoid注意力、路由专家SwiGLU）中用3次或4次BF16多项式替换原生sigmoid、tanh、SiLU。程序结合解析对称性、目标格式舍入与消费内核内的打包算术。并用同检查点开放权重消融及每任务一次配对预训练对比评估模型行为。

Result: 隔离路径在L2提升1.19–2.19倍，在HBM提升1.00–1.70倍。稠密SiLU、tanh-softcapped注意力、路由专家替换分别使完整训练步吞吐提升2.7%、2.9%、8.0%；sigmoid注意力替换使完整注意力前向提升7.4%，完整GPU步提升0.3%。在约1000亿token的常见训练视界下，四任务的最终平滑训练损失差异（多项式减原生）为−0.107至+0.079。

Conclusion: 短BF16多项式程序可有效替代原生SFU运算，在保持训练损失基本不变的前提下带来实际训练吞吐收益，且收益幅度随任务与工作集所在层级而不同。

Abstract: Graphics processing unit (GPU) generations scale matrix, special-function, and memory pipelines at different rates, so kernel bottlenecks move as hardware evolves. FlashAttention-4 exposed this imbalance inside attention on NVIDIA Blackwell. We test whether short polynomial programs can accelerate other special-function-unit (SFU) operations in large language models (LLMs). We first compare native PyTorch evaluation with packed fused multiply--add (FMA) programs in an isolated IEEE binary16 (FP16) sweep spanning L2-resident and high-bandwidth-memory (HBM)-resident working sets. We then replace native sigmoid, tanh, and sigmoid linear unit (SiLU) with degree-3 or degree-4 bfloat16 (BF16) programs in four GB200 integration tasks: dense SiLU, tanh-softcapped attention, sigmoid attention, and routed-expert Swish-gated linear unit (SwiGLU). The programs combine analytical symmetry, target-format rounding, and packed arithmetic inside consuming kernels. The isolated paths improve by 1.19--2.19x in L2 and 1.00--1.70x in HBM. The dense-SiLU, tanh-softcapped-attention, and routed-expert substitutions improve complete training-step throughput by 2.7\%, 2.9\%, and 8.0\%, respectively. The sigmoid-attention substitution improves complete-attention forward by 7.4\% and the complete GPU step by 0.3\%. Same-checkpoint open-weight ablations and one paired pre-training comparison per task extend the evaluation to model behavior. At common horizons near 100 billion tokens, the final smoothed training-loss differences (polynomial minus native) range from $-0.107$ to $+0.079$ across the four tasks.

</details>


### [165] [SW-KAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Stieltjes-Wigert q-Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00050)
*Amirhosein Azarpour,Seyyed Moein Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SW-KAN，用定义在半无限域(0,∞)的Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式替代KAN中的固定激活，配合exp-of-tanh映射与三步递推，实现高效、可解释的函数逼近与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN用可学习的单变量边函数取代固定节点激活，兼具可解释性与参数效率。但已有多项式KAN变体存在一个被忽视的根本问题：无界实数输入与正交多项式基的有界或半无限支撑之间的"域不匹配"，限制了其表现。

Method: 采用定义于半无限域(0,∞)的Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式构建SW-KAN；引入平滑的tanh指数映射以稳定桥接域间隙并保持良好梯度条件；利用数值稳定的三项递推，在O(N)操作内完成多项式展开评估，无需特殊函数调用。

Result: 在图像分类与连续函数逼近任务上，SW-KAN取得更优的精度-效率权衡。其对数正态权重结构与可学习q参数带来独特归纳偏置，在特征维度降低、训练数据有限的资源受限条件下仍保持稳健表现。

Conclusion: SW-KAN在标准基准上超越现有多项式KAN基线，能以极少参数逼近复杂多变量函数，是资源受限场景下高效函数逼近与分类的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) represent a paradigmatic shift in deep learning by replacing fixed node activations with learnable univariate functions on edges, offering enhanced interpretability and parameter efficiency. While recent polynomial-based KAN variants have addressed the computational overhead of original B-spline implementations, they introduce a fundamental yet underexplored challenge: the domain mismatch between unbounded real-valued inputs and the bounded or semi-infinite support of orthogonal polynomial bases. To address this limitation, we propose the Stieltjes-Wigert Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (SW-KAN), a novel architecture that employs Stieltjes-Wigert q-orthogonal polynomials defined on the semi-infinite domain (0, infinity). We introduce a smooth exponential-of-tanh mapping that stably bridges the domain gap while preserving well-conditioned gradients, and leverage a numerically stable three-term recurrence that evaluates polynomial expansions in O(N) operations without special-function calls. Through comprehensive experiments spanning image classification and continuous function approximation, we demonstrate that SW-KAN achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across diverse tasks. The log-normal weight structure and learnable q-parameter of Stieltjes-Wigert polynomials provide a distinct inductive bias that enables robust performance under resource-constrained conditions, including reduced feature dimensionality and limited training data. The proposed architecture not only outperforms established polynomial KAN baselines on standard benchmarks but also exhibits strong representational capacity for approximating complex multivariate functions with remarkably few parameters, making it a compelling alternative for efficient function approximation and classification in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [166] [Format-Aware Fusion for Fast FP4 Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00053)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"格式感知融合"方法，通过协同设计量化生产者、缩放域与消费者布局，加速FP4张量核矩阵乘法，在Llama-3 8B预训练中实现高吞吐同时保持训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4张量核虽能加速矩阵乘法，但缩放计算、操作数打包、布局构建及反向状态保存会抵消收益，需要端到端协同设计以释放性能。

Method: 提出format-aware fusion，将每个量化生产者与其缩放域和消费者布局协同设计，支持native mxfp、global nvfp及CTA-local nvfp三种格式；采用行梯度随机舍入与固定符号32值Hadamard权重梯度预处理。

Result: 在Llama-3系列8B、160B token预训练中，bfloat16与TE nvfp分别达18.8K和27.6K tokens/s/GPU，最快自定义路径达37.9K；mxfp方案达37.2K（86.3% bfloat16 FLOP利用率），训练损失终点高2.11%；TE配方高0.87%但吞吐27.1K。

Conclusion: FP4训练结果依赖缩放契约、操作数与执行路径的联合设计；下游任务排名与训练损失排名不一致，说明需综合评估。

Abstract: Four-bit floating-point (FP4) Tensor Cores accelerate matrix multiplication, but scale computation, operand packing, layout construction, and saved backward state can erase the gain. We present \emph{format-aware fusion}, which co-designs each quantization producer with its scale domain and consumer layout for native \mxfp{}, global \nvfp{}, and cooperative-thread-array-local \nvfp{}. We evaluate Llama-3-family 8B pretraining through 160 billion tokens using bfloat16 output projections and compiled cross entropy. In matched same-accelerator probes, bfloat16 and Transformer Engine \nvfp{} reach 18.8K and 27.6K tokens/s/GPU, while our fastest custom route reaches 37.9K. \mxfp{} with row-gradient stochastic rounding and fixed-sign 32-value Hadamard weight-gradient preconditioning reaches 37.2K tokens/s/GPU (86.3\% bfloat16 model FLOP utilization) and ends 2.11\% above the raw bfloat16 training-loss endpoint. A Transformer Engine recipe with four final bfloat16 blocks ends 0.87\% above bfloat16 at 27.1K tokens/s/GPU. Downstream rankings differ from training-loss rankings, showing that FP4 outcomes depend jointly on scale contract, operand, and execution path.

</details>


### [167] ["very likely" Means "uncertain"? How LLMs Diverge from Humans in Linguistic Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00083)
*Jinhao Duan,Zicheng Liu,Zijie Liu,Kaidi Xu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM与人类在言语不确定性量化上的差异，并提出METHODNAME算法，从LLM输出中直接学习不确定性标记的最优不确定性映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数LLM不确定性量化依赖似然或一致性信号，而人类通过"可能""很可能"等言语标记表达不确定性，反映元认知监控。作者想探究LLM与人类在言语不确定性量化上的分歧，以及言语标记能否可靠量化LLM不确定性。

Method: 从心理学与决策科学文献中整理人类不确定性标记语料库，并以此对LLM进行基准测试；提出METHODNAME——一种基于优化的算法，直接从LLM输出中学习不确定性标记的最优不确定性画像，通过拟合标记-不确定性映射以最佳解释经验正确性。

Result: LLM编码言语不确定性时所对应的数值水平与人类存在显著差异；METHODNAME能够发现每个言语标记应传达多少概率质量，而非通过重复采样估计不确定性。

Conclusion: METHODNAME实现了人类与LLM在置信语义上的标记级直接比较，解耦了二者之间的不匹配，并揭示了言语表达中的系统性置信差异。

Abstract: Humans express uncertainty verbally via markers (e.g., "possible," "likely"), yet most LLM uncertainty quantification (UQ) relies on costing likelihood- or consistency-based signals. From a cognitive perspective, accurate verbal uncertainty reflects metacognitive monitoring, representing knowledge boundaries ("knowing that you don't know") to support regulation and information seeking. In this paper, we investigate how LLMs diverge from humans in verbal uncertainty quantification and whether verbal markers can reliably quantify LLM uncertainty. We curate a corpus of human uncertainty markers from psychology and decision-science literature and benchmark LLMs against it. We observe that LLMs encode verbal uncertainty with numerical levels that differ substantially from those of humans. We then introduce METHODNAME, a novel optimization-based algorithm that learns an optimal uncertainty profile over uncertainty markers directly from LLM outputs. By fitting a marker-uncertainty mapping to best explain empirical correctness, METHODNAME discovers how much probability mass each verbal marker should convey, rather than estimating uncertainty via repeated sampling. METHODNAME enables a direct, marker-level comparison of confidence semantics between humans and LLMs, disentangling mismatch and revealing systematic confidence disparities in verbal expressions.

</details>


### [168] [Nous: Learning and Certifying Memory Decisions Before Source Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00094)
*Pranav Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分离记忆系统中的学习、校准与修订认证，证明了在隐马尔可夫模型下，学习并认证有用决策所需的样本量可远少于源校准，并提出了无需纯类锚点的有限样本证书及稳健性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于信念的智能体记忆需要可靠的状态决策，但其证据可能噪声、复制或过时。核心疑问是：记忆是否必须先校准来源可靠性，才能改进决策？论文旨在厘清学习、校准与修订认证之间的统计关系。

Method: 在一个四模型隐马尔可夫族上分析学习未知贝叶斯决策、认证改进与固定精度源估计的样本复杂度；在未知身份加背景报告信道下刻画策略改进的锐利可识别区间，利用可观测见证区域构造有限样本证书；扩展至有界历史相关误设定与条件复制，并用分裂训练见证处理任意历史空间。同时将策略界收据与Nous Dimensions集成，在45,000条留存可变状态历史和三个外部MiniGrid记忆环境中测试。

Result: 学习贝叶斯决策需Theta(l^-2)记录，认证其相对信息性 incumbent 的改进需O(l^-2)新记录，而固定精度源估计需Theta(l^-4)；即学习与认证可比源校准少用平方级记录。新证书在MiniGrid中接受9/9超过常数基线和4/9超过last-write-wins的改进，而旧证书为零；强基线方法仍有竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: 论文提供了关于记忆决策何时可在不恢复源可靠性的情况下被学习与论证的统计解释，而非一个普遍更优的记忆算法。

Abstract: Belief-based agent memory needs reliable decisions about current state, yet its evidence may be noisy, copied, or stale. Must a memory calibrate its sources before it can improve its decisions? We separate learning, calibration, and revision certification. On one four-model hidden Markov family, learning an unknown Bayes decision requires Theta(l^-2) records and certifying its improvement over an informative incumbent takes O(l^-2) fresh records from the same observation law, while fixed-precision source estimation requires Theta(l^-4) as persistence l vanishes. Thus learning and certifying useful decisions can require quadratically fewer records than source calibration. A broader model class retains the decision rate and source lower bound. Under an unknown identity-plus-background report channel, we characterize the sharp identified interval for policy improvement and derive a finite-sample certificate using observable witness regions, without pure-class anchors. A robustness extension tolerates bounded history-dependent misspecification and conditional copying; split-trained witnesses apply to arbitrary history spaces with explicit power conditions. We integrate policy-bound receipts with Nous Dimensions and test 45,000 held-out mutable-state histories and 9,000 episodes in three external MiniGrid memory environments with an introduced noisy-report interface. The new certificate accepts 9/9 improvements over a constant incumbent and 4/9 over last-write-wins, versus none for the earlier certificate in MiniGrid. Strong established inference baselines remain competitive or better. The result is a statistical account of when memory decisions can be learned and justified without recovering source reliability, not a universally superior memory algorithm.

</details>


### [169] [One Mastery Threshold Does Not Fit All Knowledge Tracing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00095)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 掌握阈值与知识追踪(KT)模型耦合，同一数值阈值在不同模型/教学取向下会带来差异巨大的决策效果，需重新校准并多维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅导系统用掌握阈值决定学生何时停止练习并进阶，但阈值的效果依赖底层KT模型与教学优先级，现有做法可能直接沿用固定阈值，忽略模型输出含义与公平性影响。

Method: 在四个公开教育数据集上比较六种KT模型，评估0.50–0.99之间12个阈值，指标包括进阶后表现、进阶覆盖率、练习负担及不同先前表现群体间的差异，并在30种预设教学情境下识别平衡阈值。

Result: BKT对阈值变化相对不敏感，神经模型随阈值升高明显更具选择性，部分原因是BKT估计潜在掌握概率而神经模型估计下一次答对概率；半数情境中两类模型所选阈值相差超过0.10；更严阈值不能可靠缩小表现差距，反而可能使强先验学生进阶概率最高达弱先验学生的3.26倍。

Conclusion: 掌握阈值应随KT模型或教学优先级变化而重新校准，并依据其对表现、练习、进阶与机会公平的综合影响来评估，而非沿用统一固定阈值。

Abstract: Tutoring systems use mastery thresholds to decide when students can stop practicing and advance, but the same numerical threshold can lead to very different decisions when the underlying knowledge tracing (KT) model changes. We examine six KT models across four public educational datasets and evaluate 12 thresholds from 0.50 to 0.99 using post-advancement performance, advancement coverage, practice burden, and disparities across prior-performance groups. We also identify thresholds that balance performance, extra practice, and advancement under 30 predefined instructional settings. Bayesian Knowledge Tracing (BKT) is relatively insensitive to threshold changes, while neural models become much more selective as thresholds increase. This partly reflects different model outputs: BKT estimates latent mastery probability, whereas neural models estimate the probability of a correct next response, so the same cutoff does not represent the same level of mastery. The best-balanced threshold varied substantially across models and settings. In half of the tested settings, neural models and BKT differed by more than 0.10 in their selected thresholds, although this gap became smaller when greater priority was placed on reducing extra practice and allowing more students to advance. Stricter thresholds also did not reliably reduce performance gaps and could disproportionately restrict advancement, with stronger-prior students advancing up to 3.26 times as often as weaker-prior students. These results show that mastery thresholds should be recalibrated when the KT model or instructional priorities change and evaluated by their effects on performance, practice, advancement, and access.

</details>


### [170] [Uncertainty-Aware Learning from Multi-Expert Interval Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00102)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出保留个体专家区间的方法，将标签不确定性的两个来源（标签内不精确性与专家间变异）分离，并通过对齐预测不确定性组分验证其与标签侧来源的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习应用依赖专家标签，专家对同一观测可能给出不同但合理的解释，这种差异可能反映真实分歧或模糊性而非标注错误。当专家以区间而非精确值标注时，监督信号包含两种不同的标签不确定性：标签内不精确性和专家间变异。现有方法将两者分开处理：多专家方法将标签坍缩为共识，区间目标方法通常只给出单一预测，而预测不确定性方法很少用观测到的专家分歧来验证其不确定性估计。

Method: 1）将异质标签词表统一到共同的概率标签空间，分离编码差异与专家判断；2）保留个体标签区间，用Beta混合分布建模，并以proper Cramér距离目标训练，以保留不同的专家报告标签；3）将预测不确定性分解为组分内、组分间和模型不确定性，并评估其是否分别对应标签内不确定性、标签间不确定性和模型误差；由于此对应关系不保证成立，引入分解匹配（decomposition matching）将预测组分对齐到预期的标签侧来源。

Result: 在海冰浓度（sea-ice concentration）任务上，该模型相比硬标签将MAE降低31%，并优于聚合、区间分布和区间回归基线。

Conclusion: 通过保留个体专家区间并分离标签内不精确性与专家间变异，并引入分解匹配来验证预测不确定性组分与标签侧来源的对应关系，该方法能更真实地反映专家分歧，并在海冰浓度任务上显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Many machine learning (ML) applications rely on expert labels, and qualified experts may provide different but plausible interpretations of the same observation. Such variation across expert labels may reflect genuine disagreement or ambiguity rather than annotation error. When individual experts additionally report intervals rather than exact values, the supervision contains two distinct sources of label uncertainty: within-label imprecision and between-expert variation. Existing methods treat these forms separately: multi-expert approaches collapse labels to a consensus, interval-target methods often yield a single prediction, and predictive-uncertainty methods rarely validate their uncertainty estimates against observed expert disagreement. To address this problem, we propose an approach that preserves individual expert intervals, separates within-label imprecision from between-expert variation, and validates the corresponding predictive uncertainty components. First, heterogeneous label vocabularies are harmonized into a common probabilistic label space, separating encoding differences from expert judgement. Second, individual label intervals are retained and modeled with a mixture of Beta distributions trained using a proper Cramér-distance objective, preserving distinct expert-reported labels. Third, we decompose predictive uncertainty into within-component, between-component, and model uncertainty, and evaluate whether these components correspond to within-label uncertainty, between-label uncertainty, and model error, respectively. Because this correspondence is not guaranteed, we introduce decomposition matching, which aligns the predictive components to their intended label-side sources. On sea-ice concentration the model reduces MAE by 31\% over hard labels and outperforms aggregation, interval-distribution, and interval-regression baselines.

</details>


### [171] [The Hidden Costs of 99% Accuracy: A Trustworthiness Audit of the Telco Customer Churn Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00118)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文审计了IBM Telco客户流失基准的四个可信性缺陷（预划分SMOTE泄漏、TotalCharges冗余、校准失配、阈值不当），并提出四要素报告清单与可复现实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该基准上普遍报告95%以上准确率（最高达99.01%），但以准确率和F1为核心的报告方式掩盖了若干可信性问题，作者希望揭示这些对文献和实际决策有害的失效模式。

Method: 对n=7,043的IBM Telco基准进行审计：十个分类器、十五个随机种子下比较预划分SMOTE与类加权流程；测量合成样本的最近邻插值比例；用R2诊断TotalCharges冗余并对比TreeSHAP排名；进行15种子校准审计（保序回归 vs 温度缩放）；计算成本最优阈值（50美元挽留优惠、24个月CLV代理）；并在Iranian Telecom与Bank Customer Churn上复现F1、F2。

Result: 预划分SMOTE使流失类F1虚高13.1个百分点（每个分类器Wilcoxon p<10^-4），类加权流程无此膨胀，约36%合成训练点接近测试实例的最近邻插值；TotalCharges近乎由tenure×MonthlyCharges决定（R2=0.999），移除后准确率变化不足0.2个百分点却仍居TreeSHAP第九位；保序回归为最强默认校准器，温度缩放在双峰概率分布下失效；成本最优阈值比F1最优阈值低约5-10倍，每千客户约节省77,000美元；F1可跨域泛化，F2仅在电信域内泛化。

Conclusion: 基准上的高准确率并不等同可信模型；作者提出包含流程披露、冗余诊断、校准审计与成本敏感阈值的四要素报告清单，并发布可复现实现。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (n = 7,043) routinely reports test accuracies above 95%, with the most cited published study reporting 99.01%. We audit this benchmark for four trustworthiness failures invisible to the accuracy- and F1-centred reporting that dominates the literature. First, pre-split SMOTE inflates churn-class F1 by 13.1 percentage points across ten classifiers and fifteen seeds (Wilcoxon p < 10^-4 per classifier); the same leaky pipeline ordering paired with class weighting yields no inflation, isolating the effect to SMOTE's geometric construction. We measure the mechanism directly: approximately 36% of synthetic training points are nearest-neighbour interpolations of test-set instances. Second, the TotalCharges field is approximately determined by tenure multiplied by MonthlyCharges (R2 = 0.999); removing it changes accuracy by less than 0.2 percentage points, yet TreeSHAP ranks it ninth in mean absolute attribution - a pattern that materially corrupts SHAP-based interpretation. We propose an R2 > 0.95 pre-modelling diagnostic. Third, in a 15-seed calibration audit, isotonic regression is the strongest default; temperature scaling fails on class-weighted tree ensembles whose predicted-probability distribution is bimodal.
