Fourth, the cost-optimal decision threshold (under a 50 USD retention offer and 24-month CLV proxy) is approximately 5-10 times lower than the F1-optimal threshold, saving approximately 77,000 USD per 1,000 customers. We replicate F1 and F2 on Iranian Telecom Churn (within domain) and Bank Customer Churn (across domain): F1 generalises; F2 generalises only within telecom. We synthesise these findings into a four-component reporting checklist - pipeline disclosure, redundancy diagnostic, calibration audit, and cost-sensitive thresholds - and release a reproducible implementation.

</details>


### [172] [ibUMAP: Coherent and Scalable Field Evaluation for UMAP Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01445)
*Bin Chen,Yumeng Xue,Patrick Paetzold,Yunhai Wang,Oliver Deussen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ibUMAP，一种基于相干场的 UMAP 替代方案，通过共享嵌入快照同步施加吸引与排斥力，实现更快且更稳定的布局优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 UMAP 依赖随机负采样来实现可扩展的布局优化，但这种随机性会导致多次运行及下游复用中的嵌入不稳定，因为估计的排斥力依赖于采样事件顺序。

Method: ibUMAP 从共享嵌入快照出发同步评估吸引与排斥力；其度加权排斥场由固定嵌入下负采样的条件期望启发，用三个标量矩表示，并借助基于插值的 FFT 方案在 CPU 和 GPU 上高效评估，从而避免显式全对计算。

Result: 同步性与核截断改变了局部-全局保真度权衡，FFT 评估仅带来较小的最终质量平均变化。端到端基准测试显示：CPU 上相较 umap-learn 中位加速 3.29 倍（无种子）和 5.79 倍（有种子），GPU 无种子执行下相较 cuML 在百万级数据集上加速 1.44 倍，并伴随更高的运行间稳定性和可测量的保真度权衡。

Conclusion: ibUMAP 通过场式同步优化在提升速度与运行稳定性的同时，引入了与在线 UMAP 不同的优化动力学和可测量的保真度权衡。

Abstract: UMAP achieves scalable layout optimization through stochastic negative sampling. However, this stochasticity can lead to unstable embeddings across reruns and downstream reuse, as the estimated repulsive forces depend on the ordering of sampling events. We present ibUMAP, a coherent field-based alternative that evaluates attraction and repulsion from a shared embedding snapshot and applies them synchronously. Its degree-weighted repulsive field is motivated by the conditional expectation of negative sampling for a fixed embedding and represented by three scalar moments, which are evaluated efficiently on CPUs and GPUs using an interpolation-based FFT scheme. This formulation avoids explicit all-pairs computations while inducing optimization dynamics that differ from those of standard online UMAP. Controlled experiments show that synchrony and kernel capping alter the local-global fidelity trade-off, whereas FFT evaluation produces small average changes in final quality. End-to-end benchmarks show median speedups of 3.29x unseeded and 5.79x seeded over umap-learn on CPU, and 1.44x over cuML on million-scale datasets under unseeded GPU execution. These gains accompany greater run-to-run stability and measurable fidelity trade-offs.

</details>


### [173] [Generalized Biomedicine Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00120)
*Luyao Tang,Yingkai Yang,Hanqi Chen,Jiewei Zheng,Chaoqi Chen,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出广义生物医学发现（GBD）任务及统一基准，涵盖长尾、异常与层级分类发现，并提出认知启发的SCAN方法以提升新颖概念发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床医学图像存在开放世界偏移：长尾罕见病、被正常解剖结构主导的细微病灶以及层级分类体系，而现有多数开放世界范式假设标签空间平坦且平衡，无法满足这些生物医学需求。

Method: 提出SCAN（Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN），遵循认知启发的感知进程：先用预测抑制过滤预期常态，再通过惊讶诱发显著性突出意外偏差，最后进行互补性适应，将这些偏移整合进全局表示。

Result: 大量实验表明SCAN提升了新颖概念发现能力，同时总体保留了已有临床知识，并可嵌入现有架构以更好权衡医学影像中的已知-未知取舍。

Conclusion: SCAN为医学影像中的开放世界发现提供了有效且可插拔的方案，兼顾新概念发现与既有知识保持。

Abstract: In real-world clinical practice, medical images face open-world shifts: (i) long-tailed rare diseases, (ii) subtle lesions dominated by normal anatomy, and (iii) hierarchical taxonomies. Yet most open-world paradigms assume flat, balanced label spaces, leaving these biomedical demands unresolved. We introduce Generalized Biomedicine Discovery (GBD) and a unified benchmark spanning long-tail, anomaly, and taxonomy-aware discovery. Our key insight is that dominant known patterns form a visual manifold that masks subtle novelty. Inspired by expert diagnosis, we propose SCAN (Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN), which follows a cognition-inspired perceptual progression: it applies predictive suppression to filter expected norms, triggers surprise-evoked salience to highlight unexpected deviations, and performs complementary accommodation to integrate these shifts into global representations. Extensive experiments show that SCAN improves novel concept discovery while generally preserving established clinical knowledge, and it plugs into existing architectures to better navigate the known-unknown trade-off in medical imaging. Code is available at https://github.com/lytang63/generalized-biomedicine-discovery.

</details>


### [174] [Classification Based on Association Rules Algorithm for Breast Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00174)
*Ali Alsalama,Ahmed Kubba,Ghaith Jamjoum,Zaher Al Aghbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于加权分类的关联规则数据挖掘技术，用于乳腺癌分类，包含规则生成、规则剪枝和规则预测三个核心算法，并在测试样本上验证了其可行性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌是全球女性死亡率的重要来源，发病率在各类癌症中位居前列，因此早期检测需求迫切。关联规则分类因其结果易于被医疗专业人员理解，逐渐成为受青睐的方法。

Method: 采用基于加权分类的关联规则挖掘技术，包含三个核心算法：规则生成（识别频繁项集并创建关联规则）、规则剪枝（按特定标准剔除规则，并依据其对训练数据的影响分为主要组和次要组）、规则预测（应用剪枝后的规则对测试数据进行分类）。

Result: 最终预测算法在若干测试样本上进行了测试，展示了该方法的可行性和性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能够为乳腺癌分类提供一种结果易理解、可行的关联规则挖掘方案，具备一定的分类性能。

Abstract: Breast cancer is a significant contributor to female mortality across the world, displaying one of the highest oc currence rates among the various cancer types. In response to the need for early breast cancer detection, researchers have increasingly turned to association rule-based classification as a favored method. Association Rule mining is a data mining approach which offers the benefit of yielding results that are readily understandable for medical professionals. This paper introduces a novel association rule-based data mining technique for breast cancer classification based on a weighted classification approach. This implementation employs three core algorithms: Rule Generation, Rule Pruning, and Rule Prediction. Rule Generation identifies frequent itemsets and creates association rules. Rule Pruning eliminates rules using specific criteria and separates them into major and minor groups based on their influence on training data. Rule Prediction applies the pruned rules to classify test data. The final prediction algorithm was tested on several testing samples to show the feasibility and performance of the approach.

</details>


### [175] [Four Ways to Grow a Classifier and Why One of Them Cannot Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00180)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一协议下衡量构造式分类器四种"生长决策"的实际增益，并对其中唯一无增益的一种（软决策树加层）给出精确诊断与修复：新门梯度恒为零，需随机扰动子节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造式分类器在训练中不断加结构（树加层、加隐藏单元、叶子分裂），但缺乏统一下各生长决策"究竟买到什么"的清晰衡量，尤其是看似最自然的软决策树加层方式可能存在隐患。

Method: 在树结构与构造式模型中用同一固定协议比较四种生长决策。理论分析最自然的加层方式：将每个叶子变成门，两个子节点继承父节点类分布，使函数不变。通过梯度分析证明新门梯度恒为零、门在1/2时两子节点梯度相同；并与Net2Net的噪声破对称、分裂最速下降的二阶信息逃逸鞍点作对比。修复方法为对子节点施加小随机扰动。另三项决策（隐藏单元拟合残差、按最大期望误差分裂叶子、显著性地限制表达性分裂）分别进行评估。

Result: 加层构造在三种子五折交叉验证下相对同深度从头训练损失19.6（Iris）、19.1（Wine）、55.6（Digits）个准确率点；随机扰动修复有效且扰动大小几乎无关。隐藏单元拟合残差在全部数据集上得到更小网络但非更准，且在Digits上显著掉点。最大期望误差分裂带来稀疏性：0.885准确率仅用3.7次分裂，而完整深度六树用63次，但在更难问题上损失4.3点。显著性门槛无增益：树更大且更不准。

Conclusion: 四种生长决策中三项各买到一样东西（更小网络、稀疏性），而显著性门槛与"新门继承父分布"的加层方式无增益，后者因梯度恒为零而必然学不到东西。实践规则：新增参数后应断言其梯度非零。论文所有数字均由测量脚本插入。

Abstract: Constructive classifiers add structure while they train: a level to a tree, a unit to a hidden layer, a split at a leaf. This paper asks what each of four such growth decisions actually buys, measured under one fixed protocol in tree-structured and constructive models, and gives an exact diagnosis and a fix for the one that buys nothing.
  The diagnosis concerns the most natural way to deepen a soft decision tree: turn every leaf into a gate whose two children inherit the parent's class distribution, so that the function is unchanged. I prove that this leaves the gradient of every new gate identically zero and, with the gate at 1/2, gives the two children identical gradients, so the added level can never learn. Unlike the symmetry that Net2Net breaks with noise or the saddle point that splitting steepest descent escapes with second-order information, first-order information here is not weak but absent. Over three seeds of five-fold cross-validation the construction loses 19.6 accuracy points on Iris, 19.1 on Wine and 55.6 on Digits against the same depth trained from scratch. The fix is a small random perturbation of the children, whose size barely matters. The practical rule is one line in a test: after adding parameters, assert that their gradient is nonzero.
  The other three decisions each buy one thing. Fitting a new hidden unit to the residual error before installing it buys a smaller network on every dataset, though not a more accurate one, and on Digits it costs accuracy significantly. Splitting the leaf with the largest expected error buys sparsity, reaching 0.885 with 3.7 splits where a complete depth-six tree uses 63, but loses 4.3 points on a harder problem. Requiring statistical significance before a node receives a more expressive split buys nothing: the tree gets larger and less accurate.
  Every number in the paper is inserted from the measurement script.

</details>


### [176] [Uncertainty-Aware RL-Controlled Adaptive 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00188)
*Alpay Ozkan,Tunc Ozan Aydin,Marc Pollefeys,Jelena Trisovic,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于语义熵、曲率与纹理的自适应体素细分框架，并用强化学习在用户指定内存预算下学习细分策略，实现可控的精度-内存权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定分辨率体素建图效率低：均匀区域浪费内存，复杂区域丢失细节；现有自适应方法如MAP-ADAPT依赖几何与人工语义类别列表的启发式，需专家调参、泛化差，且无法显式控制内存使用。

Method: 以语义熵（标签不确定性）作为核心线索，结合几何曲率与纹理丰富度衡量场景复杂度，进行原则性的分辨率分配，无需依赖语义分类体系；并引入强化学习智能体，在用户指定的目标内存预算下学习体素细分策略，用单一直观控制参数替代手工阈值，生成多分辨率TSDF。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，相比MAP-ADAPT和固定分辨率基线，获得更高的几何精度、更好的语义一致性以及更优的内存-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架以语义熵与几何纹理线索实现无需语义分类体系的自适应细分，并通过强化学习将精度-内存权衡显式化、用户可控，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Voxel-based volumetric mapping is fundamental to 3D reconstruction, yet fixed-resolution grids remain inherently inefficient - wasting memory in uniform regions and losing detail in complex ones. Existing adaptive methods, such as MAP-ADAPT, partially address this by varying resolution based on geometry and user-defined semantic class lists, but these heuristics require expert tuning, lack generalization to unseen objects, and provide no explicit mechanism to control memory usage. We propose an adaptive framework that refines voxels based on semantic entropy, which captures label uncertainty, together with geometric curvature and texture richness as scene complexity cues, yielding principled resolution allocation without reliance on semantic taxonomies. To make the accuracy-memory trade-off explicit and user-controlled, we further introduce a reinforcement learning agent that learns voxel subdivision policies under a user-specified target memory budget, replacing hand-tuned thresholds with a single intuitive control parameter. The resulting multi-resolution TSDF achieves higher geometric accuracy, better semantic consistency, and improved memory-accuracy trade-offs compared to MAP-ADAPT and fixed-resolution baselines on both synthetic and real-world datasets. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/UnRL.

</details>


### [177] [Energy Time-Series Imputation with Differentially Private Diffusion Models via Clipping-Aware Objective Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00209)
*Huizhen Huang,Yu Li,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对差分隐私差分模型在能源时序插补中的目标-裁剪交互问题，提出时间步感知的目标条件化方法（v-预测+动态权重），提升插补效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源时序数据细粒度测量含敏感时间信息，需在隐私保护下可靠恢复缺失值。DP-SGD扩散模型是可行框架，但在余弦扩散调度下，晚期时间步处于低信噪比状态，标准ε-预测会产生较大的裁剪前梯度，易被裁剪从而损失优化信号。

Method: 将目标-裁剪交互建模为固定阈值DP-SGD下的目标优化问题，提出时间步感知的目标条件化：采用v-预测缓解晚期时间步梯度放大，使用静态损失加权作为均匀缩放对照，并引入扩散调度感知的动态加权以在裁剪前实现更强衰减。

Result: 在五个真实能源时序数据集上，涵盖随机点缺失、连续块缺失、持续中断及多种缺失严重度。在匹配的DP-SGD设置下，所提方法一致优于ε-预测基线。梯度诊断显示裁剪前梯度范数上尾更低、裁剪比例下降、晚期低信噪比时间步衰减更强。

Conclusion: 裁剪感知的目标条件化对能源时序插补有效，可在差分隐私约束下改善插补效用并降低梯度裁剪影响。

Abstract: Reliable recovery of missing measurements is important for monitoring and analysis in energy time-series systems, where fine-grained measurements may contain sensitive temporal information. Diffusion models trained with differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) provide a promising framework for privacy-sensitive energy time-series imputation. Under cosine diffusion schedules, late timesteps correspond to low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions, where standard $\varepsilon$-prediction can induce large pre-clipping gradients. Such gradients are more likely to be clipped, reducing the retained optimization signal. The artificial intelligence (AI) contribution lies in formulating this objective--clipping interaction as an objective optimization problem under fixed-threshold DP-SGD and developing timestep-aware objective conditioning for diffusion-based energy time-series imputation. The method adopts $v$-prediction to mitigate late-timestep gradient amplification, uses static loss weighting as a uniform-scaling control, and introduces diffusion-schedule-aware dynamic weighting for stronger attenuation before clipping. For the engineering application, we evaluate the method on five real-world energy time-series datasets across random point missingness, contiguous block missingness, persistent outages, and multiple missing-data severities. Under matched DP-SGD settings, the proposed method consistently improves imputation utility over the $\varepsilon$-prediction baseline. Gradient diagnostics reveal lower upper-tail pre-clipping gradient norms, reduced clipping fractions, and stronger attenuation at late low-SNR timesteps, supporting the effectiveness of clipping-aware objective conditioning for energy time-series imputation.

</details>


### [178] [Useful to Whom? Sample Value Is Defined Only Relative to the Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00221)
*Yangze Liu,Xiao-Long Yin,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究coreset selection中"易样本优先"与"几何覆盖"准则的交叉边界是否依赖于目标学习器，通过冻结子集、仅改变训练学习器的受控实验证明了该边界随学习器结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究认为易样本优先准则与几何覆盖准则在不同预算区间各自占优，且由一个交叉边界分隔。但该边界究竟由数据固定，还是随目标学习器变化尚不清楚，这关系到选择策略的评估方式。

Method: 采用受控实验：固定所选子集不变，仅操纵训练学习器。在低分辨率ImageNet-100上改变ResNet-18宽度，进行输入网格与容量的扫描，并通过stride控制在不改变输入网格的情况下复现和反转网格效应；同时在原生224px ImageNet-1k协议下比较宽度效应，并将卷积学习系统替换为ViT。

Result: 在ImageNet-100上，ResNet-18宽度翻倍使交叉点从每类57个样本移至85个；增大输入网格在保持图像信息不变时使边界左移，且该位移随宽度增加而减弱；仅去除最后一个下采样stride即可恢复左移。ImageNet-1k上宽度效应较小且依赖探针（LFrac近乎平坦，EL2N略微右移）。替换为ViT后，覆盖准则在整个测量范围内均占优。

Conclusion: 研究通过冻结子集干预确立了选择边界的学习器依赖性，并指出可移动该边界的网络结构因素，但未给出普适的缩放定律。实践上，选择策略的偏好预算区间必须针对目标学习器进行评估。

Abstract: What kind of data does a model need in order to learn? Coreset selection makes this question concrete: under a budget, keep the samples most useful for training. Easy-first and geometric coverage criteria can win in different budget regimes, separated by a crossover boundary. We ask whether this boundary is fixed by the data or changes with the target learner. Controlled experiments freeze the selected subsets and manipulate only the training learner. On low-resolution ImageNet-100, doubling ResNet-18's width moves the crossover from 57 to 85 samples per class: the learner changes the relative value of the same samples. A wider sweep reveals an interaction between input grid and capacity. Enlarging the grid while retaining the same image information shifts the boundary left, and this shift weakens as width increases. Stride controls reproduce and reverse the grid effect without changing the input grid; removing only the last downsampling stride is sufficient to recover the leftward shift. Under the native-224px ImageNet-1k protocol, width effects are smaller and depend on the probe: LFrac remains nearly flat, while EL2N shifts modestly right. Swapping the convolutional learning system for a ViT makes coverage win throughout the measured range, even when the easy subsets come from the convolutional proxy. These results establish learner dependence through frozen-subset interventions and identify network structure that can move the boundary. They do not yield a universal scaling law. Their practical implication is direct: a selection strategy's preferred budget regime must be evaluated with respect to the target learner.

</details>


### [179] [Constant-Memory Recall: Learned Associations in a Fixed Matrix State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00232)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 固定大小循环记忆的DeltaNet在训练后能近完美召回32个键值对，但参数相当的基线与Transformer仍接近随机，导致效率比较无法完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定大小循环记忆限制了推理时的存储增长，但成功召回依赖具体任务与训练设置，因此需要弄清这种记忆机制何时能可靠工作。

Method: 研究一个小型DeltaNet变体，配备固定的token特定key偏置，训练其在每条序列中记住32个新的键值对；使用32 KiB的循环矩阵状态，并在三个训练种子上测试，同时观察填充内容延长查询前上下文至1,798 token时的表现，并做置零首个记忆块的消融。

Result: 三个种子下均值准确率达99.95%（在序列值中选择）；填充将上下文延长至1,798 token时召回仍近完美；置零首个记忆块会消除该召回；48对探索性测试在四分之一主训练预算后仍接近随机；参数匹配的向量与Transformer基线也接近随机，即使Transformer经过额外训练搜索。

Conclusion: 该DeltaNet变体可在特定训练任务下实现近乎完美的键值召回且抗填充干扰，首个记忆块对召回必要；但基线失败使记忆效率比较无法进行，48对测试也未定位容量上限。

Abstract: Fixed-size recurrent memory limits storage growth during inference, but successful recall depends on the task and training. We study a small DeltaNet variant with fixed token-specific key biases, trained to remember 32 new key-value pairings per sequence. With 32 KiB of recurrent matrix state, it achieves 99.95% mean accuracy across three training seeds when choosing among the sequence's values. Recall remains near perfect when filler extends the pre-query context to 1,798 tokens without adding pairings. Zeroing the first memory block removes this recall. An exploratory 48-pair test remains near chance after one quarter of the primary training budget and does not locate a capacity limit. Parameter-matched vector and Transformer baselines remain near chance, including the Transformer after additional training searches. This unresolved baseline failure prevents a memory-efficiency comparison.

</details>


### [180] [How Many Categories Are Enough? Distribution-Free Certification Limits for Few-Shot Anomaly Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00236)
*Gia Huy Thai,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究少样本异常检测中为未见类别认证报警阈值所需的正常样本量问题，发现仅用目标图像校准受分辨率限制且对分布偏移脆弱，并推导出类别级别的可行性下界，提出CRESS协议区分源证据能否支持可迁移的可靠性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本异常检测常用排序指标评判，但实际部署需要一个受控虚警率（FAR）的报警阈值；核心问题是：要为一个未见类别认证这样的阈值，究竟需要多少正常证据（图像级或类别级）。

Method: 在15个MVTec和12个VisA类别、四类损坏下，使用冻结的DINOv2 PCA残差排序器；分析target-only的留一图像（LOIO）校准；推导类别计数的可行性演算（distribution-free 95% UCB所需的最小类别抽样数）；提出CRESS协议，将源类别划分为参考、提议与认证三类角色。

Result: LOIO校准时rank值无法低于1/(k+1)，在α=0.20、k=4时高斯损坏MVTec上经验FAR达0.341（名义值的1.7倍）；任何确定性、均匀有效、无分布假设的95% UCB在α=0.20、0.10、0.05时至少需14、29、59个iid类别抽样（必要非充分）；CRESS在仅3-4个认证类别下，960个冻结配置全部返回fail-closed阈值τ*=0，最小类别级UCB为0.950；图像级分析在36.7%-60.3%的目标单元中选出正阈值。

Conclusion: 论文给出了量化的可行性边界和估计目标感知（estimand-aware）的协议，明确指出源证据何时能够、何时不能支持可迁移的可靠性声明；图像级界限仅适用于选定源混合，不适用于新类别抽取的边缘风险。

Abstract: Few-shot anomaly detectors are judged by ranking metrics, yet deployment requires an alarm threshold with a controlled false-alarm rate (FAR). We ask how much normal evidence, in images or category units, is needed to certify such a threshold for an unseen category. Using a frozen DINOv2 principal component analysis (PCA) residual ranker on 15 MVTec and 12 VisA categories under four corruption types, we show that target-only leave-one-image-out (LOIO) calibration is resolution-limited and shift-fragile: rank values cannot fall below $1/(k+1)$, and at the attainable level $α=0.20$, empirical FAR reaches 0.341 on Gaussian-corrupted MVTec at $k=4$, 1.7 times the nominal level. A category-count feasibility calculus is then derived: even with all-zero category losses and no multiplicity charged, any deterministic, uniformly valid, distribution-free 95% upper confidence bound (UCB) requires at least 14, 29, and 59 independent and identically distributed (iid) category draws at $α=0.20$, $0.10$, and $0.05$; these counts are necessary but not sufficient. The Cross-category Reliability Estimation with Source Support (CRESS) protocol splits source categories into disjoint reference, proposal, and certification roles. With only three or four certification categories, all 960 frozen configurations return the fail-closed threshold $τ^\star=0$, and the smallest category-level UCB is 0.950. Image-unit analyses of the same archive select positive thresholds in 36.7% to 60.3% of target cells; these bounds hold for the selected source mixture, not for the marginal risk of a new-category draw. The contribution is a quantitative feasibility boundary and an estimand-aware protocol specifying when source evidence can, and cannot, support a transferable reliability claim.

</details>


### [181] [Contingent Exposure Routing for Financial AI: Outage Risk and the Cost of Indivisible Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00239)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把故障转移下的路由建模为局部市场冲击响应矩阵问题，研究期望平方价格位移，给出对称构造、不可分决策的精确二阶矩、条件期望取整与交换算法的理论分析，并在合成网络与API回放上验证风险降低效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型故障转移虽能恢复可用性，却改变了哪些金融机构共享决策误差，因此需要研究故障情境下的路由分配及其风险后果，而现有工作缺少对这类集中度与分散化权衡的可审计分析框架。

Method: 构建 outage-contingent routing 的 local market-impact response matrix，分析 expected squared price displacement；用对称构造刻画共享备份导致的常数级集中度下限；对不可分决策推导独立随机路由的精确二阶矩与有效暴露粒度；提出 conditional-expectation rounding 的有限智能体界与保持端点计数的 swap 过程，并用对偶下界评估；在60个合成组合网络、11340次情景评估及1024条API响应回放上测试。

Result: swap 过程在中心反馈设定、独立误差下，对单端点和双端点移除分别降低风险6.57%和10.53%；API回放任务上的留出降低为3.30%（配对bootstrap区间2.07–4.57%）。强相关误差、劣质端点与不可分性会限制分散化收益。

Conclusion: 本文提供的是可审计的路由压力测试与实现分析，而非对真实市场崩盘概率的估计；共享备份存在集中度下限，均衡回退风险随存活端点数量反比下降。

Abstract: Model failover restores availability, but changes which financial institutions share decision errors. We formulate outage-contingent routing through a local market-impact response matrix and study expected squared price displacement. A symmetric construction shows that a shared backup can leave an order-one concentration floor as the number of primary endpoints grows, while balanced fallback risk decreases inversely with the surviving endpoint count. For indivisible decisions, we derive the exact second moment of independent randomized routing and an effective-exposure granularity that determines its gap from fractional allocation. Conditional-expectation rounding gives a finite-agent bound without coupled quotas; a separate swap procedure preserves endpoint counts and is assessed against dual lower bounds. Across 60 synthetic portfolio networks and 11,340 scenario evaluations, the latter reduces risk by 6.57% and 10.53% for single and double endpoint removals at the central feedback setting with independent errors. A replay of 1,024 recorded API responses on constructed rebalancing tasks gives a smaller held-out reduction of 3.30% (paired bootstrap interval 2.07--4.57%). Strongly aligned errors, inferior endpoints, and indivisibility limit diversification. The contribution is an auditable routing stress test and implementation analysis, not an estimate of real-market crash probabilities.

</details>


### [182] [The Null Is the Hard Part: Exact Tests for Memorization in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00251)
*Sushovan Majhi,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出生成模型记忆化审计通常直接将相似度分数与阈值比较而缺乏零分布，导致结论可能错误；作者为此提出两类校准零假设方法，并用其重新评估MemBench等基准，发现原有审计结论被大幅推翻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆化审计方法读取相似度分数并对照阈值，没有零分布，所支持的结论可能错误，因此需要为记忆化检测建立严格的零假设校准。

Method: 提出两种零假设：针对整个模型，利用训练图像与留出图像在给定样本下可交换性构造置换检验（在留出图像为随机划分时对任意统计量精确）；针对单张图像，使用匹配对照进行校准。并推荐以Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile的小尺度质量作为尺度受限统计量，用于计数不同复制图像并检验两个模型是否复制相同图像。

Result: 按MemBench规则其缓解措施约将Stable Diffusion记忆化减半，但经假发现率控制后，随机提示扰动下三分之二认证图像不再被检出，注意力缩放下降为六分之一，embedding优化下全部不再检出。数据复制测试以其自身零假设读取时，24个生成器中有10个被判为复制但实际无复制。置换检验下最近邻偏好在24个中仍有7个触发，而尺度受限计数无触发（McNemar p=0.016）；单图像自然零假设失败：随机图像排序在2,365个对照中产生596个假发现，独立生成重采样使零假设过窄三倍。校准后，61个MemBench图像中36个在5%假发现率下被认证，留出对照仅0.01%校准划分中被认证，每图像两次生成即可恢复该计数；校准最大值认证46个。该统计量还能计数不同复制图像并检验两模型是否复制相同图像。

Conclusion: 记忆化审计中零假设是关键难点，作者提供的两种校准零假设方法能显著纠正原有审计的错误结论，尺度受限的IECP小尺度质量是推荐的稳健统计量。

Abstract: Memorization audits of generative models read similarity scores against thresholds, with no null distribution, and the conclusions they support can be wrong. By MemBench's rule, the benchmark's mitigations roughly halve Stable Diffusion's memorization; audited with false-discovery control, two thirds of the certified images are no longer detected under random prompt perturbations, five sixths under attention rescaling, and all of them under embedding optimization. The field's data-copying test, read against its own null, flags ten of twenty-four generators that reproduce nothing. We argue that for memorization the null is the hard part, and supply two. For a whole model, training and held-out images are exchangeable given its samples, and relabelling them is a permutation test, exact for any statistic when the held-out images are a random split; under it, a nearest-neighbour preference still fires on seven of those twenty-four, and a count restricted to the near-duplicate scale on none (McNemar p=0.016). For single images, the natural nulls fail twice, measurably: ranking an image among random images yields 596 false discoveries among 2,365 controls, and resampling independent generations makes the null three times too narrow. Calibrated against matched controls, the audit certifies 36 of 61 MemBench images at 5% false-discovery rate, held-out controls are certified in 0.01% of calibration splits, and on this benchmark two generations per image recover that count. A calibrated maximum, which reads occasional rather than typical copying, certifies 46. As the scale-restricted statistic we recommend the small-scale mass of the Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile, which also counts distinct images copied and tests whether two models copy the same ones.

</details>


### [183] [Sharp Oracle-Regret Tradeoffs for Projection-Free Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00254)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了在线凸优化中，当可行域访问仅限精确线性优化预言机时的可达后悔界。对于G-Lipschitz凸损失、直径上界D、总预言机调用预算Q及每轮严格上限B，得到维度无关的极小极大期望后悔为Θ(GD·max{√T, T/(1+min{Q,BT})^{1/4}})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在线凸优化在受限预言机模型下的性能极限：学习者只能通过精确线性优化预言机访问可行域，且给定内切球与直径界，必须保证每个一致实例上的可行性。目标是刻画这种受限访问对可达后悔的理论下界与上界。

Method: 下界方面：可行性约束迫使每个动作落在所提供球与先前预言机回复的凸包内；构造固定体（fixed-body）实例，将新的相位方向耦合到共享单纯形，使有用回复反复付出代价，即使所有损失有共同最小点。上界方面：设计一种带交错块（interleaved blocks）的计数近似梯度方法（counted approximate-gradient method），达到匹配速率。

Result: 得到维度无关的极小极大期望后悔为Θ(GD·max{√T, T/(1+min{Q,BT})^{1/4}})，下界适用于任意随机化学习器。总预算与严格每轮保证作为特例：每轮一次调用时得到T^{3/4}速率，达到√T后悔所需的总预算为二次量级。对给定光滑度β，解析构造给出依赖于曲率的下界，并确定一般刻画保持紧致的阈值。

Conclusion: 在仅能使用精确线性优化预言机的在线凸优化中，预言机调用预算（总量与每轮上限）与后悔之间存在精确的权衡关系，其极小极大速率由√T与T/(1+min{Q,BT})^{1/4}的较大者决定；该刻画在光滑情形下于某阈值之上仍保持紧致。

Abstract: We characterize the regret attainable in online convex optimization when access to the feasible set is limited to an exact linear optimization oracle. The learner is given an inscribed ball and a diameter bound and must remain feasible on every consistent instance. For convex $G$-Lipschitz losses, diameter at most $D$, a total allowance of $Q$ oracle calls, and a strict limit of $B$ calls per round, the dimension-free minimax expected regret is $Θ(GD\max\{\sqrt T,T/(1+\min\{Q,BT\})^{1/4}\})$. The lower bound applies to arbitrary randomized learners. Universal feasibility first forces each action into the hull of the supplied ball and the preceding oracle replies. A fixed-body construction then couples fresh phase directions to a shared simplex, making useful replies costly repeatedly even though all losses have a common minimizer. A counted approximate-gradient method with interleaved blocks attains the matching rate. Total-budget and strict per-round guarantees follow as special cases, including the $T^{3/4}$ rate with one call per round and the quadratic total budget needed for $\sqrt T$ regret. For prescribed smoothness $β$, an analytic construction yields a curvature-dependent lower bound and identifies the threshold above which the general characterization remains sharp.

</details>


### [184] [Verification Pulses and the Cost of Escaping Wrong Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00256)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究固定验证预算的时机与投放方式对异步二元寄存器群体恢复的影响，给出跨越盆地边界的最小"燃料"、有限群体出生-死亡精确概率与临界预算窗口N log(x0/b)+O(√N)，并用语言模型实验加以检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部验证能纠正个体输出，却可能让自我强化的群体仍留在错误共识的吸引域内。核心问题是：在验证预算固定时，何时投放、如何投放验证才能帮助群体脱离错误共识。

Method: 研究异步二元寄存器模型：对一般非线性响应，推导在峰值验证约束下跨越盆地边界所需的最小燃料；对有限群体用精确出生-死亡计算给出脉冲后最终错误共识的概率；做渐近分析得到临界预算窗口；并开展前瞻性实验，采集13,392条语言模型响应，含校准与108条留出轨迹，对四种调度进行对比与分布审计。

Result: 渐近结果显示临界预算窗口主项为N log(x0/b)，修正项为O(√N)，其方差分别来自重复验证目标与验证停止后的自主放大。在16个多数更新槽、初始14个错误的情形下，9次随机检查即可越过平均场预算阈值，而精确模型要求23次才能达到95%的最终恢复率。实验中校准拟合出不同的响应区间，但四种调整后的调度比较区间均包含零；分布审计发现较小的均值预测误差可能掩盖对最终共识占位的严重低估。

Conclusion: 结果支持在明确更新契约下进行风险校准的重置调度，并将其与针对不同目标的检查以及无限制的证据广播明确区分开来。

Abstract: External verification can correct individual outputs while leaving a self-reinforcing population in the basin of a wrong consensus. We study how the timing and addressing of a fixed verification budget affect recovery in an asynchronous binary register. For a general nonlinear response, we derive the minimum fuel required to cross a basin boundary under a peak verification constraint. For a finite population, an exact birth--death calculation gives the probability of subsequent wrong consensus after a pulse. Our main asymptotic result identifies the critical budget window: a leading term $N\log(x_0/b)$ and a correction of order $\sqrt N$, with separate variance contributions from repeated verification targets and autonomous amplification after verification stops. The distinction is substantial: with 16 majority-updated slots and 14 initially wrong, 9 random checks cross the mean-field budget threshold, whereas 23 are required for 95% eventual recovery in the exact model. A prospectively specified experiment records 13,392 language-model responses, including calibration and 108 held-out trajectories. Calibration produces different fitted response regimes, but all four adjusted schedule-comparison intervals include zero. A distributional audit also finds that modest mean-prediction error can conceal a large underestimate of terminal consensus occupancy. The results support risk-calibrated reset scheduling under a specified update contract, while explicitly separating it from distinct-target checking and unrestricted evidence broadcast.

</details>


### [185] [Attention Manifolds: Steering or Blocking Language Models by Editing Learned B-Spline Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00257)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"注意力流形"(attention manifolds)：用可学习的二维B样条曲面 S_d(q_d, k_d) 调制每个 value 维度，突破标准注意力中"查询只决定关注多少、不决定提取什么"的限制，在 LLaMA 3.2-1B/3B-Instruct 上以 0.3% 参数开销显著降低困惑度并改变/提升生成质量，同时具备可机械编辑与安全引导的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 注意力中，源 token 向每个接收者发送相同的 value 向量，query 仅决定注意力权重（关注多少），而无法决定从 value 中提取什么内容。作者希望赋予注意力一种更细粒度的、依赖 query-key 交互的内容调制能力，从而提升表达力与可控性。

Method: 引入注意力流形：为每个 value 维度学习张量积三次 B 样条曲面 S_d(q_d, k_d)，基于 query-key 交互对该维度进行调制；曲面初始化为零以保持预训练行为不变。基于该参数化还设计了系数反转（编辑/导向）与置为 -1 形成"注意力墙"以阻断特定维度的信息流。

Result: 在 LLaMA 3.2-1B/3B-Instruct 上，WikiText-2 验证困惑度降低 2–2.5 点，参数开销仅 0.3%。在 112 个多样化提示中，贪心解码输出改变率分别为 69%(1B) 与 83%(3B)，歧义/多义输入上达 94–100%。输出质量提升：纠正事实错误、提高精确性、增加具体性（如引用归于圣奥古斯丁）。曲面可机械编辑：反转某层系数可改变 9/10 提示的输出（KL≈0.010）；系数置 -1 形成"注意力墙"，初步实验中层级墙可将某一具体指令型提示重定向为一般性教育内容。

Conclusion: 注意力流形以极低参数代价增强了注意力的内容选择性，兼顾性能提升与输出质量改善，并提供了一种几何化的模型编辑与潜在安全引导机制（manifold shaping），为注意力机制设计与可控生成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: In standard transformer attention, a source token sends the same value vector to every receiver. The query determines \emph{how much} to attend but not \emph{what} to extract. This work introduces \textbf{attention manifolds}: learned 2D B-spline surfaces $S_d(q_d, k_d)$ that modulate each value dimension based on the query-key interaction. Each surface is a tensor-product cubic B-spline initialized to zero, preserving pretrained behavior. Applied to LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct and 3B-Instruct, attention manifolds reduce WikiText-2 validation perplexity by 2--2.5 points with 0.3\% parameter overhead. Across 112 diverse prompts, surfaces change greedy-decoded output for 69\% (1B) to 83\% (3B) of cases, with the strongest effects on ambiguous and polysemous inputs (94--100\% change rate). The surfaces improve output quality: correcting factual errors (\emph{``the CAP theorem has three main components''} $\to$ \emph{``it is impossible to guarantee all three''}), increasing precision (\emph{``impossible to know certain properties''} $\to$ \emph{``impossible to know both position and momentum''}), and adding specificity (a generic quote $\to$ an attributed Saint Augustine citation, consistently at both scales). The learned surfaces are also mechanically editable: inverting a layer's coefficients changes greedy output for 9/10 prompts (KL~0.010), providing a geometric mechanism for model steering. Setting surface coefficients to $-1$ creates ``attention walls'' that block value flow through specific dimensions. In a preliminary experiment, a layer-wide wall redirects an explosive-device prompt from specific instructions to general educational content, suggesting a path toward safety-oriented manifold shaping.

</details>


### [186] [Large Language Bayes Is Not Reparameterisation-Invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00265)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出LLB（Large Language Bayes）的候选程序加权依赖于概率程序的具体书写方式（参数化），因为边际似然虽对重参数化不变，但证据下界并非不变，从而导致模型权重出现显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLB通过从语言模型采样候选概率程序、对每个程序做近似推断，并按指数化证据界的权重进行平均来回答建模问题。作者关注到这种加权方式可能受程序书写形式（parameterisation）影响，即同一测度的不同写法应当给出相同权重，但证据界不具备重参数化不变性。这一潜在缺陷威胁LLB方法的可靠性与公正性。

Method: 作者对比同一测度的中心化与非中心化程序（如eight schools），计算它们在几乎相同测度下权重的差异；使用重要性加权缓解偏差；并复现LLB实际运行的推断方式——匹配后验矩的全协方差高斯，评估其残留差异。实验覆盖不同似然族、维度和漏斗严重程度，并从六个语言模型生成的360个程序中统计参数化书写（中心化比例）的分布。进一步分析等价程序统计检测对真实不同模型的误合并风险，以及通过验证自行生成的重参数化来封闭该窗口。

Result: 在eight schools上，中心化与非中心化程序测度相同（5.7×10⁻¹⁴级别），但权重相差6.1倍；重要性加权仅将差异降至2.2倍；复现实际推断方式后仍有1.9倍差异，在64维下达8.9倍。跨似然族、维度与漏斗严重程度差异最高达31.9倍且符号反转，说明没有单一书写形式普遍更优。该偏差会反转与八个自然竞争者的贝叶斯因子，模型后验及下游目标的误差受证据界缺口散布Δ的Hilbert距离上界控制。360个程序参数化书写中心化比例从0%到100%，且在单一模型内稳定。统计检测等价程序在实用样本预算下可能错误合并真正不同的模型；而验证自行生成的重参数化则不会，并能封闭该窗口。

Conclusion: 证据界不具备重参数化不变性，导致LLB加权依赖程序书写形式，偏差显著且方向不一，会扭曲贝叶斯因子与下游推断。该问题无法通过重要性加权或复现推断完全消除。应通过验证自行生成的重参数化而非统计合并等价程序来应对，参数化书写在语言模型间差异大但在模型内稳定。

Abstract: Large Language Bayes (LLB) answers an informal modelling question by sampling candidate probabilistic programs from a language model, running approximate inference on each, and averaging them with weights proportional to an exponentiated evidence bound. We show that this weighting depends on how a model is written. The log marginal likelihood is invariant to reparameterisation; the evidence bound is not. On eight schools the centered and non-centered programs are the same measure to $5.7\times10^{-14}$, yet their weights differ by $6.1\times$; importance weighting reduces this only to $2.2\times$, and reproducing the inference LLB actually runs, a full-covariance Gaussian matched to the posterior moments, still leaves $1.9\times$ on eight schools and $8.9\times$ in $64$ dimensions. Across likelihood families, dimensions and funnel severities the discrepancy reaches $31.9\times$ and reverses sign, so no single writing is uniformly preferable. It inverts Bayes factors against eight natural competitors, and the induced error in the model posterior, and in any downstream target, is controlled by the spread $Δ$ of the bound shortfalls through a known sharp Hilbert-distance bound. Across $360$ programs from six language models the parameterisation written ranges from $0\%$ to $100\%$ centered and is stable within a model. Detecting equivalent programs statistically can falsely merge genuinely different models at practical sample budgets; verifying reparameterisations we generate ourselves cannot, and closes the window.

</details>


### [187] [MOVE: Multimodal Open-world Verification and Expansion for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00268)
*Zekai Chen,Jiayang Xing,Xun Wu,Miao Zhang,Xunkai Li,Kairui Yang,Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOVE：面向多模态图学习的开放世界类别验证与扩展框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图学习中，新类别可能在部署后出现，而模型仅有固定标签空间。现有方法虽能检测未知节点并用LLM生成候选类描述，但无法判断现有类是否不足以覆盖未知节点，也无法评估生成类是否可靠、是否冗余。

Method: MOVE联合考虑视觉token、文本属性和图上下文来识别无法归入现有类的节点；利用多模态LLM生成候选类；仅当候选类获得多模态证据一致支持且不引入冗余类别时，才选择性地扩展类空间。

Result: 实验显示，MOVE在未知节点识别、开放域标注和下游图学习任务上平均提升11.87%。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了未知节点识别需要超越单一模态的多模态信息、LLM描述可能不完整、直接添加候选类会带来冗余等问题，并提出MOVE以验证与选择性扩展的方式有效应对开放世界类别演化。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning faces a fundamental challenge: new classes may emerge after deployment, while models are trained with a fixed label space. Existing approaches typically detect unknown nodes and use LLMs to generate candidate class descriptions, but they do not determine whether existing classes are insufficient to cover these nodes or whether a generated class is reliable enough to expand the class space. Our empirical study reveals three challenges: multimodal information beyond individual modalities is required for unknown-node identification, LLM-generated class descriptions may not fully capture multimodal class characteristics, and directly adding candidate classes can introduce redundant categories. Based on these observations, we propose MOVE, a multimodal open-world class verification and expansion framework. MOVE identifies nodes that cannot be assigned to existing classes by jointly considering visual tokens, textual attributes, and graph context, leverages a multimodal LLM to generate candidate classes, and selectively expands the class space only when candidates are consistently supported by multimodal evidence without introducing unnecessary categories. Experiments demonstrate that MOVE achieves an average improvement of 11.87\% across unknown recognition, open-domain annotation, and downstream graph learning tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Coupling Perception and Reasoning in Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00277)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Hailin Zhang,Xunkai Li,Yu Liu,Kairui Yang,Muyan Huang,Xuaner Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCORE框架，通过共享低维隐状态联合优化Encoder与GNN更新，解决联邦多模态图基础模型适配中的模块配对敏感问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图基础模型适配方法仅更新图侧模块而冻结多模态Encoder，只改变信息传播方式而不改变信息提取内容。实证发现Encoder与GNN适配并非独立：Encoder适配受图关系影响，且跨客户端模块交换显示两者存在显著的配对敏感性。

Method: FedCORE通过一个共享的低维隐状态（core）来表示Encoder和GNN的更新，从多模态信号与结构信号中联合优化该core，并在共享状态空间中直接进行联邦演化，从而保持感知与推理适配之间的兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明，FedCORE将Encoder–GNN配对差距从30.6降至5.9，相比独立联合适配降低了80.7%。

Conclusion: 通过共享隐状态空间联合适配感知与推理模块，可有效缓解联邦多模态图基础模型中Encoder与GNN更新的配对敏感问题，显著提升适配兼容性。

Abstract: Federated multimodal graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to adapt pretrained multimodal models to decentralized graph data, where each client owns a private multimodal graph and cannot share raw information. These models typically combine a multimodal Encoder that extracts semantic evidence from heterogeneous modalities and a graph neural network (GNN) that performs relational reasoning over graph structures. However, existing federated GFM adaptation methods mainly update graph-side modules while keeping the multimodal Encoder frozen, limiting adaptation to \emph{how information is propagated} while fixing \emph{what information is extracted}. Through empirical studies, we reveal that Encoder and GNN adaptations are not independent: Encoder adaptation is affected by graph relations, while cross-client module swapping reveals substantial pairing sensitivity between separately parameterized Encoder and GNN updates. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{FedCORE}, a federated adaptation framework that represents Encoder and GNN updates through a shared low-dimensional latent state. FedCORE jointly optimizes this core from multimodal and structural signals and performs federated evolution directly in the shared state space, preserving compatibility between perception and reasoning adaptations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedCORE reduces the Encoder--GNN pairing gap from $30.6$ to $5.9$, corresponding to an $80.7\%$ reduction over independent joint adaptation.

</details>


### [189] [Stable and Counterfactually Robust Physical World Models from Imposed Structure and Learned Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00280)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究世界模型需要硬编码多少通用物理结构、又能从数据中学到多少系统特定物理，提出一种由“学习能量函数的梯度 + 固定可逆算子 + 单向耗散端口”构成的结构化模型，在约九千参数下同时满足耗散第二定律、参数干预响应、超长时稳定和外扰鲁棒四项性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型从轨迹中学习物理系统演化，但既不保证遵守物理定律（可能凭空造能量、长期发散、用训练时的规律回答被改变规律的查询），也难以在干预、长时程和外扰下保持正确。核心问题是：多少通用物理结构必须硬编码，多少系统特定物理可从数据学习，才能同时实现耗散合规、干预正确、百倍训练时程稳定和抗扰四项要求。

Method: 施加通用（非系统特定）结构：动力学由学习能量函数经固定可逆算子的梯度生成；能量限制在封闭（confining）类内；单向端口只移除能量、从不注入；驱动通道已知；被干预参数经可分离映射进入。模型需学习能量泛函、本构关系、耗散率与耦合项。

Result: 在电磁腔、粒子在网格（PIC）、浅水流体三类系统上，约九千参数的模型恢复出单位斜率的本构函数；用同一组权重以四个数量级的差距区分守恒与耗散世界；能将符号、大小、速率和重力的改变迁移到未见取值，而同等容量但无此结构的模型表现为随机或更差。非线性本构律在保持曲率的情况下被恢复，对留出的干预预测比收敛的线性模型好 2–17 倍。

Conclusion: 少量通用物理结构（可逆算子 + 封闭能量 + 单向耗散 + 已知驱动 + 可分离参数映射）加上数据学习的系统特定物理，足以在小参数量下统一获得耗散合规、干预响应、百倍时程稳定与抗扰四项性质，且显著优于无结构等容量基线。

Abstract: A world model learns to forecast how a physical system evolves from recorded trajectories, yet the systems it imitates obey physical laws that are neither fully supplied nor reliably respected. The model may create energy, drift or diverge over long rollouts, and answer a changed law query using the law observed during training. We ask how much general physical structure must be hard coded into a world model, and how much system-specific physics can then be learned from data, for four properties to hold simultaneously: second law compatible dissipation, correct responses to interventions on physical parameters, stability out to one hundred times the training horizon, and robustness to disturbances. The imposed structure is general: dynamics are generated from the gradient of a learned energy through a fixed reversible operator, the energy is restricted to a confining class, a one way port can remove energy but never inject it, the drive channel is known, and the intervened parameter enters through a separable map. The model learns the energy functional, constitutive relations, dissipation rate, and couplings. Across an electromagnetic cavity, a particle in cell grid, and a shallow-water fluid, models with roughly nine thousand parameters recover constitutive functions with unit slope, separate conserving from dissipating worlds by four orders of magnitude using a single set of weights, and transfer changes in sign, magnitude, rate, and gravity to unseen values, where equal-capacity models without the same structure perform at chance or worse. A nonlinear constitutive law is recovered with its curvature preserved and predicts a held-out intervention $2$-$17\times$ better than a converged linear model.

</details>


### [190] [Partial AUC Maximization from Positive-unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00284)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Taishi Nishiyama,Hiroshi Takahashi,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需负样本、仅依赖正样本和无标签数据（PU数据）即可最大化部分AUC（pAUC）的分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pAUC是二分类中衡量特定假阳性率范围内真阳性率的重要指标，在网络安全、医疗、广告等场景中需要高pAUC分类器。但已有pAUC最大化方法通常需要标注的正负样本，而实际中负样本因隐私问题或标注需高专业性而难以获取。

Method: 在经验风险最小化框架下，证明pAUC（包括其依赖假阳性率的阈值）可仅用正类密度和边缘密度表示，并据此从PU数据推导出经验估计量；随后通过最大化所导出的平滑经验pAUC估计量来训练分类器。

Result: 在十个真实数据集上进行的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在没有负样本的情况下，仅利用正样本和无标签数据有效地最大化pAUC，为负样本难以获取的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The partial area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (pAUC) is an important performance metric for binary classification that summarizes true positive rates within a specific range of false positive rates (FPRs). Classifiers that achieve high pAUC need to be obtained in many real-world applications such as cybersecurity, medical care, and advertising. Although many methods for maximizing the pAUC have been proposed, they typically require both labeled positive and negative data for training. However, in practice, labeled negative data are often difficult to collect due to privacy concerns or the need for high expertise to annotate them. In this paper, we propose a method for maximizing the pAUC from positive and unlabeled (PU) data without negative data. Within an empirical risk minimization framework, we show that the pAUC, including its FPR-dependent thresholds, can be represented using only the positive and marginal densities, and derive an empirical estimator from PU data. A classifier is then trained by maximizing the derived smoothed empirical pAUC estimator. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with ten real-world datasets.

</details>


### [191] [Bellman-Certified Rounding for Sparse Policy Deployment in MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00325)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有限MDP中连续策略舍入为稀疏二值策略时的折扣回报保持问题，提出基于Bellman求解的可复用包络与轨迹曲率积分方法，并给出下界与复杂性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续策略优化的一次更新可能分散到大量状态，而部署仅允许少量完整状态级改动，因此需要理解舍入为稀疏二值策略后能保留多少折扣回报；同时策略相关访问分布使行编辑相互耦合，长时域又使全局曲率界过于保守。

Method: 用2d+2次Bellman求解导出可复用包络，支持舍入前的统一和候选特定保证；对每个交换构造秩二有理表示，从而沿实际轨迹进行加权曲率积分；并分析预算缩放下的维度依赖与全局曲率阈值化的复杂性。

Result: 候选特定界将结构化测试集上的舍入前认证覆盖率从48.2%提升到74.1%；在γ=0.95的耦合实例上，局部积分将中位界-损失比从402.3降至2.08。

Conclusion: 在预算缩放时线性维度依赖不可避免，精确全局曲率阈值化是困难的；所提包络与轨迹局部分析能显著提高认证覆盖率并大幅收紧界-损失比。

Abstract: Continuous policy optimization may spread an update across many states, even when deployment permits only a few complete state-level changes. We study how much discounted return can be retained when continuous row mixtures are rounded to sparse binary policies in finite MDPs. Policy-dependent visitation couples the row edits, while long horizons make global curvature bounds conservative. From $2d+2$ Bellman solves, we derive reusable envelopes that support uniform and candidate-specific guarantees before rounding. A rank-two rational representation of each exchange further permits weighted curvature integration along the realized trajectory. We prove that linear dimension dependence is unavoidable when the budget scales, and that exact global curvature thresholding is hard. Candidate-specific bounds raise pre-rounding certification coverage from $48.2\%$ to $74.1\%$ on the structured suite. At $γ=0.95$, local integration lowers the median bound-to-loss ratio from $402.3$ to $2.08$ on coupled instances.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Diagonal State Space Models: Exact Non-Abelian Group Tracking, Solvability Barriers, and Geometric Physical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00329)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非交换状态空间模型（NC-SSM）及其SO(3)-SSM与Cayley-SSM变体，将状态转移提升到紧李群SU(2)/SO(3)/SO(N)，通过闭式Euler-Rodrigues映射与Cayley变换实现精确等距，突破对角SSM的交换性限制，在群追踪、流形保持与长程记忆任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba、S4D、LRU）受限于转移矩阵的可交换性（A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t）以及可解仿射变换群（派生长度≤2的Aff_D）。因此，堆叠的多层对角网络在非可解单群（如A_5）上因需要指数级电路模拟深度来仿真非阿贝尔交换子，面临严重的优化退化问题。作者希望突破这一结构性瓶颈。

Method: 提出非交换状态空间模型（NC-SSM）、实正交对应SO(3)-SSM以及任意维度的Cayley-SSM，将状态转移提升到紧李群SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)。利用闭式Euler-Rodrigues映射和有理Cayley变换实现精确保范等距（||U_t||=1）。引入纯Hopf纤维化Bloch投影读出（S^3/{±1}≅S^2≅SO(3)）消除符号歧义，采用真四元数并行前缀扫描（T=2048时9.06倍加速），并提出Identity-Gated Lie SSM消除稀疏语法相位漂移。

Result: 在14种实验设定下：(1) NC-SSM在S_3、D_4、Q_8及单群A_5上达到100%追踪，而3层深对角基线崩溃至6.60%（p=8.81e-4）；(2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM打破Klein 1884年对对称群S_5的上限（50.92% vs 对角5.25%，p=0.0015，相比SO(3)降低7.8倍方差）；(3) SO(3)-SSM在300步内保持黎曼流形（漂移<3.12e-6，优势>580,000倍），实现0.04度航位推算误差与主动切空间去噪；(4) NC-SSM在Dyck-2上达74.36%，在深层AST作用域追踪上达30.26%（p=0.0081）；(5) 消融实验确认严格等距是无损长程联想记忆的数学必要条件。

Conclusion: 通过将状态转移从可交换/可解群提升到紧李群并保持严格等距，NC-SSM系列突破了传统对角SSM在非阿贝尔群上的优化退化瓶颈，在群追踪、黎曼流形保持、长程联想记忆和语法结构建模等任务上全面优于对角基线，且严格等距被证明为无损长程记忆的必要条件。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, S4D, and LRU, are bounded by transition matrix commutativity (A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t) and solvable affine transformation groups (Aff_D of derived length <= 2). Consequently, stacked multi-layer diagonal networks face severe optimization degradation on non-solvable simple groups such as A_5 due to the exponential circuit emulation depth required to simulate non-abelian commutators. We propose Non-Commutative State Space Models (NC-SSM), their real-orthogonal counterpart SO(3)-SSM, and arbitrary-dimension Cayley-SSM, lifting state transitions to compact Lie groups SU(2), SO(3), and SO(N). Via closed-form Euler-Rodrigues maps and rational Cayley transforms, NC-SSM achieves exact norm-preserving isometry (||U_t|| = 1). We introduce pure Hopf-fibration Bloch projective readouts (S^3/{+-1} =~ S^2 =~ SO(3)) to eliminate sign ambiguity, true quaternion parallel prefix scans (9.06x speedup at T=2048), and Identity-Gated Lie SSMs to eliminate sparse syntax phase drift. Extensive benchmarks across 14 experimental regimes show: (1) NC-SSM achieves 100% tracking on S_3, D_4, Q_8 and simple group A_5, where a 3-layer deep diagonal baseline collapses to 6.60% (p = 8.81e-4); (2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM breaks Klein's 1884 ceiling on symmetric group S_5 (50.92% vs diagonal 5.25%, p = 0.0015, delivering 7.8x variance reduction over SO(3)); (3) SO(3)-SSM preserves Riemannian manifolds across 300 steps (< 3.12e-6 drift, > 580,000x advantage), achieving 0.04 deg dead-reckoning error and active tangent denoising; (4) NC-SSM achieves 74.36% on Dyck-2 and 30.26% on deep AST scope tracking (p = 0.0081); and (5) ablation confirms strict isometry is mathematically necessary for lossless long-range associative memory.

</details>


### [193] [The Weakest Link: Distilling LLM Reasoning with Worst-Case Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00332)
*Matthieu Zimmer,Xiaotong Ji,Tu Nguyen,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将推理蒸馏建模为带最坏情况约束的强化学习问题，通过无增广的约束MDP奖励变换，在数学推理与代码生成任务上同时取得高准确率与高逻辑保真度，获得最高的推理成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大模型推理能力蒸馏到小模型时存在根本矛盾：纯任务奖励（如GRPO）会导致奖励作弊，学生靠错误中间逻辑得到正确答案；而基于KL的软散度正则会稀释任务性能，且允许学生用其他步骤的高教师一致性来补偿某一步的严重逻辑违规。论文认为这种平均化与推理本质不符——思维链的合法性取决于其最薄弱的一环。

Method: 将推理蒸馏形式化为约束强化学习问题：在轨迹每个前缀上对教师对数似然施加最坏情况约束，同时最大化任务奖励。为避免对偶拉格朗日求解器的高昂成本以及Saute等状态增广方法的测试时教师依赖，推导出一个无增广的约束MDP，其奖励变换保留硬约束语义，可分解为单步项与长期项的低方差策略梯度，并证明在惩罚极限下几乎必然满足最坏情况约束。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，该方法显著扩展了准确率-保真度的帕累托前沿：既达到了纯RL的高最终答案正确率，又大幅减少了教师约束违规，在所有评估设置下取得最高的严格推理成功率。

Conclusion: 以“最薄弱环节”视角约束推理蒸馏，可在不依赖测试时教师、不引入对偶求解复杂度的前提下兼顾任务性能与逻辑保真，为高效部署小模型提供更严谨的推理蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Distilling the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) into smaller students is a central challenge for efficient deployment. Current approaches face a fundamental tension: optimizing purely for verifiable task rewards (e.g., via GRPO) leads to reward hacking, where students arrive at correct final answers through flawed intermediate logic, while regularizing with soft divergence penalties against a teacher (e.g., KL-based distillation) dilutes task performance and, critically, allows the student to compensate for severe logical violations at one step with high teacher agreement at others. We argue that this averaging is fundamentally misaligned with the nature of reasoning: a chain-of-thought is only as valid as its weakest link. Motivated by this observation, we formulate reasoning distillation as a constrained reinforcement learning problem in which the task reward is maximized subject to a worst-case constraint on the teacher log-likelihood along every prefix of the trajectory. To avoid the prohibitive cost of dual Lagrangian solvers and the test-time teacher dependence of state-augmented methods such as Saute, we derive an unaugmented constrained MDP whose reward transformation preserves the hard-constraint semantics, admits a low-variance policy gradient decomposition into single-step and long-term terms, and provably satisfies the worst-case constraint almost surely in the penalty limit. Through extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks, we demonstrate that our method significantly expands the accuracy-fidelity Pareto front. By matching the high Final Answer Correctness of pure RL and drastically reducing teacher constraint violations, we ultimately achieve the highest rigorous Reasoning Success Rate across all evaluated settings.

</details>


### [194] [Benchmarking System One decision models against trained classifiers and language models for automated decision gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00346)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在匹配条件下系统比较了类型化决策模型（System One）、监督分类器与生成式语言模型在决策门控任务上的表现，发现最优模型类别取决于任务条件（有无标签），并给出相应的设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件将分支决策交给模型时，需要一个可声明选项且可设定阈值的概率输出。类型化决策模型能直接返回此类概率而不生成文本，但监督分类器与生成式语言模型是既有替代方案，二者在不同条件下孰优孰劣缺乏统一比较。

Method: 使用统一测试框架，向六大家族共八个决策模型检查点（含托管模型Jev）以及两个生成式对比模型发送相同的语义请求；在同一套工作流、意图与社会科学条目上，对监督分类器和零样本分类器进行评分，并考察温度标定、风险阈值、选项顺序扰动及两阶段级联等设置。

Result: 模型类别的排名随条件变化：有任务标签时，小型训练分类器在意图上最准，在工作流上与最优决策模型无显著差异；无标签时，除基于编码器的检查点外，所有决策模型在工作流和意图上均超过零样本蕴含分类器。通过选项键似然读取时，较大生成模型在工作流和意图上与Jev持平，并在5%风险下接受更多工作流决策；微调后的决策检查点较其未微调骨干提升意图准确率。在小选项集上拟合的存储温度会随选项增多而抬高标定误差；即使采用5%范围内风险的留出阈值，Jev仍会接受0.310的域外请求。交换"是/否"使Jev每百个答案翻转50.5个，而微调检查点减少了其骨干在社会科学上的翻转。意图训练的第一阶段级联到Jev，在满GPU利用率下以0.43的成本达到其准确率。

Conclusion: 研究给出了面向自动化决策门控的、随条件变化的设计规则：是否具备标签、风险阈值设定、选项顺序稳健性与成本约束共同决定模型类别与级联策略的选择。

Abstract: Software that hands branching decisions to a model needs a declared option and a probability it can threshold. Typed decision models, also called System One models, return such probabilities without generating text, while supervised classifiers and generative language models are the established alternatives. Under matched conditions, one harness sends eight decision-model checkpoints from six families, including the hosted model Jev, and two generative comparators the same semantic requests, and scores supervised and zero-shot classifiers on the same workflow, intent and social-science items. The ranking of the model classes depends on the conditions. With the task's own labels, small trained classifiers are the most accurate on intents and not significantly different from the best decision models on workflows. Without labels, every decision model except the encoder-based checkpoints exceeds a zero-shot entailment classifier on workflows and intents. Read through option-key likelihoods, a larger generative model is level with Jev on workflows and intents and accepts more workflow decisions at five percent risk, and fine-tuned decision checkpoints gain intent accuracy over their untuned backbones. Stored temperatures fitted on few options raise calibration error with many options, and a held-out threshold for five percent in-scope risk still lets Jev accept 0.310 of out-of-scope requests. Swapping yes and no flips 50.5 answers per hundred for Jev, while fine-tuned checkpoints cut their backbones' social-science flips. An intent-trained first stage escalating to Jev matches its accuracy at 0.43 of its cost at full graphics-processor utilization. The results yield condition-dependent design rules for automated decision gates.

</details>


### [195] [Deep Learning for Anomaly Detection in Railway Systems: A Structured Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00363)
*Ammar Bouketta,Smail Niar,Hamza Ouarnoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于深度学习铁路异常检测的结构化综述，提出统一分类体系并给出面向决策的部署框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代铁路系统安全可靠运行日益依赖数据驱动的监测与智能故障检测，机车车辆与基础设施传感器数据（异构）不断丰富，深度学习成为有效范式，但缺乏系统化的方法梳理与选型指导。

Method: 以统一分类体系组织现有方法，涵盖异常位置、数据表示与表现形式、感知模态、时间特性；将卷积、循环、注意力架构、自编码器、生成对抗网络、Transformer等归入基于分类、基于预测、基于重构与混合学习四类范式；并考察数据挑战、评估实践、性能指标及部署问题（边缘—云架构、计算约束、硬件感知优化）。

Result: 形成涵盖方法、数据、评估与部署的系统梳理，并提出将异常特征、数据属性与运行约束映射到合适检测范式与部署配置的决策导向框架。

Conclusion: 该综述为铁路异常检测中深度学习方案的选择与部署提供结构化参考，并指出面向可靠、可扩展智能监测系统的开放挑战。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and reliable operation of modern railway systems increasingly relies on data-driven monitoring and intelligent fault detection. Deep learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for railway anomaly detection, driven by the growing availability of heterogeneous sensor data from rolling stock and infrastructure. This paper presents a structured survey of deep learning-based anomaly detection approaches for railway systems. The surveyed methods are organized using a unified taxonomy covering anomaly location, data representation and manifestation, sensing modality, and temporal characteristics. Existing approaches, including convolutional, recurrent and attention-based architectures, autoencoders, generative adversarial networks, and transformers, are structured into classification-based, prediction-based, reconstruction-based, and hybrid learning paradigms. The survey also examines data-centric challenges, evaluation practices, performance metrics, and practical deployment aspects, including edge-cloud architectures, computational constraints, and hardware-aware optimization. Finally, a decision-oriented framework links anomaly characteristics, data properties, and operational constraints to suitable detection paradigms and deployment configurations. This work provides a structured reference for selecting and deploying deep learning solutions for railway anomaly detection and highlights open challenges toward reliable and scalable intelligent monitoring systems.

</details>


### [196] [Manifold-Constrained Initial Noise Optimization for Efficient Generative Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00365)
*Jinho Chang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeNOVA：一种无梯度的初始噪声对齐方法，通过退火软值引导、流形约束超球朗之万动力学和Metropolis-Hastings跳跃，在图像与视频生成模型中稳定高效地将初始噪声优化至更高奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏与流映射模型实现了确定性一步或少步生成，催生了直接优化高斯初始噪声的奖励对齐新方向。但现有初始噪声优化方法多依赖一阶梯度信息，在黑盒奖励场景中不适用或存在不稳定、低效问题。

Method: 提出ZeNOVA，以无梯度方式实现稳定高效的初始噪声对齐，核心包括三项技术：退火软值引导、流形约束的超球面朗之万动力学，以及Metropolis-Hastings跳跃。

Result: 在图像和视频生成模型上的大量实验表明，ZeNOVA优于所有评测的零阶基线，能显著更稳定地将初始噪声优化至高奖励，同时利用高斯先验的几何结构。

Conclusion: ZeNOVA证明了其在各类黑盒奖励对齐任务上的实际适用性，为无梯度初始噪声优化提供了稳定高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in distillation and flow-map models have enabled deterministic one- or few-step generation for high-quality data, facilitating a new branch of reward alignment approaches that directly optimize the initial noise from a Gaussian distribution. However, most existing initial-noise optimization methods rely on first-order gradient information, which is either inapplicable or suffers from instability and inefficiency in black-box reward scenarios. Here, we introduce ZeNOVA, a stable and efficient initial noise alignment method in a gradient-free manner. Specifically, we address existing algorithms' major challenge in black-box scenarios through annealed soft-value guidance, manifold-constrained hyperspherical Langevin dynamics, and Metropolis-Hastings jumping. Extensive experiments on image and video generative models show that ZeNOVA outperforms all evaluated zeroth-order baselines by optimizing the initial noise toward higher rewards substantially more stably while exploiting the geometry of the Gaussian prior, demonstrating its practical applicability to various black-box reward alignment.

</details>


### [197] [MoRA: MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00367)
*Yushuai Sun,Zikun Zhou,Lin Gao,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoRA框架，通过可学习路由偏置和专家近似实现MoE结构化剪枝，在多个模型和基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽能减少每token计算量，但部署时仍需将完整专家池加载到内存。现有结构化专家剪枝方法存在不足：排序准则与模型性能对齐不佳，或依赖计算昂贵的专家子集搜索，且忽视保留专家间的路由行为冗余。

Method: 为每个专家引入可学习路由偏置，通过最小化语言建模损失和路由多样性正则项来优化偏置，使路由概率分布更尖锐以识别关键专家并鼓励选择路由偏好多样的专家；同时引入专家近似机制，作为剪枝后增强手段，利用剩余专家通过仿射变换近似被剪枝专家的输出。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Moonlight-16B-A3B上每层移除25%和50%的路由专家，在九个零样本基准上的大量实验表明MoRA优于最先进的剪枝算法。

Conclusion: MoRA为结构化MoE专家剪枝提供了有效框架，兼顾性能对齐与计算效率，并通过专家近似进一步提升剪枝模型表现，代码将开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable parameter scaling with limited per-token computation by activating only a small subset of experts for each token, but deploying them still requires loading the complete expert pool into memory. Structured expert pruning can effectively reduce the memory usage by removing experts. However, existing pruning methods either use expert ranking criteria that are not well aligned with model performance or rely on effective expert subset searching that is computationally expensive. Moreover, these methods typically overlook the routing-behavior redundancy among the retained experts. In this paper, we propose MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation (MoRA), a framework for structured MoE expert pruning. We introduce a learnable router bias for each expert and optimize these biases by minimizing the language-modeling loss and a routing-diversity regularizer. The learned router biases sharpen the routing probability distributions to identify experts critical to model performance while encouraging the selection of experts with diverse routing preferences. In addition, we introduce an expert approximation mechanism as a post-pruning enhancement. It leverages the remaining experts to approximate the outputs of pruned experts by affine transformation, further improving the performance of the pruned model. We evaluate MoRA on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, removing 25\% and 50\% of the routed experts in each MoE layer. Extensive experiments on nine zero-shot benchmarks show that MoRA outperforms state-of-the-art pruning algorithms. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [198] [M$^2$Weather: A Benchmark for Joint Multi-Station and Multi-Variable Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00370)
*Rongwen Li,Xiao Wang,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M²Weather 基准，用于联合多站点与多变量天气预报，统一数据与协议，并通过轻量即插即用适配器验证站点-变量关系建模的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将站点间空间依赖与变量间物理耦合分开处理，且使用不同数据集与实验设置，难以系统评估二者各自的及联合的贡献。

Method: 构建多准则质控与站点分层流程，收集法国、欧洲、全球三个空间尺度下 2,809 个高质量站点与 5 个物理耦合变量；提出统一训练与评估协议；设计轻量即插即用适配器，在不重训原模型的情况下引入缺失的站点或变量关系。

Result: 对 16 个代表性模型的系统评估表明联合建模站点与变量关系具有优势；补全缺失关系后，所有适配模型在三个数据集上的 MSE 均进一步下降。

Conclusion: 站点与变量间的互补信息是提升站点天气预报的重要资源，多尺度设计支持结论从国家到全球网络的推广。

Abstract: Station weather forecasting is fundamentally shaped by both complex spatial dependencies across stations and strong physical coupling among weather variables. However, existing studies often consider these relationships separately and use different datasets and experimental settings, hindering systematic assessment of their individual and joint contributions. In this paper, we introduce $M^2$Weather, a benchmark for joint multi-station and multi-variable weather forecasting. Through multi-criteria quality control and station stratification, we collect 2,809 high-quality stations with 5 physically coupled weather variables across three spatial scales: France, Europe, and Global. This multi-scale design lets us examine whether conclusions persist from national to global station networks. We also introduce unified training and evaluation protocols to enable fair comparison of different station-variable modeling paradigms. To further examine the benefits of modeling station-variable relationships, we design a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter. With a trained weather forecasting model, this adapter can introduce missing station or variable relationships without retraining the model. This enables fair and efficient investigation of station-variable relationships. Systematic evaluation of 16 representative models shows the benefits of jointly modeling station and variable relationships. Completing missing relationships further reduces MSE for all adapted models on all three datasets. Together, these results identify the complementary information across stations and variables as an important resource for improving station weather forecasting. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/M2-Weather.

</details>


### [199] [When Do Attention-Head Ablations Support Causal Claims? Projection-Level Confounds, Floor Effects, and Matched Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00373)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以GPT-2 small为对象，证明单头消融的因果推断只有在干预语义、评估指标与对照设计都经过验证时才可靠：post-projection消融近乎无效、二元准确率会掩盖效应，而修正后的pre-projection消融配合对数概率指标与匹配对照可得到高度稳定的头重要性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力头消融（将某个头置零并测量任务性能变化）是推断语言模型哪些组件对某行为具有因果责任的常用方法。但作者指出，这种推断可能十分脆弱，除非对干预语义、评估指标和控制条件进行严格验证；因此需要系统检验该方法的可靠性。

Method: 在GPT-2 small上比较两种"置零一个头"的实现：一种自然的post-projection实现，与修正后的pre-projection消融进行相关性对比及top-5重要头集合比较；同时对比二元准确率与gold-token对数概率两种评估指标。采用发现集/留出集划分，并进行1,000次匹配的随机头与层级匹配头对照抽样，检验排序稳定性与top-5头的显著性；另在DistilGPT2上进行复现。

Result: post-projection实现与修正的pre-projection消融几乎不相关（Pearson r = 0.057），且选出的top-5重要头集合完全不相交。二元准确率在行为下限和上限附近会掩盖效应，而gold-token对数概率保持渐进性。修正后的每头效应排序在划分间高度稳定（Spearman rho = 0.974），top-5头显著超过两种对照分布（Monte Carlo p = 0.001）。但任务特异性证据在GPT-2上不稳健。DistilGPT2复现保留了干预语义与匹配对照方面的结论。

Conclusion: 单头消融本身不足以支撑因果论断；要做出可辩护的解读，必须满足三个条件：正确的干预位置、非饱和的连续指标，以及匹配的留出对照。

Abstract: Attention-head ablation, zeroing a head and measuring the resulting change in task performance, is a common method for inferring which components of a language model are causally responsible for a behavior. We show using GPT-2 small that this inference can be fragile unless the intervention semantics, evaluation metric, and controls are carefully validated. A natural post-projection implementation of "zeroing a head" is nearly uncorrelated with a corrected pre-projection ablation (Pearson r = 0.057) and selects a completely disjoint top-5 set of important heads. We also show that binary accuracy can hide effects at behavioral floors and near ceilings, whereas gold-token log-probability remains graded. Using a discovery/held-out split and 1,000 matched random-head and layer-matched-head control draws, the corrected per-head effect ranking is highly stable across splits (Spearman rho = 0.974), and the top-5 selected heads significantly exceed both control distributions (Monte Carlo p = 0.001). However, evidence for task specificity is not robust on GPT-2. Replication on DistilGPT2 preserves the intervention-semantic and matched-control findings. These results show that single-head ablation does not by itself justify a causal claim; defensible interpretation requires correct intervention placement, a non-saturated continuous metric, and matched held-out controls.

</details>


### [200] [A First Glance at Jev for Network Traffic Classification: Accuracy, Processing Time, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00376)
*Shenghe Xu,Lifan Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在CESNET-QUICEXT-25数据集上，作者首次将通用决策模型Jev用于网络流应用分类：固定40个标注样例可将Jev准确率从9.80%提升至28.42%，但仍不及随机森林（69.95%）和Extra Trees（66.80%）；与GPT-5.6 Sol相比，Jev延迟更低、费用更省但精度无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用决策模型（如Jev）在推理与决策任务上日益受到关注，但尚缺乏将其用于网络流量应用分类的实证研究。本文希望检验此类模型在仅有少量数据包统计信息（前10个包的大小、方向、包间隔）时能否完成应用识别，并明确其相对传统机器学习与生成式大模型的位置。

Method: 使用CESNET-QUICEXT-25中10个数据集定义的应用标签，仅输入每条流前10个包的大小、方向与包间时间。评估通用决策模型Jev在训练期之后26个采集周、共52,000条记录上的表现，并以固定40个标注样例为基线，扩展至150个样例。同时训练随机森林与Extra Trees（8,000条记录）作为对照，并在100条记录的配对子集上与经Azure调用、高推理强度的OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol比较准确率、请求延迟与API费用。

Result: 40个固定标注样例使Jev准确率从9.80%升至28.42%；将上下文扩展到150个样例后首个测试周达34.50%。随机森林与Extra Trees分别达69.95%与66.80%，且在每一周均优于Jev。配对子集上，Jev（40样例）准确率29%、请求时间中位数0.750秒；GPT-5.6 Sol为37%与6.036秒，且Jev的API费用更低，但配对子集不足以证明任一服务的准确率优势。

Conclusion: 标注样例能显著提升Jev的性能，但所测试的Jev配置仍不及训练好的树集成模型。由于监督预算不对等且配置固定，无法将差距归因于单一原因；本文为通用决策模型用于网络流应用分类提供了首个实证基线。

Abstract: We evaluate Jev on ten dataset-defined application labels in CESNET-QUICEXT-25 using only the first ten packets' sizes, directions, and inter-packet times. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first empirical study of general-purpose decision models, represented here by Jev, for application classification of network flows. Across 52,000 records from 26 collection weeks following the training period, 40 fixed labeled examples raise Jev's accuracy from 9.80% to 28.42%. Random Forest and Extra Trees trained on 8,000 records achieve 69.95% and 66.80% and outperform Jev in every week. Increasing Jev's context to 150 examples yields 34.50% on the first test week. On a paired 100-record subset, Jev with 40 examples achieves 29% accuracy at a median request time of 0.750 s, versus 37% and 6.036 s for the generative language model OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol with high reasoning effort through Azure; Jev also incurs lower API charges. The paired subset does not establish an accuracy advantage for either service, and the timing reflects different service configurations. Thus, labeled examples substantially improve Jev, but the tested Jev configurations remain less accurate than trained tree ensembles; unequal supervision budgets and fixed configurations prevent attributing the gap to a single cause.

</details>


### [201] [STCFormer: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Modeling with Dynamic Cluster Transformer for Station-based Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00377)
*Rongwen Li,Haixin Xie,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STCFormer，一种自适应时空Transformer，在每个时间片段内根据站点局部演变动态分组，结合簇内局部注意力与区域全局注意力，并通过Lipschitz上界分析及InfoLoss设计，在八个气象预测任务上取得最优或次优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于站点的天气预报需要建模站点间复杂的空间依赖。近期基于聚类的选择性建模比密集的站点间交互更有前景，但存在两个问题：一是整个观测窗口共享的分组会掩盖站点关系的局部变化，二是仅靠簇内交互会丢失重要的全局上下文信息。此外，选择性交互相对于密集连接的理论优势也研究不足。

Method: 提出STCFormer，一种自适应时空Transformer，在每个时间片段内依据站点的局部演变动态分组。其Cluster-Guided Attention Block将簇内细粒度局部注意力与基于区域状态摘要的全局注意力相结合，使每个站点能获取自身簇之外的信息。同时推导出簇条件局部注意力的Lipschitz上界不大于全连接对应版本，解释其潜在鲁棒性优势，并据此设计InfoLoss。

Result: 在三个真实世界气象数据集、涵盖八个温度与风速预测任务上进行实验，STCFormer在所有八个任务上取得最低的24小时均方误差，并在48项指标与预测时长对比中有47项排名第一或第二。消融实验与案例研究进一步验证了局部自适应分组与互补的局部-全局交互带来的收益。

Conclusion: STCFormer通过局部自适应分组及局部-全局互补交互，有效提升了站点级天气预报的精度与鲁棒性，理论和实验均支持选择性交互优于密集连接的设计思路。代码已开源。

Abstract: Station-based weather forecasting supports daily life and economic activity, yet accurate forecasts require modeling complex spatial dependencies among stations. Recent clustering-based selective modeling offers a promising alternative to dense inter-station interactions. However, a grouping shared across an observation window may obscure local changes in station relationships, while intra-cluster interactions alone may miss important global context. The theoretical advantages of selective interactions over dense connectivity also remain insufficiently understood. We therefore propose STCFormer, an adaptive spatio-temporal Transformer that dynamically groups stations according to their local evolution within each temporal patch. Its Cluster-Guided Attention Block combines fine-grained local attention within clusters and global attention over regional state summaries, allowing each station to access information beyond its own cluster. We further show that a derived Lipschitz upper bound for cluster-conditioned local attention is no larger than its fully connected counterpart, explaining a potential robustness benefit and motivating the design of InfoLoss. Experiments on three real-world weather datasets spanning eight temperature and wind forecasting tasks show that STCFormer achieves the lowest 24-hour mean squared error on all eight tasks and ranks first or second in 47 of 48 comparisons across metrics and forecasting horizons. Ablations and case studies further confirm the benefits of locally adaptive grouping and complementary local-global interactions. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/STCFormer.

</details>


### [202] [Fusion techniques of time frequency-based images to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00380)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Mohammed Aledhari,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究用EEG数据预测rTMS治疗抑郁症的疗效，提出montage和blending两种融合技术从时频图像中提取特征，并设计轻量级CNN。在严格的被试间交叉验证下，几乎所有配置的表现都接近随机水平，表明此前的高准确率主要源于数据泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症治疗结果预测是临床难点，rTMS是常用的非侵入性疗法，但用EEG预测rTMS响应受限于被试间高变异性和单域特征不足。

Method: 提出montage和blending两种融合技术，从EEG时频图像中提取更丰富特征，并构建轻量级定制CNN在融合时频表征上训练。使用15人主数据集和46人次数据集，测试4种时频方法、6种融合机制、14种模型架构，进行两套实验：分段级10折交叉验证与严格被试不重叠交叉验证，并做患者级置换检验。

Result: 分段级交叉验证下Montage CWT_ST融合在主数据集达99.90%、次数据集达91.90%准确率；但在严格被试不重叠验证下性能崩塌，除一个例外所有配置的AUC在0.31—0.54之间且95%置信区间均含0.5，最佳独立方法置换检验p=0.703。最佳被试级结果为Montage CWT_ST在主队列上AUC 0.874±0.183、准确率82.7%。

Conclusion: 分段级交叉验证的高性能主要由同一被试数据同时出现在训练和测试中导致的数据泄漏造成；在严格被试不重叠评估下模型基本无预测能力，说明该任务仍具挑战性，需更严谨的评估协议。

Abstract: Depression is a mental condition that can lead to suicide and self-harm. Predicting the outcome of depression treatment is one of the most difficult tasks for clinicians. Among various treatment options, repetitive Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (rTMS) is a widely used non-invasive method. Predicting rTMS response using Electroencephalogram (EEG) data is difficult because of high inter-subject variability and limited features from single-domain analysis. We introduce two fusion techniques, montage and blending, to overcome these limitations and extract richer features from EEG-derived Time-Frequency (TF) images. We then propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained on fused TF representations. \textcolor{black}{We use a primary dataset of 15 patients and a secondary dataset of 46 patients. We run two sets of experiments. The first set uses segment-level 10-fold cross-validation. In this setup segments from the same patient can appear in both training and testing. The Montage CWT\_ST fusion reaches 99.90\% accuracy on the primary dataset and 91.90\% on the secondary dataset. The second set uses strict subject-disjoint cross-validation. All segments of a patient stay in one fold and no patient appears in both training and testing. Performance collapses. We test four time-frequency methods, six fusion mechanisms, and fourteen model architectures. With one exception, every configuration on both cohorts falls between AUC 0.31 and 0.54 and every 95\% confidence interval contains 0.5. A patient-level permutation test on the best standalone method returns $p = 0.703$. The best subject-level result is Montage CWT\_ST on the primary cohort, which reaches AUC $0.874 \pm 0.183$ and 82.7\% accuracy.

</details>


### [203] [OmniMed-Jev: Calibrating LVLM Confidence for Trustworthy Medical Multimodal Decisions via System One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00381)
*Luyao Tang,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OmniMed-Jev，将医学决策建模为对候选集的概率分布，显著改善置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态医学模型将诊断、发现或细胞计数等决策表达为生成文本，导致报告的置信度反映的是下一个 token 的概率而非决策本身，置信度与实际正确性不匹配。

Method: 受决策原生接口（如 Jev）启发，将每个医学决策表示为 Choice、Noul 或 Score 决策，作用于运行时提供的候选集，并返回该集合上的完整分布：互斥类别、发现的二值存在性或有序边界值。该设计在三个层面具备 omni 特性：接受多种影像模态、覆盖不同预测任务、通过统一的候选条件概率模型表达，使异构输出变为可比较的概率。

Result: 在与生成式基线（相同骨干、数据和训练计划）的接口控制对比中，OmniMed-Jev 的报告概率更贴合实际正确性，校准误差最多降低一个数量级，可靠性误差最多降低两个数量级，同时点预测性能相当；计数是生成式基线仍占优的唯一任务族。

Conclusion: 将决策分布作为模型输出并非格式变化，而是让报告的数字成为名副其实的概率。结果支持显式决策建模作为让置信度在评估任务内有意义的方式，但不构成临床可用性证据；对比无法区分接口与相关训练差异，需与结果一并说明。

Abstract: Medical models are judged not only on correctness, but on whether reported confidence matches actual accuracy. Generalist multimodal medical models have expanded what a single model can perceive, yet they still express bounded decisions such as diagnoses, findings or cell counts as generated text, so the reported probability reflects the next token rather than the decision itself. Motivated by decision-native interfaces such as Jev, we introduce OmniMed-Jev, which represents each medical decision as a Choice, Noul or Score decision over a runtime-supplied candidate set and returns a full distribution over that set: mutually exclusive classes, binary presence of a finding, or a bounded ordered value. The design is omni in three respects: it accepts diverse imaging modalities, covers different prediction tasks, and expresses them through one candidate-conditioned probability model, so heterogeneous outputs become comparable probabilities rather than task-specific strings. In an interface-controlled comparison against a generative baseline trained on the same backbone, data and schedule, OmniMed-Jev's reported probabilities track observed correctness far more closely, reducing calibration error by up to an order of magnitude and reliability error by up to two, while point-prediction performance remains comparable; counting is the one family where the generative baseline stays ahead. Making the decision distribution the model's output is not a format change but what turns reported numbers into probabilities that mean what they say. These results support explicit decision modeling as a way to make reported confidence meaningful within the evaluated tasks, and they are not evidence of clinical readiness: the comparison cannot separate the interface from associated training differences, which we state alongside the results. Code is available at github.com/lytang63/OmniMed-Jev.

</details>


### [204] [EvoGen-Harness: Learning Where and How to Evolve Image-Generation Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00383)
*Jiabin Luo,Yinan Liu,Chunlei Meng,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EvoGen-Harness 框架与 Trace 方法，在不修改生成器参数的前提下，实现多责任协同进化的图像生成"外部挂具"，并通过失败归因引导高效搜索，显著提升冻结 T2I 系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练式 T2I 改进方法通常在预设的单一维度（如提示词、路由、工作流）上做优化，限制了可纠正生成失败的空间；而允许多个"生成器外部责任"共同演化虽能扩大适应空间，却带来新难题：视觉反馈只能揭示"哪里失败了"，无法指示持续进化应发生在何处、以及如何高效探索该空间。

Method: 提出生成器无关框架 EvoGen-Harness，用于多责任图像生成挂具演化；配套方法 Trace（Trajectory-Relative Attribution and Coordinated Evolution）：1）跨随机执行聚合证据；2）将失败归因作为搜索先验，聚焦候选更新；3）逐步重新归因残余失败，以协调多个责任的演化；4）采用 No-Patch 与留出验证，避免无必要或有害的更新。

Result: 在 GenEval2、T2I-CompBench++、WISE 三个基准上，相较最强基线分别提升 +0.2633、+0.0720、+0.0752；归因召回率达 87.9%–91.4%，No-Patch 准确率 94.8%，回归率仅 1.9%。

Conclusion: 结果证明，归因引导的多责任演化能显著增强冻结的 T2I 系统，效果超出单维度适应方法。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image (T2I) systems can be improved without modifying generator parameters by adapting the external system around frozen generators. However, existing approaches typically optimize a predefined dimension, such as prompts, routing, or workflows, restricting the space in which generation failures can be corrected. Allowing multiple generator-external responsibilities to evolve provides a broader adaptation space, but introduces a new challenge: visual feedback reveals what failed, but not where persistent evolution should occur or how this space should be explored efficiently. We introduce EvoGen-Harness, a generator-agnostic framework for multi-responsibility image-generation harness evolution, together with Trace (Trajectory-Relative Attribution and Coordinated Evolution). Trace aggregates evidence across stochastic executions, uses failure attribution as a search prior to focus candidate updates, and progressively re-attributes residual failures to coordinate evolution across responsibilities, while No-Patch and held-out validation prevent unnecessary or harmful updates. Across GenEval2, T2I-CompBench++, and WISE, EvoGen-Harness improves over the strongest evaluated baselines by +0.2633, +0.0720, and +0.0752, respectively, while achieving 87.9-91.4% attribution recall, 94.8% No-Patch accuracy, and only 1.9% regression. These results demonstrate that attribution-guided multi-responsibility evolution can substantially enhance frozen T2I systems beyond single-dimension adaptation.

</details>


### [205] [FAER: Auditable Utility-Aligned Trajectory Replay for Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00385)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"选择—学习"差距，提出可审计的全轨迹回放框架 FAER，并构建学习者感知选择器 FAER-UTILITY，在 GSM8K 上验证学习者感知选择优于格式反馈与均匀选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
