Motivation: 现有回放选择器多依赖格式反馈、置信度、新鲜度或响应长度等启发式信号进行排序，而缓存层面的正确性与下游学习者的实际效用是两个不同目标，二者之间存在未被形式化的"选择—学习"差距。

Method: 提出 FAER 可审计全轨迹回放框架：包含免训练的固定选择器（协议基线）与基于不相交校准块拟合的学习者感知选择器 FAER-UTILITY；以归一化梯度对齐为基线，并用一次性的优化器感知虚拟更新提供幅度感知的效用曲面；审计契约在加入评估标签前冻结观测字段与回放轨迹。

Result: 在 GSM8K、Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、128 次更新下：固定选择器质量 0.6329，格式反馈 0.6037，均匀 0.5482；仅用元数据的交叉拟合校准达 0.6476±0.0139（8 个随机种子，中位数 0.6481，配对 95% 区间 [+0.079,+0.122]），目标运行 63,276 tokens，含校准总成本 189,642 tokens 与 3.48 GPU 小时；完整 FAER-UTILITY 行达 0.6624（62,844 tokens，4.26 GPU 小时）。格式反馈所选记录正确率 0.6953，远高于固定选择器的 0.3594，却对应不同的下游排序。

Conclusion: 缓存记录的表面正确率与下游学习者效用并不一致，学习者感知选择可显著提升回放效益；框架给出了免训练基线、校准方案、成本核算与零样本迁移等完整对比行，并强调评估前冻结轨迹的可审计性。

Abstract: Replay selectors often rank cached trajectories by format feedback, confidence, freshness, or response length, although cache-level correctness and downstream learner utility are distinct objectives. We formalize this selection-to-learning gap and introduce FAER as an auditable full-trajectory replay framework. Its training-free fixed selector is a protocol baseline; FAER-UTILITY is the learner-aware selector fitted on disjoint calibration blocks. The normalized gradient alignment is reported as a baseline, while a disposable optimizer-aware virtual update supplies a magnitude-aware utility surface. The audit contract freezes observed fields and replay traces before evaluation labels are joined. On GSM8K with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, the matched learner study reports quality 0.6329 for the fixed selector, compared with 0.5482 for uniform and 0.6037 for format-feedback under 128 updates. Metadata-only cross-fitted calibration reaches $0.6476\!\pm\!0.0139$ over eight seeds (median 0.6481; paired 95% interval $[+0.079,+0.122]$) at 63,276 target-run tokens; its recorded full cost is 189,642 tokens and 3.48 GPU-hours including calibration. The completed FAER-UTILITY row reaches 0.6624 at 62,844 target-run tokens and 4.26 GPU-hours. Format-feedback selects records with correctness 0.6953, compared with 0.3594 for the fixed selector, despite the different downstream ranking. The completed comparison surfaces report the learner-aware ablation, same-seed gap, policy-optimization rows, and strict zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [206] [T2SPO: Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00388)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Maoqi Liu,Feiran Li,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T2SPO方法，利用历史成功轨迹训练TabPFN回归器估计剩余距离，为LLM智能体的中间步骤提供信用分配，在ALFWorld和WebShop上优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中很多交互任务仅有最终结果奖励，无法指导哪一步中间决策有效。成功轨迹中的中间状态蕴含可用的监督信息，但现有方法未充分利用。

Method: T2SPO从成功轨迹中提取“剩余距离”目标，与访问状态表示配对，用预训练TabPFN回归器在新rollout的每个状态估计剩余距离。相邻状态间该估计值的变化作为步骤级辅助信用，与任务级监督共同训练。训练中新的成功轨迹持续刷新估计器上下文，无需更新参数。

Result: 在1.5B和7B模型上于ALFWorld和WebShop实验中，T2SPO相比GRPO持续提升整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: 利用历史轨迹的状态-剩余距离对应关系可有效提供步骤级信用分配，TabPFN的上下文学习使经验无需参数更新即可积累，是一种有效的LLM智能体策略优化方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables large language model (LLM) agents to learn multi-step behaviors through interaction with their environments. However, rewards in many interactive tasks reflect only the final outcome, providing limited guidance on which intermediate decisions advance the task. Successful training trajectories contain intermediate states that can provide supervision for subsequent interactions. We introduce Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization (T2SPO), a method that uses past interaction trajectories to provide step-level feedback for policy learning. T2SPO derives remaining-distance targets from successful trajectories and pairs them with representations of the states visited along the way. Conditioned on these examples, a pretrained TabPFN regressor estimates the remaining distance to success at each state of a new rollout. Changes in this distance estimate across consecutive states yield auxiliary credit for agent steps alongside task-level supervision. As training proceeds, newly completed trajectories refresh the estimator's context, incorporating new experience without updating its parameters. Experiments with 1.5B and 7B language models on ALFWorld and WebShop show that T2SPO consistently improves overall task success over GRPO.

</details>


### [207] [MatrixReward: Reward from Rubric Matrix for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00389)
*Zihan Shen,Qi Liu,Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Chenglong Zhao,Xiaozhao Wang,Lei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatrixReward，通过构建"rollout×rubric"胜率矩阵来生成开放式生成任务的奖励，在四个开放式问答基准上以 Qwen3-8B 取得平均 63.02 分，超过最强基线约 2.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式查询生成缺乏标准答案，需要有效的奖励机制。逐点评分细则（pointwise rubric）对同一提示下多个候选答案的相对质量信息有限，且将多个细则评分合并为单一分数可能掩盖答案之间的差异。

Method: MatrixReward 通过在每个评分细则下对所有采样回答两两比较，构建"rollout×rubric"胜率矩阵。矩阵每列的离散程度反映该细则对当前回答的区分能力，列间相关性揭示细则重复度，二者共同给出数据依赖的细则权重。将权重与细则先验权重结合，经列归一化与加权后，由各列观测到的最大值和最小值定义正、负理想画像，再根据每个 rollout 到这两个理想的距离计算相对接近度质量奖励。

Result: 在四个开放式问答基准上使用 Qwen3-8B 评估，MatrixReward 平均得分 63.02，较最强基线高出约 2.0%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持这样一个观点：由相对比较导出的矩阵可用于为开放式生成强化学习更合理地构造奖励。

Abstract: Open-ended query generation lacks standard answers, thus necessitating an effective reward mechanism. Pointwise scoring rubrics provide limited information about the relative quality of sample answers under the same prompt; merging multiple rubric judgments into a single score may also mask the differences between these answers. We propose MatrixReward, which constructs rewards from a rollout-by-rubric win-rate matrix obtained by comparing every pair of sampled responses under each rubric. The spread of each matrix column captures how strongly that rubric distinguishes the current rollouts, while correlations between columns reveal rubric repetition; together, these statistics yield data-dependent rubric weights. We combine these weights with the prior weights of rubrics. After column normalization and weighting, the observed per-rubric maxima and minima define positive and negative ideal profiles. Each rollout's distances to these two ideals determine its relative-closeness quality reward. Evaluated using Qwen3-8B on four open-ended query-answering benchmarks, MatrixReward achieves an average score of 63.02, outperforming the strongest baseline by approximately 2.0%. These results support the idea that matrices derived from relative comparisons can be used to construct rewards more reasonably for open-ended generative reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [208] [Interpretable Synthetic Medical Tabular Data Generation for Clinical Decision Support Using Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00391)
*Michael Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模糊认知图(FCM)用于合成医学表格数据生成，通过模糊集与因果边权重建模特征依赖，在CPU上实现兼具可解释性、因果性与隐私保护的合成数据，性能媲美或超过TVAE和高斯Copula。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床数据因隐私、伦理与可获得性限制难以使用，而现有概率与深度生成模型缺乏可解释性，难以保留临床上有意义的依赖关系，不适合安全攸关的医疗系统开发与验证。

Method: 用语言可解释的模糊集描述临床特征，以模糊集交集计算FCM边权重来编码特征间依赖；通过将随机初始化的语言激活向量在FCM中迭代传播至收敛，再经去模糊化生成临床一致的数值，原生支持混合数据类型与领域约束。

Result: 在UCI医学基准上按TSTR协议评估：心脏病数据集准确率最高0.81、AUROC最高0.90，媲美或超过TVAE与高斯Copula，且仅用CPU运行；KS Complement达0.91、相关相似度达0.95，DCR基线保护分数持续高于TVAE，隐私保障充分。

Conclusion: 因果驱动、可解释的模糊建模为CBMS中可信合成数据生成提供了计算高效且透明的替代方案。

Abstract: Synthetic medical tabular data generation has become essential for developing and validating computer-based medical systems (CBMSs) when real clinical data is restricted due to privacy, ethical, or data availability limitations. Existing probabilistic and deep generative models often lack interpretability and fail to preserve clinically meaningful dependencies, limiting their suitability for safety-critical applications. This paper proposes a novel application of Fuzzy Cognitive Maps (FCMs) in a framework for synthetic medical tabular data generation with explicit causality and privacy preservation. Clinical features are described using linguistically interpretable fuzzy sets, and inter-feature dependencies are encoded as FCM edge weights computed from fuzzy set intersections. Synthetic patient records are generated by propagating randomly initialized linguistic activation vectors through the FCM until convergence, followed by defuzzification to produce clinically coherent numerical values. The approach natively handles mixed data types, and domain constraints common in health records. Experimental evaluation on UCI medical benchmark datasets demonstrates competitive performance under a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) protocol. The proposed method achieves accuracy of up to 0.81 and AUROC of up to 0.90 on the Heart Disease dataset, matching or exceeding TVAE and Gaussian Copula baselines while running exclusively on CPU. Fidelity metrics including KS Complement (up to 0.91) and Correlation Similarity (up to 0.95) confirm strong statistical coherence, and DCR Baseline Protection scores consistently exceed those of TVAE, confirming adequate privacy guarantees. These results demonstrate that causally grounded, interpretable fuzzy modeling offers a computationally efficient and transparent alternative to deep generative models for trustworthy synthetic data generation in CBMSs.

</details>


### [209] [Forking: Sudden Overfitting Under Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00394)
*Shanbin Yu,Shaoyang Guo,Haoran Zhao,Danni Yu,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在 NanoGPT 自动研究中发现的"分叉"（forking）泛化失败现象：在数据回放下，带过度编码 n-gram 记忆分支的模型在 epoch 边界出现训练损失与验证损失的急剧分离，形似叉状。作者在受控的 vanilla NanoGPT 及 DeepSeek 风格的 Engram 模型中复现了该现象，并分析其机制与抑制条件，提醒人们谨慎对待自动研究智能体产出的"有用"结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是揭示深度学习中的又一奇特现象——分叉（forking），它在 NanoGPT 自动研究（autoresearch）中被发现，可作为 grokking 和 double descent 之外的新现象；同时提醒研究者：自动研究智能体能产出海量看似有用的技巧，但这些技巧可能带来意外且不良的副作用，需要保持警惕。

Method: 在受控的 vanilla NanoGPT 设置中研究分叉现象，并在带 Engram 的 DeepSeek 风格模型中复现。通过机制分析考察重复更新对训练续写与未见过续写概率的影响，分析 n-gram 模块所构建的弱交互、上下文特定子空间如何放大该效应，并检验低频上下文、训练预算规模及表拥挤程度对损失差距的贡献与抑制作用。此外，还在短预算、高度重复的 SFT 与类 RL 训练范式中观察分叉。

Result: 分叉表现为 epoch 边界处训练与验证损失急剧分离。机制上，重复更新会锐化训练中出现的续写，同时压低未见续写的概率，其损失随每轮遍历而增长；n-gram 模块产生的弱交互上下文特定子空间放大了该效应。低频上下文贡献了大部分损失差距，而更大的训练预算和高度拥挤的哈希表可抑制分叉。该现象同样出现在短预算、高度重复的 SFT 和类 RL 训练场景中。

Conclusion: 论文有两点贡献：（1）分叉揭示了深度学习中除 grokking 和 double descent 之外的又一奇特现象；（2）分叉是自动研究智能体所提技巧带来的意外且不愉快的副产品——尽管这些智能体产出大量看似有用的结果，我们仍应始终对其结果保持谨慎。

Abstract: This paper studies forking, a generalization failure discovered in NanoGPT autoresearch. Under data replay, models with an over-encoding n-gram memory branch show a sharp separation of training and validation loss at epoch boundaries, resembling the shape of forks. We study this phenomenon in a controlled vanilla NanoGPT setting and reproduce it in a DeepSeek-style model with Engram. Mechanistically, repeated updates sharpen the continuations observed in training while suppressing the probability of unseen continuations, whose loss grows with each pass. The n-gram module creates weakly interacting context-specific subspaces, amplifying this effect. Low-frequency contexts contribute most of the gap, whereas larger training budgets and heavily crowded tables suppress it. We also observe forking in short-budget, heavily repeated SFT and RL-like regimes. The contributions of this paper are twofold: (1) Forking reveals yet another curious phenomenon in deep learning, in addition to grokking and double descent. (2) Forking is an unexpected and unpleasant by-product of tricks proposed by autoresearch agents. While these agents produce an enormous number of results that seem useful, we should always be careful with their results.

</details>


### [210] [Specificity-Aware Diffusion Steering via Variance-Reduced Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00395)
*Luran Wang,Linrui Ma,Hannes Stärk,Regina Barzilay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将特异性感知引导（specificity-aware steering）建模为目标设计问题，基于重叠目标推导出目标分布，仅在两参考分布偏好差异足够大的区域保留期望分布，给出特异性的似然比解释；并提出方差最小化局部提议的SMC采样器与固定噪声优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时引导可让预训练扩散模型无需重训即可满足新约束，但特异性感知生成困难：从负参考分布排斥样本会侵蚀正负分布重叠处的正分布。核心挑战是在抑制负质量的同时尽量小地扰动正分布。

Method: 1) 将特异性感知引导形式化为目标设计问题，从基于重叠的目标推导出目标分布，只在期望分布相对非期望分布具有足够偏好的区域保留该分布，从而给出特异性的似然比解释；2) 为从对应的时间相关目标路径采样，提出带方差最小化局部提议的序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样器；3) 进一步提出实用的固定噪声优化过程，利用期望与非期望得分场的雅可比-向量积（JVP）。

Result: 在合成任务、类别对比生成、文本到图像任务以及肽-MHC（p-MHC）结合物实验中，相比负引导基线，本方法更有效地抑制非期望区域，减少模态偏移，并通过降低SMC权重崩塌提升采样稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过将特异性感知引导重新表述为目标设计并结合似然比解释、方差最小化SMC采样与固定噪声优化，能够在不重训扩散模型的前提下实现更精准、稳定的特异性生成，并在多类任务上优于负引导基线。

Abstract: Inference-time steering enables pretrained diffusion models to satisfy new constraints without full retraining. However, specificity-aware generation is difficult: repelling samples from a negative reference distribution can also erode the positive distribution where the two overlap. The key challenge is to suppress negative mass while minimally distorting the positive distribution. We address this problem by formulating specificity-aware steering as a target-design problem and deriving a target distribution from an overlap-based objective. The resulting target keeps the desired reference distribution only in regions where it is sufficiently preferred over the undesired reference distribution, giving a likelihood-ratio interpretation of specificity. To sample from the corresponding time-dependent target path, we develop a Sequential Monte Carlo sampler with a variance-minimized local proposal. We further introduce a practical fixed-noise optimization procedure with the Jacobian--vector products with the desired and undesired score fields. Experiments on synthetic task, class-contrastive generation, text-to-image tasks and peptide-MHC (p-MHC) binder show that the proposed method suppresses undesired regions more effectively, reduces mode shift, and improves sampling stability by decreasing the SMC weight collapse compared with negative-guidance baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/WangLuran/Specificity-Aware-Diffusion-Steering

</details>


### [211] [NEUROTOKEN: Joint Source and Directional AAD with Envelope Decoding via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00397)
*Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NEUROTOKEN 框架，用条件似然（流匹配）替代单一统计量来做听觉注意力解码，在多个数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代助听器和脑机接口需要判断听者关注哪位说话人（鸡尾酒会问题）。现有听觉注意力解码（AAD）研究割裂：方向型 AAD 只分类左右却无法映射到声源；基于回归的源 AAD 依赖在 1-5 秒窗口下噪声很大的单一 Pearson 相关；包络重建则缺乏原生 AAD 规则。作者主张真正的对象应是给定 EEG 下被关注包络的条件似然。

Method: 提出 NEUROTOKEN：单一网络中三个头共享一个 EEG 前端，其中条件流匹配头（ATTUNEFLOW）通过积分速度残差似然比来给候选打分。推理时用两个集成：QUADTRACK（四个互补统计量）和 ENV-FLOW（z 标准化的 QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW），以吸收各统计量的失败模式。

Result: 在 KU Leuven、DTU、NJU 上于 5 秒窗口，ATTUNEFLOW 相较最强非生成基线将每段源 AAD 提升 9%-16%，跨被试方差缩小约 3 倍；在三个数据集中有两个的试次级融合超过 93%。平行复现显示，经典 95-97% 方向 AAD 数字在严格试次不相交协议下下降 17%-45%。

Conclusion: 结果表明应使用基于似然的公式化方法，并明确了方向 AAD 的真实上限，同时验证了似然式源 AAD 的优越性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Identifying which speaker a listener is attending to in a noisy room -- the cocktail-party problem -- is the missing ingredient for next-generation hearing aids and brain-computer interfaces: it tells the device whose voice to amplify. Auditory attention decoding (AAD) reads this answer from EEG, but the literature splits into disconnected pieces: directional-AAD classifies side but does not map side to stream; regression-based source-AAD ranks candidate streams by a single Pearson correlation that is intrinsically noisy at the 1-5 s windows real devices need; and envelope reconstruction has no native AAD rule. We argue the right object is not any single statistic but the conditional likelihood of the attended envelope given EEG, and we make this practical with NEUROTOKEN: a single network whose three heads share one EEG front-end, with a conditional flow-matching head (ATTUNEFLOW) that scores candidates by an integrated velocity-residual likelihood ratio. Two inference-time ensembles -- QUADTRACK (four complementary statistics) and ENV-FLOW (z-normalised QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW) -- absorb per-statistic failure modes for free. On KU Leuven, DTU, and NJU at 5 s, ATTUNEFLOW lifts per-segment source-AAD by 9%-16% over the strongest non-generative baseline and shrinks across-subject variance by ~3x; trial-level fusion exceeds 93% on two of three datasets. In parallel reproductions we show that canonical 95-97% direction-AAD numbers collapse by 17%-45% under a strict trial-disjoint protocol, clarifying both the true ceiling and why a likelihood-based formulation is needed.

</details>


### [212] [WIPSNet: Deep Learning for Paediatric Wheeze Detection from Overnight Impedance Pneumography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00398)
*Felix Oury,Harley Day,Karina Mayoral,Ville-Pekka Seppä,Sejal Saglani,Reiko J. Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WIPSNet（基于小波尺度图的 3D ResNet），用于夜间阻抗呼吸监测的喘息分类，AUC 由 EVI 的 0.633 提升至 0.783。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床指标 EVI 将整段 IP 记录压缩为单一标量，信息损失大，夜间喘息分类 AUC 仅 0.633，性能有限，需要更能刻画夜间呼吸动态的建模方法。

Method: 对夜间 IP 信号做连续小波变换得到尺度图并堆叠为体积输入，使用 3D ResNet 进行学习；在 15 名受试者、60 个夜晚、281 小时的队列上，与 EVI、状态空间模型 Mamba 及两种现代睡眠分期架构对比。

Result: WIPSNet 取得 AUC 0.783 ± 0.026，优于 EVI、Mamba 及两种睡眠分期架构；性能在对应约 32 分钟时间上下文的体积深度处达到峰值。

Conclusion: 结构化的时频表示结合 3D 卷积架构，能有效从长时、不规则的生理时间序列中学习；多尺度时间聚合对建模夜间呼吸动态较为重要。

Abstract: Overnight impedance pneumography (IP) is used to monitor paediatric respiratory health. Its current clinical readout, the Expiratory Variability Index (EVI), compresses each IP recording into a single scalar and achieves an AUC of 0.633 for night-level wheeze classification. We introduce Wheeze Impedance Pneumography Scalogram Network (WIPSNet), a 3D ResNet operating on stacked continuous wavelet transform scalograms of overnight IP signals. On a 15-patient cohort (60 nights, 281 hours), WIPSNet achieves an AUC of $0.783 \pm 0.026$, outperforming EVI, a state-space model (Mamba), and two modern sleep-staging architectures. Performance peaks at a volumetric depth corresponding to 32 minutes of temporal context, suggesting that multi-scale temporal aggregation is important for modelling nocturnal respiratory dynamics. Overall, these results indicate that structured time-frequency representations combined with 3D convolutional architectures provide an effective approach for learning from long, irregular physiological time series.

</details>


### [213] [Metacognitive Reasoning in Energy Based Models using Instance Based Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00399)
*Tailia Malloy,Prateek Kumar Rajput,Serge Lionel Nikiema,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MERITED框架，结合基于实例的学习理论与能量模型的能量动力学，实现基于不确定性的元认知推理与计算资源动态分配，并公开了一个191M参数的推理EBM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的AI系统无法在生成输出前估计不确定性，也无法动态分配计算资源，因此难以实现人类式的元认知过程（如根据信心决定投入的时间与精力）。虽然能量模型（EBM）能提供可解释的不确定性建模与计算资源动态分配，但如何基于不确定性决定计算分配这一元认知任务尚未被直接解决。

Method: 作者提出MERITED框架（MEtacognitive Reasoning with Instance-based Learning Theory and Energy Dynamics），以实例学习理论（IBLT）为基础，通过控制EBM中的计算努力程度，在保持计算效率的同时实现对推理努力的元认知控制。具体工作包括训练并开放共享一个191M参数的推理EBM，以及实现一个使用IBL模型进行动态计算分配的MERITED框架。

Result: 该工作有两项主要贡献：（1）训练并开放权重共享一个191M参数的推理EBM；（2）实现了一个基于IBL模型的动态计算分配MERITED框架。

Conclusion: MERITED框架将IBLT与EBM的能量动力学结合，使系统能够在推理前基于不确定性进行元认知控制并动态分配计算资源，同时保持计算效率，为AI系统实现类人元认知推理提供了新路径。

Abstract: Metacognition involves reasoning about cognitive processes themselves. An example is in resource allocation where we choose how much time and effort to put into a reasoning task before we begin based on our confidence. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems that rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot estimate their uncertainty about an output without first responding, and cannot dynamically allocate resources to producing an output, making this type of metacognitive process difficult. A recently proposed alternative to classic transformer architectures that addresses these two concerns is the Energy Based Model (EBM) which allows for interpretable uncertainty modeling and dynamic allocation of compute resources. While EBMs can allow for control of these two processes, the actual metacognitive task of determining compute allocation based on uncertainty is not directly addressed. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides an approach to modeling human-like decisions from experience that has previously been applied to predicting human metacognitive reasoning. In this paper we introduce a framework for MEtacognitive Reasoning with Instance-based Learning Theory and Energy Dynamics (MERITED). Grounded in IBLT, this framework allows for control of the computational effort allocated in an EBM to allow for metacognitive control over reasoning effort based on uncertainty while remaining computationally efficient. This work has two main contributions, the training and open weight sharing of a 191M parameter reasoning EBM, and an implementation of the MERITED framework for dynamic compute allocation using an IBL model.

</details>


### [214] [Representation Transitions Reveal Emerging Safety Risks in Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00400)
*Haoyu Wang,Wei Zhao,Yedi Zhang,Christopher M. Poskitt,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART是一个无需额外模型和运行开销的运行时框架，通过去噪后的表征转移信号检测、归因并干预多轮智能体攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮攻击可将单独合规的动作组合成有害结果，令孤立评估动作或状态的防御失效。作者希望找到一种可检测的内部信号来应对这类攻击，并解决良性表征漂移对检测的干扰。

Method: 作者发现有害行为表现为跨上下文更新的累积表征转移，且触发上下文可由同一信号识别。为消除良性漂移的混淆，对对比安全方向进行去噪：将良性流量锚定为零并移除其主要变化方向。基于此提出DART运行时框架，检测并归因表征偏移，并以定向提醒进行干预。

Result: 在六个模型和两个多轮基准上，DART将MT-AgentRisk攻击成功率从84%降至25%（捕获全部攻击，平均误报率12%），将ASEval从97%降至52%（良性非拒答代价分别为8%和0%）。在MT-AgentRisk上，六个模型均优于SOTA防御ToolShield（同协议下仅55%）。去噪至关重要：ASEval上未去噪监控仅捕获7%-40%，去噪后为60%-85%。同一监控器无需修改即可覆盖单轮间接注入，每步仅增加0.14-0.56秒开销。

Conclusion: 累积表征转移是检测多轮智能体攻击的有效信号，去噪是发挥其作用的关键。DART轻量、无需辅助模型，可作为计算密集型推测防御的高效补充。

Abstract: Multi-turn attacks on agentic systems can compose individually permissible actions into harmful outcomes, challenging defenses that assess actions or states in isolation. We show that such attacks leave a detectable signature in the agent's internal representations: harmful behavior emerges as an accumulated representation transition across context updates, whose triggering context can be identified from the same signal. We further find that naive aggregation is confounded by benign representation drift, as a contrastive safety direction need not assign zero to benign transitions. We address this by denoising the direction, anchoring benign traffic at zero and removing its leading variation directions, with no runtime cost.
  These findings motivate DART, a runtime framework that detects and attributes representation shifts and intervenes with targeted reminders. Across six models and two multi-turn benchmarks, DART reduces attack success from 84% to 25% on MT-AgentRisk, catching every attack at a mean false-alarm rate of 12%, and from 97% to 52% on ASEval, at costs in benign non-refusal of 8% and 0%, respectively. On MT-AgentRisk, it outperforms ToolShield, the state-of-the-art multi-turn defense, on all six models: under the same protocol, ToolShield reaches only 55%. Denoising is critical: on ASEval, the undenoised monitor catches only 7%-40% of attacks, while the denoised monitor catches 60%-85%. The same monitor covers single-turn indirect injection without modification and adds only 0.14-0.56 s overhead per monitored step without requiring an auxiliary model, making it a lightweight complement to computation-heavy speculative defenses.

</details>


### [215] [The Conflict Between Logic and Memory: Learning Higher-Order Interactions in Shallow MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00403)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究单隐层MLP在合成任务上"拟合训练样本但未学到真实规则"的现象,揭示优化器与干扰输入学习如何约束浅层网络所能实现的高阶规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络可以拟合训练样本却无法恢复生成标签的规则,作者希望通过可控的合成任务(控制交互阶数与干扰输入)来刻画这种"记忆与规则学习"的分离。

Method: 使用单隐层MLP与合成任务:纯奇偶(parity)任务作为基准(无低阶预测边际、有精确贝叶斯后验、可由宽度k的ReLU网络在干净潜在输入上表示);进行匹配的2-4阶扫描对比SGD、Adam、Muon;设计混合阶任务,通过冻结/移除与独立干扰输入相连的第一层权重来干预;并进行偏置干预以探究目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示的关系。

Result: 2阶时SGD、Adam、Muon均达100%峰值测试精度;3阶时分别为96.25%、50.87%、76.82%,Muon在4阶达99.21%。混合阶任务中,冻结连接干扰输入的第一层权重使AdamW第10轮精度从44.73%升至95.07%,仅在测试时移除这些输入则升至48.38%。紧凑纯信号机制下,SGD与Muon均学到5-8阶,9-11阶时SGD峰值精度更高。

Conclusion: 优化过程与干扰输入的学习共同约束了浅层网络所实现的高阶规则,说明拟合数据并不等同于恢复底层规则。

Abstract: A network can fit its training examples while failing to recover the rule that generated their labels. We examine this separation in single-hidden-layer multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), using synthetic tasks that control interaction order and the presence of nuisance inputs. We establish elementary benchmark properties: pure parity contains no predictive lower-order marginals, admits an exact Bayes posterior, and can be represented on clean latent inputs by a width-$k$ ReLU network. Experiments then identify distinct optimization outcomes. In a matched order-2--4 sweep, SGD, Adam, and Muon all reach 100\% peak test accuracy at order two; at order three they reach 96.25\%, 50.87\%, and 76.82\%, respectively, while Muon reaches 99.21\% at order four. In a separate mixed-order task, freezing only the first-layer weights connected to independent nuisance inputs raises AdamW's epoch-10 accuracy from 44.73\% to 95.07\%. Removing the same inputs only at test time raises it to 48.38\%. Thus, nuisance-weight learning changes the training outcome beyond its immediate effect on prediction. Bias interventions expose a connection between target symmetry and shallow ReLU representations. In a compact signal-only regime, both SGD and Muon learn orders five through eight, with higher SGD peak accuracy at orders nine through eleven. Together, the results show how optimization and nuisance learning constrain the higher-order rules realized by a shallow network.

</details>


### [216] [RACE: Residual-Aware Test-Time Adaptation for Neighbor-Rich Time-Series Foundation Model Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00405)
*Hao-Nan Shi,Tong Wu,Chen-Cong Sun,Yuan Jiang,Han-Jia Ye,De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RACE框架，利用同域邻居历史序列的残差证据对时序基础模型（TSFM）预测进行测试时校正，无需修改骨干网络，在CGM与Web/云负载领域提升预测精度，并验证了跨骨干的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM在单序列预测上表现强，但其逐序列推理不适合"邻居丰富"的预测场景（如CGM、Web/云负载），这类场景中查询序列存在相关但不同的历史序列可参考。现有方法要么为每个目标域微调TSFM（成本高、跨骨干迁移性差），要么直接拼接检索序列而未验证其是否支持当前预测，主要难点在于邻居残差证据相互冲突、且残差模式随TSFM与任务变化。

Method: 形式化"测试时邻居缩放"问题：在不修改骨干的前提下利用同域邻居证据。提出RACE两阶段框架：第一阶段检索与查询兼容的邻居，将其残差对齐到查询尺度，聚合成一致证据得到免训练的RACE-TF校正；完整RACE进一步用轻量、领域特定的Gate决定何时应用校正有益，训练流程在不同TSFM骨干间可复用。

Result: 在四个TSFM骨干、两个主要域上，RACE均提升全部三个域聚合指标，在高误差查询上增益最大；同一域内，在一个TSFM上训练的Gate无需适配即可迁移到其他骨干，最终流程在匹配的冻结目标上改善了全部72个跨骨干指标对比。

Conclusion: RACE证明仅凭同域邻居残差证据即可在测试时有效校正TSFM预测误差，且门控机制具备跨骨干可迁移性，为邻居丰富场景下的时序基础模型部署提供了免微调、可复用的通用方案。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) perform strongly across forecasting tasks, but their per-series inference is ill-suited to neighbor-rich forecasting, where each query has access to related but nonidentical historical series. Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) and Web/cloud workloads exemplify this setting: CGM trajectories share physiological patterns but vary across individuals, devices, and conditions, while Web/cloud workloads combine common operating regimes with non-stationarity, heavy tails, and bursts. These histories share useful structure, yet neighbors are not equally relevant. Existing methods either fine-tune TSFMs for each target domain, incurring additional costs and offering limited transferability across backbones, or append retrieved series without verifying whether they support the current forecast. The key challenges are conflicting residual evidence from neighboring series and residual patterns that vary across TSFMs and forecasting tasks. We formulate test-time neighborhood scaling: using same-domain neighbor evidence without modifying the backbone. We propose RACE (Residual-Aware Correction of Forecasting Errors), a two-stage framework for using historical neighbors. We first retrieve query-compatible neighbors, align their residuals to the query scale, and aggregate coherent evidence into the training-free RACE-TF correction. Full RACE then uses a lightweight, domain-specific Gate to determine when applying the correction is beneficial, with a reusable training workflow across TSFM backbones. Across four TSFMs, RACE improves all three domain-aggregate metrics on both primary domains, with the largest gains on high-error queries. Within each domain, a Gate trained on one TSFM transfers to other backbones without adaptation, and the resulting pipeline improves all 72 cross-backbone metric comparisons over the matched frozen targets.

</details>


### [217] [VANDAM: Viewing a nucleotide sequence with DNA molecular priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00411)
*Jeremy Levy,Ariel Larey,Yury Nahshan,Raizy Kellerman,Elay Dahan,Amit Bleiweiss,Guy Leib,Omri Nayshool,Dan Ofer,Tal Zinger,Dan Dominissini,Gideon Rechavi,Marissa Wirth,Simon Lee,Dung Hoang,Noam D. Beckmann,Shane O'Connell,Nicole Bussola,Alexander W. Charney,Yoli Shavit,Nati Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VANDAM框架，通过引入DNA分子先验扩展基因组基础模型训练，在四种架构族和九项下游任务上稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因组基础模型（GFM）将DNA视为字符串，仅采用掩码token预测目标进行预训练，未显式建模生物功能所依赖的生化、结构和物理性质。由于这些分子性质可由序列通过已有生物物理模型估计，其价值在于为训练目标提供可显式利用的先验，而非独立模态。

Method: VANDAM在自监督训练中从池化表示预测区域分子性质；当存在功能标签且保留分子先验可获得奖励时，额外在输入端注入局部特征。该方法与基于token的目标互补。

Result: 在四种架构族和九项留出基因组任务上，VANDAM均稳定提升下游性能；探针实验表明分子先验的使用可泛化到其他未见过的分子性质。

Conclusion: 显式引入分子先验能有效补充token级目标，提升基因组基础模型的通用性与下游表现。

Abstract: Contemporary Genomic Foundation Models (GFMs) rely on a DNA-as-a-string paradigm that employs masked token prediction objectives for pretraining. However, this abstraction does not explicitly model the biochemical, structural, and physical properties essential to biological function. Many molecular properties can be estimated from sequence using established biophysical models, so their utility lies not in providing an independent modality, but in introducing priors that training objectives can explicitly exploit. We introduce VANDAM, a framework that extends the training of GFMs with DNA molecular priors. In self-supervised training, VANDAM predicts regional molecular properties from pooled representations. When functional labels are available and can reward retaining molecular priors, local features are additionally injected at the input. VANDAM consistently improves downstream performance across four architecture families and nine held-out genomic tasks by complementing token-based objectives. Probing experiments further demonstrate that the use of molecular priors generalizes to other unseen molecular properties.

</details>


### [218] [EchoPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Pruning via Virtual Context Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00412)
*Jiawei Lin,Saibo Geng,Thomas Bourgeat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EchoPress，一种免训练KV缓存剪枝方法，利用prefill阶段已有的query和key近似KVzip的重建分数，仅重建首个chunk来校准重要性，在LongBench和RULER上达到与KVzip相当的精度，同时将压缩开销降低1.7-19.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存占用大量内存，现有KVzip方法通过分块重建上下文来估计KV重要性，压缩质量好但需要额外的前向传播，开销大；而学习类近似方法又需要针对特定模型训练。因此需要一种既免训练又能降低开销的方法。

Method: 分析KVzip如何识别重要缓存信息，证明可用prefill阶段已计算出的query和key来近似其重建分数。基于此提出EchoPress：对每个请求仅重建第一个chunk，用以校准剩余上下文的重要性分数，全程无需训练。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER数据集上，使用Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型，在50%-90%的驱逐率下，EchoPress的任务精度与KVzip相当，同时将压缩开销降低1.7-19.6倍，总prefill时间最多降低2.9倍。

Conclusion: EchoPress通过复用prefill阶段信息近似重建注意力，实现了免训练、高效率的KV缓存剪枝，在保持压缩质量的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: KV cache pruning reduces long-context inference memory usage by evicting less important key-value pairs. KVzip estimates importance through context reconstruction: prompting a model to repeat the context chunk by chunk. This achieves strong compression quality at the cost of additional forward passes. Learned approximations reduce this cost but require model-specific training. We analyze how KVzip identifies important cached information and show how to approximate its reconstruction scores using information already computed during prefill. These findings motivate EchoPress, a training-free method that approximates reconstruction attention using queries and keys from standard prefill. For each request, it reconstructs only the first chunk to calibrate importance scores for the remaining context. Experiments on LongBench and RULER with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct show that EchoPress matches KVzip in task accuracy across eviction ratios from 50% to 90%, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 1.7-19.6 and total prefill time by a factor of up to 2.9. Code is available at https://github.com/ljwljwljwljw/kvpress/tree/echo-press.

</details>


### [219] [Do Better Scores Mean Better Physics? Physics-Grounded Explanations for Sim2Real Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00415)
*Somyajit Chakraborty,Xizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在NACA4418翼型绕流中，用CFD与PIV配对数据检验神经算子预测：低速度场误差并不意味着低流动统计量误差，二者可能不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型虽能加速物理仿真，但预测误差低未必等同于物理相关流动统计量误差低。论文旨在检验这一潜在脱节，并以翼型绕流为例探讨基准误差与物理诊断的一致性。

Method: 基于NACA4418翼型的配对CFD仿真与实验PIV测量；采用保均值输入干预，从观测流动历史中选定区域移除速度脉动；比较四个神经算子；并开展输出衰减压力测试。

Result: 移除最活跃10%有效观测单元的脉动，对预测的改变大于等面积随机移除；但由于掩码未对移除脉动能量进行匹配，该对比仅反映敏感性而非独立物理重要性证据。CNO速度场误差更低，但在两个分析子集上其双分量脉动能量误差显著高于参考。输出衰减测试也显示基准误差与域求和脉动能量不一致。

Conclusion: 单一基准结果提示应在总体预测评分之外报告互补的物理诊断指标；但这些结果并未确立反事实的物理正确性。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates accelerate physical simulation, but lower prediction error need not coincide with lower error in physically relevant flow statistics. We examine this question for flow around a NACA4418 airfoil using paired computational-fluid-dynamics simulations and experimental particle-image-velocimetry measurements. A mean-preserving input intervention removes velocity fluctuations from selected regions of observed flow histories. Across four neural operators, removing fluctuations from the most energetic 10% of valid observed cells changes forecasts more than equal-area random removal. Because the masks are not matched for removed fluctuation energy, this contrast measures sensitivity, not independent evidence of physical importance. Separately, a CNO has lower velocity-field error but substantially higher two-component fluctuation-energy error than the reference on both analysis subsets. An output attenuation stress test also demonstrates disagreement between benchmark errors and domain-summed fluctuation energy. These single-benchmark results motivate reporting complementary physical diagnostics alongside aggregate prediction scores; they do not establish counterfactual physical correctness.

</details>


### [220] [Source Identification Is Not Fitness Testing: Measuring the Limits of Synthetic-Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00417)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在金融风险文本上测试：生成文本的来源可追溯性在改写后大幅下降，且用来源信息选择训练数据与用参考模型打分选择数据，未检测到稳定的退化差异；说明"识别数据来源"与"识别有用数据"是两个不同问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型生成数据上的重复训练会导致后续模型退化。一个可能的应对办法是利用数据来源（provenance）来决定复用哪些生成样本。因此需要检验两件事：来源信息能否被可靠地恢复，以及它是否有助于识别更好的训练数据。

Method: 使用金融风险文本：(1) 先识别生成段落的来源，再在改写（paraphrase、style rewriting）后重复该测试；(2) 在生成与重训练的三轮迭代中，比较两种选择生成样本的方式——一种使用来源信息，另一种使用来自单独参考模型的分数，并观察结果模型的退化情况。

Result: 生成器归因在原始段落上准确率为 98.7%，改写后降至 53.1%，风格改写后降至 29.0%；而"生成 vs 人类"检测在测试的人类对照集上仍接近完美。两种选择规则选出的样本不同，但预设比较未能检测到结果模型退化上的稳定差异。

Conclusion: 识别数据来源与识别数据是否有用于训练是两个独立问题。实验区分了三件事：来源身份、面向准则的选择、递归训练结果；无论是来源分数还是所测试的面向准则的代理指标，都不足以确立对未来递归行为有效。

Abstract: Repeated training on model-generated data can degrade later models. One possible response is to use provenance when deciding which generated examples to reuse. We test both how reliably that provenance can be recovered and whether it helps identify better training data. Using financial-risk text, we first identify the source of generated passages and then repeat the test after rewriting them. Generator attribution is 98.7% accurate on the original passages but falls to 53.1% after paraphrasing and 29.0% after style rewriting. Generated-versus-human detection remains close to perfect against the tested human comparison set. We then compare two ways of selecting generated examples over three rounds of generation and retraining. One uses source information. The other uses a score from a separate reference model. The two rules select different examples, but the planned comparison does not detect a stable difference in the degradation of the resulting models. The results show that identifying where data came from and identifying which data are useful for training are separate problems. The experiment therefore separates source identity, criterion-facing selection, and recursive training outcome: neither the provenance score nor the tested criterion-facing proxy is established as sufficient for future recursive behaviour.

</details>


### [221] [CommunityKV: Efficient Long-Context Decoding via Graph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00418)
*Joe McKenna,Anastasios Alexandridis,Nathan Susanj,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommunityKV将稀疏注意力建模为社区发现问题，利用预填充阶段已计算的QK^T分数构建token图并划分为社区，实现语义连贯的token组检索，并通过局部更新规则支持流式解码，在长上下文任务中显著提升吞吐量且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文Transformer受限于自注意力的二次成本以及KV缓存内存传输的线性增长。现有稀疏注意力方法要么需要大规模训练，要么在免训练场景下依赖语义粗糙的启发式或难以在解码中高效更新的昂贵聚类。

Method: 提出CommunityKV框架，将稀疏注意力形式化为社区检测问题：从标准预填充中已计算的QK^T分数构建token图，并划分成社区以检索语义连贯的token组；引入局部更新规则，使新生成token以常数时间归入社区，从而在流式解码中实现稀疏检索而无需全局重新划分。

Result: 在Qwen3和Llama-3.1模型及三个长上下文基准上评估：每个查询头一张图时，端到端生成吞吐量最高达到稠密注意力的1.25倍；查询组图聚合方案最高可达1.71倍，且精度相当。

Conclusion: CommunityKV通过社区检测视角高效实现免训练稀疏注意力，兼顾生成吞吐量与精度，为长上下文推理提供了一种无需全局重划分的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention and the linear growth of key-value cache memory transfer. Sparse attention mitigates this by retrieving only relevant tokens, but current approaches either require large-scale training or, within the training-free regime, rely on semantically coarse heuristics or expensive clustering that is difficult to update efficiently during decoding. We introduce CommunityKV, a framework that formulates sparse attention as a community detection problem. CommunityKV constructs a token graph from the $QK^T$ scores already computed during standard prefill, and partitions the graph into communities to enable retrieval of semantically coherent token groups. A local update rule assigns newly generated tokens to communities in constant time, enabling sparse retrieval throughout streaming decoding without global re-partitioning. We evaluate CommunityKV on Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 models across three long-context benchmarks. With one graph per query head, CommunityKV delivers up to $1.25\times$ the end-to-end generation throughput of dense attention, while query-group graph aggregation yields up to $1.71\times$ with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [222] [The Life Cycle of a Massive Activation: Stochastic Birth, Weight-Decay-Driven Growth, and Competitive Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00423)
*S. Aaron McClendon,Jorge Gallego-Feliciano,Antonios Saravanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过训练轨迹分析与受控干预，揭示Transformer中巨激活（massive activations）随训练的出现、增长与固化过程，并证明权重衰减因果性地控制全局激活尺度的更替，提出AdamW预条件增长与权重衰减相抗衡的平衡模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨激活（量级远超常规激活的残差流坐标）与Transformer中的注意力汇（attention sinks）相关，但其规模在训练中如何被调控尚不完全清楚。作者希望追踪其产生、增长与固化的完整生命周期，并找出调控其尺度的训练动力学机制。

Method: 结合训练轨迹分析与受控干预：观察载汇通道在不同随机种子下的变化与早期稳定性；进行消融实验比较梯度衰减与单通道及汇token集体均方根幅度的关系；在受控续训中移除或保留权重衰减；建立AdamW预条件增长对抗权重衰减的平衡模型，并扫描衰减系数λ以检验峰值时机与峰值幅度的标度关系；辅以优化器测量。

Result: 载汇通道随随机种子不同但在每次训练早期趋于稳定；长期训练后周边通道退化、汇集中于少数冗余载体。梯度衰减跟随汇token的集体均方根幅度而非任何单通道。在峰值附近移除衰减使尺度继续上升，保留衰减则在恒定学习率下也出现下降。扫描λ显示峰值时机近似对数线性移动，峰值幅度近似按λ^{-1/2}标度，与平衡模型一致。优化器测量表明预条件可维持大通道群抵抗衰减。

Conclusion: 巨激活的生命周期与调节尺度的训练动力学密切相关，权重衰减是调控激活幅度的训练期杠杆；集体尺度（而非单通道）是理解其效应的关键。

Abstract: Massive activations, residual-stream coordinates with magnitudes far larger than typical activations, are associated with attention sinks in transformers, but how their scale is regulated during training remains incompletely understood. Combining training-trajectory analyses and controlled interventions, we trace their emergence, growth, and consolidation. Sink-carrying channels vary across random seeds but stabilize early within each run. Over longer training, surrounding channels erode and the sink concentrates onto a few redundant carriers. Across ablations, gradient attenuation follows the sink token's collective root-mean-square magnitude rather than any single channel, making collective scale central to understanding their effects. Our central result is that weight decay causally controls the turnover of global activation scale. In controlled continuations, removing decay near the peak allows this scale to keep rising, whereas retaining it produces decline even at constant learning rate. We develop a balance model for the rise and peak of massive-activation magnitude, in which AdamW-preconditioned growth opposes weight decay. Sweeping the decay coefficient $λ$ shifts peak timing approximately log-linearly and yields peak magnitudes scaling approximately as $λ^{-1/2}$, consistent with this balance. Optimizer measurements further show that preconditioning sustains the large-channel cohort against decay even when raw maintaining forces are too small to do so. Together, these findings connect the observed life cycle to scale-regulating training dynamics and establish weight decay as a training-time lever on activation magnitude.

</details>


### [223] [IrekoGPT: Turning Structured Pruning into Post-Hoc Slimmable LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00426)
*Pietro Moriello,Pietro Buzzega,Angelo Porrello,Simone Calderara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IrekoGPT 是一种事后方法，可将预训练 LLM 转换为宽度可在推理时调整的 slimmable 模型，基于 SliceGPT 保留投影矩阵以暴露嵌套子网络，并在 Llama 和 Qwen 上取得优于朴素 PCA 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要针对每个压缩率单独得到模型，缺乏一个能在推理时灵活调整宽度的统一模型。作者希望构建一种事后转换方法，使单个预训练 LLM 即可根据资源条件动态调整宽度。

Method: 基于 SliceGPT，保留其投影矩阵而不进行剪枝，使单一模型能在不同宽度下暴露嵌套子网络；通过在多个压缩率下对每一层进行校准来提升鲁棒性；并使用无梯度的岭回归（ridge regression）校正下游线性层。

Result: 在 Llama 和 Qwen 模型上，初步结果显示该方法相较朴素 PCA 剪枝方案有所提升，且在高压缩率下增益最大。

Conclusion: IrekoGPT 提供了一种有效的事后 slimmable 化途径，在高压缩场景下优势尤为明显，代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce IrekoGPT, a post-hoc method for converting pretrained LLMs into slimmable models whose width can be adjusted at inference time. Building on SliceGPT, we retain its projection matrices without pruning them, allowing a single model to expose nested subnetworks at different widths. We improve robustness by calibrating each layer across multiple compression ratios, and correct downstream linear layers through gradient-free ridge regression. Across Llama and Qwen models, preliminary results show improvements over naive PCA-based slimming, with the largest gains at high compression. Code is available at https://github.com/aimagelab/IrekoGPT

</details>


### [224] [XOR-Trellis: Ultra-Low-Complexity Dequantization and Curvature-Aware Hadamard-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00432)
*Xiaofan Que,Nir Elkayam,Spandan Pyakurel,Shuokai Pan,Dibakar Gope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM权重的超低比特Trellis量化部署问题，提出超高并行、低复杂度的反量化器与曲率感知的路径优化目标，实现无需Hadamard变换的高质量压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trellis量化可在超低比特下压缩LLM权重且无需指数级码本，但实际部署面临两大挑战：一是压缩权重的重建需足够并行吞吐以避免反量化成为推理瓶颈；二是需要在不依赖昂贵的不相干变换（如Hadamard）的情况下保持量化精度。

Method: 提出两项互补技术：(1) 超低复杂度Trellis反量化器，采用结构化、硬件高效的状态到数值映射，同时保留Trellis搜索所需的多样化重建选择；(2) 以曲率感知目标重新表述离散Trellis路径优化，直接在原始坐标空间中反映模型敏感度。

Result: 两项技术结合，实现了高质量的超低比特Trellis量化，具备低成本、高并行的运行时重建能力，且不依赖基于Hadamard的不相干处理。

Conclusion: 该方法在超低比特下兼顾了量化精度与推理效率，为Trellis量化在LLM实际部署中的瓶颈问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Trellis-coded quantization enables high-dimensional compression of large language model (LLM) weights at ultra-low bit widths without the exponentially large codebooks required by conventional vector quantization. Practical deployment, however, presents two challenges: reconstructing compressed weights at sufficient parallel throughput to avoid making dequantization an inference bottleneck, and maintaining quantization accuracy without costly incoherence transformations. We address these challenges with two complementary techniques. First, we introduce an ultra-low-complexity trellis dequantizer that uses a structured, hardware-efficient state-to-value mapping while preserving diverse reconstruction choices for trellis search. Second, we reformulate discrete trellis path optimization with a curvature-aware objective that reflects model sensitivity directly in the original coordinate space. Together, these techniques enable high-quality ultra-low-bit trellis quantization with inexpensive, highly parallel runtime reconstruction and without relying on Hadamard-based incoherence processing.

</details>


### [225] [Every Batch Is Its Own Validation Set: Leave-One-Out Gradient Matching for Online Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00436)
*Hongyu Chen,Xinyi Luo,Ming Zhao,Lin Tang,Zihan Xu,Jing Li,Yuxuan Wang,Haoran Deng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在线批选择中样本内梯度匹配为何难以超越全批训练，并提出无偏的留一法目标与 LOOM 方法，在多项微调任务上显著优于全批训练和现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线批选择旨在从候选批中挑选最有用的样本进行微调，其中梯度匹配型选择器因无需留出数据而具有吸引力，但实际很少能胜过全批训练。作者希望解释这一现象背后的原因，并设计出真正有效的选择方法。

Method: 作者指出样本内梯度匹配的目标函数将每个样本自身也纳入其匹配目标，导致目标函数给每个样本计入自身梯度噪声，形成协方差惩罚，位于梯度 Gram 矩阵对角线上，从而偏向噪声最大的样本。修复方法无需额外代价：对每个样本而言，其他候选样本构成数据分布的独立采样，因此去掉对角线后，匹配目标成为相对于总体梯度更新误差的无偏估计。该留一目标的最优解按梯度信噪比（SNR）加权样本；只要样本间 SNR 足够异质，半批即可获得比全批更低误差的更新，并给出精确条件。LOOM 在普通反向传播中于 Adam 预条件子度量下计算 Gram 矩阵，以贪心方式选择加权子集并获得 (1-e^{-γ}) 保证，且不使用留出数据。

Result: 在四个微调任务、七个 1.5B 至 8B 参数的主干模型上，LOOM 相比全批训练在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上分别提升 2.3 和 2.4 分，比所有样本内梯度匹配器高出 2.4 分，比验证引导的 GREATS 和 OPUS 高出 1.6–2.0 分，并以不到其基础率五分之一的比例选择注入的标签噪声。

Conclusion: 样本内梯度匹配的失败源于对角线的协方差惩罚，而留一法去对角线可获得无偏的总体梯度误差估计。基于该原理的 LOOM 无需留出数据，在多个任务和模型规模上稳定优于全批训练及现有选择器，同时对小样本噪声具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Online batch selection fine-tunes a language model on the most useful part of each candidate batch. Selectors that match the gradient of the candidate batch are attractive because they need no held-out data, yet they rarely beat training on the whole batch. We show why. In-sample gradient matching uses every example as part of its own target, so its objective credits each example with its own gradient noise. This is the covariance penalty that makes training error optimistic, now sitting on the diagonal of the gradient Gram matrix: it steers selection toward the noisiest examples and makes the full batch the best solution the objective can reach. The fix costs nothing. For each example, the other candidates form an independent sample of the data distribution, so removing the diagonal turns the matching objective into an unbiased estimate of the update's error with respect to the population gradient. The minimizer of this leave-one-out objective weights examples by their gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and whenever per-example SNR is heterogeneous enough, half of a batch yields a lower-error update than the whole batch; we give the exact condition. We build \method{} on this principle. It computes the Gram matrix in the metric of the Adam preconditioner during the ordinary backward pass, selects a weighted subset greedily with a $(1-e^{-γ})$ guarantee, and uses no held-out data. Across four fine-tuning tasks and seven backbones from 1.5B to 8B parameters, LOOM improves on full-batch training by 2.3 and 2.4 points on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, exceeds every in-sample gradient matcher by 2.4 points and the validation-guided GREATS and OPUS by 1.6--2.0, and selects injected label noise at under a fifth of its base rate.

</details>


### [226] [One pool, many targets: a conservation layer and what archival data can identify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00445)
*Zahra Khodagholi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可微的标量平衡层，用一个守恒方程统一 guide–transcript 打分的相互竞争效应，并给出唯一正根与隐式梯度；理论显示同一构建同一剂量下的排名无法区分平衡与独立打分，因此作者在公开脱靶数据上审计剂量与跨情境实验，发现耦合未带来可证实的额外预测价值，仅保留了描述性的 GapNet 量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规把 pairwise guide–transcript 分数独立解释为占据率，未考虑有限的、被 guide 加载的 RISC 池守恒，这在多靶点竞争时会产生概念与数值偏差。作者希望建立可微分、可解释的守恒机制，并检验其是否真的提升预测能力。

Method: 构建可微标量平衡层：单一守恒方程，具唯一正根与精确隐式梯度；由此推导重分布定理、合格的高资源极限、检索近似的分析，以及条件性秩不变性结论。随后在存档脱靶数据上审计剂量与跨情境实验，使用配对置换检验与构建聚类自助法，并分析剂量拟合的指数约束与竞争参数可辨识性。

Result: 修正后的热力学亲和力与实测抑制弱相关且方向符合可用预测器的要求；但配对置换检验与构建聚类自助法均无法证明耦合带来额外预测价值。不同置换零假设下仅存 GapNet——对平衡关联的聚类标准误的描述性 GapNetOverSE。剂量拟合异质且常违反模型隐含的指数约束（该约束为超线性而非次线性），数据无法识别竞争参数。对竞争者集合的可饱和压缩在留出 guide 家族上达到两项精度目标，但在所测规模下未更快。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个可复用的守恒算子，以及用于检验该算子的实验信息；当前数据尚不足以支持耦合带来的预测增益，需进一步实验设计。

Abstract: Pairwise guide--transcript scores do not enforce conservation of a finite guide-loaded RISC pool when they are interpreted independently as occupancies. We formulate a differentiable scalar equilibrium layer: one conservation equation with a unique positive root and exact implicit gradients. It yields a redistribution theorem, a qualified high-resource limit, an analysis of the retrieval approximation, and a conditional rank-invariance result: within one construct at one dose, rankings by fractional occupancy cannot distinguish equilibrium from independent scoring. We therefore audit the two experiments that proposition leaves open, dose and cross-context, on archival off-target data. Corrected thermodynamic affinities associate weakly with measured repression in the direction a working predictor requires, but a paired permutation test and a construct-cluster bootstrap do not establish added predictive value from the coupling: what survives their differing permutation-null baselines is \GapNet{}, a descriptive \GapNetOverSE{} of the equilibrium association's cluster standard error. The dose fits are heterogeneous and frequently violate the model-implied exponent constraint, which is superlinear rather than sublinear, so these data do not identify the competition parameter. A saturable compression of the competitor set holds both accuracy targets on held-out guide families but is not faster at the size measured. The contribution is a reusable conservation operator and the experimental information needed to test it. The code for this study is available at https://github.com/shadi97kh/One-Pool-Many-Targets.

</details>


### [227] [Exact information accounting for SGD methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00446)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用信息论方法精确分析SGD及其变体，将预条件SGD步视为高斯贝叶斯模型的后验均值更新，并给出一步遗憾的分解恒等式，统一解释多种优化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准几何分析之外，作者希望用信息论视角精确刻画SGD及其变体的行为，统一解释收敛、泛化、学习率调度、自适应优化器等现象。

Method: 将预条件SGD步解释为高斯贝叶斯模型的后验均值更新，推导其一步遗憾分解为"内在时间代价"与"比较器信息变化"两项，并将该分解扩展到目标函数本身的恒等式；在合成与真实训练中测量各项。

Result: 凸收敛、严格鞍点逃逸、平坦性与泛化联系、标准学习率调度、自适应优化器及噪声/动量/重尾/无梯度变体均对应恒等式的某一项或特例；真实网络中该分解将经典收敛界松弛归因于推导中被丢弃的项，并能区分达到相同训练损失的优化器。

Conclusion: 优化器差异与其步数及一致性相关，但何者泛化更好因网络而异；无梯度SGD中恒等式决定曲率预条件子的进入方式；基于锐度的泛化证书在网络尺度下（除非曲率谱近乎平坦）失效。

Abstract: As an alternative to the standard geometric analyses, we give an exact, information-theoretic analysis of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and its variants. We show that a preconditioned SGD step is the posterior-mean update of a Gaussian Bayes model, and that its one-step regret splits into an intrinsic-time cost and a change in comparator information. The split extends to an identity for the objective itself. Convex convergence, strict-saddle-point escape, the link between flatness and generalization, the standard learning-rate schedules, adaptive optimizers, and the noisy, momentum, heavy-tailed, and gradient-free variants of SGD each correspond to a term or a special case of this identity. We measure its terms on synthetic and real training runs. On real networks it attributes the slack of classical convergence bounds to the terms their derivations drop and separates optimizers that reach the same training loss. That separation follows the number and consistency of their steps. Its relation to which of them generalizes better differs between networks. For gradient-free SGD the identity determines how a curvature preconditioner should enter the update. The sharpness-based generalization certificate it yields, with a data-independent isotropic prior, is vacuous at network scale unless the curvature spectrum is nearly flat across all parameters.

</details>


### [228] [Score the Update, Not the Token: Descent-Aligned Routing for Combinatorial LoRA Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00493)
*Priya Nair,Lukas Brenner,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore,Wen-Hsuan Liu,Tom Saliencro,Amara Okonkwo,Rohan Desai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VANE，一种基于"对更新打分"而非"对token打分"的MoE-LoRA路由方法，通过reader-writer分解与低秩compass高效评估所有专家对的有效性，在多项基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture-of-LoRA-experts方法几乎都将路由建立在token打分上，即路由器在选择专家时并不知道各专家会写出什么更新；同时它们将输入侧因子与输出侧因子绑定，使得路由无法感知输出因子。作者认为路由器应该对"更新"本身打分，因为一个专家更新对损失的降低量，一阶近似等于该更新与输出处负损失梯度的内积，而这一有用性是token的二次函数，线性token路由器只能看到沿token均值方向的部分。

Method: 作者将每个专家拆分为reader（下投影）与writer（上投影），使得任意reader-writer对的有用性在共享的秩-r空间中成为内积，从而仅用N_A+N_B个向量即可为全部N_A·N_B个专家对打分。基于此，构建VANE：用一个低秩compass预测每个token的下降方向；在不显式构造任何更新的前提下，依据更新与compass的对齐度对所有专家对打分；以加性门控激活top-k对；并为每个对给出精确的一阶路由器梯度。

Result: 在单领域常识推理以及四领域多任务混合设置上，使用Llama-3.2-3B与Llama-3.1-8B，VANE在十二个PEFT与MoE-LoRA基线中取得最佳平均成绩，分别领先0.9–1.1分和1.3–1.5分，且可训练参数量不到8专家MoE-LoRA的一半。此外，其路由器得分比token路由器更贴近专家的实际有用性。

Conclusion: 通过对"更新"而非"token"进行路由，并利用reader-writer分解实现高效的全对打分，VANE在性能与参数效率上均优于现有MoE-LoRA方法，且路由器得分与专家真实有效性高度一致。

Abstract: Mixture-of-LoRA-experts methods raise the capacity of low-rank adaptation by routing each token to a few low-rank experts. Nearly all of them tie one input-side factor to one output-side factor per expert, and nearly all of them route by scoring the token: the router picks experts without seeing what any of them would write. We argue that the router should score the update. To first order, adding an expert's update to a layer output lowers the loss by the inner product between that update and the negative loss gradient at the output. This usefulness is quadratic in the token, so a router that is linear in the token sees only the part of it that runs through the token mean, and routers that rank experts by the norm of their own activations never see the output factor. If each expert is split into a reader (down-projection) and a writer (up-projection), the usefulness of every reader--writer pair becomes an inner product in the shared rank-$r$ space, and all $N_AN_B$ pairs can be scored from $N_A+N_B$ vectors. We build VANE on this identity. A low-rank compass predicts the descent direction of each token. VANE scores every pair by the alignment between its update and the compass without forming any update, activates the top-$k$ pairs with additive gates, and gives every pair its exact first-order router gradient. On single-domain commonsense reasoning and a four-domain multi-task mixture with Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B, VANE attains the best average among twelve PEFT and MoE-LoRA baselines, by 0.9--1.1 and 1.3--1.5 points respectively, with less than half the trainable parameters of an 8-expert MoE-LoRA. Its router scores also track the measured usefulness of experts far more closely than token routers do.

</details>


### [229] [Gumbel Straight Flow: Distilling Autoregressive Models into One-step Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00497)
*Yeongmin Kim,Arnaud Doucet,Andrew Campbell,Valentin De Bortoli,Thomas Mensink,David Ruhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gumbel Straight Flow (GSF)，一种连续流映射语言模型，利用预训练自回归模型的噪声-数据耦合实现少步采样，在多项基准上超越现有少步生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步语言生成方法在采样质量与步数效率上存在不足，需要一种既能保持高质量又能实现少量步数生成的语言模型方案。

Method: GSF基于预训练自回归（AR）模型诱导的Gumbel噪声与one-hot token序列之间的耦合，从理论上证明该耦合产生连接噪声与序列表示的非相交线性路径；并采用流映射半群目标，使切线（速度）条件直接由AR教师模型引导，以增强高质量的少步路径采样。

Result: 在包括预训练和下游任务在内的多项基准测试中，GSF的性能超过当前主流的少步语言生成基线。

Conclusion: GSF通过利用AR模型的噪声-数据耦合构建连续流映射，实现了高效且高质量的少步语言生成，为流映射语言建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present Gumbel Straight Flow (GSF), a continuous flow map language model that leverages the noise-data coupling of a pretrained autoregressive language (AR) model. We theoretically demonstrate that the coupling between Gumbel noise and one-hot token sequences induced by an autoregressive model yields non-intersecting linear paths connecting the noise to the sequence representations. To further enhance high-quality few-step path sampling, we use a flow map semigroup objective where the tangent (velocity) condition is guided directly by the AR teacher. Across various benchmarks, including pretraining and downstream tasks, GSF can outperform current few-step language generation baselines.

</details>


### [230] [Denoising Surface: Modeling and Predicting Inference Cost for Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00499)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Denoising Workload Surface (DWS)，用二维“块-步”概率曲面刻画扩散大语言模型的推理成本，并设计轻量 prompt-only 预测器，实现高精度、跨硬件可迁移的成本预测与调度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）正从研究走向真实服务场景，而调度与资源分配依赖准确的单请求推理成本估计。然而现有成本代理指标存在缺陷：输出长度忽略了单次前向可解锁多 token 的特点，去噪步数忽略了每步成本的异构性，二者都将 dLLM 的二维执行结构压缩为标量，丢失了刻画成本的关键信息。

Method: 作者观察到块自回归生成机制在输出块与块内去噪步上形成二维执行结构，据此提出 DWS（Denoising Workload Surface），将该二维块-步结构保留为概率曲面，用以加权异构的每步成本。随后设计从粗到细的训练方案，使仅依赖 prompt 的轻量预测器能准确预测复杂 DWS；该预测器可在单 CPU 核心上高效运行，避免与服务模型争用 GPU，并且由于 DWS 将请求相关的执行行为与部署相关的成本因素解耦，预测器无需重训练即可跨硬件配置迁移。

Result: 在真实服务实验中，DWS 相比基于标量的预测器将成本预测误差最高降低 2.50×；基于 DWS 引导的最短作业优先调度器在在线聊天机器人场景下将端到端延迟最高降低 1.92×。

Conclusion: DWS 通过保留二维块-步执行结构，为 dLLM 提供了一种准确、轻量且可跨硬件迁移的成本预测方案，并有效提升了实际服务中的调度性能与延迟表现。

Abstract: As diffusion large language models (dLLMs) become more capable, they are moving from research settings to real-world \textit{serving}, where request management (such as scheduling and resource allocation) relies on accurate estimation of per-request inference cost. However, common cost proxies fall short for dLLMs: output length ignores that one forward pass can unmask multiple tokens, and denoising-step count ignores the \textit{heterogeneous} per-step costs. We observe that the block-autoregressive generation mechanism induces a two-dimensional execution structure over output blocks and within-block denoising steps, whereas these proxies collapse it into a scalar, discarding information essential for characterizing the cost. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Denoising Workload Surface (DWS), which preserves this two-dimensional block-step structure as a probability surface to weight the heterogeneous per-step costs. We then design a coarse-to-fine training scheme that enables a lightweight prompt-only predictor to accurately predict the complex DWS. This predictor runs efficiently even on a single CPU core, avoiding GPU contention with the serving model. Since DWS decouples request-dependent execution behavior from deployment-specific cost factors, the predictor transfers across hardware configurations without retraining. In \textit{real-world} serving experiments, DWS reduces cost-prediction error by up to $2.50\times$ over scalar-based predictors, while the DWS-guided shortest-job-first scheduler reduces end-to-end latency by up to $1.92\times$ for online chatbots.

</details>


### [231] [One-Step Generative Modeling via Training Dynamics Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00518)
*Zhangyong Liang,Ying Huang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TDAction，一种基于训练动力学的传输目标选择方法，用于构建单步生成模型，在 ImageNet 256×256 上无需蒸馏即可达到 FID 低于 1.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一步生成模型通过迭代训练时传输构建静态生成器，但传输目标主要衡量分布层面的移动，而神经生成器需通过共享参数更新联合实现所有样本位移。由此产生核心问题：当训练本身成为构建最终一步映射的迭代过程时，应优化下一个分布移动，还是优化有限生成器学习最终映射的路径。

Method: 提出 Training Dynamics Action (TDAction)，依据局部共享参数实现代价选择传输目标，同时保留规定的分布进展。将代价形式化为软终端控制问题，推导出闭式 Batch Tangent Action-to-Go 值，兼顾参数努力与终端失配。该准则可捕捉独立成对代价遗漏的跨样本交互；在各向同性移动性下，与确定性平衡耦合的二次欧氏分配一致。随机切向探测提供低秩实现，构造共享分离目标而不增加推理时轨迹。

Result: 在受控实验中考察了生成器几何、传输选择与实现的局部动作之间的关系。在 ImageNet 256×256 上，TDAction 在无需蒸馏的情况下取得低于 1.1 的 FID。

Conclusion: TDAction 通过从训练动力学角度选择传输目标，改进了单步生成模型的构建方式，在无需蒸馏条件下实现了优异的生成质量。

Abstract: One-step generative models construct a static generator through iterative training-time transport. Existing transport objectives primarily assess distributional motion, although a neural generator needs to realize the requested sample displacements jointly through shared parameter updates. The training-time construction raises the question: \emph{once training becomes the iterative process that constructs the final one-step map, what to optimize: the next distributional move, or the route by which the finite generator learns the final map?} To address the question, we introduce \textbf{T}raining \textbf{D}ynamics \textbf{A}ction (\textbf{TDAction}), which selects transport targets according to local shared-parameter realization cost while retaining a prescribed level of distributional progress. We formulate the cost as a soft-terminal control problem and derive a closed-form Batch Tangent Action-to-Go value that accounts for parameter effort and terminal mismatch. The criterion captures cross-sample interactions omitted by independent pairwise costs; under isotropic mobility, the criterion agrees with quadratic Euclidean assignment for deterministic balanced couplings. Randomized tangent probes provide a low-rank implementation that constructs shared detached targets without adding an inference-time trajectory. Controlled studies examine the relationship between generator geometry, transport selection, and realized local action. On ImageNet $256\times256$, TDAction attains an FID below $1.1$ without distillation.

</details>


### [232] [SimplexUQ: An Evaluation Framework and Benchmark for Conformal Uncertainty on Simplex-Valued Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00523)
*Liang You,Hengyu Shi,Dongwen Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimplexUQ 是首个用于测量 simplex 值预测中 conformal 覆盖率分配问题的基准与可复现协议，通过 SimplexTasks-12 任务套件比较现有 conformal 包装器，发现全局校准可能掩盖分层覆盖不均，且没有单一包装器普遍占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conformal prediction 虽保证边际覆盖，但单一校准阈值可能使覆盖在不同区域分配不均，对易区分区域过度覆盖、对困难区域覆盖不足。该论文旨在提供首个针对 simplex 值预测的基准与可复现协议来测量这一分配问题，并比较现有 conformal 包装器而非提出新方法。

Method: 提出 SimplexUQ 基准与 SimplexTasks-12 任务套件，包含六个受控合成场景与六个冻结预测器的真实任务（类别概率、主题混合、光谱丰度、细胞类型比例、年龄分布、情绪混合）。每次比较固定预测器、评分和分层映射，仅改变包装器，报告边际覆盖、最差分层的覆盖、最大差异及任务内半径与计算量。还包括受控预测器偏差扫描。

Result: 全局校准可能看似有效却严重失败：在 CIFAR-10 上边际覆盖达 0.900，但最差熵分层仅为 0.542；Mondrian 校准将该分层提升至 0.886，同时将最大差异从 0.358 降至 0.022。没有包装器占优：平滑合成异质性下多种修复方法竞争力相当；固定映射分析显示排名依赖评估分组与协议；在 12 任务比较中，Mondrian 在其单一目标分区上对全部 12 个任务差异更低，而 BatchMVP 在重叠组上对五个任务差异更低。受控偏差扫描显示去除预测器偏差只能部分降低全局阈值差异。

Conclusion: 这些是实证比较而非新的覆盖保证。论文发布任务卡、结果溯源、允许的派生数组和重建指令，并将包装器选择视为诊断比较而非通用排名。

Abstract: Conformal prediction guarantees marginal coverage, but a single calibration threshold can still spread that coverage unevenly, over-covering easy regions and under-covering hard ones. SimplexUQ is, to our knowledge, the first benchmark and reproducible protocol for measuring this allocation problem on simplex-valued predictions; it compares existing conformal wrappers rather than proposing a new one. Its task suite, SimplexTasks-12, combines six controlled synthetic regimes with six frozen-predictor real tasks spanning class probabilities, topic mixtures, spectral abundances, cell-type fractions, age distributions, and emotion mixtures. Each comparison fixes the predictor, score, and response-free stratification map, varies only the wrapper, and reports marginal coverage, worst-stratum coverage, max disparity, and within-task radius and compute. Global calibration can look valid while failing badly: on CIFAR-10 it attains 0.900 marginal coverage but only 0.542 in the worst entropy stratum, and Mondrian calibration raises that stratum to 0.886 while reducing max disparity from 0.358 to 0.022. No wrapper dominates, however. Under smooth synthetic heterogeneity, several repairs are competitive; fixed-map analyses show that rankings depend on the evaluation groups and protocol; and in a 12-task comparison, Mondrian has lower disparity on its single target partition for all 12 tasks, whereas BatchMVP has lower disparity over overlapping groups on five. These are empirical comparisons, not new coverage guarantees. A controlled predictor-bias sweep shows that removing predictor bias only partly reduces global-threshold disparity. We release task cards, result provenance, permitted derived arrays, and rebuild instructions, and treat wrapper selection as a diagnostic comparison rather than a universal ranking.

</details>


### [233] [Random Recursive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00541)
*Jama Hussein Mohamud,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机递归模型（RRM），通过维护一个可学习的层池并在每个样本、每个递归步骤中独立有放回地采样层，实现灵活的参数复用，在推理任务上以更少参数达到或超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归模型通过参数复用来构建计算深度，是提升模型规模的一种参数高效替代方案。但现有递归模型大多重复应用单一的已学习变换或固定的变换序列，将计算限制在固定的层顺序中，缺乏灵活性。

Method: RRM 维护一个包含 L 个已学习层的层池，通过 T 个递归步骤执行计算，其中每个样本的每一步都独立地、有放回地采样一个层。这样在保留递归参数效率的同时实现了灵活的层复用。此外，RRM 支持蒙特卡洛推理与概率式测试时扩展。

Result: 在具有挑战性的推理任务上，RRM 达到或超越基线，且通常参数减少 50-75%。RRM 可在推理时改变深度（包括超出训练时见过的深度），无需重新训练或增加参数，从而改善受益于更深迭代计算的任务。蒙特卡洛推理与概率式测试时扩展也能在不重新训练的情况下提升性能。

Conclusion: RRM 通过随机层采样实现了灵活且参数高效的递归计算，支持推理时深度变化与概率式测试时扩展，为神经网络架构设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Recursive models create computational depth through parameter reuse, offering a parameter-efficient alternative to increasing model size. However, most recursive models repeatedly apply one learned transformation or a prescribed sequence of transformations, restricting computation to a fixed layer order. We introduce the Random Recursive Model (RRM), which maintains a pool of $L$ learned layers and performs $T$ recursive steps by sampling one layer independently with replacement for each example and step. This enables flexible layer reuse while retaining the parameter efficiency of recurrence. We evaluate RRM on challenging reasoning tasks, where it matches or exceeds the baselines, often with 50-75 % fewer parameters. RRM can vary its depth at inference, including beyond that seen during training, without retraining or adding parameters, improving tasks that benefit from deeper iterative computation. RRM also supports Monte Carlo inference and probabilistic test-time scaling, both of which improve performance without retraining. These insights may open new directions in neural network architecture design.

</details>


### [234] [Geometry-Dependent Bounds for Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00545)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非负非单调DR-次模函数在紧凸下闭集上的对抗在线最大化问题，证明了一个比较器一致的一阶不等式，将在线基准系数从0.401提升到4/9，并给出匹配的下界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线最大化非单调DR-次模函数时，已有方法的近似系数较低（如0.401），亟需更优的在线算法及其紧致性分析，同时需要理解离线预言机的本质极限。

Method: 通过有序坐标论证构造与目标无关的有理动作，证明比较器一致的一阶不等式，得到系数4/9及关于ζ的改进形式；同时用三组对称间隙构造给出离线预言机上界β；并对系数揭示多项式、常数目标序列、自适应对手与值反馈设置分别给出算法与保证。

Result: 在线系数提升至4/9（改进0.401），每轮一次梯度查询和一次投影，期望近似遗憾O(√T)；ζ≥1/2时上下界在1/2处匹配，最优缺口为Θ((1/2−ζ)^2)；离线上界β=0.470438...；二次多项式系数1/2，三次从8/17起；常数目标序列可得(4/9−ε)近似；值反馈下遗憾O(T^{3/4})。

Conclusion: 该工作显著提升了非单调DR-次模在线最大化的理论保证，并刻画了在线与离线系数随ζ的精确变化规律，为后续研究提供了紧致基准。

Abstract: We study adversarial online maximization of nonnegative, non-monotone DR-submodular functions over compact convex down-closed sets. A learner commits each action before observing its objective and competes with the best fixed action in hindsight. We prove a comparator-uniform first-order inequality that gives coefficient $4/9$, improving the online $0.401$ benchmark, with one gradient query and one projection per round and $O(\sqrt T)$ expected approximate regret. If $ζ{\bf 1} \in K\subseteq[0,1]^d$, the coefficient improves to $\underlineα(ζ)=\tfrac12-(1-2ζ)_+^2/[2(3-2ζ)^2]$. The proof is a direct ordered-coordinate argument with an objective-independent rational action. Conversely, a three-group symmetry-gap construction yields an offline oracle upper bound $β_*=0.470438681380894\ldots$ at $ζ=0$, even with exact value and full-gradient responses. A parameterized extension and exact finite-instance bounds define an upper function for every $ζ$. The lower and upper bounds match at $1/2$ for $ζ\ge1/2$, and show that the optimal deficit from $1/2$ is $Θ((1/2-ζ)^2)$ as $ζ\uparrow1/2$. For coefficient-revealed polynomials we obtain $1/2$ for quadratics and a geometry-dependent cubic coefficient starting at $8/17$, including $0.49$ at $ζ=1/5$. A constant objective sequence yields an offline $(4/9-\varepsilon)$ approximation with polynomially many first-order queries on the cube and projections, without requiring a supplied positive lower bound on the optimum. We also give nonanticipating adaptive-adversary and value-feedback guarantees, including $O(T^{3/4})$ regret with one noisy value per round.

</details>


### [235] [Evaluating Hybrid Quantum-Classical Models for Reduced-Order Brain Deformation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00554)
*Tao Liu,Ge He,Dongyu Liang,Wujie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了混合量子-经典机器学习在时空脑变形场降阶预测中的表现，发现经典网络在当前设置下仍优于量子变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维位移场的计算难以处理，需要降阶方法；同时需要评估近term量子机器学习（QML）在物理场学习中的实际能力，并与经典方法进行严格对比。

Method: 使用本征正交分解（POD）将数据投影到紧凑潜空间，构建两种学习目标：静态时间到潜变量回归和自回归潜状态预测。系统性地将紧凑经典基线与最小及增强型混合量子架构进行基准对比，包括VQC与增强VQC、经典LSTM与增强QLSTM。

Result: 静态回归中，经典POD-MLP优于所有量子变体，但增强型VQC显著优于最小VQC基线；时序预测中，经典POD-LSTM在不同历史窗口和随机初始化下均比增强QLSTM具有更高的预测精度和统计鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了降阶物理场学习作为近期QML的严格测试平台；混合增强能成功恢复弱量子电路的表达能力，但经典架构在保真度和稳定性上仍保持明显优势。

Abstract: We evaluate hybrid quantum-classical machine learning for the reduced-order prediction of spatiotemporal brain deformation fields. To mitigate the computational intractability of high-dimensional displacement fields, we employ Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) to project the data into a compact latent space. Within this framework, we formulate two distinct learning objectives: static temporal-to-latent regression and autoregressive latent state forecasting. We systematically benchmark compact classical baselines against both minimal and enhanced hybrid quantum architectures. Our results demonstrate that classical networks provide the strongest baselines in the present setting. For static regression, a classical POD-MLP outperforms all evaluated quantum variants, although an enhanced Variational Quantum Circuit (VQC) substantially improves upon a minimal VQC baseline. For temporal forecasting, a classical POD-LSTM delivers superior predictive accuracy and statistical robustness compared to an enhanced Quantum LSTM (QLSTM) across varying history windows and random initializations. Overall, this study establishes reduced-order physical field learning as a rigorous testbed for near-term QML, highlighting that while hybrid enhancements successfully recover expressivity in weak quantum circuits, classical architectures retain a definitive advantage in both fidelity and stability.

</details>


### [236] [Redundancy Meets Synergy: Dependency-aware Expert Selection for MoE via Submodular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00558)
*Zheng Lin,Shaoke Fang,Yuxin Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Zihan Fang,Zhe Chen,Wei Ni,Jun Luo,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DS-MoE 框架，用差子模（DS）优化重新定义 MoE 专家选择，兼顾冗余削减与协同增强，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型虽能通过稀疏激活扩展容量，但面临显存瓶颈，需提取紧凑的专家子集；而现有专家选择启发式多依赖 Top-k 排序，孤立评估单个专家，忽略了 MoE 门控网络带来的专家间复杂依赖关系。

Method: 通过分析损失退化的二阶泰勒展开，揭示专家组合中冗余（表示重叠）与协同（互补误差抵消）的功能对偶性；将选择目标形式化为差子模函数，从数学上解耦冗余削减与协同最大化；并设计带单调性保证的 majorization-minimization（MM）算法高效求解最优专家子集。

Result: 大量实验表明，DS-MoE 能有效保留不可或缺的专家组合，性能优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: DS-MoE 提供了一种理论扎实的专家选择框架，通过 DS 优化与 MM 算法解决专家间依赖建模问题，在压缩 MoE 显存的同时保持优异性能。

Abstract: While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models effectively scale model capacity through sparse activation, their deployment is often bottlenecked by prohibitive memory requirements. Extracting a compact subset of experts presents a promising solution. However, existing expert selection heuristics predominantly rely on Top-k ranking, which isolates the evaluation of individual experts and ignores the intricate inter-expert dependencies introduced by the MoE gating network. In this paper, we propose DS-MoE, a theoretically grounded framework that redefines expert selection via difference-of-submodular (DS) optimization. By analyzing the second-order Taylor expansion of the loss degradation, we reveal functional duality within expert combinations: redundancy (where experts encode overlapping representations) and synergy (where experts provide complementary error cancellation). To navigate this duality, we mathematically decouple redundancy reduction from synergy maximization by formulating the selection objective as a DS function. Furthermore, we devise a tailored majorization-minimization (MM) algorithm with provable monotonicity guarantees to efficiently identify the optimal expert subset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DS-MoE effectively preserves indispensable expert combinations, achieving superior performance compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Affine Transformations: A Soft Dominance Layer for Coordinate-Wise Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00563)
*Mariano Rivera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初探一种替代仿射变换的Soft Dominance Layer，各输出单元通过与可学习参考向量比较并聚合平滑不等式响应，Sigmoid松弛保证可微、锐度参数α控制硬阈值倾向。单次MNIST实验中最高精度0.9061（无退火）、0.9173（带退火），低于MLP基线0.9827，学习到的参考向量呈空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索仿射变换之外的可替代原语，检验其在可训练性与直接阈值解释方面的潜力，而非宣称取代仿射层。

Method: 提出Soft Dominance Layer：每个输出单元与可学习参考向量比较输入坐标，聚合平滑不等式响应；用Sigmoid松弛实现可微，锐度参数α调节向硬阈值决策的过渡。

Result: 单次MNIST实验中Soft Dominance最高精度为0.9061（无退火）与0.9173（带退火），MLP基线为0.9827；学习到的参考向量呈现空间结构，提供结构化学习的定性证据。

Conclusion: 结果仅为描述性，不足以确立可靠的配置排序或统计显著的退火收益，需多种子重复实验与更广数据集来评估鲁棒性与实际价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary study of an alternative to the affine transformation underlying conventional neural-network layers. In the proposed Soft Dominance Layer, each output unit compares input coordinates with a learnable reference vector and aggregates smooth inequality responses. A sigmoid relaxation makes the comparisons differentiable, while a sharpness parameter $α$ controls their transition toward hard threshold decisions. The aim is to examine the trainability and direct threshold interpretation of this primitive, not to claim a replacement for affine layers. In single-run MNIST experiments, the highest observed Soft Dominance accuracy is $0.9061$ without annealing and $0.9173$ with annealing, compared with $0.9827$ for the MLP baseline. These descriptive results do not establish reliable configuration rankings or a statistically supported annealing benefit. Learned reference vectors exhibit spatial structure, providing qualitative evidence of structured learning. Repeated-seed experiments and broader datasets are required to assess robustness and practical relevance beyond this proof of concept.

</details>


### [238] [Attention Kernels for Learning Maps Between Heavy-Tailed Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00564)
*Kailen Hargenrader,Edoardo Calvello,Bohan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在概率测度上进行算子学习时，针对重尾测度下softmax注意力积分发散问题，提出使用增长更慢的注意力核，并通过两个闭式解基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在概率测度上做算子学习可用transformer实现。但对于具有多项式尾部的测度，softmax中的指数加权会导致测度级注意力积分发散，因此需要将指数函数替换为增长更慢的函数。

Method: 构建两个带闭式目标的、面向重尾测度算子学习的基准；用这两个基准研究post-norm transformer中的注意力核增长与数据变换；对比softmax与三种增长更慢的核，并考察symlog预处理的作用，同时分析样本量对经验能量与Wasserstein距离关于尾部差异敏感性的影响。

Result: 不做数据变换时，softmax模型在两个重尾基准上均出现ensemble collapse，而三种慢增长核避免崩溃；symlog预处理使softmax在矩阵求逆任务上避免崩溃，但在sheared swap任务上无效；在高斯控制任务上四种核表现相似。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将增长更慢的注意力核作为post-norm transformer在重尾集合上学习的有效设计选择。

Abstract: Operator learning on probability measures can be accomplished with transformers. For measures with polynomial tails, the exponential weighting in softmax can make the corresponding measure-level attention integrals diverge. This motivates replacing the exponential with slower-growing functions. We construct two benchmarks for operator learning on measures with closed-form targets. We use these benchmarks to study attention kernel growth and data transformation in post-norm transformers. Without data transformation, the softmax models exhibit ensemble collapse on both heavy-tailed benchmarks, while the three slower-growing kernels avoid collapse. Symlog preprocessing allows softmax to avoid collapse on the matrix inverse task but not on the sheared swap task. On the Gaussian control, all four kernels perform similarly. We also examine how sample size affects the sensitivity of empirical energy and Wasserstein distances to tail differences. These results support slower-growing attention kernels as an effective design choice for post-norm transformers learning from heavy-tailed ensembles.

</details>


### [239] [Make Sparse Rewards Count: Density-Aware Reward Aggregation for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00574)
*Tong Zheng,Skylar Zhai,Zhan Cheng,TianMing Sha,Youling Huang,Shuo Zhou,Shaotong Qi,Jingcheng Liang,Xuwei Ding,Pengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出DARA（密度感知奖励聚合），通过在批次级别根据奖励的活跃组密度用逆平方根修正来重新加权各奖励信号，从而缓解GDPO中不同目标学习进度不均的问题，加快多目标行为习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励强化学习让大模型同时满足多个行为目标，但即便使用GDPO式的奖励维度归一化保留了组内相对信息，不同目标的学习进度仍不均衡。作者希望找到这种不均衡的批次级信号来源，并据此校准各奖励的贡献。

Method: 作者以“优势能量”（某奖励在批次上平方优势之和）为分析工具，在理想化GDPO归一化下证明该能量正比于活跃组密度，即该奖励产生非零相对优势的rollout组比例，从而揭示残余的批次级信号不平衡。基于此提出DARA：从每个rollout批次实时计算密度，并施加逆平方根密度修正，让较少活跃的奖励获得更大权重；该方法不改变底层策略优化目标。

Result: 在工具调用和数学推理任务上，DARA比GDPO更快学到目标行为：工具调用中最多减少26%训练步数即达到高格式合规率，数学推理中最多减少65%步数即接近饱和长度合规，同时最终性能保持有竞争力。

Conclusion: 多奖励训练中的学习不均衡可归因于活跃组密度造成的批次级信号失衡；用DARA的逆平方根密度加权可在不改变优化目标的前提下自适应校准奖励贡献，显著加快多目标行为收敛。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-reward reinforcement learning trains large language models to satisfy multiple behavioral objectives simultaneously. Reward-wise normalization, as used in GDPO, preserves reward-specific relative information within rollout groups, but different objectives can still exhibit uneven learning progress. We study this behavior through advantage energy, the sum of a reward's squared advantages over a batch. Under idealized GDPO normalization, we show that this energy is proportional to active-group density: the fraction of rollout groups in which the reward provides nonzero relative advantages. This reveals a residual batch-level signal imbalance and provides a basis for calibrating reward contributions. Based on this relation, we propose Density-Aware Reward Aggregation (DARA). We derive an inverse-square-root density correction that gives greater weight to signals from less frequently active rewards. DARA computes its weights from each rollout batch, adapting to changes in reward activity throughout training without modifying the underlying policy optimization objective. Experiments on tool calling and mathematical reasoning show that DARA learns the targeted behaviors faster than GDPO, reaching high format compliance in up to 26% fewer training steps on tool calling and near-saturated length compliance in up to 65% fewer steps on mathematical reasoning, while remaining competitive in final performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhaihaotian/DARA.

</details>


### [240] [From Task Mixtures to Specialized Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00580)
*Hojat Allah Salehi,Mehrdad Mahdavi,Andrew Arash Mahyari,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦微调中的"复合异质性"（客户端间与客户端内的数据异质），提出 FedSEE，通过将输入路由到专门专家来避免负迁移并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习只处理客户端之间的异质性，却忽略了单个客户端内部潜在的任务混合（即客户端数据是多种未知分布的混合）。在协同基础模型微调中，这种复合异质性普遍存在，若用单一模型直接适配，会导致负迁移，尤其损害服务最差的群体。

Method: 论文在共同冻结表征上进行适配分析：当任务共享相同特征几何时，平方损失下客户端任务混合的最优模型是其各子任务最优模型的凸组合；当任务最优点构成单纯形时，任务对齐的路由能获得比任何单一适配模型更低的风险。据此，作者利用少量带任务标签的公共样本，推导出一个凸规划来恢复任务专家并将其与对应任务匹配。基于路由分析，提出 FedSEE，实现输入依赖的专家选择，与各客户端的任务混合对齐。

Result: 实验中，FedSEE 避免了所评估基线出现的负迁移，整体性能提升 2.9 个点，对服务最差的四分位群体提升 3.7 个点。

Conclusion: 有效的专业化需要输入依赖的专家选择并对齐客户端任务混合；FedSEE 通过路由到专门专家，在复合异质性场景下显著优于基线，尤其改善了最差服务群体的性能。
