Abstract: In collaborative foundation model fine-tuning, client data is rarely homogeneous. Instead, clients typically possess unknown mixtures of distinct data distributions, or tasks. Conventional federated learning primarily addresses heterogeneity across clients without explicitly resolving latent task mixtures within each client. We study this setting as compound heterogeneity, where data is heterogeneous both across and within clients. We study adaptation over a common frozen representation and show that, when tasks share the same feature geometry, the optimal model for a client's task mixture under squared loss is a convex combination of the optimal models for its underlying tasks. Thus, a single locally trained model represents the client's overall task mixture, while individual inputs may be drawn from different underlying task distributions. This motivates routing inputs to specialized experts, and we show that, when the task optima form a simplex, task-aligned routing achieves lower risk than any single adapted model for genuinely mixed clients. With access to a small set of task-labeled public samples, we derive a convex program to recover task experts and match them to their corresponding tasks. Our routing analysis shows that effective specialization requires input-dependent expert selection aligned with each client's task mixture. Motivated by this analysis, we propose FedSEE. Across our experiments, FedSEE avoids the negative transfer observed in the evaluated baselines and improves performance by 2.9 points overall and 3.7 points for the worst-served quartile.

</details>


### [241] [Right In-Place (RiP) Convolution: A Simple, General, and Near-Optimal Strategy for Memory-Efficient CNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00586)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文修正了Gural与Murmann的就地卷积闭式解中的两类错误，并将其推广到任意步幅、空洞、填充和矩形卷积核，提出RiP卷积方法，在保持逐位一致输出的同时显著降低激活内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活内存而非计算量是CNN在微控制器等受限硬件上推理的瓶颈。直接就地卷积可省去双缓冲开销，但已有内存最优公式假设有效填充、单位步幅、单位空洞和奇数方形卷积核，且需非顺序遍历，导致2倍推理时间开销。

Method: 识别原闭式解失效的两种情形：(1) 欠分配恰好 (k-1)C_in mod (C_out-C_in) 个标量，导致活跃输入被静默破坏；(2) 当关键分支离开输出网格时产生无界高估，最高达2432倍。对两者进行修正，推广至任意步幅、空洞、填充与矩形核。进而提出Right In-Place (RiP)卷积：每层在共享工作区中右对齐读取输入、从索引零左对齐写出输出，负债为输出像素索引的分段仿射函数，在O(1)内求断点即可得最小安全间隙，同时保留行主序访问。

Result: 在10000个随机层上RiP无损坏；在来自25种架构的84个卷积层中，58个与herringbone工作区完全一致，81个在5%以内，平均比双缓冲少用24.8%内存。写入TinyEngine内核并部署到Raspberry Pi Pico 1和Pico 2，在11个MCUNet模型上峰值激活内存降低12.5%至33.3%，周期数不变、输出逐位一致，使Pico 1的256 KB SRAM可容纳模型数从6增至9个。

Conclusion: RiP卷积修正并推广了现有内存最优就地卷积公式，实现逐位一致输出、保留行主序访问并显著降低激活内存，可提升受限硬件上CNN模型的部署容量。

Abstract: Activation memory, not compute, limits CNN inference on constrained hardware such as microcontrollers. Direct in-place convolution removes the dual-buffer cost, but the memory-optimal formulation of Gural and Murmann assumes valid padding, unit stride, unit dilation, and odd square kernels, and needs a non-sequential traversal costing $2\times$ inference time in transposes. We identify two regimes in which their published closed form does not hold: (1) an under-allocation of exactly $(k-1)C_{in} \bmod (C_{out}-C_{in})$ scalars, active on every convolutional layer of their own deployed network and manifesting as a silent corruption of still-live input; (2) an unbounded overestimate, up to $2{,}432\times$, once the critical leg leaves the output grid. We correct both and generalize to arbitrary stride, dilation, padding, and rectangular kernels. We then propose Right In-Place (RiP) convolution, a bit-identical operation in which every layer reads its input right-aligned in a shared workspace and writes its output left-aligned from index zero. The debt is piecewise affine in the output pixel index, so evaluating its breakpoints in $O(1)$ yields the minimum safe gap without enumerating the output grid, with row-major access preserved. Across $10{,}000$ random layers RiP produced no corruption, and across 84 convolutional layers from 25 architectures it matches the herringbone workspace exactly on 58 and within 5% on 81, using 24.8% less memory than dual buffering on average. Written into TinyEngine's kernels and deployed to a Raspberry Pi Pico 1 and Pico 2, it cuts peak activation memory across eleven MCUNet models by 12.5 to 33.3% at unchanged cycle counts and bit-identical outputs, raising the number of models that fit the Pico 1's 256 KB SRAM from six to nine.

</details>


### [242] [ALER: Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00592)
*Oleg Shchendrigin,Egor Cherepanov,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文关注部分可观测强化学习中的"记忆更新"能力，提出重写（Rewriting）与经验融合（Experience Fusion）两种新要求，并设计智能体ALER（LSTM+槽记忆，独立寻址Gumbel-Softmax写入+学习门控融合）及Rune-Mazes基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL的记忆架构与基准主要测试"保留"能力，即信息在被使用前保持不变；但部分可观测场景中，后续观测可能使已存储信息过时或改变其含义，因此需要形式化并评测"重写"（将决策相关内容设为与旧值无关）和"经验融合"（按后续观测指定规则变换旧内容）这两种新能力。

Method: 提出ALER智能体：将LSTM与槽（slot）记忆配对；采用独立寻址的Gumbel-Softmax写入，将权重集中到单个槽以覆盖该槽内容；用学习门控将检索内容与循环状态融合后再送入策略与价值头。同时构建Rune-Mazes（含Endless T-Maze、Rune T-Maze、Rune Multi-Corridor、像素Rune MiniGrid Memory三类/多种环境），在向量与像素观测下通过符文观测对隐藏线索进行反转、取消、重置或重复更新。

Result: 在需要更多记忆状态的任务组合上，多个基线成功率最低；ALER在全部16种Endless T-Maze配置上成功率≥0.82，在全部5种Rune T-Maze组合上≥0.99，在含Invert符文的四分支Rune Multi-Corridor上平均成功率最高；在像素Rune MiniGrid Memory上，10种配置中有8种平均成功率高于PPO-LSTM。

Conclusion: 记忆不仅需要保留信息，还需支持重写与经验融合；ALER通过独立寻址写入与门控融合有效实现了这类更新，并在多种新基准上优于基线，表明所提形式化目标与基准对推进RL记忆研究具有价值。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning (RL), a later observation can make stored information obsolete or change what it implies for the next decision. Memory architectures and benchmarks for RL mostly test retention, the ability to keep information unchanged until it is needed. We formalize two further requirements. Rewriting sets the decision-relevant content to a value independent of the old one, and experience fusion transforms the old content by a rule that a later observation specifies. For tasks built from such updates, we count the memory states that a solution needs, and several baselines reach their lowest success rates on compositions that need more states. We introduce ALER (Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting), an agent that pairs an LSTM with a slot memory. An independently addressed Gumbel-Softmax write that concentrates its weight on one slot overwrites that slot, and a learned gate fuses the retrieved content with the recurrent state before the policy and value heads. We also introduce Rune-Mazes, three environments in which rune observations invert, cancel, reset, or repeat updates of a hidden cue under vector and pixel observations. Against seven baselines, ALER reaches a success rate of at least $0.82$ in all sixteen Endless T-Maze configurations and at least $0.99$ on all five Rune T-Maze compositions, and it has the highest mean success rate on four-branch Rune Multi-Corridor with an Invert rune. On pixel-based Rune MiniGrid Memory, it has a higher mean success rate than PPO-LSTM in eight of ten configurations. Project page: https://quartz-admirer.github.io/ALER-Adaptive-Learnable-Experience-Rewriting/.

</details>


### [243] [MIKASA-Robo-VLA: Benchmarking Memory in VLA Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00604)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MIKASA-Robo-VLA 基准，包含 90 个语言条件操作任务，大多隐藏关键线索以评估 VLA 策略的记忆能力，并发布 22,500 条轨迹数据及基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常只观测一个或少数最近帧，难以评估其如何利用任务中消失的信息，即记忆能力评估缺乏系统基准。

Method: 构建包含 90 个语言条件任务的基准，其中 80 个隐藏动作所依赖的线索、10 个为反应式对照；为 70 个任务定义环境相位时序中指定的信息间隙（28 个超过最宽固定上下文 VLA 的 16 帧窗口）；发布覆盖 10 种记忆类型的 22,500 条 oracle 轨迹（RLDS 与 LeRobotDataset v3 格式）；对参考 π0.5 基线（使用当前图像与本体感知、无历史或显式记忆模块）在 14 个任务上微调。

Result: 参考 π0.5 基线平均任务成功率为 0.211 ± 0.044；其在 Long-split 任务上成功率较低，受开环分块与该子集记忆类型混杂影响。

Conclusion: MIKASA-Robo-VLA 提供了带语言指令、可构造性保证记忆需求的操纵基准与数据，揭示无记忆 VLA 基线在需记忆任务上的明显局限，为记忆型 VLA 研究提供评估基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often see only one or a few recent frames, which makes it difficult to evaluate how they use information that disappears during a task. We introduce MIKASA-Robo-VLA, a benchmark of 90 language-conditioned manipulation tasks. All but 10 hide the cue an action depends on. Those 10 are reactive controls. MIKASA-Robo, the suite it rebuilds, has 32 tasks and uses language only in a representative VLA subset. Here every task provides an instruction, while memory-dependent tasks hide a task-relevant cue and reactive controls keep it available. For 70 tasks, environment phase timings specify an information gap, and for 28 of them the gap exceeds the 16-frame window of the widest fixed-context VLA we survey. The gap counts only the interval the cue is provably absent, not the full duration a policy must retain it, so every memory-dependent task still requires memory by construction, including the ones whose measured gap is short. We release 22,500 oracle trajectories across 10 memory types in RLDS and LeRobotDataset v3. A reference $π_{0.5}$ baseline with current images and proprioception, but no observation history or explicit memory module, is fine-tuned on 14 tasks and achieves 0.211 $\pm$ 0.044 mean task success. Its lower success on the evaluated Long-split tasks is confounded by open-loop chunking and the memory types represented in that subset. Project page: https://mikasarobo.github.io/

</details>


### [244] [Learning the identity: a case study of how SGD selects among functional decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00615)
*Andy Arditi,Weian Xie,David Bau,Liu Ziyin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度线性残差网络学习恒等映射时SGD为何偏好特定解，并用熵损失理论解释该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习恒等映射看似平凡——残差连接已实现恒等，网络只需将权重置零。但零权重解只是整个极小值流形上的一点，不同解对应恒等函数在网络各层的不同分解方式。群体损失无法区分这些解，而SGD却能可复现地偏好某些解，这一现象需要解释。

Method: 以恒等学习任务为案例，引入熵损失视角，在群体损失上加入与微批量梯度期望平方范数成正比的项（Ziyin等，2025）。在恒等流形上群体损失为常数，而熵项可区分不同分解。作者解析刻画其极小值，并据此预测SGD偏好的解结构，同时对比不同参数化方式（如将权重矩阵分解为两个矩阵乘积）的影响。

Result: 在各向异性标签噪声下，学到的层呈现依赖噪声的谱结构；即使有权重衰减，SGD一般也不会恢复零权重解。改变参数化（即使可实现函数集合不变，如权重矩阵因式分解）会使权重坍缩到零，甚至无需显式权重衰减。SGD训练得到的网络与熵损失理论预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 恒等学习任务是一个干净简单的案例，说明熵损失视角能阐明SGD为何偏好同一输入-输出函数的不同分解方式。

Abstract: One might think that learning the identity function with a deep linear residual network is trivial - the path along residual connections already implements the identity, and so the network need only drive its weights to zero. However, this zero-weight solution is just one point on an entire manifold of population-loss minimizers, each corresponding to a different decomposition of the identity across the network's layers. Although the population loss does not distinguish among these solutions, stochastic gradient descent (SGD) reproducibly favors particular ones. For instance, under anisotropic label noise, the learned layers exhibit a noise-dependent spectrum; even with weight decay, SGD does not generally recover the zero-weight solution. Changing only the parametrization, while leaving the set of realizable functions unchanged, yields different behavior: factoring each weight matrix as a product of two matrices causes the weights to collapse to zero, even without explicit weight decay.
  While perhaps mysterious and unintuitive at first, these phenomena can be understood through the lens of entropic loss, which augments the population loss with a term proportional to the expected squared norm of the minibatch gradient (Ziyin et al., 2025). On the identity manifold, the population loss is constant, while the entropic term distinguishes among these decompositions. We characterize its minimizers analytically and use them to derive predictions for the structure of solutions favored by SGD. Networks trained with SGD closely match these predictions.
  Overall, the identity learning task studied here serves as a clean and simple case study of how the lens of entropic loss can clarify why SGD favors particular decompositions of the same input-output function.

</details>


### [245] [Misalignment of Low-Loss Regions Causes Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00620)
*Yongding Tian,Zaid Al-Ars,Maksim Kitsak,Peter Hofstee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于模式连接性和低损失区域几何的分析框架，预测标准模算术任务并非总出现grokking，并通过对称保持的数据划分发现稳定的反grokking案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking现象（模型先过拟合训练集、后延迟出现验证集泛化）的潜在机制尚无定论，现有相关性解释存在不足，需要更本质的几何分析视角。

Method: 构建基于模式连接性（mode connectivity）与低损失区域几何的分析框架，并设计对称保持（symmetry-preserving）的训练/验证划分进行实验验证。

Result: 在对称保持划分下观察到稳定的反grokking案例，即验证性能不再恢复；该反例挑战了多种现有grokking的相关性解释。

Conclusion: grokking源于训练集与验证集所诱导的低损失区域错位；当二者良好对齐时，仅靠训练超参数无法产生grokking，动力学收敛为可训练或不可训练行为。

Abstract: Grokking refers to the delayed emergence of validation-set generalization after a model has already overfit the training set. Although first observed in small algorithmic tasks trained with transformers, its underlying mechanism remains unsettled. In this work, we develop an analysis framework based on mode connectivity and the geometry of low-loss regions. The framework predicts that the standard modular-arithmetic setting does not always produce grokking: under a symmetry-preserving train/validation split, we observe a stable anti-grokking case in which validation performance does not recover. This counterexample challenges several existing correlational explanations of grokking. More broadly, our analysis framework and results further suggest that grokking arises when the low-loss regions induced by the training and validation partitions are misaligned. Once these regions become well aligned, training hyperparameters alone cannot produce grokking and the observed dynamics collapse to either trainable or non-trainable behavior.

</details>


### [246] [Learning Linear Systems under Heavy-Tailed Noise: A Non-Asymptotic Analysis from A Single Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00637)
*Xiaomian Yang,Sungho Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为指数稳定向量自回归模型在重尾噪声下的最小二乘估计建立了非渐近样本复杂度界，误差为 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(r^{1/2}T^{-1/2+1/p})$，并提出统一的分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有样本复杂度分析多针对轻尾噪声（如高斯、次高斯），而重尾噪声下向量自回归模型的最小二乘估计理论保证不足，需要非渐近的误差界。

Method: 在i.i.d.噪声、有界噪声协方差和持续激励假设下，基于单条观测轨迹分析指数稳定系统的最小二乘估计；假设噪声具有有界 $p$ 阶矩（$p>2$），提出统一的样本复杂度分析框架，并推广至次指数和次高斯噪声。进一步 specialize 到带外生输入的自回归模型。

Result: 估计误差为 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(r^{1/2}T^{-1/2+1/p})$，其中 $T$ 为样本数、$r$ 为噪声维度；次指数和次高斯噪声下也得到相应误差界；带外生输入的自回归模型中误差界的维度因子与模型阶数无关。

Conclusion: 该工作为指数稳定VAR模型在重尾及多种噪声分布下的最小二乘估计提供了统一的非渐近样本复杂度理论，并揭示了带外生输入情形下误差界维度因子与模型阶数无关的性质。

Abstract: We establish non-asymptotic sample complexity bounds for the least-squares estimation of vector autoregressive models for exponentially stable systems with heavy-tailed noise based on a single observed trajectory. By assuming i.i.d. noise, bounded noise covariance, and persistent excitation, we show that the estimation error is $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(r^{1/2}T^{-1/2+1/p})$ under bounded $p$th moment for $p > 2$, where $T$ is the number of samples, $r$ is the noise dimension, and $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\cdot)$ hides logarithmic terms. We also introduce a unifying approach to sample complexity analysis applicable to broad classes of noise distributions and showcase this by deriving error bounds for sub-exponential and sub-Gaussian noise distributions. Finally, we specialize our analysis to autoregressive models with exogenous inputs and show that the dimension factor of the error bound is independent of the model order.

</details>


### [247] [Group-Invariant Statistics Determine Embedding Geometry: Harmonic Analysis of Representations from Bach to the Night Sky](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00647)
*Liam Storan,Andreas Tolias,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：当词家族的共现统计在任意有限群、紧群或齐性空间下具有对称性时，学到的词嵌入由该群不可约表示的矩阵元构成，从而统一解释了循环群的圆形与"品客"几何、二面体群的五度圈以及天体概念的球谐嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作将语言模型中月份、星期、地点等概念的圆形与鞍形"品客"流形几何追溯到词共现统计中的平移对称性，但该推导仅适用于阿贝尔格点上的距离依赖共现。作者希望将这一解释推广到更一般的对称性（任意有限群、紧群、齐性空间），以说明更广泛的结构化几何同样源于数据的统计对称性。

Method: 作者在理论上考察由任意有限群、紧群及齐性空间定义的对称性，证明：只要某词家族的共现统计在群 G 下不变，学习到的词嵌入就由 G 的不可约表示的矩阵元组成；当 G 为循环群时，不可约表示即傅里叶模式，从而给出圆形与"品客"几何。随后在三个实验场景中验证该不可约表示结构：(i) 循环群 Z12 对应的月份；(ii) 作用于大小三和弦的二面体群；(iii) 大语言模型中天体对象的表示。

Result: (i) 对一年中的月份，复现了已知的圆形几何；(ii) 对作用于大小三和弦的二面体群，统一了两项经典观察——音乐理论中移调与和弦转位构成群 T/I 作用于和弦，这"蕴含"了机器学习中学到的和弦嵌入会出现著名的"五度圈"；(iii) 将近期发现的大语言模型中对天体对象的球面表示解释并复现为由该理论导出的球谐嵌入。

Conclusion: 学习到的表示几何往往是底层数据统计对称性的结果。该理论将平移对称性推广到一般群对称性，为语言模型中多种结构化嵌入几何提供了统一的群表示论解释。

Abstract: The representations that language models learn for concepts such as months, weekdays, and places display consistent geometric structure: circles and saddle-shaped "Pringle" manifolds. Recent work traced these structures to $\textit{translation symmetry}$ in word co-occurrence statistics, deriving the observed Fourier geometry when co-occurrence depends only on distance on an abelian lattice of concepts. We demonstrate that more general notions of symmetry lead to equally structured predictions. Considering symmetries defined by arbitrary finite groups, compact groups, and homogeneous spaces, we prove that whenever the co-occurrence statistics of a word family are invariant under a group $G$, the learned word embeddings consist of matrix elements of the irreducible representations (irreps) of $G$. Circles and Pringles arise when $G$ is cyclic, in which case the irreps are Fourier modes. We verify the irrep structure in three experimental settings. (i) The cyclic group $\mathbb{Z}_{12}$: for the months of the year we recover the known circular geometry. (ii) A dihedral group acting on the major and minor triads: we unify two classical observations -- that transposition and chord inversion form a group ($T/I$) acting on chords (music theory), which $\textit{implies}$ that the well-known "circle of fifths" emerges in learned chord embeddings (machine learning). (iii) We explain and reproduce a recently discovered spherical representation of celestial objects in large language models (LLMs) as a spherical-harmonic embedding derived from our theory. Our results demonstrate that the geometry of learned representations is often a consequence of the statistical symmetry of underlying data.

</details>


### [248] [Exploring More, Reasoning Better: Stepwise Risk-Sensitive GRPO for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00661)
*Yue YU,Bowen Zuo,David Crandall,Yinglun Zhu,Dongruo Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 StepRS-GRPO，通过在去噪状态间调整组优势变换的风险系数，提升扩散大语言模型的强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）通过去噪序列或连续块来生成文本，可并行揭示多个 token。强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）在多个去噪决策间复用终端反馈，但其条件上下文会随之变化，导致优势估计与风险处理需要随状态调整，而传统 GRPO 未考虑这一特性。

Method: 提出逐步风险敏感 GRPO（StepRS-GRPO），在保留底层训练器的同时，让组优势变换的风险系数随去噪状态变化。针对二值奖励，证明该变换等价于对中心化结果优势进行依赖 prompt 与状态的重缩放。通过基于能力的校准确定系数尺度，并用端点与插值消融实验指导调度方案选择。

Result: 在多种 dLLM 骨干模型和数学推理基准上，StepRS-GRPO 相比中心化 GRPO 同时提升 pass@1 准确率和 pass@k 覆盖率，并增加答案多样性。消融实验中，质量匹配对照支持状态分配与调度方向的贡献，且在将优势 RMS 与中心化 GRPO 匹配后增益依然存在。推理轨迹诊断进一步表明，多样性增益不限于最终答案字符串。

Conclusion: StepRS-GRPO 通过状态相关的风险系数调节，有效改进了扩散大语言模型在可验证奖励强化学习中的准确率、覆盖范围与多样性，验证了逐步风险敏感设计的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text by denoising a sequence or successive blocks, allowing several tokens to be revealed in parallel. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) reuses terminal feedback across these decisions, even as their conditioning context changes. We propose stepwise risk-sensitive GRPO (StepRS-GRPO), which varies the risk coefficient of the group-advantage transformation across denoising states while retaining the underlying trainer. For binary rewards, we show that this transformation is exactly a prompt- and state-dependent rescaling of centered outcome advantages. A capability-based calibration suggests a coefficient scale, while endpoint and interpolation ablations guide schedule selection. Across multiple dLLM backbones and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, StepRS-GRPO improves both pass@1 accuracy and pass@k coverage over centered GRPO, while increasing answer diversity. In our ablation studies, mass-matched controls support the contributions of state allocation and schedule direction, and the gains persist after matching the root mean square (RMS) of the advantages to that of centered GRPO. Reasoning-trace diagnostics further show that the diversity gains from StepRS-GRPO extend beyond final-answer strings.

</details>


### [249] [Analysis of Quantized and Efficiently Adapted Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00665)
*Ilan Yaniv Zeisler,Sebastian Clancy,Pouriya Bayat,Saaim Raad,Ivan Kraskov,Matthew Xie,Vivian White,Spencer Perkins,Serena Singh,Sepehr Bayat,Keith Pardee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了4-bit量化与QLoRA在多种蛋白质语言模型上的效果，发现多数任务可保留90%以上全量微调性能，GPU显存节省近90%，但量化会带来模型相关的生成分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLM）规模庞大，导致微调和部署成本高昂。然而，量化与参数高效微调对模型性能、表征及生成能力的影响尚未被充分刻画，因此需要系统性评估。

Method: 在ESM-2、ESMC、ProtBERT、ProtT5、Ankh、Ankh3、Profluent-E1等模型上评估4-bit量化与低秩适配微调（QLoRA）；针对预测任务分析性能保持率、显存节省与层级表征变化；针对生成任务（ProLLaMA、ProtGPT2、ProGen2、ProteinGLM、ESM3）进行结构、序列及token级分布分析。

Result: 预测任务中许多模型-任务组合保留了超过90%的全量微调性能，最大模型显存节省接近90%，但性能与效率因模型、数据集和训练配置而异。QLoRA常保留早期层表征，并在中后层引入任务特定适配，表征变化小于全量微调。生成任务中4-bit量化基本保留了预测的结构和序列属性，但token级分析揭示出自回归输出分布存在模型相关偏移。

Conclusion: QLoRA与4-bit量化可显著降低PLM计算需求，尤其节省GPU显存。建议将QLoRA作为内存受限场景的首选适配策略，将全量微调保留给困难任务、不稳定架构或验证恢复较低的情况。对生成式PLM，应在序列级和结构指标之外补充分布分析。这些方法可扩大大规模蛋白质建模的可及性，但需针对模型和任务进行验证。

Abstract: Background: Protein language models (PLMs) are increasingly used for sequence generation and property prediction, but their size makes fine-tuning and deployment expensive. The effects of quantization and parameter efficient fine-tuning on performance, representations and generation remain insufficiently characterized. Results: We evaluated 4-bit quantization and low-rank adapter fine-tuning (QLoRA) across ESM-2, ESMC, ProtBERT, ProtT5, Ankh, Ankh3 and Profluent-E1. Across protein prediction tasks, many model-task pairs retained more than 90% of full fine-tuning performance. Peak GPU memory savings approached 90% for the largest models, although performance and efficiency varied by model, dataset and training configuration. QLoRA often preserved early-layer representations while inducing task-specific adaptations in middle and late layers, resembling full fine-tuning with smaller representational changes. Training speed and power effects were more varied. For unconditional generation with ProLLaMA, ProtGPT2, ProGen2, ProteinGLM and ESM3, 4-bit quantization largely preserved predicted structural and sequence-level properties, but token-level analysis revealed model-dependent shifts in autoregressive output distributions. Conclusion: QLoRA and 4-bit quantization reduce PLM computational requirements, particularly GPU memory usage. Our results support QLoRA as a first-pass strategy for memory limited adaptation, reserving full fine-tuning for challenging tasks, unstable architectures or low validation recovery. For generative PLMs, sequence-level and structural metrics should be complemented with distributional analysis, since downstream predictions alone may miss quantization-induced shifts. These approaches can broaden access to large-scale protein modelling while requiring model- and task-specific validation.

</details>


### [250] [ORBIT-FMIB: Tracking Order-Resolved Epistatic Information Through ESM-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00672)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ORBIT-FMIB 诊断框架，用于检验 ESM-2 表示层级中高阶上位性信息是否比低阶信息保留得更差；首轮实验显示明显差异，但完整复现后对比大幅缩小且符号不稳定，故该方向性问题仍无定论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质基础模型的预测性能本身无法说明模型深度中保留了哪些形式的生物相互作用信息，尤其无法判断高阶上位性信息是否与一阶、二阶信息同等可及。

Method: 提出 ORBIT-FMIB 诊断框架，结合基于 Walsh 的相互作用分解与子集条件化的神经依赖估计；在具有已知相互作用结构的合成适应度景观上验证后，应用于冻结的 ESM-2 表示与密集四站点 GB1 适应度景观。并进行了完整测量网格的独立复现，匹配 GPU 硬件与相同 critic 随机种子，并考察多种评估配对选择。

Result: 初始生产运行显示 ESM-2 对高阶上位性信息的保留弱于低阶信息（Δ_HO-LO = -0.107）；独立复现将该差异大幅缩小至 -0.017，且符号在同批训练好的 critic 上因不同但合理的评估配对而变化（-0.011 至 +0.015）。

Conclusion: 目前没有稳健证据表明 ESM-2 会选择性丢失高阶上位性信息，也不能断定各阶信息保留相等，方向性问题仍开放。测量协议本身（含对池化 critic 中位置子集捷径的修正）仍然有效。ORBIT-FMIB 可作为探测蛋白质基础模型相互作用结构的诊断工具，本研究自身的复现结果说明此类探测在作为生物学结论前需要足够统计功效的可重复性检验。

Abstract: Protein foundation models support mutation-effect and structural prediction, but predictive performance alone does not reveal which forms of biological interaction information remain accessible through model depth. We ask whether ESM-2 retains higher-order epistatic information as strongly as first- and second-order information across its representation hierarchy, introducing ORBIT-FMIB, a diagnostic framework combining Walsh-based interaction decomposition with subset-conditioned neural dependence estimation. The method is validated on synthetic landscapes with known interaction structure before being applied to the dense four-site GB1 fitness landscape using frozen ESM-2 representations.
  An initial production run suggested ESM-2 retains higher-order epistatic information less well than lower-order information ($Δ_{\mathrm{HO-LO}}=-0.107$). An independent replication of the complete measurement grid, under matched GPU hardware and identical critic seeds, substantially reduced this contrast ($Δ_{\mathrm{HO-LO}}=-0.017$), and its sign was unstable across otherwise-defensible evaluation-pairing choices applied to the same trained critics ($-0.011$ to $+0.015$). We therefore do not currently have robust evidence that ESM-2 selectively loses higher-order epistatic information, nor that retention is equal across orders; the directional question remains open. The measurement protocol itself, including its documented removal of a positional-subset shortcut in pooled critics, remains validated and is unaffected by this finding. ORBIT-FMIB is offered as a diagnostic framework for probing interaction structure in protein foundation models; this study's own replication result illustrates why such probing requires adequately-powered reproducibility checks before its output is treated as a biological finding.

</details>


### [251] [LabBook: Harnessing Experimental History for Efficient LLM-Driven Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00675)
*Bo Yuan,Wenqian Ye,Zelin Zhao,Lama Moukheiber,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LabBook：一种由智能体维护的记忆机制，兼具检索完整实验日志中相关证据与指导新解生成的双重作用，用单智能体发现框架取代显式种群搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的进化发现方法通常从少量被选中的祖先程序生成新程序。这样上下文可控，但会遗漏其他实验中的有用证据；而纳入全部实验历史又会导致上下文冗长、冗余。

Method: 构建简单单智能体发现框架，核心为 LabBook——智能体维护的记忆。每轮迭代中，同一智能体将记忆与检索到的证据结合，联合产出下一个程序与更新后的 LabBook，从而将“完整历史保留”与“选择性上下文构建”分离，无需显式种群或分支搜索结构。

Result: 在 49 个 Frontier-CS 问题上，LabBook 在两种骨干模型下改善了观测到的质量—成本权衡，优于所评估的进化基线；在另外九项数学、系统与启发式设计任务上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: LabBook 证明以智能体维护记忆为核心的单体框架可兼顾完整历史利用与上下文精简，在多种任务上取得更优的质量—成本权衡，代码将开源。

Abstract: Evolutionary approaches to LLM-driven discovery often generate new programs from a small set of selected ancestors. This keeps contexts manageable but can omit useful evidence from other experiments, whereas including the full experimental history produces long, redundant contexts. We introduce a simple, single-agent discovery harness built around LabBook, an agent-maintained memory that serves two complementary roles: guiding retrieval of relevant evidence from a complete experimental log and informing the generation of new solutions. At each iteration, the same agent combines its memory with retrieved evidence and jointly produces the next program and an updated LabBook. This separates complete history retention from selective context construction, without requiring an explicit population or branching search structure. On 49 Frontier-CS problems, LabBook improves the observed quality-cost trade-off over the evaluated evolutionary baselines with two backbones, while remaining competitive across nine additional mathematical, systems, and heuristic-design tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/BoYuanVisionary/LabBook.

</details>


### [252] [Learning Transferable Skills using Goal-Conditioned Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00676)
*Mohammad Amin Abbasfar,Farbod Azimmohseni,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无监督技能发现方法泛化能力差、难以迁移到新布局的问题，提出学习动作感知的时间表示，并结合双模拟（bisimulation）进行技能发现，从而实现跨布局的强分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督技能发现方法要么依赖专家数据，要么泛化能力有限，无法有效迁移到未见过的布局。核心挑战在于学习既能捕捉环境时间结构、又能对布局变化保持鲁棒的表征。

Method: 1）提出一个学习"动作感知时间表示"的目标函数，满足函数等变性（functional equivariance）并保留环境的局部时间结构；2）在该嵌入基础上，提出使用双模拟的无监督技能发现方法，将技能行为仅以直接影响其执行的状态特征子集为条件。

Result: 通过在多种布局环境中进行综合实验评估，表明在某一环境中学习到的技能可以有效应用于不同环境布局下的下游任务，展现出较强的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过在状态特征子集上进行条件化，强制技能在不同布局间保持一致行为，从而使技能能够有效迁移到其他配置，验证了所提表示学习与技能发现框架的泛化优势。

Abstract: Unsupervised skill discovery has emerged as a promising approach for leveraging reward-free datasets to pretrain general-purpose policies. However, current skill discovery methods either require access to expert data or exhibit limited generalization, failing to transfer effectively to previously unseen layouts. A key challenge is to learn representations that capture the temporal structure of the environment while remaining robust to variations across layouts. To address this issue, we present an objective for learning action-aware temporal representations that satisfy the functional equivariance property while preserving the local temporal structure of the environment. Building upon this embedding, we further propose unsupervised skill discovery using bisimulation, which learns transferable skills by conditioning the behavior of skills exclusively on the subset of state features that directly affect their execution. This enforces invariant behavior across different layouts, enabling skills to transfer effectively to other configurations. Finally, through comprehensive empirical evaluations, we show that skills learned in a given environment can be effectively applied to solve downstream tasks in various environment layouts, demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [253] [Curvature Under Attack in hZACH-ViT: Gauge Symmetry, Boundary Saturation, and Adversarial Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00680)
*Athanasios Angelakis,Marta Gomez-Barrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严格匹配的 hZACH-ViT 对比中，固定曲率 c=1 时 Poincaré 原型头的对抗攻击成功率最低但干净 MacroF1 也最低；将曲率降至 c=0.1 会提升干净性能并消除边界截断，却反而使强攻击成功率大幅上升（OrganAMNIST 上 +9.57 个百分点）。因此曲率对鲁棒性的表观效应取决于坐标尺度、边界接近程度和数值实现细节，不能据此断言双曲几何本身具有内在鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲率常被当作表示的内在属性，但其实际效果还受坐标尺度、学到的 logit 温度与数值保护措施影响。作者希望厘清"双曲/球面原型头带来鲁棒性"这一结论在多大程度上是几何内在性质、多大程度上是尺度与实现敏感的伪象。

Method: 构建紧凑 ViT（hZACH-ViT），含欧氏、Poincaré、球面三种原型头；在三个 MedMNIST 数据集、五个随机种子上匹配骨干结构、初始化、每类 50 样本训练子集和优化流程。进行固定曲率对比（c=1）与端到端曲率干预（c=1→0.1），并用 PGD 与更强的 CE+DLR 多重启攻击评估鲁棒性；辅以上下文分析：硬截断比例、继承映射的径向 Jacobian、无量纲攻击轨迹长度；球面头作为精确尺度规范对照。

Result: 在 c=1 下，Poincaré 在全部 12 个"数据集-预算"单元中类宏平均 PGD 攻击成功率最低，并在三个数据集上对更强攻击同样最低，但干净 MacroF1 也最低。将曲率降至 c=0.1 后，15 组配对的种-数据集比较中干净 MacroF1 全部提升且不再发生硬边界截断，但 OrganAMNIST 上强攻击成功率从 89.7% 升至 99.3%（配对差 +9.57 点，95% 分层自助 CI [+5.52,+14.03]）。c=1 时 40–47% 的干净 Poincaré 特征被硬截断、继承映射径向 Jacobian 接近零、无量纲攻击轨迹异常冗长低效。球面头的曲率变化在浮点精度内是精确尺度规范，产生的攻击差异小得多。

Conclusion: 这些结果不能证明双曲几何具有内在鲁棒性。它们揭示了一个实现敏感区制：曲率、尺度与对 Poincaré 边界的接近程度共同决定了干净识别性能与对抗表示运动，报告曲率效应时必须同时说明尺度与数值处理方式。

Abstract: Curvature is often treated as an intrinsic property of a representation, although its empirical effect also depends on coordinate scale, learned logit temperature, and numerical safeguards. We study this interaction in hZACH-ViT, a compact Vision Transformer with Euclidean, Poincare, and spherical prototype heads. The backbone architecture, seed-specific initialization, 50-per-class training subset, and optimization protocol are matched across three MedMNIST datasets and five seeds. At the fixed comparison curvature $c=1$, Poincare has the lowest class-macro PGD attack-success rate in all 12 dataset-budget cells and under a stronger CE+DLR multi-restart attack on all three datasets, but it also has the lowest clean MacroF1. An end-to-end curvature intervention changes the interpretation. Reducing Poincare curvature to $c=0.1$ improves clean MacroF1 in every one of the 15 paired seed-dataset comparisons and removes hard boundary clipping, yet on OrganAMNIST it increases strong attack success from $89.7\%$ to $99.3\%$ (paired difference $+9.57$ points; 95\% hierarchical bootstrap CI $[+5.52,+14.03]$). At $c=1$, $40$-$47\%$ of clean Poincare features are hard-clipped, the radial Jacobian of the inherited map is nearly zero, and dimensionless attack trajectories are unusually long and inefficient. The spherical head provides a control: its curvature change is an exact scale gauge to floating-point precision and produces much smaller attack differences. These results do not establish intrinsic hyperbolic robustness. They identify an implementation-sensitive regime in which curvature, scale, and proximity to the Poincare boundary jointly organize clean recognition and adversarial representation motion.

</details>


### [254] [Grand Canonical Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00683)
*Andreas Burger,Malte Franke,Luka Mucko,Kjell Jorner,Alan Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出巨正则生成器（GCG），将玻尔兹曼生成器推广到巨正则系综，给出两种设计并在Lennard-Jones流体与甲烷吸附任务上验证其准确性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻尔兹曼生成器通常局限于正则系综（固定粒子数），而许多物理化学过程（如吸附、相变）发生在粒子数可变的巨正则系综下，需要新的生成式建模方法。

Method: 提出两种设计：一是以化学势为条件、联合采样粒子数与构型的可变尺寸生成模型；二是将巨正则分布分解为粒子数分布与对应正则玻尔兹曼密度，后者可复用现有玻尔兹曼生成器，并解析编码化学势的线性依赖，得到可计算的似然以支持自归一化重要性采样（SNIS）。

Result: 在Lennard-Jones流体与沸石中甲烷吸附两个体系上，GCG准确复现了巨正则可观测量，展现出跨化学势的泛化能力，并可通过SNIS与巨正则蒙特卡洛进行校正。

Conclusion: GCG将生成式建模有效扩展到巨正则系综，尤其因式分解设计兼具灵活性与可计算似然，为可变粒子数体系提供了实用的采样与推断工具。

Abstract: We introduce Grand Canonical Generators (GCG), a generative framework that extends Boltzmann generators to the grand canonical ensemble. We present two designs. The first conditions a variable-size generative model on the chemical potential, sampling particle number and configuration jointly. The second factorizes the grand canonical distribution into a particle-number distribution and the corresponding canonical Boltzmann density. This factorized formulation can use any existing Boltzmann generator for the canonical component, encodes the known linear chemical-potential dependence analytically, and yields a tractable likelihood that supports self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS). Empirically, GCG accurately reproduces grand canonical observables on a Lennard--Jones fluid and methane adsorption in a zeolite, demonstrating generalization across chemical potentials and correction via SNIS and grand canonical Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [255] [SkillSpec: Consensus-Gated Agent Skill Evolution via Representation Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00704)
*Huancheng Chen,Xiaodi Sun,Zhaoqiong Huang,Shenyang Huang Shreya Singhal,Jingwen Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SkillSpec 双阶段框架：先通过共识门控演化确定保留哪些知识，再通过表示专门化决定如何组织技能结构，在六个基准和三个模型上平均成功率提升 6.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将自然语言技能视为静态产物或单一文档，并用聚合验证分数作为反馈进行优化。这使得优化仅限文本内容，无法显式建模程序性知识的检索与执行结构。论文指出关键区别在于"保留什么知识"与"如何组织知识"是两类不同问题：文本更新应先经执行证据验证，再按程序性依赖与检索需求进行结构化。

Method: SkillSpec 包含两个阶段。第一阶段为共识门控演化（consensus-gated evolution），由互补的编辑意图生成完整候选技能，仅当配对评估达成共识时提交更新，要求整体改进充分且每次重复评估中聚合配对增益非负。第二阶段为表示专门化（representation specialization），从完整优化轨迹（含被接受与被拒绝的候选）中导出过程敏感性和冗余敏感性信号，据此选择扁平、图或混合表示。

Result: 在六个基准和三个目标语言模型上，SkillSpec 相较 SkillOpt 平均成功率提升 6.89%（三个模型平均）。

Conclusion: 可靠的技能演化与表示专门化针对互补目标：决定保留哪些知识，以及如何为推理组织这些知识。二者结合可有效提升 LLM 智能体的任务成功率。

Abstract: Natural-language skills are textual procedural memories through which large language model (LLM) agents retain reusable task knowledge without updating model weights. Existing methods typically treat skills as either static artifacts or monolithic documents optimized using aggregate validation scores as feedback. However, representing a skill as a monolithic document restricts optimization to its textual content, without explicitly modeling the structure through which procedural knowledge is retrieved and executed. We identify a key distinction between learning what knowledge to retain and determining how to organize it: textual updates should first be validated through execution evidence, after which the retained knowledge should be structured according to its procedural dependencies and retrieval requirements. To this end, we introduce SkillSpec, a two-phase framework comprising consensus-gated evolution and representation specialization. In the consensus-gated phase, complementary editing intents generate complete candidate skills. An update is committed only when paired evaluations reach consensus, requiring sufficient overall improvement and non-negative aggregate paired gain in every repeated evaluation. In the specialization phase, signals of process and redundancy sensitivity derived from the full optimization trajectory, including accepted and rejected candidates, guide the selection of a flat, graph, or hybrid representation.Across six benchmarks and three target language models, SkillSpec improves average success rate over SkillOpt by 6.89%, averaged across the three models. These results demonstrate that reliable skill evolution and representation specialization address complementary objectives: deciding what knowledge to retain and how to structure it for inference.

</details>


### [256] [Initialization Improves LLM-Driven Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00707)
*Mansi Sakarvadia,Marco Ciccone,Colin Raffel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM驱动发现任务中历史迭代样本群体与最终发现成功率的关系，提出通用干预方法，通过并行探索初始化迭代优化，在多种框架与任务上获得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM发现类工作依赖"harness"（提示LLM迭代优化目标）来发现算法、定理、药物等，但发现成功率对框架设计极为敏感且脆弱，存在模式塌缩问题，而现有SOTA框架及多样性干预带来的增益并不稳定。作者希望厘清历史迭代样本群体与发现成功之间的关系，找到更可靠的干预方式。

Method: 1) 将过往框架设计工作推广为12种harness组成的模块化套件"Modular"；2) 在5个多样化发现任务上刻画其性能；3) 分析模式塌缩（迭代样本多样性骤降）这一常见失败模式；4) 评估SOTA harness与多样性诱导干预的效果；5) 基于"早期发现表现可预测最终成功"的发现，提出通用干预：先用并行探索阶段初始化，再进行后续迭代优化。

Result: 发现成功率脆弱且对harness设计高度敏感；模式塌缩是常见失败模式；流行的SOTA harness和旨在延缓塌缩的多样性干预带来不一致的增益；早期发现表现对最终成功具有预测性；所提并行探索初始化方法在多个harness与目标应用上带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 在LLM驱动的发现任务中，初始化阶段至关重要。通过在迭代优化前加入并行探索的初始化阶段，可以跨多种框架和应用稳定提升发现成功率，为harness设计与干预提供新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been used for novel discovery of algorithms, theorems, drugs, and other tasks through the use of harnesses that prompt an LLM to iteratively optimize an objective. In this work, we study the relationship between the population of previous iterates and eventual discovery success. We generalize past work on harness design to develop a suite of 12 harnesses called 'Modular' and characterize their performance across 5 diverse discovery tasks, finding that discovery success is brittle and sensitive to harness design. We uncover mode collapse, characterized by a dramatic drop in the diversity of iterates, as a common failure mode. We find that popular state-of-the-art harnesses and diversity-inducing harness interventions, which aim to prolong this collapse, yield inconsistent gains. Our results instead uncover that the performance of early discoveries is predictive of eventual success. We therefore propose a universally applicable intervention that performs an initial stage of parallel exploration in order to initialize subsequent iterative optimization. Our method provides consistent gains across many harnesses and target applications, confirming the importance of initialization in LLM-driven discovery.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Unimodal Bases: Pullback Geometry for Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00708)
*Honglei Brinkmann,Lucas Ng,Georgios Batzolis,Mark Girolami,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Willem Diepeveen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向多流形混合数据的拉回黎曼几何，用潜在高斯混合定义度量，并实例化于自适应混合学习的归一化流中，实现更真实的高似然插值与局部结构估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展拉回几何通常假设单峰高斯潜在分布，即数据接近单一流形。对多模态数据，将分离的模态映射进同一高斯区域需要较大的传输变形，损害几何质量。

Method: 采用潜在高斯混合，将黎曼度量定义为责任加权期望分量精度的矩阵平方；证明度量的光滑性与正定性，并在单分量极限下退化为已有高斯构造。对结构化重叠混合，给出沿测地线对数密度凹性的条件，建立几何与高似然路径的联系并推导局部曲率关系。将几何实例化于带自适应混合学习的归一化流，使活跃分量数从数据中涌现，支持分量重构与局部有效维度估计。

Result: 在合成几何数据、具有已知参考轨迹的受控多视图图像设置以及 MNIST 上，实验显示传输畸变降低、路径支持具竞争力、能较接近地恢复参考轨迹，并提升插值真实感。

Conclusion: 该工作将可扩展拉回几何拓展到不接近单一流形的数据集，同时保持可处理且可解释的局部结构。

Abstract: Data-driven Riemannian geometry provides nonlinear interpolation and geometric representations of high-dimensional data. For these operations to be statistically meaningful, paths between observations should preferentially traverse high-likelihood regions. Existing scalable pullback constructions typically use a unimodal Gaussian latent distribution, assuming that the data reside close to a single manifold. For multimodal data, mapping separated modes or local structures into one Gaussian region can require substantial transport deformation and compromise the resulting geometry.
  We introduce a pullback geometry for data supported on mixtures of manifolds. Using a latent Gaussian mixture, we define its Riemannian metric as the matrix square of the responsibility-weighted expected component precision. The metric is smooth and positive definite and recovers the existing Gaussian construction in the single-component limit. For structured overlapping mixtures, we establish conditions under which the log-density is concave along geodesics, providing a formal connection between the proposed geometry and paths through high-likelihood regions, and derive the corresponding local curvature relations.
  We instantiate this geometry in a normalizing flow with adaptive mixture learning, allowing the number of active components to emerge from the data and supporting component-wise reconstruction and local effective-dimension estimation. Experiments on synthetic geometric data, a controlled multi-view image setting with a known reference trajectory, and MNIST show reduced transport distortion, competitive path support, close reference-trajectory recovery, and improved interpolation realism. These results extend scalable pullback geometry beyond datasets that reside close to a single manifold while retaining tractable and interpretable local structure.

</details>


### [258] [WOMBAT: Whitebox Oracle for Molecular Benchmarking and Attribution Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00713)
*Dominik Matuszek,Bartosz Zieliński,Tomasz Danel,Dawid Rymarczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WOMBAT基准，包含14个白盒GNN模型，每个模型的决策规则（检测特定SMARTS基序）由人工设置的消息传递权重确定，从而提供归因的真实标签，用于识别和解释归因方法的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当GNN解释器在分子上产生意外归因时，仅凭归因结果无法判断是解释器失败还是模型学到了捷径（shortcut）。因此需要一个决策规则已知、具备归因ground truth的基准来区分这两类问题。

Method: 构建WOMBAT基准，包含14个白盒GNN模型，每个模型通过手工设置消息传递权重来检测特定SMARTS基序；在数百万PubChem分子上验证模型；评估GNNExplainer、PGExplainer、Integrated Gradients等事后解释器；并基于定性分析构造一个使Integrated Gradients将归因散布到整图、但模型仍能可靠检测目标基序的模型。

Result: 模型在数百万PubChem分子上得到验证；评估了多种事后解释器；构造出的特殊模型能可靠检测目标基序，却使Integrated Gradients将归因散布到整个图上，暴露出解释器的失败模式。

Conclusion: WOMBAT为GNN的可解释性研究提供了带归因真实标签的基准，可帮助区分解释器失败与模型学到捷径，作者公开了数据集、模型和评估代码以支持新型GNN XAI工具的研发。

Abstract: When a graph neural network (GNN) explainer produces an unexpected attribution on a molecule, the attribution alone cannot reveal whether the explainer has failed or the model has learned a shortcut. We introduce WOMBAT, a benchmark of 14 whitebox GNNs, each with message-passing weights set by hand to detect a specific SMARTS motif. Each model's decision rule is known by construction, providing attribution ground truth against which explainer errors can be identified and studied. We validate the models on millions of PubChem molecules and evaluate post-hoc explainers including GNNExplainer, PGExplainer, and Integrated Gradients. Guided by our qualitative analysis, we construct a model that causes Integrated Gradients to spread attribution across the graph, even though the model reliably detects the intended motif. We release the dataset, models, and evaluation code to help researchers in the development of newer XAI tools for GNNs.

</details>


### [259] [JEPA-TTT: Persistent Test-Time Training of Latent World Models for Planning under Dynamics Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00722)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Suyu Ye,Nakul Agarwal,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Daniel Khashabi,Tianmin Shu,Vaishnav Tadiparthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JEPA-TTT在测试阶段对预训练的世界模型潜在动力学预测器进行自监督在线适应，在八种动力学变化下均提升规划性能，500个测试回合后潜在预测误差平均降低83%，规划性能提升153%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型虽能让智能体通过预测未来状态来规划，但当测试时的动力学与训练时不同，预测会变得不可靠。因此需要一种能在测试阶段适应变化动力学的方法。

Method: 提出JEPA-TTT，在测试阶段持续自适应预训练的、以动作为条件的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）世界模型中的潜在动力学预测器。自监督更新跨回合累积，视觉编码器与奖励头保持固定，以保留预训练表示与任务目标。规划不需要目标图像，也不需要在线环境奖励。采用密集回放（dense replay）：在每个时间偏移处构建预测窗口，存入不断增长的缓冲区，并从中采样小批量来更新预测器。

Result: 在四个连续控制环境的八种动力学变化中，JEPA-TTT在每一种变化上都提升了规划性能。经过500个测试回合后，自回归潜在预测误差平均降低83%，规划性能相比冻结的JEPA世界模型提升153%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，持续的自监督测试时训练能够使预训练的潜在世界模型在动力学发生变化时实现适应。

Abstract: World models enable agents to plan by predicting future states of the environment, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time dynamics differ from those seen during training. We present JEPA-TTT, which adapts the latent dynamics predictor of a pretrained action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture world model throughout test time. Self-supervised updates accumulate across episodes, while the visual encoder and reward head remain fixed, preserving the pretrained representation and task objective. Planning requires neither a goal image nor online environment reward. JEPA-TTT uses dense replay, which forms prediction windows at every temporal offset, retains them in a growing buffer, and samples minibatches from that buffer for predictor updates. Across eight dynamics shifts in four continuous-control environments, JEPA-TTT improves planning on every shift. After 500 test-time episodes, it reduces autoregressive latent prediction error by 83% on average and improves planning performance by 153% over the frozen JEPA world model. These results show that persistent self-supervised test-time training can adapt a pretrained latent world model under changed dynamics.

</details>


### [260] [Benchmarking Generative Models for Weather Data Assimilation on Real Station Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00728)
*Ruizhe Huang,Qidong Yang,Jonathan Giezendanner,Sherrie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在真实气象站点观测上对生成式天气数据同化进行受控基准测试，发现学习到的生成先验优于3D-Var的高斯先验，全梯度引导效果最好，而扩散与流匹配等选择差异不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气再分析依赖计算密集的数值天气预报与数据同化。深度生成模型提供了更廉价的替代方案，将成本从推理转移到离线训练。但现有生成方法多在合成观测或不同数据集与评估方案下评估，导致哪些设计选择真正改善真实数据同化尚不明确。

Method: 使用美国本土11,849个NOAA MADIS站点和四个气象变量，在固定数据集、观测算子和深度学习架构的前提下进行受控基准测试，比较扩散与流匹配、像素空间与潜空间公式、多种推理时条件策略，并与经典3D-Var基线对比；同时评估稠密与稀疏站点设置。

Result: 第一，学习到的生成先验优于3D-Var的高斯先验（相对ERA5的RMSE降低35.7% vs 33.3%），且推理时不使用ERA5背景场。第二，全梯度引导一致优于停止梯度和初始噪声优化。第三，其他选择收益不明显：扩散与流匹配在匹配条件下表现几乎相同，潜空间变量混合无帮助。稀疏站点下生成式AI和全梯度引导的优势更明显。

Conclusion: 本文明确了生成式天气数据同化中哪些组件能在真实站点观测上提升性能，并建立了标准化基准供未来研究使用。

Abstract: Weather reanalysis products rely on computationally intensive numerical weather predictions followed by data assimilation that corrects the forecast toward observations. Deep generative models offer a cheaper alternative that shifts much of this cost from inference to offline training. However, existing generative approaches have been evaluated on synthetic observations or under different datasets and evaluation schemes, making it unclear which design choices actually improve real-world data assimilation. We present the first controlled benchmark of generative weather data assimilation on real weather station observations. Using 11,849 NOAA MADIS stations across the contiguous United States and four weather variables, we evaluate methods while holding the dataset, observation operator, and deep learning architecture fixed. The benchmark compares the major design choices, including diffusion versus flow matching, pixel versus latent-space formulations, and multiple inference-time conditioning strategies, against a classical 3D-Var baseline. The benchmark reveals three clear conclusions. First, learned generative priors outperform the Gaussian prior of 3D-Var (35.7% vs. 33.3% RMSE reduction over ERA5) despite using no ERA5 background field at inference. Second, full-gradient guidance consistently outperforms stop-gradient and initial-noise optimization. Third, other choices provide little measurable benefit: diffusion and flow matching perform nearly identically under matched conditions, and latent-space variable mixing does not help. We further evaluate both dense and sparse station settings and find advantages from generative AI and full-gradient guidance more pronounced under sparsity. Together, these results identify which components of generative weather data assimilation improve performance on real station observations and establish a standardized benchmark for future work.

</details>


### [261] [Reward as Observation: Learning Reward-Based Policies for Rapid Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00729)
*Morgan Byrd,Maks Sorokin,Robert Wright,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅以奖励和动作为条件的基于奖励的策略，实现观测空间完全不同的源环境与目标环境之间的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络策略在适应新环境时往往表现不佳，且成功迁移需要大量样本；而人类具备出色的适应能力。为了克服观测空间差异带来的迁移困难，作者希望借助奖励这一跨环境共性信号，实现无需目标环境样本的零样本迁移。

Method: 提出一种新颖的、仅以奖励和动作为条件的基于奖励的策略（reward-based policy），不依赖观测；讨论了该策略的挑战与可行性，并给出一个实用的训练算法；同时在 Pointmass、Cartpole、2D Car Racing 等环境中训练该策略。

Result: 该基于奖励的策略可在三种不同环境中成功训练，并能以零样本方式迁移到观测完全不同的环境，例如不同配色、3D 渲染，以及 Habitat-Sim 中的 Stretch 机器人导航任务；此外该策略还能进一步指导目标环境中基于观测的策略的训练。

Conclusion: 基于奖励的策略能够有效应对完全不同的观测空间，实现零样本迁移，并为目标环境中的观测型策略训练提供引导，验证了奖励信号作为跨环境通用条件的可行性与实用价值。

Abstract: This paper explores a reward-based policy to achieve zero-shot transfer between source and target environments with completely different observation spaces. While humans can demonstrate impressive adaptation capabilities, deep neural network policies often struggle to adapt to a new environment and require a considerable amount of samples for successful transfer. Instead, we propose a novel reward-based policy only conditioned on rewards and actions, enabling zero-shot adaptation to new environments with completely different observations. We discuss the challenges and feasibility of a reward-based policy and then propose a practical algorithm for training. We demonstrate that a reward policy can be trained within three different environments, Pointmass, Cartpole, and 2D Car Racing, and transferred to completely different observations, such as different color palettes or 3D rendering, or Stretch robot navigation in Habitat-Sim, in a zero-shot manner. We also demonstrate that a reward-based policy can further guide the training of an observation-based policy in the target environment.

</details>


### [262] [Reformulation-Contrastive Learning for Mixed Integer Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00730)
*Ousema Bouaneni,Mathis Le Bail,Clément Elliker,Maël Jenny,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReMILP，利用 MILP 的等价重构作为自监督信号，通过等变对比学习训练图神经网络与超网络，学习变量与约束的通用表示，无需求解器标签即可在下游预测任务中取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MILP 广泛用于现实决策问题，利用重复结构加速求解的机器学习方法受到关注。MILP 存在多种等价表述（保持整数性的变量替换与冗余约束），这些重构可提供自监督信号，但如何将其系统化用于学习通用表示尚不明确。

Method: 作者刻画了对任意输入实例都有效的仿射重构，并区分不改变变量的"重描述"与可预测变换变量的"替换"两类情形。基于等变自监督学习，提出 ReMILP：联合训练图神经网络和超网络，预测变量嵌入在变量替换下的变换方式，无需求解器标签。

Result: ReMILP 学到的表示在未见问题类别上展现出预期的不变性与等变性；在二元解预测、约束活跃度和整数间隙预测任务中，冻结表示即可携带任务相关信息，并为微调提供有用初始化。

Conclusion: MILP 的等价重构可作为有效的自监督来源，ReMILP 能在无标签条件下学习通用且可迁移的 MILP 表示，为下游求解相关任务提供有效特征与初始化。

Abstract: Mixed-integer linear programs (MILP) model many real-world decision problems, motivating machine-learning methods that exploit recurring structure to accelerate MILP solving. MILPs can admit many equivalent formulations: integrality-preserving changes of variables and the addition of redundant constraints can alter their formulations while preserving the optimization problem. We leverage these reformulations as a source of self-supervision for learning general-purpose representations of MILP variables and constraints. We characterize the affine reformulations that are valid for every input instance, and distinguish re-descriptions, which leave variables unchanged, from substitutions, which transform them predictably. Building on equivariant self-supervised learning, we introduce ReMILP (reformulation-contrastive MILP representation learning), which jointly trains a graph neural network and a hypernetwork to predict how variable embeddings transform under changes of variables. Without solver-derived labels, ReMILP learns representations that exhibit the intended invariance and equivariance on unseen problem classes. Across binary solution, constraint activity and integrality gap prediction, these representations carry task-relevant information when frozen and provide a useful initialization for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [263] [Signal-Noise Factorization Isolates Nuisance Variation into Removable Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00751)
*Sakin Kirti,Joel Zylberberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建正则化器以强化表征几何中的信号-噪声分解(SNF)与信号-信号分解(SSF)，发现SNF正则化提升分类性能（尤其在被破坏的医学图像上），而SSF无此效果；SNF使噪声表征于独立子空间，可估计并投影去除，从而大幅提分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论指出表征几何的性质（信号-噪声分解SNF、信号-信号分解SSF）决定深度网络的推理能力，但尚不清楚能否通过训练时的正则化主动强化这些性质，以及它们如何影响模型性能与表征结构，尤其在含分布外失真的任务上。

Method: 设计强化SNF与SSF的正则化器；在CIFAR-100分类任务上与L2正则化基线对比；进而在经MedMNIST-C五种严重程度腐蚀的BloodMNIST数据集上评估；并分析不同正则化机制下的噪声/干扰子空间，在留出数据上估计主要腐蚀方向并将其从表征中投影剔除。

Result: 强化SNF的正则化提升了模型性能，强化SSF则未提升；在腐蚀后的BloodMNIST上SNF正则化带来更大增益。分析显示SNF正则化模型将噪声表征于与类别相关信号分离的独立子空间，据此估计并投影去除主要腐蚀方向后，准确率获得显著提升。

Conclusion: 强制信号-噪声分解的正则化器能在推理时含分布外图像失真的视觉任务上带来显著改进；同时表明表征塑造中，将干扰变量与类别变量分离比保持类别变量自身的分解表征更为重要。

Abstract: Recent theoretical work identified fundamental properties of representation geometry that shape inference ability of deep neural networks. These include signal-noise factorization (SNF), the ability to segregate signal from noise, and signal-signal factorization (SSF), the ability to segregate task-specific and task-irrelevant signals. Here, we built regularizers that reinforce these two properties during training. We compared networks trained with these regularizers to $L_2$-regularized baseline networks on the CIFAR-100 classification task to understand how our regularizers shape representation geometry and impact performance on a well-known computer vision baseline. Enhancing SNF via regularization improved model performance but enhancing SSF did not. Motivated by biomedical applications, we investigated how our regularizers affected performance on the BloodMNIST dataset treated with MedMNIST-C corruptions at five severity levels, and found even larger performance gains using the SNF regularizer. To understand the mechanism by which SNF-regularization produces improved performance, we analyzed the nuisance subspaces across regularization regimes, finding that the SNF-regularized models represent noise in distinct subspaces, separate from class-relevant signal. Because this geometry is explicit, the dominant corruption-induced directions can be estimated on held-out data and projected out of the representations. This manipulation led to a substantial gain in accuracy. These results show that regularizers that enforce signal-noise factorization can produce substantial improvements on computer vision tasks that contain out-of-distribution image distortions at inference time. They also highlight how shaping representations affects model performance: isolating nuisance variables from categorical ones is more important than maintaining factorized representations of categorical variables.

</details>


### [264] [Increasing Width Allows Greedy Layer-wise Training to Rival End-to-End Backpropagation in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00753)
*Syon Mansur,Joel Zylberberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在自监督学习背景下考察网络宽度与深度如何影响逐层贪婪训练与端到端反向传播的效果，发现宽度可在一定程度上补偿受限的信用分配，并指出表征几何差异是性能提升的潜在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端反向传播是深度学习训练的主流方法，但已有研究表明更简单的局部训练机制有时能达到相近性能。然而，在何种网络结构条件下，局部优化的网络能够学到与端到端训练相当的表示，仍不明确。作者希望结合自监督学习这一重要的大规模预训练框架来回答该问题。

Method: 在卷积网络中，系统研究网络宽度与深度对逐层贪婪（greedy layer-wise）自监督训练和端到端自监督训练效果的影响，并对两种训练方式所形成的表示进行分析。

Result: 在更宽的网络中，端到端反向传播相对逐层贪婪训练的优势缩小；在相对较浅且非常宽的网络中，逐层贪婪训练甚至取得更高性能。对表示的进一步分析显示，非常宽的贪婪训练网络比端到端反向传播网络具有更有利的表征几何。

Conclusion: 网络宽度可以补偿受限的信用分配（即局部训练带来的限制），而表征几何的差异是贪婪训练网络性能提升的潜在机制。

Abstract: End-to-end backpropagation has been the dominant mode of training in deep learning, allowing for the coordination of parameter updates across layers of a neural network. Prior studies have explored alternative -- and, in some cases, simpler -- training mechanisms, showing that they can sometimes achieve performance similar to backpropagation. However, the architectural conditions under which locally optimized networks, which avoid end-to-end backpropagation of error, can learn representations comparable to those learned through end-to-end training remain unclear. We aim to answer this question in the context of self-supervised learning, an important framework for large-scale pretraining in artificial intelligence. Here, we investigate how network width and depth affect the efficacy of greedy layer-wise and end-to-end self-supervised training in convolutional networks. We find that in wider networks, the benefits of end-to-end backpropagation over greedy layer-wise training shrink: in relatively shallow and very wide networks, we even observed higher performance in models trained with greedy layer-wise training. Subsequent analysis of the representations formed by these networks shows that very wide greedy-trained networks exhibit more favorable representational geometry than do networks trained end-to-end with backpropagation. This work shows that width can compensate for restricted credit assignment and identifies differences in representational geometry as a potential mechanism for their improved performance.

</details>


### [265] [Scalable Multi-Task Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00758)
*Allen Tran,Jia Wan,Nathan Kallus,Aurélien Bibaut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多任务逆强化学习方法，通过在同环境下跨智能体汇总数据并利用低秩假设，缓解覆盖要求并支持可扩展的反事实评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆强化学习可用于在新环境下对智能体进行反事实评估，但迁移学习对数据覆盖要求严格，因为目标环境会改变智能体的状态占用分布。单独处理每个任务难以满足覆盖需求，且任务增多时规划计算开销大。

Method: 提出多任务IRL方法，在同一环境下对具有不同奖励的多个智能体数据进行汇总，并在低秩假设下进行学习。该方法使各任务无需覆盖全部状态，只需被其他任务覆盖即可，同时将规划计算复杂度与秩而非任务数量关联，从而实现多任务在新环境下的可扩展评估。

Result: 在奖励恢复与策略学习方面给出了有限样本保证。实验表明该方法对有限覆盖具有鲁棒性，能够恢复各任务支撑集内外的奖励，在新环境迁移中具有比基线更低的遗憾，且相对逐任务方法的计算优势随任务数量增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 跨任务共享低秩结构的IRL能有效缓解覆盖难题并提升多任务反事实评估的可扩展性，理论与实验均支持其奖励恢复与迁移性能优势。

Abstract: By learning transferable rewards, inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) enables counterfactual evaluation of agents under modified environments. Such transfer places strict requirements on coverage since target environments affect agents' state occupancy. We propose a multi-task IRL method that pools data across multiple agents with different rewards in the same environment under a low-rank assumption. In addition to alleviating coverage requirements, so each task need not visit every state as long as others do, the method offers scalable evaluation of multiple tasks under new environments as computationally intensive planning scales with rank rather than the number of tasks. We provide finite sample guarantees on reward recovery and on policy learning in new environments. Experiments show our method is robust to limited coverage, recovers rewards on and off of each task's support, transfers to target environments at lower regret than baselines, with its computational advantage over per-task methods widening as tasks grow.

</details>


### [266] [Pre-training interventions, ex post facto: Grafting model beliefs across checkpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00767)
*Peter Nutter,Dani Roytburg,Clément Dumas,Jinghua Ou,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"嫁接"(grafting)方法：在预训练检查点上训练SDF适配器，再将其权重更新加到已后训练的模型上，从而近似忠实的预/中训练干预，同时复用现有后训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练阶段形成的信念会影响模型后续泛化，因此预训练干预对对齐研究至关重要。合成文档微调(SDF)旨在改变模型信念，理想做法是将其混入预训练或中训练，但每次改动语料都需完整跑一遍后训练才能评估效果，迭代慢且昂贵。实践中常直接把SDF施加到已后训练模型上，这会留下伪迹、损害能力，并导致"现实漂移"——模型把与文档无关的虚构实体当作真实存在。

Method: 提出"嫁接"(grafting)：在预训练检查点上训练SDF适配器，然后把学到的权重更新加到已后训练的模型中，以此近似忠实的预/中训练方式，同时复用已有的后训练结果。论文通过植入虚假事实、训练失准模型生物体、以及施加宪法式中训练干预来验证，覆盖最大284B参数的多个模型族。

Result: 嫁接方法安装目标信念的强度与直接在后训练模型上做SDF相当，但平均将现实漂移和偏好连贯性损失降低了一半以上，且更接近忠实的中间训练运行。由于嫁接无需后训练，同一适配器可应用于任何后续检查点。

Conclusion: 嫁接让研究者能快速迭代预训练干预，代价仅为一次微调运行，兼顾了干预忠实性与迭代效率。

Abstract: Pre-training interventions are critical to alignment research, since beliefs formed during pre-training shape how a model generalizes from later training. One recently popular technique for such interventions is synthetic document fine-tuning (SDF), which aims to alter what the model believes. Ideally, synthetic documents would be mixed into pre- or mid-training, but every change to a pre-training corpus must be followed by a full post-training run before its effect can be measured, making iteration slow and expensive. Common practice instead applies SDF to an already post-trained model. This is known to leave artifacts and degrade capabilities, and, as we show, it makes the model treat fabricated entities unrelated to the documents as real, a failure we call reality drift. We propose grafting: train the SDF adapter on the pre-trained checkpoint, then add the learned weight update to the post-trained model, which approximates the faithful approach while reusing the existing post-training. We demonstrate this by installing false facts, training misaligned model organisms and applying a constitutional mid-training intervention, across model families up to 284B parameters. Grafting installs the target belief as strongly as SDF on the post-trained model while reducing both reality drift and the loss of preference coherence by more than half on average, and it stays closer to a faithful mid-training run. Because grafting requires no post-training, the same adapter can be applied to any later checkpoint, enabling researchers to iterate quickly on pre-training interventions at the cost of a single fine-tuning run.

</details>


### [267] [Localizing Transfer Between Memorization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00771)
*Yimiao Yu,Florentin Guth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过随机输入输出映射的记忆任务研究了迁移学习机制，发现等效迁移与非等效迁移两种模式，并将其分解为最后一层的"平凡"幅度驱动迁移和其他层的"非平凡"结构驱动迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习的核心谜题在于：为何在一个任务上预训练能加速或提升另一任务的训练，其背后机制尚不清晰。本文旨在揭示这一迁移机制。

Method: 以随机输入输出映射的记忆任务为研究对象，考察任务间的迁移现象，并通过消融实验将迁移效应进行分解与定位。

Result: 发现两种迁移模式：等效迁移（每增加一个预训练轮次约节省一个下游微调轮次）和非等效迁移（在不匹配任务上预训练甚至比直接在下游任务上训练更高效）。消融实验将迁移分解为最后一层幅度驱动的"平凡"迁移，以及部分归因于其他层协方差的"非平凡"结构驱动迁移。

Conclusion: 研究深化了对迁移学习底层机制的理解，有望指导有原则的预训练策略设计。

Abstract: A central puzzle in transfer learning is why pre-training on one task can accelerate training or improve performance on another task, and what mechanisms underlie this transfer. In this work, we examine the transfer between memorization tasks of random input-output mappings. We find two surprising transfer patterns: equivalent transfer, where each additional pre-training epoch saves approximately one downstream fine-tuning epoch; and non-equivalent transfer, where pre-training on a mismatched task can be even more efficient than directly training on the downstream task itself. Through ablation experiments, we decompose and localize the transfer into two separate effects: a "trivial" magnitude-driven transfer in the last layer, and a "non-trivial" structure-driven transfer, partially attributable to the covariance of the other layers. These results advance our understanding of the underlying mechanisms of transfer learning and have the potential to lead to principled pre-training strategies.

</details>


### [268] [Learning Goal-Reaching Quasimetric Geometry From Finite-Time Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00778)
*Daisuke Yamada,Travis Pence,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ReQRL，通过有限时域可达性约束 critic 的价值梯度，将动力学可达性与边界几何解耦并从数据中估计，以改进准度量学习在目标条件强化学习中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中，准度量学习将目标达成代价建模为准度量距离。但其局部约束应同时反映有限时域内控制复合的方向依赖性效应以及环境的可行性，而现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 提出 ReQRL，通过有限时域可达性来约束 critic 的价值梯度；借鉴状态约束最优控制，将动力学可达性与边界几何解耦，并分别从数据中估计二者。

Result: 在 OGBench 基准上，该方法优于或媲美现有的准度量方法及其他离线 GCRL 方法。

Conclusion: ReQRL 通过显式建模有限时域可达性并解耦动力学与边界几何，有效提升了准度量学习在离线 GCRL 中的表现。

Abstract: In goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), quasimetric learning models goal-reaching costs as quasimetric distances, connecting local constraints to global value geometry. Its local constraints, however, should reflect the direction- dependent effects of control composition over a finite horizon together with environmental feasibility. We propose ReQRL, which constrains the critic's value gradients through finite-horizon reachability. Drawing on state-constrained optimal control, we decouple dynamical reachability from boundary geometry, estimating both from data. On OGBench, our method outperforms or rivals existing quasimetric approaches and other offline GCRL methods.

</details>


### [269] [Training-Aware Target Coverage for Synthetic Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00814)
*Yang Ba,Michelle V. Mancenido,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性理论刻画合成数据在LLM训练中的价值权衡，并据此设计TATC选择方法，在数学推理任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据被广泛用于扩展LLM训练规模，但更多合成数据并不必然带来更好模型。有效数据须补充目标任务相关信息且不引入抵消收益的错误，且样本价值会随训练集增长而变化。

Method: 建立线性理论刻画上述权衡，确定合成数据何时有用、应添加多少以及边际价值；据此提出Training-Aware Target Coverage (TATC)方法，识别对目标任务训练效应有益的候选样本，并从中选择以扩展尚未被现有数据覆盖的目标相关方向。

Result: 在文本与图像数据上验证了线性理论；在数学推理任务中，TATC为Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct选择合成解答进行微调，在GSM8K上各选择预算下均优于其他合成数据选择方法。

Conclusion: 通过量化并最大化合成数据对目标任务的价值，提供了有原则的合成数据选择方法。

Abstract: Synthetic data are increasingly used to scale LLM training, yet more synthetic data do not necessarily produce better models. Useful synthetic data must add information relevant to the target task without introducing errors that offset their benefit, and the value of an example can change as the training set grows. We develop a linear theory that characterizes this tradeoff and determines where synthetic data are useful, how much should be added, and the marginal value of adding one example to an existing set. The analysis shows the conditions when input coverage alone is sufficient and when synthetic errors must also be considered. Guided by these results, we introduce \emph{Training-Aware Target Coverage} (TATC), a synthetic data selection method for LLM fine-tuning. TATC identifies candidates whose training effects are beneficial to the target task and selects among them to expand coverage of target-relevant directions not already represented by the available data. Experiments on text and image data verify the linear theory. With mathematical reasoning tasks, TATC selects synthetic solutions for fine-tuning Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct and outperforms alternative synthetic-data selection methods on GSM8K across selection budgets. In summary, we provide a principled approach to synthetic data selection by quantifying and maximizing its value to the target task.

</details>


### [270] [Quantifying the Impact of Ambulance Ramping: A Multi-Year Analysis of Victorian Emergency Medical Services Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00818)
*Ayesha Tanveer,Khandakar Ahmed,Assefa Teshome,Oyetunde Gbadeyan,Ziad Nehme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对澳大利亚维多利亚州全州EMS系统2020年1月至2024年3月共285万余次救护车出勤进行探索性数据分析，量化了救护车"滞留交接"（ramping）的规模、趋势及运营关联因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 救护车ramping（到达医院至患者交接之间的延迟）是EMS的关键运营瓶颈，但其系统性规模与动态在大范围尺度上仍缺乏充分刻画，亟需在全州系统层面进行实证量化。

Method: 对2,850,575次出勤进行系统预处理，形成覆盖59家有急诊科医院、79个地方政府区域的2,026,569例急诊转运分析队列；采用区间分解、帕累托集中度分析、小时级互相关、医院到达并发度以及按优先级分层的运营比较等方法开展EDA。

Result: 累计救护车损失工时达1,491,127小时，相当于研究期内每天约损失96个十小时班次；59家医院中10家贡献了57.8%的损失工时（对应50.9%的病例）。年度损失上升57%并在2022年达峰，而转运需求下降3.7%，表明是单例交接恶化而非需求增长所致。交接时长与患者急症程度关系甚微，但随前一小时到达同一医院的救护车数量单调上升，且该效应在一天中各时段均存在。小时需求与2至4小时后的ramping呈中度相关（r = 0.365）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了面向医院状态感知的救护车调度分流的实证前提，即ramping损失高度集中、由交接效率恶化驱动，并可通过到达并发度与需求时滞进行预测。

Abstract: Ambulance ramping, the delay between hospital arrival and patient handover, is a critical operational bottleneck in Emergency Medical Services (EMS), yet its systemic magnitude and dynamics remain inadequately characterised at scale. This paper quantifies the scale, trajectory, and operational correlates of ramping across an entire statewide EMS system, analysing 2,850,575 ambulance attendances in Victoria, Australia from January 2020 to March 2024 using an Exploratory Data Analysis (EDA). After systematic preprocessing, an analytical cohort of 2,026,569 Emergency Department (ED) transports across 59 hospitals with ED and 79 Local Government Areas (LGA) was examined through interval decomposition, Pareto concentration, hourly cross-correlation, hospital arrival concurrency and priority-stratified operational comparisons. Cumulative Ambulance Hours Lost (AHL) totalled 1,491,127 hours, equivalent to approximately 96 ten-hour ambulance shift lost every day of the study window. Ten of 59 hospitals account for 57.8% of lost hours from 50.9% of cases. Annual losses rose 57% to a 2022 peak while transported demand fell 3.7%, indicating deterioration in per-case handover rather than growth in demand. Handover duration varies little with patient acuity, but rises monotonically with the number of ambulances arriving at the same hospital in the preceding hour, an effect persisting within every hour of the day. Hourly demand is moderately associated with ramping two to four hours later (r = 0.365). These findings establish the empirical preconditions for hospital-state aware ambulance routing.

</details>


### [271] [On-the-fly Weight Generation: A Hypernetwork Proof of Concept on ARC-1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00820)
*Fabio J. Fehr,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从少量示例中直接生成专用模型参数的方法，通过超网络将上下文编译为紧凑的可执行模型参数，在ARC-1D测试平台上验证了其可行性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型能通过上下文适应多种任务，但专用模型需要特定任务的训练或适应才能获得。作者希望探索是否可以直接从少量示例中生成专用模型，从而避免任务特定的训练过程。

Method: 使用ARC-1D作为受控测试平台。首先证明单个变换可以用极小的专用模型表示，然后使用超网络从上下文中生成这些模型的参数。实验考察生成参数是否形成结构化权重空间，以及专用模型的部分组合泛化能力和对训练中未见变换的泛化能力。此外还比较了移除显式任务标识符对泛化的影响。

Result: 生成的参数形成了结构化的权重空间；专用模型展现出部分组合泛化能力，以及对训练中未见变换的泛化能力；在这两种设置下，移除显式任务标识符能够提升超越训练变换的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 研究提供了概念验证，表明少样本任务上下文可以被即时编译为紧凑的可执行模型参数，且生成的权重空间能够支持超越已知函数的复用与泛化。

Abstract: General-purpose models can adapt to many tasks from context, while specialised models can execute individual functions with less capacity. Yet obtaining such specialists requires task-specific training or adaptation. We ask whether they can instead be generated directly from a few demonstrations. Using ARC-1D as a controlled testbed, we show that individual transformations can be represented by tiny specialist models, and that a hypernetwork can generate their parameters from context. The generated parameters form a structured weight space, while the resulting specialists show partial compositional generalisation and generalisation to transformations not seen during training. In both settings, removing explicit task identifiers improves generalisation beyond the training transformations. Together, these results provide a proof of concept that few-shot task context can be compiled on-the-fly into compact executable model parameters, and that the resulting weight space can support reuse and generalisation beyond known functions.

</details>


### [272] [AnyJev Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00831)
*Jiamu Zhang,Tianze Yang,Yucheng Shi,Evan Chen,Zixiang Nie,Kelly Wan,Liangjie Hong,Ninghao Liu,Liang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnyJev 通过一次 prefill、除以标签先验并对选项列表的 K 次循环旋转取平均，从指令微调语言模型中读出带类型的决策，无需梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要带类型决策的系统通常要使用专门训练的模型；本文希望直接利用预训练的指令微调语言模型，在一次 prefill 中读出概率形式的决策，从而避免专门训练。但简单的读出方式存在两个缺陷：模型会无论输入如何都偏好某些标签，以及偏好选项列表中的某些位置。

Method: AnyJev 在答案位置把下一 token 分布限制在选项 token 上。为消除缺陷，它不进行任何梯度更新、也不改变参数：一是除以从无标注输入估计的标签先验；二是把选项列表的 K 次循环旋转下的对数概率取平均。此外提出停止规则，在对无标注状态的全旋转决策上选择阈值，以在部分旋转后停止，减少所需的 prefill 次数。

Result: 在两个 20 选项任务上，旋转把顺序翻转率从 0.33 降到 0.14 和从 0.33 降到 0.18，并在 11 个模型上全部提升了准确率。停止规则在无标注状态下选择：在一个无标注划分上选阈值、在第二个划分上约束其不一致性时，四个单元中有两个只需读取 18 次旋转中的 10.6 次，且验证界为 0.008；在单一划分上选择并约束时读取 7.3 次，并在 vLLM 上每秒服务的决策数是原来的 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: AnyJev 表明无需训练或参数修改，仅靠先验校正与选项旋转平均，即可从预训练指令微调模型中获得较可靠的带类型决策，并通过停止规则显著降低计算开销、提升服务吞吐。代码已开源。

Abstract: A typed decision is a choice among a fixed set of options, returned as a probability rather than as text. Systems that need typed decisions today use models trained for that purpose. This report describes AnyJev, which reads a typed decision from one prefill of a pretrained instruction-tuned language model. The readout restricts the next-token distribution at the answer position to the option tokens. It has two defects: the model assigns higher probability to some labels whatever the input, and to some positions in the option list. AnyJev corrects both with no gradient steps and no parameter changes: it divides out a label prior estimated from unlabelled inputs, and it averages log-probabilities over the K cyclic rotations of the option list. On two 20-option tasks the rotations lower the order-flip rate from 0.33 to 0.14 and from 0.33 to 0.18, and raise accuracy on 11 of 11 models on both. Reading every rotation requires K prefills. A stopping rule selected against the full-rotation decision on unlabelled states cuts that. Selecting the threshold on one unlabelled split and bounding its disagreement on a second, it reads 10.6 rotations of 18 at a verified 0.008 bound on two of four cells; selected and bounded on one split, as our serving run did, it reads 7.3 and serves 2.2 times as many decisions per second on vLLM. The code is open source.

</details>


### [273] [TrueMuse: A Benchmark for Data Attribution in Text-to-Music Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00835)
*Jiawei Yu,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrueMuse，一个用于文本到音乐生成数据归因的受控数据集与基准，并系统评估了现有黑盒归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音乐生成模型基于海量音乐数据训练，需要量化单个训练样本贡献的数据归因方法；但现有归因方法因缺乏可靠的真值（ground truth）而难以被严格评估，其实际有效性难以可靠衡量。

Method: 构建TrueMuse：在精心挑选的归因样本上微调三个基于扩散的文本到音乐模型，其已知的被纳入情况为评估提供受控的归因目标。基准涵盖四种归因设置（旋律结构、音色特征、艺术家级风格特征、流派级共享模式），包含133个属性、648个微调模型、95,456个生成样本、两种提示类型。基于该基准，从四个维度系统评估现有黑盒归因方法：微调提升、提示类型难度、多任务训练、微调数据规模。

Result: 归因仍然具有挑战性，现有方法在不同评估设置下表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: 文本到音乐生成领域亟需更可靠、更具泛化能力的归因方法；代码与数据集将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Text-to-music generation models are trained on massive music collections, creating a growing need for data attribution methods that can quantify the contribution of individual training samples. However, existing attribution methods are difficult to rigorously evaluate due to the lack of reliable ground truth, making it challenging to reliably assess their actual effectiveness. To address this gap, we introduce TrueMuse, a controlled dataset and benchmark for text-to-music data attribution. TrueMuse is constructed by fine-tuning three diffusion-based text-to-music models on carefully curated attribution samples, whose known inclusion in fine-tuning provides controlled attribution targets for evaluation. The benchmark covers four attribution settings, spanning melodic structure, timbral characteristics, artist-level stylistic signatures, and genre-level shared patterns, and includes 133 attributes, 648 fine-tuned models, and 95,456 generated samples across two prompt types. Using TrueMuse, we systematically evaluate existing black-box attribution methods along four dimensions: fine-tuning improvement, prompt-type difficulty, multi-task training, and fine-tuning data size. Our results show that attribution remains challenging, with existing methods exhibiting substantial variation across evaluation settings, highlighting the need for more reliable and generalizable attribution methods for text-to-music generation. Code and Dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [274] [SHARPO: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00838)
*Xinchen Du,Zhengze Zhou,Wenhui Zhu,Han Yu,Sen Na,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SHARPO，一种在环境交互片段层面精细化GRPO信用分配的机制，在ALFWorld和WebShop上超越GRPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习训练LLM进行长程多步交互时，单个局部错误即可导致任务失败，而轨迹级奖励难以为单个决策分配信用。

Method: 受在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）启发，SHARPO在每个片段内计算教师-学生对数概率差，据此生成有界乘子作用于GRPO优势，该乘子由片段内所有token共享，使信用可在不同片段间变化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上，SHARPO在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上优于GRPO、SDAR、RLSD和StepOPSD等现有基线。

Conclusion: 片段级事后优势重加权为长程智能体RL提供了更细粒度的信用分配，是一种有效的策略优化改进方案。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) trains a large language model (LLM) to act over long, multi-step interactions. However, a single localized error can cause task failure, while trajectory-level rewards provide limited guidance for assigning credit to individual decisions. To address this limitation, we introduce Segment-level Hindsight Advantage Reweighting for Policy Optimization (SHARPO), a credit-assignment mechanism that refines Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) at the level of environment-facing segments. Inspired by the existing on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) method, SHARPO computes teacher-student log-probability gaps within each segment and uses the resulting signal to compute a bounded multiplier on the GRPO advantage. This multiplier is shared by all tokens within the segment, allowing credit to vary across different segments. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, SHARPO outperforms existing baselines on the ALFWorld and WebShop benchmarks, including GRPO, SDAR, RLSD, and StepOPSD.

</details>


### [275] [Fixing a Model That Learned Worse Cancer Means Lower Risk: Monotonic Constraints in Bladder Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00858)
*Saram Abbas,David Thomas,Naeem Soomro,Rishad Shafik,Rakesh Heer,Kabita Adhikari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出反事实方向检验以检测机器学习模型学到的"风险反转"，并用单调约束在不损失性能的前提下消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生预期癌症越严重复发风险越高，但无约束XGBoost模型却学到更高肿瘤分期和原位癌预示更低复发风险，而常规的区分度、校准和SHAP检查均未能识别这一反转。

Method: 基于BOXIT多中心试验（472例患者，51个英国站点，435例有至少两年随访，153例复发），开发反事实方向检验与单调约束校正，约束方向取自EORTC和EAU风险系统，在18个预测变量（7个有向）上通过50折交叉验证与无约束XGBoost和逻辑回归对比。检验通过逐一恶化患者某一有向特征，观察风险是否下降，并评估SHAP方向和校准。

Result: 肿瘤分期和原位癌与更低复发相关，与医学直觉相反；无约束模型在90.2%的病例中反转了原位癌反事实，74.3%反转了分期。区分度（ΔAUC 0.005，p=0.47）、校准和SHAP幅度均未察觉该反转。单调约束消除了所有违规且不损失区分度（0.723 vs 0.718），并优于EORTC（p=8.9e-16）。局限为单一试验、仅内部-外部验证。

Conclusion: 学到该反转的模型通过了所有常规检查；反事实方向检验作为一次带预设单调约束的重拟合，可在不损失性能的情况下捕捉此类失败，应在临床部署前常规进行。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Clinicians expect recurrence risk to climb with cancer severity. In a UK multicentre trial, an unconstrained XGBoost model learnt that higher tumour stage and carcinoma in situ predicted lower recurrence risk, and discrimination, calibration, and SHAP were all blind to it. We developed a counterfactual testing framework to detect this inversion and a monotonic-constraint framework to remove it without hurting performance. Methods: BOXIT enrolled 472 patients with protocol-mandated cystoscopy across 51 UK sites (2007-2012); 435 had at least two years' follow-up (153 recurrences, 35.2%). We developed a counterfactual direction test and a monotonic-constraint correction, with constraint directions drawn from the EORTC and EAU risk systems, and evaluated both against unconstrained XGBoost and logistic regression on 18 predictors (seven directed) over 50 cross-validation folds. The test worsened each patient on one directed feature at a time to check whether risk fell; SHAP direction and calibration were also assessed.
