*Jinhwan Sul,Panagiotis Theodoropoulos,Vincent Pacelli,Jaemoo Choi,Evangelos Theodorou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布鲁棒薛定谔桥（DRSB），在初始分布不确定时学习单一控制器最小化最坏情况目标，用交替算法求解并在二维传输与图像翻译任务上验证鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薛定谔桥（SB）学习给定初始与目标分布间的随机传输，但测试时初始分布发生偏移会导致学习到的动力学无法恢复目标分布。为应对初始分布不确定性，需要一种对初始分布扰动鲁棒的传输方法。

Method: DRSB 目标由控制能量与终端分布和目标分布间的 KL 惩罚组成，在初始分布的名义分布周围歧义集内取最坏情况最小化。论文给出该目标的精确变分形式，并将其固定终端代价子问题与随机最优控制、分布鲁棒优化相联系。基于此提出交替算法：更新对抗初始分布、估计终端对数密度比、训练控制器；并利用随机控制最优性条件近似对抗更新所需梯度，构造 Wasserstein 与 Sinkhorn 两种变体。

Result: 在二维传输任务和图像到图像翻译上，相对标准 SB 提升了对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但名义性能存在折衷。在高斯混合传输上，Sinkhorn DRSB 在两个测试未见噪声水平下的平均切片 Wasserstein 距离均低于固定水平噪声增强方法。

Conclusion: DRSB 通过分布鲁棒优化框架为薛定谔桥提供对初始分布偏移的鲁棒性，单一控制器即可应对歧义集内扰动，代价是牺牲一定名义性能；Sinkhorn 变体在实证中还优于噪声增强基线。

Abstract: Schrödinger bridge (SB) learns stochastic transport between prescribed initial and target distributions. When the initial distribution shifts at test time, the learned dynamics can fail to recover the target distribution. We introduce the Distributionally Robust Schrödinger Bridge (DRSB), which learns a single controller that accounts for uncertainty in the initial distribution. The DRSB objective consists of control energy and a KL penalty between the resulting terminal distribution and the target distribution. DRSB seeks a single controller that minimizes the worst-case value of this objective as the initial distribution varies within an ambiguity set around the nominal distribution. We derive an exact variational formulation of this objective and connect its fixed-terminal-cost subproblem to stochastic optimal control and distributionally robust optimization. This formulation motivates an alternating algorithm that updates the adversarial initial distribution, estimates the terminal log-density ratio, and trains the controller. We develop Wasserstein and Sinkhorn variants using stochastic control optimality conditions to approximate the gradients required for adversarial updates. Experiments on two-dimensional transport tasks and image-to-image translation show improved robustness to input perturbations relative to standard SB, with a tradeoff in nominal performance. On Gaussian mixture transport, Sinkhorn DRSB also achieves lower mean sliced Wasserstein distance than fixed-level noise augmentation at both tested unseen noise levels.

</details>


### [382] [Physics-Refined Spatiotemporal Forecasting on Open-Boundary Hydrologic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01765)
*Haoyang Jiang,Zhengui Wang,Shenghan Gao,Y. Joseph Zhang,Xingquan Zhu,Yi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对开放边界水文图时空预测中的不稳定性问题，提出融合“幽灵节点代理”与两个物理精炼器的计算框架，以补偿缺失的外部边界强迫并抑制自回归误差累积，从而提升长时预测精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界水文系统中的边界节点会受到外部强迫（如河流上游入流、海岸潮汐信号），但这些信息在预测时通常不可获取。缺失信息会在自回归预测中不断放大误差，导致长时域性能下降。因此需要解决两个问题：一是信息缺失时何种边界强迫进入预测域，二是该强迫应如何在域内传播而不引发误差放大。

Method: 提出一个新的计算框架，包含两个关键组件：其一，从边界节点与内部节点学习“幽灵节点代理”，以近似未观测到的外部输入，从而补偿边界强迫；其二，使用两个物理精炼器控制由学习代理带来的误差累积。其中，一个精炼器通过将幽灵代理与其两跳邻居（边界节点及其直接内部节点）对齐来强制局部一致性；另一个精炼器通过物理引导的图神经算子修正模型预测，以增强全局稳定性并减少长时数值漂移。

Result: 在两个真实水文图上进行实证评估。对比结果表明，该提案在学习型和物理知情型模型竞争者中均取得了更高的预测精度和更好的长时域稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过建模未观测边界强迫并施加局部一致性与全局稳定性约束，有效缓解了开放边界水文图预测中的不稳定性，并在真实数据上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal forecasting on hydrologic graphs is especially prone to instability in open-boundary systems, where the forecast domain exchanges fluxes with an unobserved exterior. In such systems, boundary nodes receive external forcing, e.g., upstream inflows in rivers or tidal signals in coastal regions, that is typically unavailable at prediction time. The absence of this information can compound errors as forecasts unfold in an autoregressive fashion, leading to inferior long-horizon performance. This paper dissects this instability issue by exploring two questions. 1) What boundary forcing enters the forecast domain when information beyond the boundary is missing? 2) How should this forcing propagate through the domain without incurring error amplification under autoregressive rollout?
  To address both, we propose a new computing framework comprising two key components. First, to compensate for the boundary forcing, our framework learns ghost node proxies from the boundary and interior nodes, striving to approximate unobserved external inputs. Second, to control error accumulation from these learned proxies, we leverage two physics refiners. In particular, one refiner enforces local consistency by aligning ghost proxies with their two-hop neighbors (i.e., boundary nodes and their immediate interiors). The other refiner enhances global stability by correcting the model forecasts through a physics-guided graph neural operator, reducing long-horizon numerical drift. Two real-world hydrologic graphs are employed for empirical evaluation. Comparative results show that our proposal enjoys higher prediction accuracy and long-horizon stability over both learning-based and physics-informed model competitors.

</details>


### [383] [Local Support Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02126)
*Assaf Ben-Kish,Akarsh Kumar,James Glass,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将灾难性遗忘视为各权重矩阵输入空间中的几何问题，提出 Local Support Learning (LSL)，通过"权重适配器 + GMM 门控"的组合，在无旧数据的情况下保留大模型多阶段训练中的既有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大预训练模型在新阶段学习时会遭遇灾难性遗忘。作者从几何视角分析：基于梯度的优化器产生的更新在"保留能力"这一自然目标下是次优的，因此需要一种能约束更新局部性的方法，且不依赖旧数据。

Method: LSL 为通用框架，包含两个角色分明的组件：(1) 标准权重适配器，照常最小化损失训练；(2) 门控函数，仅在本阶段训练分布的输入激活上启用适配器，使更新局部化。门控采用高斯混合模型（GMM），其似然在远离训练数据时迅速衰减，因而对旧阶段数据天然趋于关闭。

Result: 在多达 70 亿参数的 LLM 上，该方法可解决遗忘问题，在多个训练阶段中同时保留预训练与微调能力；内存与计算高效，对超参数选择鲁棒，并展现出扩展潜力。

Conclusion: 将遗忘建模为输入空间几何问题并约束更新局部性，是无需旧数据即可缓解大模型灾难性遗忘的有效后训练思路。

Abstract: We explore catastrophic forgetting in the context of large pre-trained models. By considering forgetting as a geometric problem in the input space of each weight matrix, we uncover a natural retention objective under which updates produced by gradient-based optimizers are suboptimal. Following this observation, we propose Local Support Learning (LSL), a general-purpose framework that augments gradient-based training for retention of prior capabilities without access to prior data. During a new learning phase, LSL pairs two components with distinct roles: a standard weight adapter, trained as usual to minimize the loss, and a gating function that enables the adapter only on input activations from its own training distribution, making the update local to that distribution. The key challenge is that this gate must route data from all learning phases while training only on data from the current one. We address this with a gate based on a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), whose likelihood decays rapidly away from its training data, giving it a natural tendency to stay closed on data from prior phases. We show that this post-training approach can resolve forgetting in LLMs of up to 7 billion parameters, retaining both pretrained and finetuned capabilities across multiple training phases, while being efficient in memory and compute, robust to hyperparameter choice, and showing scaling potential.

</details>


### [384] [Inferring Multi-Timescale Neural Dynamics with Switching Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01786)
*Lulu Gong,Yongxu Zhang,Shreya Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出多时间尺度切换线性动力系统（MTS-SLDS），用于从连续或脉冲神经观测中识别随行为状态变化的多重时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经活动常呈现随行为状态和任务条件变化的多重时间尺度，识别这些时间尺度有助于理解神经计算与功能。但传统基于自相关拟合的方法难以扩展到高维群体记录，且当神经动态随行为变化时可能变得不可靠；标准状态空间模型未显式考虑多重时间尺度，无法保证准确恢复潜在时间结构。

Method: 提出MTS-SLDS框架，结合多滞后矩初始化（跨多个观测滞后捕捉时间结构）与以状态区制为条件的Laplace-EM推断（regime-conditioned Laplace-EM），以减少不确定区制间动态统计的混叠；随后可直接从学习到的潜在转移矩阵特征值中提取特征时间尺度。

Result: 在高斯和泊松脉冲观测的合成与神经实验中，MTS-SLDS在多个数据集上均能准确恢复时间尺度及切换结构。

Conclusion: MTS-SLDS为从高维神经群体活动中识别区制特异的多重时间尺度提供了有效框架，克服了传统自相关方法与标准状态空间模型的局限。

Abstract: Neural activity often exhibits multiple timescales that can vary with behavioral states and task conditions. Identifying these timescales from neural recordings is important for better understanding neural computation and function. However, traditional approaches based on autocorrelation fitting are difficult to scale to high-dimensional population recordings and can become unreliable when neural dynamics change with behavior. State-space models have been a powerful framework for modeling high-dimensional neural population activity through latent dynamical systems, but standard formulations and inference methods do not explicitly account for multiple timescales and therefore do not guarantee accurate recovery of the underlying temporal structure. Motivated by these questions, we introduce the Multi-Timescale Switching Linear Dynamical System (MTS-SLDS), a framework for identifying regime-specific latent timescales from continuous or spiking neural observations. MTS-SLDS combines a multi-lag moment initialization, which captures temporal structure across multiple observation lags, with \textit{regime-conditioned} Laplace-EM inference, which reduces mixing of dynamical statistics across uncertain regimes. Characteristic timescales can then be extracted directly from the eigenvalues of the learned latent transition matrices. In synthetic and neural experiments with Gaussian and Poisson spike observations, MTS-SLDS accurately recovers timescales and switching structure over multiple datasets.

</details>


### [385] [Finetuning with Sampling: SFT Learns Better Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02140)
*Aayush Karan,Sitan Chen,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种MCMC采样算法，将离策略（off-policy）数据逐步转化为更接近在策略（on-policy）分布的数据，从而使SFT能够达到甚至超越主流后训练方法的效果，在新任务上泛化更好、遗忘更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练中，SFT擅长利用专家离策略数据但泛化弱且易灾难性遗忘；RL泛化与保能力较强但依赖重复采样寻找成功轨迹且无法直接使用离策略数据。作者希望在保留在策略学习优势的同时利用离策略数据中的特权信息，且不修改学习目标，而是调整数据分布以适配学习器。

Method: 引入Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) 采样算法，在给定参考微调模型的情况下，将离策略轨迹逐步变换为更接近在策略的数据分布，而非改动学习目标本身。

Result: 在科学技能习得、数学推理和开放式专业知识等任务上，该采样算法使SFT能够媲美主流后训练技术，通常比强在策略基线泛化更好、遗忘更少；微调后模型表现出较强的分布性能，且能在锐化基础模型分布之外进行学习。

Conclusion: 该工作将采样视为一种模型原生算子，用于塑造更易学习的数据，为后训练流程提供通用基础性原语，具有更广泛的适用价值。

Abstract: Introducing new capabilities to frontier models has long been the goal of posttraining, which predominantly employs supervised finetuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) to this end. Conventional wisdom dictates that RL enables strong generalization on new tasks without losing existing capabilities, while SFT is prone to weak generalization and catastrophic forgetting. At the same time, SFT can learn from off-policy expert data, whereas RL must rely on a model's ability to find successful trajectories with repeated sampling. In our work, we seek to leverage the strength of on-policy learning while utilizing the privileged information contained in off-policy data. However, rather than modifying the learning objective to accommodate this data, we instead tailor the data distribution to better suit the learner. We introduce a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling algorithm that progressively transforms off-policy traces to be more on-policy given a reference model for finetuning. Across tasks like scientific skill acquisition, mathematical reasoning, and open-ended expertise, our sampling algorithm enables SFT to rival prevailing posttraining techniques, often generalizing better and forgetting less than strong on-policy baselines. In addition, the resulting finetuned models exhibit strong distributional performance and are capable of learning beyond sharpening the base model distribution. At a higher level, our approach presents sampling as a model-native operator that shapes data for learnability, offering broader utility as a general-purpose primitive throughout the posttraining stack.

</details>


### [386] [SAGE: Similarity-Based Cleaning of Poisoned Training Data from Verified Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01788)
*Chaeeun Han,Soodeh Atefi,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Aron Laszka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE方法，仅依赖少量经专家验证的干净与中毒样本（混合小验证集），通过无参相似度加权预测检测数据投毒，在七种干净标签攻击上显著优于现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习依赖不可信的公开数据，数据投毒（尤其干净标签攻击）威胁日益严重。现有防御要么假设没有真值信息，要么假设拥有大量已验证干净样本；后者成本高昂且对干净标签攻击尤为不现实（中毒样本与干净数据视觉上无法区分）。因此需要一种只依赖少量已验证样本的防御方案。

Method: 论文提出SAGE（Similarity-based Approach for Ground-truth-driven Exclusion）。其思路是：验证集不仅包含干净样本，也包含少量由专家检查确认的中毒样本，规模小到直接训练分类器会过拟合。SAGE在另一个独立数据集上训练一个通用特征提取器，然后基于该验证集，用非参数的、相似度加权的预测来标记训练集中的中毒样本。

Result: 在标准基准上与七种干净标签攻击方法对比，实验表明即使只获得少量已验证的中毒样本，也能带来显著的防御优势；同时发现已验证干净样本在各类别间的分布比验证样本数量本身更为重要。

Conclusion: 依赖少量同时含干净和中毒样本的验证集是可行且有效的防御思路；SAGE通过通用特征提取器加相似度加权非参数预测克服小样本过拟合问题，为真值驱动（ground-truth-driven）的投毒检测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: As machine learning increasingly relies on public, untrusted data sources, data poisoning attacks, which inject malicious examples into training data to induce misclassification of a chosen target, pose a growing threat. Existing defenses either assume zero ground-truth information about which examples are poisoned, or they assume access to a large set of examples verified to be clean. Satisfying the latter assumption incurs significant cost since reliable verification can be very resource- or labor-intensive. This cost is particularly high for clean-label attacks, where poisoned examples are visually indistinguishable from clean data. Since requiring a large set of verified examples is impractical, we propose relying on a small set of verified examples including both clean and poisoned ones, i.e., each example verified either to be clean or poisoned through inspection by a forensic expert. The challenge is then to detect poisons based on a set of verified examples that is so small that most classification models would overfit. To address this challenge, we propose Similarity-based Approach for Ground-truth-driven Exclusion (SAGE), which trains a generic feature extractor on a separate dataset and then flags poisoned training examples using a non-parametric, similarity-weighted prediction based on the verified set. On standard benchmarks against seven clean-label attack methods, we demonstrate that having access to even a handful of verified poisoned examples provides a substantial advantage. We also find that the distribution of verified clean examples across classes matters more than the number of verified examples.

</details>


### [387] [SoftServe: A Scalable Quasi-Newton Method for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02182)
*Joohwan Ko,Tetiana Parshakova,Diana Cai,Robert M. Gower*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftServe，一族无需线搜索或启发式曲率修正的拟牛顿方法，可从变分目标导出正定曲率估计，并借助稳定的耦合Newton-Schulz迭代实现大规模深度学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟牛顿方法在凸优化中高效，但用于深度学习受两大障碍限制：非凸性和巨大参数量。现有方法常需线搜索或启发式曲率修正，难以扩展且易失去正定性。

Method: SoftServe基于Berglund等（2025）的变分目标导出正定曲率估计，即使存在负曲率也可保持正定；发展了保持正定性且可扩展至超大规模网络的对角与Kronecker分解变体；采用稳定的耦合Newton-Schulz迭代完成矩阵运算，用GPU友好的矩阵乘法替代昂贵的矩阵分解。

Result: 在严重病态问题上表现优异，涵盖循环网络、深度自编码器、物理信息神经网络及1.36亿参数的物理信息扩散模型，通常取得比Adam、Muon和SOAP等主流基线更低的损失。

Conclusion: SoftServe在无需线搜索和启发式修正的前提下，兼顾正定性、可扩展性与GPU效率，为大规模非凸深度学习优化提供了有效的拟牛顿方案。

Abstract: Quasi-Newton (QN) methods have long been among the most effective methods for large-scale unconstrained convex optimization. Two obstacles have limited their use in deep learning: non-convexity and enormous parameter sizes. We introduce SoftServe, a family of QN methods designed to overcome these obstacles without line searches or ad hoc curvature corrections. SoftServe derives positivedefinite curvature estimates from the variational objective of Berglund et al. (2025), even in the presence of negative curvature. We develop diagonal and Kroneckerfactored variants that preserve positive definiteness by construction and scale to massive neural networks. Finally, SoftServe relies on the stable coupled Newton-Schulz iteration for the required matrix operations, replacing costly matrix decompositions with GPU-friendly matrix multiplications. SoftServe excels on problems that are severely ill-conditioned, including tasks such as recurrent networks, deep autoencoders, physics-informed neural networks, and a 136M-parameter physics-informed diffusion model, often achieving lower losses than established baselines including Adam, Muon, and SOAP.

</details>


### [388] [Higher-Order Molecular Grammars for Generative and Foundation Models in Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02186)
*Yiming Huang,Yujie Zeng,Vijay Prakash Dwivedi,Simone Foti,Jianmin Wang,Jure Leskovec,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高阶文法表示（HGR），将分子提升为组合复形并用上下文无关高阶文法解析成紧凑的产生式规则序列，从而在保持高阶拓扑表达能力的同时兼容标准序列模型；同时构建环富集基准RingDiv并引入RDI指标。实验显示HGR在生成任务中实现100%有效性与最优FCD，在表示学习中取得最高平均AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子学习模型高度依赖其底层表示，但标准的序列和图表示难以显式编码环系统、重复模体等高阶拓扑；已有高阶表示虽能直接捕获这些结构，却计算开销大且难以解码为有效分子。此外，现有基准偏向简单环系统，存在评估偏差。

Method: 提出高阶文法表示（HGR）：一种拓扑感知的原则性框架，将分子提升为组合复形，并在上下文无关高阶文法下把每个复形解析为紧凑的产生式规则序列。通过将高阶拓扑序列化为规则序列，使其与标准序列模型直接兼容，避免显式高阶编码的计算开销。同时构建RingDiv基准（118万分子，含RingDiv300k子集）并提出环多样性指数（RDI）。

Result: 在分子生成中，基于HGR的模型天然具备100%有效性，并具有领先的分布对齐能力，在全部五个生成基准的FCD上排名第一。在表示学习中，HGR-FM在两种迁移协议下于七个MoleculeNet基准上取得最高平均AUC，相比最强基线在探测和全量微调下分别提升8.3和3.3个AUC点。

Conclusion: HGR是一种高效的高阶表示方法，适用于分子生成与可迁移的表示学习，兼顾拓扑表达力与计算效率。

Abstract: Molecular learning models are strongly shaped by their underlying representations. Yet standard sequential and graph formalisms struggle to explicitly encode higher-order topology, such as ring systems and recurring motifs. Existing higher-order representations can capture these structures directly, but they are often computationally demanding and difficult to decode into valid molecules. Here, we introduce Higher-order Grammar Representation (HGR), a principled, topology-aware framework that lifts molecules to combinatorial complexes and parses each complex into a compact sequence of production rules under a context-free higher-order grammar. By serialising higher-order topology into rule sequences, HGR makes these structures directly compatible with standard sequence models, avoiding the computational overhead of explicit higher-order encodings while preserving topological expressiveness. To reduce benchmark bias towards simple ring systems, we construct RingDiv, a ring-enriched benchmark containing 1.18 million molecules, including the curated RingDiv300k subset, and introduce the ring diversity index (RDI) to quantify ring-system coverage. In molecular generation, HGR-based models uniquely combine 100% validity by construction with leading distributional alignment, ranking first in FCD on all five generation benchmarks. In representation learning, HGR-FM achieves the highest mean AUC across seven MoleculeNet benchmarks under both transfer protocols, improving on the strongest baseline by 8.3 and 3.3 AUC points under probing and full fine-tuning, respectively. Collectively, these results establish HGR as an efficient higher-order representation for molecular generation and transferable representation learning.

</details>


### [389] [SkillEvoLean: Mutation-enhanced skill evolution for Lean provers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01799)
*Kuo Zhou,ZiXion Yang,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种突变增强的技能自进化框架，用于构建技能增强的Lean证明器，在无需更新参数的情况下提升大模型在形式化定理证明中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能进化方法主要面向自然语言推理，当所有采样轨迹都失败时缺乏成功轨迹来推断有效的更新方向；同时这些方法多聚焦于根指令文件，忽视了对数学概念与证明技巧等参考知识的进化。

Method: 框架联合进化高层求解策略及其参考知识，通过渐进式进化与基于突变的更新实现。渐进式进化从成功与失败轨迹中获取局部改进；当无法生成完整证明时触发突变，采样数学概念以生成并选择新的技能候选，并在验证器反馈下筛选。

Result: 在MiniF2F、PutnamBench、IMO 2025与USAMO 2026上，相同骨干模型、采样预算与测试时将下，以GPT-5.5取得证明成功率100.0%、90.6%、4/6、4/6，优于基线。概念引导突变在MiniF2F与PutnamBench上分别比随机文本引导突变高6.9与8.2个百分点，并在IMO 2025与USAMO 2026上各多解一题。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合进化求解策略与概念化参考知识、并在失败时引入概念引导突变，能有效提升Lean形式化定理证明的技能进化效果与证明成功率。

Abstract: Skill evolution offers a promising way to improve large language model agents without updating their parameters, but its use in formal theorem proving remains underexplored. Existing methods mainly target natural-language reasoning, improving skills by analyzing successful and failed trajectories and incrementally revising solving strategies. Although the Lean verifier provides reliable execution feedback, when all sampled trajectories fail, existing skill evolution methods lack successful trajectories from which to infer effective update directions. Furthermore, these methods also focus mainly on the root instruction file, thus underexploring the evolution of reference knowledge including mathematical concepts and proving techniques. To address these limitations, we propose a mutation-enhanced skill self-evolution framework for building skill-augmented Lean provers. The framework jointly evolves a high-level solving policy and its reference knowledge through progressive and mutation-based updates. Progressive evolution derives local improvements from successful and failed trajectories, while mutation is triggered when no complete proof can be generated, sampling mathematical concepts to produce and select new skill candidates under verifier feedback. We evaluate our method on MiniF2F, PutnamBench, the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO 2025), and the 2026 USA Mathematical Olympiad (USAMO 2026). Under the same backbone model, trajectorysampling budget, and test-time compute, our method achieves proof success rates of 100.0%, 90.6%, 4/6, and 4/6, respectively, with GPT-5.5, outperforming the baseline methods. Further analysis shows that concept-guided mutation outperforms random-text-guided mutation by 6.9 and 8.2 percentage points on MiniF2F and PutnamBench, respectively, while solving one additional problem on both IMO 2025 and USAMO 2026.

</details>


### [390] [FERPO: Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02198)
*Sebastian Sanokowski,Alireza Sarmadi,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FERPO，一种不使用critic对动作求导的最大熵在线强化学习算法，通过前向KL目标拟合由熵和KL正则化导出的最优目标动作分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线连续控制强化学习常通过critic的动作梯度改进策略，但critic通常只被训练预测回报，准确的价值预测未必带来准确的动作导数，可能导致不可靠的策略更新。

Method: FERPO从熵和KL散度正则化的策略改进目标中导出最优目标动作分布；在rollout策略采样的动作上，用自归一化重要性采样（SNIS）估计前向KL目标并拟合actor；KL正则项限制目标分布与rollout策略的偏离，使重要性权重保持良好性质。

Result: 在MuJoCo Playground和ManiSkill上的实验与消融显示具有竞争力的性能和样本效率提升；计算基准表明actor更新比REPPO更快。

Conclusion: 前向KL目标鼓励覆盖多个高价值模态从而促进探索，FERPO在避免critic动作求导的同时实现高效、可靠的策略改进。

Abstract: Several state-of-the-art methods for online reinforcement learning in continuous control improve policies using action gradients of a learned critic. However, critics are typically trained to predict returns, and accurate value predictions do not necessarily yield accurate action derivatives, potentially leading to unreliable policy updates. We propose Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization (FERPO), an on-policy maximum entropy reinforcement learning algorithm that performs policy improvement using critic values without differentiating the critic with respect to actions. FERPO derives an optimal target action distribution from a policy-improvement objective regularized by entropy and Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. We then fit the actor to this target by minimizing a forward-KL objective, estimated using self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS) with actions drawn from the rollout policy. By limiting the target distribution's deviation from the rollout policy, the KL regularization helps keep these importance weights well behaved. In contrast to reverse-KL objectives, which can favor a subset of the target distribution's modes, the forward-KL objective encourages coverage of multiple high-value modes and thereby promotes exploration. Experiments and ablations on MuJoCo Playground and ManiSkill show competitive performance and sample-efficiency gains. Computational benchmarks also demonstrate faster actor updates than Relative Entropy Pathwise Policy Optimization (REPPO).

</details>


### [391] [A Safe Prototype Is Not a Safety Direction: Reference Dependence and Prompt Confounds in Response-Safety Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01801)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了"以已知安全响应的均值嵌入的余弦相似度来评分响应安全性"这一做法，发现原始的"正质心"规则无法确定区分安全与不安全响应的方向，需要明确的安全减不安全参考方向才能有效识别安全响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期某"睡眠代理检测器"提出用与已知安全响应均值嵌入的余弦相似度来给响应安全性打分。然而该原始正质心规则并未被充分验证：正向观测只能定位安全类相对于编码器原点的位置，却无法确定哪个方向能真正区分安全与不安全响应。作者希望审计这一规则的有效性。

Method: 在两个提示受控、人工标注的语料库及一个辅助的陪审团标注来源控制上，使用四种冻结编码器和按提示分组的划分进行审计。比较两类评分：安全原型（正质心余弦相似度）与显式的"安全减不安全"参考方向。还进行了验证校准的5%假安全阈值分析、完全无标签的留出参考测试，以及仅提示消融实验。

Result: 在人工标注语料上，安全原型仅达到ROC-AUC 0.457-0.545，有两个单元格显著低于随机，一个高于随机；而显式的安全减不安全参考在同一嵌入上达到0.588-0.738。在陪审团控制上原型反转（0.358-0.405），参考达0.754-0.793。在校准后的5%假安全阈值下，参考在PKU-SafeRLHF（0.153-0.263 对 0.039-0.061）和Aegis（0.189-0.291 对 0.004-0.045）上接受更多安全响应，但在BeaverTails上不可靠。完全无标签的留出参考能恢复部分至大部分参考排序，但仅5%不安全时恢复较差，而80-634条标注不安全响应可恢复大部分排序。仅提示消融显示提示标签组成会抬高未受控评估。

Conclusion: 这是关于原始正质心方法的有限结论，不涉及所有单类方法或安全专用防护。类均值只是一个位置，不一定代表安全方向；只有声明一个具有足够不安全样本量的参考方向，才能识别出正确的方向。

Abstract: Can response safety be scored by cosine similarity to the mean embedding of known-safe responses? A recent sleeper-agent detector proposes exactly this score, yet the raw positive-centroid rule is not identified: positive observations locate the safe class relative to an encoder origin, but do not determine which direction separates safe from unsafe responses. We audit the rule on two prompt-controlled, human-labeled corpora and one auxiliary jury-labeled source control, using four frozen encoders and prompt-grouped splits. On the human-labeled corpora the safe prototype reaches ROC-AUC 0.457-0.545, with two cells significantly below chance and one above, while an explicit safe-minus-unsafe reference reaches 0.588-0.738 on the same embeddings; on the jury control the prototype is inverted (0.358-0.405) and the reference reaches 0.754-0.793. At validation-calibrated 5% false-safe thresholds, the reference accepts more safe responses on PKU-SafeRLHF (0.153-0.263 versus 0.039-0.061 across encoders) and Aegis (0.189-0.291 versus 0.004-0.045), but not reliably on BeaverTails. A fully unlabeled held-out reference recovers part to most of the referenced ranking, much less when only 5% of the pool is unsafe, whereas 80-634 labeled unsafe responses recover most of it. Prompt-only ablations show that prompt-label composition can inflate uncontrolled evaluations. This is a bounded result about a raw positive centroid, not all one-class methods or safety-specialized guards. A class mean is a location, not necessarily a safety direction; a declared reference with enough unsafe mass identifies orientation.

</details>


### [392] [Debias Anything: Fairness with Diversity without Supervision in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01815)
*Théau d'Audiffret,Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用方法，在扩散模型生成过程中同时提升公平性与多样性。通过适配器连接冻结的扩散模型与预训练视觉-语言嵌入空间，无需敏感属性标注即可进行引导。利用文本提示对定义属性方向以控制批次构成实现公平，并引入语义分歧分数衡量多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量图像，却会复制并放大训练数据中的人口统计不平衡。现有去偏方法（如分类器引导或文本额外条件）依赖敏感属性标注，降低了适用性和输出多样性；而单纯的多样性方法又不能保证公平的属性表示。因此需要一种能同时兼顾公平与多样性、且通用性强的方法。

Method: 提出连接冻结扩散模型与预训练视觉-语言嵌入空间的适配器，从而在无需敏感属性标注的情况下实现公平与多样性引导。公平性方面：用文本提示对定义属性方向，将批次构成引导至特定比例。多样性方面：引入一个分数，衡量从该表示中得到的语义估计之间的一致性分歧程度。该公式支持无条件与文本条件扩散模型，且不需要关于敏感属性的先验知识或数据。

Result: 实验证实，该方法在达到可比公平水平的同时，提升了生成图像的质量分数与多样性分数。

Conclusion: 所提方法对任意扩散模型和任意敏感属性均通用，无需敏感属性标注即可联合优化公平性与多样性，并在实验中实现了质量与多样性的提升，为扩散模型后训练去偏提供了更适用、更多样的解决方案。

Abstract: Although diffusion models produce high-quality images, they also reproduce and amplify demographic imbalances in their training data. Debiasing their generation process post-training w.r.t. some sensitive attribute usually relies on classifier guidance or explicit text extra-conditioning, but this reduces methods' applicability and output diversity. Conversely, methods promoting diversity alone do not ensure fair attribute representation. In this paper, we propose a method tackling fairness and diversity jointly that is generally applicable to any diffusion model and any sensitive attribute. To this end, an adapter connects the frozen diffusion model to a pretrained vision-language embedding space, enabling fairness and diversity guidance without sensitive-attribute annotations. For fairness, pairs of text prompts define attribute directions which guide batch composition towards specific proportions. For diversity, we introduce a score measuring disagreement between the semantic estimates derived from this representation. The formulation supports unconditional and text-conditional diffusion models, while requiring no prior knowledge or data of sensitive attribute. Experiments confirm that our method improves quality and diversity scores at comparable fairness levels.

</details>


### [393] [MECHVAR: Variance-Guided Mechanism Discrimination for Autonomous Machine Learning Experiment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01819)
*Yifan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MECHVAR：一种基于后验加权预测方差的探针选择准则，用于有限机制库的机制判别，兼具可解释性与高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准提升往往机制不明，复现改进无法说明其成因；需要在有限实验预算下顺序选择探针，对候选机制进行判别。

Method: 将问题形式化为后验加权候选机制、可执行探针与有限预算的顺序实验选择问题；MECHVAR以最大化预测响应的后验加权方差来选下一个探针，在共享高斯预测模型下该分数与Box-Hill后验加权成对KL准则成比例，支持O(KE)向量化重打分与机制对上的可加审计，并可通过局部展开在响应间隔较小时关联到EIG。

Result: 25块压力审计中，MECHVAR在若干中度误设情形优于confirmation-first，但与EIG的主要比较统计上未定论；留出Digits循环中机制识别归一化AUC为MECHVAR 0.8975、score-greedy 0.7825、EIG 0.9092；K=100、E=200时单线程全库打分为MECHVAR 10.36微秒，六节点求积EIG为57.69毫秒。

Conclusion: 当共享预测尺度是可接受的近似时，MECHVAR为有限库实验选择提供了一种轻量、可审计的采集准则。

Abstract: Benchmark gains are often mechanism-ambiguous: reproducing an improvement does not by itself identify why it occurs. We study finite-library mechanism discrimination, where posterior-weighted candidate mechanisms, executable probes, and a limited experimental budget define a sequential experiment-selection problem. MECHVAR selects the next probe by maximizing the posterior-weighted variance of its predicted responses. Under a shared-Gaussian predictive model, this score is exactly proportional to the classical Box--Hill posterior-weighted pairwise-KL criterion, yet it admits O(KE) vectorized rescoring and a transparent additive audit over mechanism pairs. A local expansion further links the score to expected information gain (EIG) when predicted response separations are small. In a 25-block stress audit, MECHVAR outperforms confirmation-first in several moderate misspecification regimes, while its primary comparisons with EIG remain statistically unresolved. In a held-out Digits loop, normalized mechanism-identification AUC is 0.8975 for MECHVAR, 0.7825 for a score-greedy policy, and 0.9092 for EIG. At K = 100, E = 200, median single-thread full-library scoring is 10.36 microseconds for MECHVAR versus 57.69 ms for six-node quadrature EIG in the recorded environment. MECHVAR therefore provides a lightweight, auditable acquisition rule for finite-library experiment selection when a shared predictive scale is a defensible approximation.

</details>


### [394] [Beyond Linear Concepts: Discovering and Aligning Non-Linear Concept Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01821)
*Tido Specht,Elias Benedict Krey,Nils Neukirch,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将非线性的多维概念发现方法(NLMCD)迁移到LLM的token级激活上，把概念建模为低维流形，并设计概念对齐分数(CBA)来跨层、跨模型比较概念流形，得到六项关于LLM内部几何组织的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械可解释性方法在提取概念时依赖强线性假设，但已有证据表明特征流形是非线性的，因此需要跳出线性概念框架来刻画LLM内部token表示的几何组织。

Method: 将计算机视觉中的非线性多维概念发现(NLMCD)适配到LLM的token级激活，把概念建模为低维流形；提出概念对齐分数(CBA)，一种广义Rand指数，在不需要显式特征匹配的情况下度量流形间的几何接近度，并与PCA、CKA等线性基线对比。

Result: (i)邻近层检验显示CBA比PCA/CKA线性基线更敏感；(ii)逐层对齐矩阵揭示中后期层存在两个块结构，跨模型一致但被线性指标掩盖；(iii)概念组成在网络大部分层由句法主导，后期转向句法与语义混合并越来越面向输出；(iv)英汉多语言概念共享依赖训练而非普遍存在，Qwen最强、Llama较弱、GPT-2没有；(v)模型间对齐与上述结构一致，同族Qwen不同规模对应强，跨模型族则弱；(vi)在Tulu-3训练阶段中，相邻阶段对齐最高，base到SFT变化最大，DPO与RLVR等偏好对齐阶段对早期层影响很小，RLVR基本保留DPO的晚期层概念。

Conclusion: 非线性流形视角结合概念对齐分数能揭示线性方法无法观测的层级与跨模型结构，说明LLM内部概念组织具有非线性和训练依赖性，为机械可解释性提供了超越线性假设的分析工具。

Abstract: Understanding information processing in large language models (LLMs) requires dissecting the geometric organization of their internal token representations. While existing mechanistic interpretability (MI) methods seek to extract concepts, they are constrained by a strong linearity assumption challenged by evidence of non-linear feature manifolds. We move beyond linear concepts by adapting Non-Linear Multi-Dimensional Concept Discovery (NLMCD) from computer vision to token-level LLM activations, modeling concepts as low-dimensional manifolds. To compare concept manifolds across layers and models, we introduce a concept-based alignment (CBA) score, a generalized Rand index that measures geometric proximity without explicit feature matching. Our analysis yields six key findings: (i) a neighboring-layer sanity check shows CBA is more sensitive than PCA- or CKA-based linear baselines; (ii) layer-by-layer alignment matrices reveal two block structures in intermediate and late layers, consistent across models and obscured by linear metrics; (iii) concept composition remains syntax-dominated through most of the network before giving way to increasingly mixed syntactic-semantic concepts in later layers, with increasing output-orientation toward the final layers; (iv) multilingual concept sharing between English and Mandarin is training-dependent rather than universal, strongest in Qwen, weaker in Llama, and absent in GPT-2; (v) inter-model alignment mirrors this structure, with strong correspondence between same-family Qwen models of different scale but weak alignment across model families; and (vi) across Tulu-3 training stages, alignment is highest between adjacent stages, with the largest shift between the base model and SFT, while subsequent preference-alignment stages (DPO, RLVR) leave early layers largely unchanged and RLVR mostly preserves DPO's concepts in late layers.

</details>


### [395] [Scientific Discovery under Validation Congestion via Multi-Fidelity Pairwise Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01827)
*Kevin Tirta Wijaya,Alston Lo,Michael Sun,Wojciech Matusik,Vahid Babaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISMS 提出以专家成对排序而非绝对分数驱动候选筛选，在专家保真度和成本不同时按 Fisher 信息准则逐级升级查询，在筛选与优化任务中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算方法能以空前规模生成候选分子、材料与设计，但实验验证能力有限，形成"验证拥堵"，因此关键问题变成如何挑选少量最有希望的候选进行昂贵实验。现有筛选方法多依赖预测绝对分数的数据驱动回归模型，而训练这类模型本身就需要大量实验数据。作者指出科学发现常具比较性质，成对排序信号比绝对测量更易获得。

Method: 提出 PRISMS 框架，使用来自一个或多个专家的成对排序来识别最有希望的候选，不依赖数据饥渴的回归器。专家可来自计算工具或不同保真度的人类输入，从经验法则到智能体工作流与资深科学家。当专家在保真度与成本上存在差异时，PRISMS 依据 Fisher 信息准则，将成对查询从低保真度排序者逐级升级到高保真度排序者。

Result: 在固定药物发现库的迭代筛选中，PRISMS 达到 50% top-10 发现召回率，比仅用回归的主动学习少约 42% 轮次，比无选择性升级的基于排序方法少约 15% 轮次。在不受预定义库限制、生成新设计的优化任务中，PRISMS 的超体积比贝叶斯优化基线高约 18.8%。

Conclusion: 成对排序可作为科学设计筛选的主要驱动信号，在减少实验轮次与提升优化效果方面均具优势，尤其通过基于 Fisher 信息的保真度逐级升级机制，可在有限高保真专家资源下高效定位优质候选。

Abstract: Modern computational methods can now propose candidate molecules, materials, and other scientific designs at an unprecedented scale, creating a validation congestion where candidates are abundant, but experimental capacity to physically evaluate them remains scarce. Discovering novel scientific designs has therefore become increasingly dependent on curation: selecting a small set of promising designs for slow and costly experiments. Existing curation methods typically rely on data-driven regression models that predict absolute scores, but training these models requires substantial experimental data to begin with. Yet, useful curation signals do not have to take the form of absolute measurements, as scientific design discovery is often comparative in nature. Here, we propose that curation can instead be primarily driven by expert pairwise rankings, which are substantially easier to gather. The expertise can come from computational tools or human input of multiple levels of fidelity, ranging from empirical rules of thumb to agentic workflows and experienced scientists. We introduce PRISMS, a framework that uses pairwise rankings from one or more experts, potentially spanning multiple levels of expertise, to identify the most promising candidates without relying on data-hungry regressors. When experts differ in fidelity and cost, PRISMS escalates pairwise queries from lower- to higher-fidelity rankers based on a Fisher-information criterion. In iterative screening that selects designs from fixed drug discovery libraries, PRISMS achieves 50% top-10 discovery recall in ~42% fewer rounds than regression-only active learning, and in ~15% fewer rounds than the ranking-based method with no selective escalation. In optimization that generates new designs without restriction to a predefined library, PRISMS achieves ~18.8% higher hypervolume than the Bayesian optimization baseline.

</details>


### [396] [Pooling Helps, Learned Weighting Hurts In-Context: Decomposing Group Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01831)
*Michael Fore,James Mason Inder,Mrishika Nair,Praneetha Vaddamanu,Sharlina Keshava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文拆解 Chronos-2 的组注意力机制，发现其收益主要来自 V/O 的均匀池化而非 Q/K 学习权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chronos-2 提出的组注意力被整体用于多变量和上下文学习预测，但尚不清楚该机制中哪部分真正带来收益，以及它是否同样适用于两种范式。

Method: 通过在推理时编辑注意力矩阵 α，将注意力头中的 V/O 路径（对组进行加权汇总）与 Q/K 路径（决定权重）分离；并逐一隔离不同层的影响。

Result: 均匀池化（无 Q/K 加权）在 20 个传感器网络配置中的 18 个上取得正向效果；学习到的 Q/K 加权按组类型分化：对 MV 为正或可忽略，但显著损害 10 个传感器网络 ICL 配置中的 8 个，其中 4 个甚至差于单变量预测。仅在第一个 block 中对 α 做均匀化即可改善所有测试的 ICL 配置。

Conclusion: 组注意力的收益源于跨变量均匀池化而非学习到的权重分配，Q/K 加权在 ICL 场景下反而有害，提示应对该机制进行拆分使用或层选择性调整。

Abstract: Group attention, introduced by the time series forecasting model Chronos-2, attends over the variates of a group at a fixed patch index and serves both multivariate (MV) and in-context learning (ICL) forecasting. Rather than evaluating this cross-variate attention design as a whole, we ask which part of the mechanism earns the benefit and probe its applicability to both MV and ICL regimes. By editing the attention matrix $α$ at inference we separate the two pathways a head comprises: V/O, which projects a weighted summary of the group, and Q/K, which decides the weights. Uniform pooling (V/O without any Q/K weighting) is positive on 18 of our 20 sensor-network configurations, while the learned weighting (Q/K) splits by group type: its contribution is positive or negligible for MV, but materially degrades 8 of the 10 sensor-network ICL configurations, leaving 4 of them worse than univariate inference. By isolating the impact of different layers, we find that uniforming $α$ in the first block alone improves every ICL configuration we test.

</details>


### [397] [Stochastic Rounding in Low-Precision Transformer Inference: A Variable-Precision Emulation Study of a Small GPT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01889)
*Yohan Chatelain,Pablo de Oliveira Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究低精度Transformer推理中随机舍入(SR)与就近舍入(RN)的取舍，发现最优选择取决于网络中的位置：MLP下投影处SR明显优于RN，而语言模型头部处RN更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度推理中舍入规则的选择会显著影响精度，但此前缺乏对"在网络的哪个位置使用哪种舍入规则"的系统性理解。作者希望隔离舍入规则这一单一变量，在固定数值格式的前提下研究各运算位置的舍入取舍。

Method: 1) 将PRISM向量化舍入库扩展到任意虚拟精度，提出可变精度随机舍入(VPSR)算法，并证明舍入决策在硬件浮点下被精确计算；2) 给出线性投影的前向误差概率界，分析SR误差包络为O(√n·u)而RN为O(n·u)（n为归约长度）；3) 对softmax输出的期望交叉熵损失变化做二阶分解，分为有符号漂移、漂移曲率和Fisher加权方差惩罚三项。

Result: 在DistilGPT-2、t=6有效位下：MLP中使用SR使困惑度升至全精度参考的1.15倍，而RN为2.21倍；在语言模型头部顺序反转，因SR引入非均匀方差而确定性RN不引入方差。混合配置（MLP输出t=6，MLP用SR、头部用RN）将困惑度控制在1.10倍以内，相比同位数RN降低28%。

Conclusion: 舍入规则不应全局统一，而应按位置选择：MLP噪声近似为均匀logit偏移，softmax对其不变，故SR的方差被大幅折扣；头部噪声在词表上非均匀，故RN更优。理论与实验一致，支持位置感知的混合舍入策略以提升低精度Transformer推理精度。

Abstract: Should low-precision transformer inference use stochastic rounding (SR) or round-to-nearest (RN)? The answer depends on where in the network you look. We isolate this effect by holding the numerical format fixed and varying only the rounding rule at individual operation sites. To enable experiments at freely chosen precisions, we extend the PRISM vectorized rounding library to arbitrary virtual precision via a variable-precision stochastic rounding (VPSR) algorithm, proving that the rounding decision is evaluated exactly in hardware floating point.
  We develop two analyses providing complementary insight into this site-level trade-off. First, a probabilistic forward-error bound for linear projections shows that SR's error envelope grows as $O(\sqrt{n} u)$ in reduction length $n$, versus $O(n u)$ for RN, a gap that widens rapidly at low precision and is most pronounced in the long multilayer perceptron (MLP) down-projection. Second, a second-order decomposition of expected cross-entropy loss change at the output softmax into signed drift, drift curvature, and a Fisher-weighted variance penalty reveals why the two sites behave oppositely: MLP noise is predominantly a uniform logit shift to which softmax is invariant, so SR's variance is largely discounted; head noise is non-uniform across the vocabulary and is not.
  On DistilGPT-2 at $t=6$ significand bits, observations match theory: SR in the MLP raises perplexity to 1.15x the full-precision reference, versus 2.21x for RN. At the language-model head, the ordering reverses because SR introduces non-uniform variance, whereas deterministic RN carries none. In a mixed-precision configuration (MLP output at $t=6$), assigning SR to the MLP and RN to the head brings perplexity within 1.10x of the full-precision reference, a 28% reduction over matched-bit RN.

</details>


### [398] [A foundation for systematic analysis of transformers and RNNs for tractography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01894)
*Emmanuelle Renauld,Philippe Poulin,Hugo Larochelle,Antoine Théberge,Maxime Descoteaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作系统评估RNN与Transformer用于迭代式dMRI纤维束追踪，通过生成-验证训练阶段在流线级别引入监督，在ISMRM2015挑战数据集上取得迄今最佳性能，并量化了训练集缺陷的影响及体内数据的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI纤维束追踪受限于局部扩散信息与全局解剖合理性之间的内在矛盾，机器学习被视为改进手段，但训练策略、输入表示与超参数选择等关键设计缺乏系统评估。

Method: 系统对比RNN与Transformer用于迭代追踪，重点考察训练策略、输入表示（CNN嵌入与EOS token）及超参数；提出生成-验证阶段，在训练中对流线级别进行监督，以缓解局部损失函数与全局流线质量不匹配的问题；在ISMRM2015数据集上开展受控实验。

Result: 在ISMRM2015追踪挑战数据集上达到目前报告的最高性能；量化缺失纤维束、含噪或不完美训练流线、无效纤维对结果的影响；最佳模型可应用于Tractoinferno数据库的体内数据。

Conclusion: 结果既展示了Transformer、RNN等序列式深度模型用于纤维束追踪的潜力，也揭示其局限，强调需要更好的体模与评估方法来进行体内验证，并为后续序列式监督追踪研究提供了经验与建议。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has emerged as a promising approach for improving diffusion MRI (dMRI) tractography, a task that remains limited by the intrinsic tension between local diffusion information and global anatomical plausibility. In this work, we systematically evaluate recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and Transformer models for iterative tractography, with particular attention to training strategies, input representations (including convolutional neural network (CNN)-based embeddings and end-of-sequence (EOS) tokens), and hyperparameter selection. We introduce a generation-validation phase enabling supervision at the streamline level during training, allowing supervision despite the mismatch between local loss functions and global streamline quality. Using the ISMRM2015 tractography challenge dataset, our models achieve the highest reported performance to date. Through controlled experiments, we quantify the impact of missing bundles, noisy or imperfect training streamlines, and invalid fibers in the training set. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of our best-performing models for in vivo data from the Tractoinferno database. Overall, our results highlight both the potential and the limits of sequence-based deep learning models such as Transformers and RNNs for tractography, and emphasize the need for improved phantoms and evaluation methods for in vivo validation. We provide takeaways and recommendations for future researchers training and validating sequence-based supervised methods for tractography.

</details>


### [399] [Higher-Order Positional Encodings for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01903)
*Caleb Stam,Aagrim Hoysal,Sanjukta Krishnagopal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出高阶位置编码（HOPE）范式，将图提升为单纯复形，用Hodge Laplacian导出的位置编码为普通图学习模型注入高阶拓扑信息，无需改动骨干网络，并从理论上证明其表达力优于标量图谱滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统常存在成对关系无法刻画的高阶交互，而现有图位置编码仅由原始图计算，无法直接捕获观测到的高阶交互；拓扑深度学习虽通过提升到单纯复形解决该问题，但通常需在高阶神经网络表示上做消息传递或注意力，改变了骨干结构。

Method: 提出一种表示学习范式，通过位置编码把高阶拓扑注入图表示，使标准图学习模型可利用提升后的关联结构而不修改骨干。理论上刻画高阶位置编码的表达力，证明高阶提升诱导的节点级算子能以标量图谱滤波器无法实现的方式混合图Laplacian频率；据此用团复形导出的Hodge Laplacian实例化高阶位置编码。

Result: 在ZINC和受控合成基准上，Graph Transformer的预测性能得到提升；固定1-骨架实验表明，当单元独立于图提供时，该流程可传递高阶信息。

Conclusion: 高阶位置编码成为连接图位置编码与拓扑深度学习的原理性桥梁。

Abstract: Many real-world systems exhibit higher-order interactions among groups of entities that cannot be captured by pairwise relationships alone. Graph Transformers and Graph Neural Networks increasingly rely on positional encodings to enrich graph representations, yet existing positional encodings are computed solely from the original graph and therefore cannot directly capture observed higher-order interactions. Topological Deep Learning addresses this limitation by lifting graphs to simplicial complexes, but typically requires performing message passing or attention on higher-order neural network representations. We introduce a representation learning paradigm that enriches graph representations with higher-order topology through positional encodings, enabling standard graph learning models to exploit lifted incidence structure without modifying the backbone. We derive a theoretical characterization of the expressivity of higher-order positional encodings, proving that node-level operators induced by higher-order lifts can mix graph Laplacian frequencies in ways that scalar graph spectral filters cannot. Guided by this theory, we instantiate higher-order positional encodings using Hodge Laplacians derived from clique complexes. Experiments with Graph Transformers on ZINC and controlled synthetic benchmarks demonstrate improvements in predictive performance, while a fixed-1-skeleton experiment shows that the pipeline can transmit higher-order information when cells are supplied independently of the graph. Together, our results establish higher-order positional encodings as a principled bridge between graph positional encodings and topological deep learning.

</details>


### [400] [Same Reward, Different Skills: When Multimodal RL Learns to Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01908)
*Haocun Ye,Xinlong Jiang,Qile Chen,Bingyu Wang,Teng Zhang,Shubai Chen,Tingyu Wu,Zhenkun Zheng,Yiqiang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出RLVR中"提示里的图像不等于学习信号里的图像"，提出"视觉可解性"设计原则，并在反事实坐标场景任务上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR即使训练时不提供视觉信息也能提升视觉语言基准分数，说明基准增益未必来自真正的视觉学习；长时间真实图像训练甚至可能损害grounding能力，需要一个明确的设计原则来确保视觉证据真正被学习利用。

Method: 提出"视觉可解性"设计原则：正确答案必须依赖视觉证据，且任务保持可学习。在反事实坐标场景中构造问题固定、目标不被命名的任务，使答对必须通过图像找到目标。使用标准GRPO与正确性加格式奖励训练7B模型，并设置对照实验：测试时用灰色画布替换图像、训练时使用灰色画布（匹配步数30、四种随机种子）、以及在相同图像上加入能直接回答训练问题的caption。

Result: 7B模型在比训练场景更密集的留出场景上，目标发现准确率从0.425提升至0.875，并泛化到未训练过的问题类型；测试时换成灰色画布，发现率降为零；训练时用灰色画布则几乎无增益；加入可直接回答问题的caption后增益减少近三分之二。学习到的技能还能迁移到独立于训练语料构建的grounding任务上。

Conclusion: 改变奖励所要求的内容会改变RL实际学到的东西；只有满足视觉可解性、让视觉证据成为正确回答的必要条件，才能获得可迁移的真实视觉能力增益，而非单纯的基准分数提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language benchmark scores even without visual information during training. With images at test, blind-trained models recover roughly half of the real-image gain at 3B and nearly four fifths at 7B. Prolonged real-image training can erode grounding while benchmark gains persist. Both findings expose the same gap: an image in the prompt is not an image in the learning signal. Our design rule, visual resolvability, asks that visual evidence be necessary for a correct answer and that the task remain learnable. We test it on counterfactual coordinate scenes in which the question stays fixed and the target is never named, so a correct answer requires finding the target in the image. With standard GRPO and correctness-and-format rewards, a 7B model raises its accuracy at finding the target (discovery) from 0.425 to 0.875 on held-out scenes denser than any it trained on, and it improves on question types it never trained on. Two controls locate the source of the gain. Replacing test images with gray canvases drops discovery to zero; training on gray canvases instead, at matched step 30 and in each of four seeds, yields essentially none of the gain even when the model is then tested with real images. The learned skill carries over to grounding tasks built independently of the training corpus. A caption that answers the training question, added to the same images, reward and budget, cuts the gain by nearly two thirds. Changing what reward requires changes what RL learns.

</details>


### [401] [Learning to Predict Distributions over Weight Updates for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01934)
*Azal Ahmad Khan,Keshav Ramji,Tahira Naseem,Ali Anwar,Ramón Fernandez Astudillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅用输入查询作为适应信号的查询条件超网络，并提出分布型超网络为LoRA生成分布，实现权重采样式的测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超网络已能基于任务描述或样例动态适应LLM参数，但作者想探究：仅依靠LLM的输入查询本身，能获得多少适应信号？同时希望超越参数的点估计，探索参数不确定性的建模价值。

Method: 研究查询条件超网络用于LoRA估计；提出分布型超网络，输出LoRA参数的概率分布；使用可微蒙特卡洛近似构造简单端到端损失；探索回归与凸组合等多种分布参数化方式。

Result: 即使只用学习到的分布均值，也优于确定性超网络；所学生成分布支持测试时权重采样，多个适应模型性能随权重采样数增加而提升，且优于对应的token采样适应基线；生成的权重更新能跨查询迁移。

Conclusion: 查询条件的权重更新分布既能支撑模型适应，也能支撑测试时扩展，表明超网络学到了可复用的适应结构。

Abstract: Hypernetworks have recently shown success in dynamically adapting the parameters of Large Language Models (LLMs) at runtime based on signals such as task descriptions or additional demostrations. Here we ask: how much adaptation signal can be obtained using only the input query to an LLM?. To answer this, we study query-conditioned Hypernetworks for LoRA estimation. Further, we introduce distributional Hypernetworks, able to produce not only point estimates of parameter adaptors, but also a distribution over possible LoRAs. For this we propose a simple end-to-end loss using a differentiable Monte Carlo approximation and explore multiple distribution parametrizations including regression and convex combination variants. Results show that even using the mean of the learned distribution can outperform deterministic hypernetworks. Crucially, the learned distribution enables a different form of test-time scaling: instead of spending additional compute only by sampling more token sequences from a fixed model, we sample weight updates, yielding multiple adapted models for the same query. Performance improves as more weight samples are considered and remains stronger than corresponding token-sampling adaptation baselines. Finally, we find that generated updates can transfer across queries, suggesting that the hypernetwork learns reusable structure in how the model should adapt. Together, these results show that query-conditioned distributions over weight updates can support both adaptation and test-time scaling.

</details>


### [402] [Graph Representation via Elements of Discrete Morse and Cobordism Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01937)
*Jennifer Rozenblit,Chenguang Yang,Yuxin Liu,Yuzhou Chen,Yulia Gel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张将低维拓扑（如Morse理论、配边理论）引入机器学习，提出MG-Diff流程，利用离散Morse理论改进图扩散模型，并给出稳定性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑天然适合处理非线性、多尺度、非平稳结构，但在机器学习中其应用大多局限于拓扑数据分析。低维拓扑工具（如Morse理论）几乎只存在于纯数学领域，为理解数据生成过程及学习任务的隐藏结构提供了互补且几乎未被探索的视角。

Method: 引入配边理论（cobordism theory）的概念，并利用离散Morse理论的工具，构建MG-Diff流程来改进图扩散模型的性能；同时推导理论保证与充分条件，使在正决策间隔下，Morse理论工具及其用于诱导扩散引导的过程在小扰动下保持稳定。

Result: 在时空图预测与图再生任务中验证了离散Morse理论用于图扩散模型的有效性，并证明在正决策间隔下相关工具与扩散引导对小扰动具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 离散Morse理论在图扩散模型上的应用只是一个小的窗口，低维拓扑有望为机器学习领域提供更广泛的贡献。

Abstract: Topology is, by its nature and design, suited to structure that is nonlinear, multiscale, and nonstationary - however, within machine learning, its use remains largely confined to topological data analysis. We advocate that tools from low-dimensional topology which have remained almost exclusively contained within the domain of pure mathematics (such as Morse theory) offer a strong, complementary, and yet virtually unexplored perspective on the hidden structure of data-generating processes and learning tasks built upon them. Here we introduce concepts from cobordism theory and harness tools from discrete Morse theory to improve the performance of graph diffusion models through our pipeline MG-Diff. Further, we derive theoretical guarantees and sufficient conditions so that under a positive decision-gap, the Morse-theoretic tools and their application for induced diffusion guidance are stable under small perturbations. Finally, we illustrate the utility of discrete Morse theory in application to graph diffusion models for spatio-temporal graph forecasting and graph regeneration, and argue that these applications are only a small window into the part of what low-dimensional topology can offer to the field of machine learning.

</details>


### [403] [Latent JEPA: Abstract Future Prediction for Latent Reasoning in Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01947)
*Xinjian Zhao,Yaoyao Xu,Xuemin Chen,Xiaozhuang Song,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Latent JEPA 框架，将自回归学习与联合嵌入预测结合，用连续潜在思维预测未来解题信息与分子结果，在化学推理任务上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学直觉常在完整推导前给出对结果的初步预期，这种预期能够补充显式分析。作者希望借鉴这一特点，让连续潜在思维在不逐步口头化中间步骤的情况下，预先捕捉未来解题过程中有信息量的方面。

Method: 提出 Latent JEPA 框架，将自回归学习与对一个或多个未来视图的联合嵌入预测相结合。针对化学推理，设计文本与分子两类预测目标，将潜在思维分别与后续推理过程和分子结果相连接。

Result: 在 ChemCoTBench 上，分子优化任务取得提升，并在若干编辑与反应指标上有所改善。表征分析显示，未来预测使潜在思维对分子结果更具信息量，并增强了其与化学结构之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 抽象的未来预测可作为一种学习原则，将连续潜在推理与科学结果有效连接。

Abstract: Large language models offer a promising foundation for chemical reasoning, bringing together chemical knowledge and multistep problem solving. Chemical intuition can provide an initial sense of plausible outcomes before the details of a solution are fully worked out. Inspired by how such expectations complement explicit analysis, we study how continuous latent thoughts can be trained to anticipate informative aspects of future solutions without verbalizing every intermediate step. We introduce Latent JEPA, a framework that combines autoregressive learning with joint-embedding prediction of one or more future views. For chemical reasoning, we develop textual and molecular prediction objectives that connect latent thoughts to both subsequent reasoning and molecular outcomes. Experiments on ChemCoTBench show gains in molecular optimization and on several editing and reaction metrics. Representation analyses show that future prediction makes latent thoughts more informative about molecular outcomes and strengthens their correspondence with chemical structure. These findings support abstract future prediction as a learning principle for connecting continuous latent reasoning with scientific outcomes.

</details>


### [404] [Sharp Non-Asymptotic Analysis of the Penalized Challenger in $β$-EB-TCI for Bernoulli Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01951)
*Nam Nguyen,Tuan Quang Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Bernoulli bandits中β-EB-TCI（top-two best-arm identification算法）的非渐近尖锐行为，证明当经验最优臂已为真实最优且其采样比例接近β后，停止时间为T_β^★(μ)log(1/δ)加上低阶项，并给出期望样本复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-two算法在固定置信度最佳臂识别中简单有效，但其尖锐的非渐近行为尚未被充分理解；作者希望刻画该算法的非渐近高概率界与期望样本复杂度。

Method: 针对Bernoulli bandits研究Jourdan等人的经验最优top-two规则β-EB-TCI，其挑战者通过带对数计数惩罚的Bernoulli运输代价选出；分析在经验领导者成为真实最优臂且采样比例保持接近β的regime下的停止时间与挑战者采样行为，并讨论有限均值充分探索条件及加入轻度强制探索规则。

Result: 证明停止时间为T_β^★(μ)log(1/δ)（含低阶集中项），该regime下每个挑战者被线性采样；对无强制探索的原算法，主要难点在于控制经验领导者何时永久正确；由此得到所有唯一最优臂Bernoulli实例的非渐近高概率界；在有限均值充分探索条件下进一步得到尖锐期望样本复杂度；对无防护Bernoulli规则在所有臂均值两两不同时给出尖锐期望结果；加入贡献O(√(Kt))次拉动的轻度强制探索后，得到任意臂数下自包含的期望样本复杂度定理；并指出通过单一索引比较论证处理相等次优均值的证明策略的局限性。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了β-EB-TCI的非渐近尖锐性能，给出了高概率与期望样本复杂度保证，并揭示了处理相等次优均值时证明策略的局限。

Abstract: Top-two algorithms are simple and effective for fixed-confidence best-arm identification, but their sharp non-asymptotic behavior is still not well understood. We study this problem for Bernoulli bandits through $β$-EB-TCI, the empirical-best top-two rule of Jourdan et al., whose challenger is chosen using a Bernoulli transportation cost with a logarithmic count penalty. We prove that, after the empirical leader has become the true best arm and its sampling fraction stays close to $β$, the stopping time is $T_β^{\star}(μ)\log(1/δ)$ up to lower-order concentration terms. We also show that, in this regime, every challenger is sampled linearly often. Thus, for the original algorithm without forced exploration, the main remaining difficulty is to control when the empirical leader becomes permanently correct. These results imply a non-asymptotic high-probability bound for all Bernoulli instances with a unique best arm. If the algorithm satisfies a finite-mean sufficient-exploration condition, the bound further yields the sharp expected sample complexity. In particular, this gives the sharp expectation result for the unguarded Bernoulli rule when all arm means are pairwise distinct, using the sufficient-exploration result of Jourdan et al. Finally, if we add a mild forced-exploration rule that contributes only $O(\sqrt{Kt})$ pulls up to time $t$, we obtain a self-contained expected sample-complexity theorem for any number of arms under the unique-best-arm assumption. We also identify a limitation of proof strategies that try to handle equal suboptimal means through a single index-comparison argument.

</details>


### [405] [Do Your Own Research: Learning to Forecast by Learning to Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01955)
*Yusuf Afifi,Artur Kiulian,Anton Polishko,Mykola Khandoga,Hamudi Naanaa,Alina Krasnobrizha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个基于2100+已结算Polymarket问题的智能体预测环境、数据集与测试框架，让模型在训练时自主搜集证据，并用单epoch GRPO配合Brier分数奖励训练Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，显著提升校准度、减少搜索次数，并在同等评测下以约5%推理成本超越包括Claude Opus 4.5在内的四个前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果导向的强化学习可训练语言模型预测真实世界事件，但既有预测工作要么在训练前冻结研究上下文，要么仅在测试阶段才启用智能体式研究，导致"搜集证据"这一能力从未被奖励信号塑造。作者希望让证据搜集技能本身成为可被优化的对象。

Method: 1) 基于2100+已结算的Polymarket问题，构建智能体预测环境、数据集与测试框架；2) 智能体在rollout时自行获取上下文，包括网页搜索、页面阅读和金融时间序列，并通过分层泄漏过滤限制为问题截止时间前发布的信息；3) 用单epoch GRPO、以Brier分数为奖励，训练Qwen3.5-35B-A3B（3B激活参数）；4) 在统一框架下与四个前沿模型对比评测。

Result: 训练改变了智能体与信息交互的方式：校准度提升30-40%，每次rollout的搜索尝试次数从3.8降至2.25，说明其学会了证据纪律。在完全相同的测试框架下，训练后的策略在基于证据的预测上领先所有被测前沿模型，包括Claude Opus 4.5（soft-Brier 0.254 vs 0.256，n=265），且推理成本约为其5%；在人群本身都未形成定论的最难问题上，优势最为明显。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将证据搜集纳入奖励塑造可以同时提升预测校准度、信息获取效率与最终预测表现，并使小规模模型以极低成本超越前沿大模型。作者开源了环境、数据集与逐rollout记录，作为可复用的时间预测智能体测试框架。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning can train language models to forecast real-world events, but prior forecasting work either freezes research context before training or deploys agentic research only at test time, so the skill of gathering evidence is never shaped by the reward. We introduce an agentic forecasting environment, dataset, and harness built from 2,100+ resolved Polymarket questions; the agent acquires its own context at rollout time (web search, page reading, and financial time series, all restricted by layered leak filtering to information published before each question's cutoff), and we train Qwen3.5-35B-A3B (3B active parameters) on it with single-epoch GRPO under a Brier-score reward. Training changes how the agent interacts with information: calibration improves 30-40%, and search attempts fall from 3.8 to 2.25 per rollout as evidence discipline is learned. Evaluated in an identical harness against four frontier models, the trained policy also finishes ahead of every frontier model tested at evidence-based forecasting, including Claude Opus 4.5 (soft-Brier 0.254 vs. 0.256, n=265), at about 5% of the inference cost, and its margin is widest on the hardest questions, the ones the crowd itself had not decided. We release the environment, dataset, and per-rollout records as a reusable harness for temporal forecasting agents.

</details>


### [406] [SIEVE: Selective attention-value Suppression for Vision-Language Models Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01962)
*Si Qi Goh,Cap Dang Xuan Kiet,Tat-Jen Cham,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIEVE是一个针对视觉-语言模型的选择性遗忘框架，通过正则化注意力值表示并结合输出控制，实现对个人身份信息的选择性遗忘，同时保留允许的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型能够将视觉身份与个人传记信息关联，由此产生了选择性遗忘个人身份信息（PII）的需求，同时需要保留关于同一人的被允许知识。该任务的挑战在于：敏感信息与需保留信息可能共享相同的视觉输入和中间表示。

Method: SIEVE直接正则化注意力值表示并控制模型输出：对遗忘样本的注意力值抑制至恒定零，同时通过将需保留样本的表示与冻结参考模型匹配来保持其表示。这些目标与序列级的遗忘和保留监督相结合，实现针对性遗忘。

Result: 大量实验表明SIEVE在多种模型-模态设置下取得了最先进的遗忘性能，同时保持有竞争力的保留效用。消融研究显示，值抑制和负交叉熵提供互补的遗忘信号，而基于参考的值匹配显著减少效用退化。

Conclusion: 注意力值为敏感知识与保留知识密切相关时的选择性多模态遗忘提供了有效的干预点。

Abstract: The ability of vision-language models (VLMs) to associate visual identities with biographical information creates a need for selective unlearning of personally identifiable information (PII) while preserving permitted knowledge about the same individual. This setting is challenging because both sensitive and retained information can share the same visual inputs and intermediate representations. We introduce SIEVE, a simple and effective framework for selective VLM unlearning. SIEVE directly regularizes attention-value representations while also controlling model outputs. SIEVE suppresses attention values for forget examples toward a constant zero, while preserving retain-example representations by matching them to a frozen reference model. These objectives are combined with sequence-level forget and retain supervision, enabling targeted forgetting without largely affecting retained knowledge. Extensive experiments show that SIEVE achieves state-of-the-art performance on unlearning with multiple model-modality settings, while maintaining competitive retained utility. Ablation studies further show that value suppression and negative cross-entropy contribute complementary forgetting signals, while reference-based value matching substantially reduces utility degradation. These results demonstrate that attention values provide an effective intervention point for selective multimodal unlearning when sensitive and retained knowledge are closely related.

</details>


### [407] [FastCI: Efficient GPU-Intensive CI for LLM Training Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01967)
*Tianshuo Qiao,Naiqian Zheng,Xiaopeng Liu,Shuguang Wang,Diandian Gu,Xuanzhe Liu,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastCI是一个面向LLM训练框架的CI效率优化框架，通过运行时证据筛选、优先级排序和工作负载优化，将CI延迟降低77.5%、GPU资源使用降低63.9%，并提升3.2%的修改代码覆盖率保留率，已在字节跳动LLM训练框架CI流水线中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模和复杂度持续增长，其训练框架迭代迅速，持续集成（CI）对维护框架质量与稳定性至关重要。但与传统软件不同，LLM训练框架的CI依赖GPU密集型测试（通常涉及完整模型训练或评估），导致CI本身成为快速开发的瓶颈。

Method: FastCI利用运行时证据选择受影响的测试，并剪除在等价上下文中执行变更代码的测试；随后对高风险测试进行优先级排序，以更早暴露潜在失败；并沿测试预期验证范围之外的维度优化测试工作负载。

Result: 在LLM训练框架的CI工作负载上评估，相比当前部署的CI流水线，FastCI将CI延迟降低77.5%、GPU资源使用降低63.9%，同时将修改代码覆盖率保留率提升3.2%。

Conclusion: FastCI通过运行时证据驱动的测试选择、优先级排序与工作负载优化，显著提升了LLM训练框架CI的效率与资源利用率，并已在字节跳动LLM训练框架的CI流水线中实际集成部署。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) keep growing in size and complexity, their training frameworks evolve at a rapid pace as well. Therefore, continuous integration (CI) is critical for maintaining the quality and stability of these frameworks. However, unlike traditional software, CI for LLM training frameworks relies on GPU-intensive tests, which usually involve complete model training or evaluation. This leads CI itself to become a new bottleneck for fast-paced development. In this paper, we introduce FastCI, a framework that improves the efficiency of CI for LLM training frameworks. FastCI leverages runtime evidence to select affected tests and prune tests that execute changed code in equivalent contexts. Then FastCI prioritizes high-risk tests to expose potential failures earlier, and optimizes test workloads along dimensions outside the intended validation scope of each test. Evaluated on the CI workload of our LLM training framework, FastCI reduces the CI latency by 77.5% and the GPU resource usage by 63.9%, while improving the modified code coverage retention by 3.2%, compared with the currently deployed CI pipelines. FastCI has now been integrated into the CI pipelines of our LLM training framework at ByteDance.

</details>


### [408] [Sim+Real: Joint Simulation - Experiment Training Improves Balanced Prediction in Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01974)
*Mahindra Rautela,Alexander Scheinker,Ayan Biswas,Diane Oyen,Nathan DeBardeleben,Earl Lawrence*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将仿真-实验预测建模为多目标学习问题，在四个流体系统上比较四种训练策略，发现联合训练在平衡性能上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真与实验测量为学习时空物理系统提供互补数据，但标准的仿真到实验微调（Sim→Exp）在迁移后仅优化实验目标，可能损害仿真性能，存在仿真域遗忘的问题。

Method: 将仿真-实验预测形式化为具有域特定仿真风险和实验风险的多目标学习问题。基于RealPDEBench的四个流体系统和两种模型容量，对比Simulation only、Experiment only、Sim→Exp和Joint training四种方案，并在两个域的留出集上评估每个最终模型。

Result: Sim→Exp更强烈地偏向实验数据，以仿真域遗忘为代价；Joint training在广泛的仿真-实验评估权重范围内始终取得最佳的平衡性能，同时相较Sim→Exp显著提升仿真保持能力；Joint还更好地保留了实验测量中缺失的纯仿真场。

Conclusion: 将仿真-实验预测作为多目标学习问题处理，联合训练能有效缓解仿真域遗忘，在仿真与实验域之间实现更优的平衡与保持。

Abstract: Simulation and experimental measurements provide complementary data for learning spatiotemporal physical systems, but standard simulation-to-experiment fine-tuning optimizes only the experimental objective after transfer and can degrade simulation performance. We formulate simulation--experiment prediction as a multi-objective learning problem with domain-specific simulation and experimental risks. On four fluid systems from RealPDEBench and two model capacities, we compare Simulation only, Experiment only, Sim$\rightarrow$Exp, and Joint training, evaluating every final model on both held-out domains. Sim$\rightarrow$Exp tends to specialize more strongly to experimental data at the cost of simulation-domain forgetting. Joint training consistently achieves the best balanced performance over a broad range of simulation--experiment evaluation weightings, while substantially improving simulation retention over Sim$\rightarrow$Exp. Joint also better preserves simulation-only fields absent from experimental measurements. Project page: https://mahindrautela.github.io/morph.

</details>


### [409] [The Curvature of Regret in Contextual Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01980)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Alice E. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性优化中决策聚焦学习因优化器不连续而导致的非光滑问题，证明数据分布平均后局部曲率呈二次型，给出闭式表达与可行近似，并应用于决策感知的场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性优化中的决策聚焦学习面临优化器不连续性的困难：成本的小误差可能不改变决策，也可能使决策跳变到另一个顶点。这种非光滑的点态行为给基于梯度的学习带来挑战，因此需要理解其在分布层面上的平均行为。

Method: 作者从理论上分析点态非光滑行为在数据分布平均后的性质，推导出曲率的闭式形式——一个支撑于规范扇（normal fan）壁面上的矩阵值测度，该测度仅依赖可行集，数据分布仅作为权重进入。随后提出一种仅需一次到可行集投影即可计算的可处理曲率近似，并证明该近似弱收敛于真实的总体曲率。

Result: 理论结果包括：平均后非光滑行为局部呈二次型；曲率以矩阵值测度闭式给出；近似方法可仅用一次投影计算且弱收敛于真实总体曲率。实验验证了二次性与弱收敛规律，并在电池套利任务上相对均匀分配取得30.8%的遗憾改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明决策聚焦线性优化中的非光滑性在分布平均下表现为二次曲率，其结构仅由可行集决定并可通过单次投影高效近似；该发现可用于决策感知的场景生成，并在实际任务中显著降低遗憾。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning for linear optimization is complicated by the discontinuity of the optimizer, where small cost errors may leave the decision unchanged or move it to a different vertex. We show that this non-smooth pointwise behavior becomes locally quadratic after averaging over the data distribution, and we derive the curvature in closed form, specifically, a matrix-valued measure supported on the walls of the normal fan. This measure depends only on the feasible set, with the data distribution entering only as a weight. We then offer a tractable approximation for this curvature, computable with just one projection to the feasible set. We prove that the approximation weakly converges to the true population curvature. We offer one application of our findings, a decision-aware scenario generation method for expected-cost linear optimization. Our experiments test the quadratic and weak convergence laws and show a 30.8% regret improvement over uniform allocation on battery arbitrage.

</details>


### [410] [Universal interpolation for deep residual self-attention networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01981)
*Sibylle Marcotte,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究深度自注意力模型的"对偶"机制：仅靠深度、层间强参数共享（固定两组冻结的softmax注意力块），通过变换应用顺序、符号与时长，实现对任意序列集合的插值，并给出因果掩码下的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用逼近性质是学习架构能从规模化规律中获益的必要定性条件。以往研究多在无限宽度极限下验证该性质，本文转而关注"对偶"机制：由深度而非宽度提供逼近能力，并强调跨层的强参数共享，这一动机来自Looped Transformers等近期模型。

Method: 考察残差softmax注意力结构，仅使用两个冻结的单头注意力块，其投影矩阵按高斯初始化。参数在输入输出集合上完全固定，仅允许改变块的施加顺序、符号及持续时长（duration）来完成插值任务；同时分析因果掩码施加的限制。

Result: 主要结果证明：仅用两个冻结的单头块即可实现通用插值，能将任意N个含n个token的序列集合映射到任意另一个同类集合，且该结论在连续深度与有限深度两种情形下均成立；并刻画了因果掩码带来的限制，给出了相应的通用插值保证。

Conclusion: 深度本身（配合强参数共享与有限冻结参数）即可提供通用逼近/插值能力，为Looped Transformers类深度主导模型的理论可行性提供支持，并明确了因果掩码下的适用边界。

Abstract: Universal approximation is a necessary qualitative property of learning architectures to benefit from scaling laws. While it is generically verified on a variety of neural architectures and random feature models, it typically involves infinite width limits. In this work, we focus on deep self-attention models and consider instead the `dual' regime, where approximation power is enabled entirely by depth, and featuring strong parameter sharing across layers, motivated by recent models such as the Looped Transformers. More specifically, we ask whether one can find a predefined finite set of parameters, each defining an attention block, such that the resulting finite set of transformations can map any collection of $N$ sequences of $n$ tokens to any other collection of $N$ sequences of $n$ tokens. Crucially, these transformations are \emph{fixed independently of the input and output} collections: only the order in which the blocks are applied, their signs, and their durations depend on the particular interpolation task. Our main result establishes it for residual softmax attention using only two frozen single-head blocks with Gaussian-initialized projection matrices. The result holds at both continuous and finite depth. We also characterize the restrictions imposed by causal masking and establish corresponding universal interpolation guarantees.

</details>


### [411] [Bellman Meets Lyapunov: Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning via Mastering Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02012)
*Tristan Shah,Wooyoung Chung,Volodomyr Makarenko,Juan Wachs,Stas Tiomkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F-CIP，一种由系统动力学定义、无需选择信息变量的内在动机目标，实现了无监督强化学习中对平衡、可控性及协调步态等行为的自动发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖人类工程师为每个新任务设计奖励信号，而无监督RL通过内在动机减少这种工程负担，但现有IM目标仍需选择信息变量，重新引入了本应消除的领域专业知识。

Method: 提出Forward CIP（F-CIP），将Controllable Information Production（CIP）目标以RL原生方式形式化，仅由系统动力学定义，无需选择信息变量，并证明其与RL兼容，可与现有算法结合使用。

Result: 用F-CIP训练智能体可无监督发现平衡、维持可控性等基础行为；搭配简单的前向速度奖励，还能产生跳跃、奔跑等协调步态，而这些通常需要奖励工程才能学会。

Conclusion: F-CIP为无监督强化学习提供了一种无需领域专业知识的IM目标，可自动发现基础行为与复杂步态，减少了奖励设计的人工工程负担。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful paradigm for training agents, yet its success rests on domain expertise of human engineers who design informative reward signals for every new task. Unsupervised RL aims to reduce this engineering with intrinsic motivation (IM): reward signals that emerge from the agent environment interaction itself. Existing IM objectives, however, involve the selection of information variables, which re-introduces domain expertise the field has sought to eliminate. We introduce Forward CIP (F-CIP), an RL-native formulation of the Controllable Information Production (CIP) objective, which is defined by the system's dynamics alone and requires no such selection. We prove that F-CIP is compatible with RL and demonstrate its effectiveness with existing algorithms. Training agents with F-CIP results in unsupervised discovery of primitive behaviors such as balancing and maintaining controllability, which are essential for more complex robot behaviors. Paired with a simple forward-velocity reward, our method produces coordinated gaits such as hopping and running which otherwise require reward engineering to learn.

</details>


### [412] [BranchIP: Learning Adaptive Equivariant Computation for Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02013)
*Laura Zichi,Gil Harari,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Albert Zhu,Menghang Wang,Yoel Zimmermann,H. T. Kung,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BranchIP是一个单模型框架，通过学习自适应张量积计算并配合新的蒸馏损失，对等变MLIP进行加速，在保持物理保真度的同时实现最高2.4倍加速和2.6倍内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）革新了原子尺度建模，但精确处理复杂材料与分子系统需要昂贵模型，限制了模拟的时空尺度。张量积是主要计算瓶颈，而近期出现的基座规模MLIPs进一步加剧了这一挑战。

Method: 提出BranchIP（Branch Interatomic Potential），一个用于学习自适应张量积计算的单模型框架，并使用一种新颖的蒸馏损失进行训练。

Result: 在两个具有物理意义的研究系统（一个多相催化系统和一个质子传导固体酸电解质）上的实验中，BranchIP在不同模型规模下最高实现2.4倍加速，内存占用降低最高2.6倍，同时保持物理保真度。

Conclusion: 学习到的自适应计算还提供了模型可解释性，能够揭示哪些相互作用需要更深的计算，并展示计算深度与化学复杂性及动力学之间的关系。

Abstract: Equivariant machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have revolutionized atomistic modeling, but accurate treatment of complex materials and molecular systems demands expensive models. This limits simulation length- and time-scales, with tensor products a key computational bottleneck. The recent emergence of foundation-scale MLIPs further exacerbates this challenge. We present Branch Interatomic Potential (BranchIP), a single-model framework for learned adaptive tensor product computation, trained with a novel distillation loss. In our experiments on two systems of physical interest, a heterogeneous catalysis system and a proton-conducting solid acid electrolyte, BranchIP accelerates MLIPs across model sizes by up to $2.4\times$ while reducing memory usage by up to $2.6\times$. This is achieved while maintaining physical fidelity. Furthermore, the learned adaptive computation provides model interpretability by revealing which interactions demand deeper computation and showing how computational depth relates to chemical complexity and dynamics.

</details>


### [413] [Relative Transitions, Not Absolute Destinations: A Transfer-and-Ground Framework for Target-Trajectory-Free Human Mobility Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02033)
*Yidi Wang,Yunhe Zhang,Bangchao Deng,Dingqi Yang,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Nomad，一种无需目标城市轨迹的轨迹生成框架，通过“迁移-落地”分解，从源城市学习与城市无关的相对转移先验，并利用目标城市POI坐标与类别将转移落地，实现跨城市轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹生成器依赖部署城市的观测数据，而许多城市恰恰缺乏轨迹数据，尽管其POI及属性可从公开地图获取。因此作者研究“目标无轨迹”生成问题：训练、模型选择与生成阶段均无目标城市轨迹或其衍生统计量。现有生成器预测绝对目的地，将可复用的移动行为与城市特定的POI身份和空间布局纠缠在一起，导致难以跨城市迁移。

Method: Nomad采用“迁移-落地”框架，将“人如何移动”与“移动在何处实现”分离。具体地，用历史条件流匹配模型从源城市轨迹中学习POI上下文间的语义位移、地理位移与时间间隔上的转移先验；推理时，通过行为图和“探索-返回”游走，将采样得到的相对转移落到目标POI地图上。该因式分解使表示层面的可迁移性可直接检验，而无需假设完整移动分布不变。

Result: 在十个城市、14组跨城市迁移上的大量实验表明，Nomad在轨迹保真度和下游效用上均优于适配基线；相比每项指标的最佳基线，分布保真度平均误差降低约15%，下游效用平均误差降低约3%。

Conclusion: 通过将轨迹生成从依赖绝对目的地转为上下文条件下的相对转移，Nomad在不使用任何目标城市轨迹的情况下实现了有效的跨城市轨迹生成，验证了移动行为表征层面的可迁移性，并为缺乏轨迹数据的城市提供了可行的轨迹生成方案。

Abstract: Individual mobility trajectories support urban analysis and location-based services, yet most trajectory generators require observations from their deployment city. This assumption excludes precisely the cities where trajectories are unavailable even though points of interest (POIs) and their attributes can be obtained from public maps. We study target-trajectory-free generation: learning from POIs and trajectories in source cities while utilizing only POI coordinates and categories in a target city, with no target trajectory or trajectory-derived statistic available for training, model selection, or generation. Existing trajectory generators typically predict absolute destinations, entangling reusable movement behavior with city-specific POI identities and spatial layouts. Our core insight is to replace this city-bound output with context-conditioned relative transitions. We propose Nomad, a transfer-and-ground framework that separates learning how people move from determining where those movements are realized. Specifically, a history-conditioned flow-matching model learns from source trajectories a transition prior over semantic displacement between POI contexts, geographic displacement, and elapsed time; at inference, a behavior graph and an exploration--return walk ground sampled transitions onto the target POI map. This factorization enables a direct test of representation level transferability without assuming invariance of the full mobility distribution. Extensive experiments across ten cities and 14 transfers show that Nomad outperforms adaptation baselines in trajectory fidelity and downstream utility, lowering the average error over the best baseline of each metric by about 15% in distributional fidelity and about 3% in downstream utility.

</details>


### [414] [Foundations without Fundamentals: Zero-Shot Blind Spots in Time Series FMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02058)
*Nafiseh Ghoroghchian,Haipeng Zhang,Shuyi Han,Alex Labach,George Stein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimpleTimeBench，用"单元测试"式的诊断基准检验时间序列基础模型（TSFM）对基本时序逻辑（趋势、周期、领先指标协变量）的掌握程度，发现主流多元 TSFM 在零样本下表现欠佳，微调亦会牺牲其他基本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 TSFM 在广泛基准上取得成功，但其是否真正内化了基本时序逻辑（尤其是在有外生协变量支持的场景下）仍缺乏检验。作者希望用近乎"平凡可解"的场景来探测模型是否具备应有的归纳偏置。

Method: 构建 SimpleTimeBench：一个包含单变量与多变量的诊断性"单元测试"套件，覆盖单调趋势、周期信号、领先指标协变量等基本原语（这些场景理论上应可近乎完美预测）。据此对 Chronos-2、Moirai、Toto 等主流多元 TSFM 进行零样本评测，并考察 Chronos-2 微调后的表现迁移，最后在真实传感器预测数据上验证。

Result: 主流多元 TSFM（Chronos-2、Moirai、Toto）在这些简单输入上常给出次优的零样本预测；微调虽能改善 Chronos-2 在特定任务上的表现，却会削弱其在其他基本模式上的能力，而非提升可泛化的基础能力。在真实世界传感器预测中这些失败同样存在，模型持续未能充分利用观测协变量中的领先指标。

Conclusion: 预训练规模与基本时序推理能力之间存在落差，当前 TSFM 可能缺乏捕捉简单可预测函数所需的归纳偏置；这种缺陷并非仅存在于合成场景，而是限制了多元模型的实际可用性与可靠性。

Abstract: Despite the success of Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) on broad benchmarks, their ability to internalize basic temporal logic, especially in settings supported by exogenous covariates, remains under-examined. We introduce SimpleTimeBench, a diagnostic univariate and multivariate "unit test" suite for primitives such as monotonic trends, periodic signals and leading indicator covariates, scenarios where near-perfect forecasts should be trivial. Surprisingly, prominent multivariate TSFMs (Chronos-2, Moirai and Toto) frequently produce suboptimal zero-shot forecasts for these inputs. While fine-tuning Chronos-2 improves its behaviour on specific tasks, we show that this adaptation degrades performance on other fundamental patterns rather than enhancing its generalizable foundational capabilities. This reveals a gap between pre-training scale and basic temporal reasoning, suggesting that current TSFMs could potentially lack the inductive biases needed to capture simple predictable functions. We further demonstrate that these failures are not merely synthetic curiosities: they persist in real-world sensor forecasting, where TSFMs consistently underutilize leading indicators available in observed covariates. This inability to capture simple relationships limits the practical utility and reliability of current multivariate models.

</details>


### [415] [Learn the Directions, Normalize the Gains: Post-Training Normalization for LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02067)
*Zailong Tian,Yanzhe Chen,Zhuoheng Han,Houfeng Wang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了LoRA微调中的"适应失衡"问题，即少数奇异方向主导更新，并提出无需训练的后处理归一化方法LoRA-Norm，通过谱重平衡和核范数恢复来均衡增益，提升专业化和能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽能高效实现任务专业化，但其学到的更新可能损害目标任务之外的能力。作者发现"适应失衡"现象：少数奇异方向主导了训练更新，导致性能对增益分配方式非常敏感。作者认为，学习"在哪里适应"并不能保证适应增益被良好平衡，因此需要一种方法来重新平衡增益。

Method: 提出LoRA-Norm，一种训练后归一化方法，在保留已学方向的同时重新平衡其增益。它结合了两个部分：（1）谱重平衡（spectral rebalancing），对奇异值施加固定的非线性变换；（2）核范数恢复（nuclear-norm restoration），保持原始的总谱质量。该方法无需校准数据、无需额外训练，也不引入推理开销。

Result: 在两个骨干网络和三个适应任务上，LoRA-Norm提升了平均专业化和能力保持，在这两项指标上均优于所评估的后处理谱剪枝和梯度引导编辑配置。更强的函数均衡化并未带来一致的额外收益。

Conclusion: 平衡适配器增益与均衡其响应是两个不同的目标。LoRA-Norm通过重平衡增益而非完全均衡响应，有效缓解适应失衡，兼顾专业化与能力保持。

Abstract: While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient task specialization, its learned updates can compromise capabilities beyond the target task. We identify \textbf{adaptation imbalance}: a few singular directions dominate the trained update, leaving its performance sensitive to how gains are allocated.
